In this work, we resolve this problem by determining the optimal scaling of the unitary $k$-design formation rate for $k\leq O(\sqrt{\log N})$ across a wide range of circuit geometries, including lattices, expander graphs, and all-to-all interactions. In particular, for $k\geq 2$, we find that the formation rate is governed by a two-particle encounter rate---the rate at which two random walkers encounter each other in a given circuit geometry. This encounter rate is parametrically smaller than the single-particle transport rate in many geometries, thereby disproving the previous conjecture. We further establish that this encounter mechanism indeed sets the design formation rate by deriving its matching lower bound with a novel proof technique based on an auxiliary subgroup ensemble. Although this encounter mode imposes a robust bottleneck on design formation in $U(1)$-symmetric random circuits, we also show that this bottleneck can be circumvented by using symmetry-breaking local gates and provide an explicit protocol that achieves faster design formation. These results establish a new picture of randomness formation under conservation laws and provide guiding principles for characterizing and efficiently generating symmetry-constrained randomness.

</details>


### [95] [Excitation gap of a blockade structure with $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39475)
*Simon Fell,Tobias F. Maier,Hans Peter Büchler,Nicolai Lang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Rydberg平台启发的双体阻塞哈密顿量在热力学极限下存在有限激发间隙，并推广至实现非阿贝尔量子双重相的一类阻塞哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统在热力学极限下谱隙的严格证明极为困难，但对量子物相分类具有根本重要性。既有研究提出了Rydberg启发的阻塞哈密顿量，其基态处于toric code拓扑有序相，但缺乏严格的谱隙证明。

Method: 证明建立在已知的谱隙稳定性结果之上，并利用所研究模型的局域对称性。

Result: 证明了该哈密顿量在热力学极限下存在有限激发间隙；该结果同样适用于实现非阿贝尔量子双重相的一类更广泛的阻塞哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 为Rydberg启发的拓扑有序及非阿贝尔量子双重相模型提供了严格的有限谱隙保证，有助于量子物相的严格分类。

Abstract: Mathematically rigorous statements on the spectral gap of quantum many-body systems in the thermodynamic limit are notoriously difficult to prove -- yet they are of fundamental importance for classifying quantum phases of matter. Here we prove the existence of a finite excitation gap for a particular Hamiltonian which was proposed in [T. F. Maier et al., PRX Quantum 6, 030340 (2025)] and is motivated by the Rydberg platform. The Hamiltonian exhibits only two-body blockade interactions between two-level systems and has a topologically ordered ground state in the toric code phase. We show that our result also applies to a broader class of blockade Hamiltonians which realize non-Abelian quantum double phases and were proposed in [H. P. Büchler et al., Phys. Rev. B 114, 065113 (2026)]. The proof builds on known gap stability results and exploits the local symmetry of the studied models.

</details>


### [96] [Generation of large-amplitude squeezed cat states with near-unity efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39482)
*Simon K. Yung,Matthew S. Winnel,Timothy C. Ralph,Jie Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种全光学方案，利用量子非破坏相互作用与光子数测量，以接近确定性的概率制备大振幅猫态，为GKP编码的玻色子量子纠错提供高质量资源态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP编码是连续变量容错量子计算的重要候选，但光子架构下高质量资源态制备困难：需从多个大振幅猫态确定性合成，而现有光学猫态制备方法尺寸有限且高度概率性。因此需要一种能高效制备大振幅猫态的方案。

Method: 采用全光学方案，基于量子非破坏（QND）相互作用和光子数测量来制备大振幅猫态。关键设计是不对特定光子数结果进行后选择：所有测量结果都累加到累积光子数中，重复该协议直至达到所需阈值。

Result: 该方案能以接近单位的概率制备大振幅猫态，且对实际水平的光子损耗（光学系统主要误差来源）具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了主动高斯操作在态制备中的能力，为利用玻色子编码实现高效量子纠错铺平了道路。

Abstract: The Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill encoding has emerged as a leading candidate for fault-tolerant quantum computation with continuous variables. For photonic architectures, the major challenge is preparing high-quality resource states, which can be deterministically synthesised from many large-amplitude cat states. Thus far, only modest-sized optical cat states have been prepared experimentally, and the implemented methods are highly probabilistic. We propose an all-optical scheme utilising quantum non-demolition interactions and photon-number measurements to prepare large-amplitude cat states with near-unity probability. Importantly, no particular photon-number outcome is postselected: all outcomes contribute to the accumulated photon number, with the protocol repeated until a required threshold is reached. We demonstrate that the scheme is robust to realistic levels of photon-loss, the dominant source of error in optical systems. Our results highlight the power of active Gaussian operations for state preparation and pave the way for efficient quantum error correction using bosonic codes.

</details>


### [97] [On quantum interactive proofs with a laconic prover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39495)
*Zihan Hu,Yupan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入类 QIP_{ℓ-bit}(2)，研究简洁证明者的两消息量子交互证明系统，给出其由多状态可区分性的完全刻画，证明 QSD 是 QIP_bit-完全的、其位于 QSZK 之上，并刻画了塌缩为 QSZK 的易情形，同时改进极化技术（解决 Sahai-Vadhan 公开问题），并证明量子公共硬币使交互无效（qc-QAM 塌缩到 BQP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典简洁证明者（Goldreich-Vadhan-Wigderson）对应对数通信可验证问题；量子两消息版本中，单比特证明者响应即含 QSZK，而将验证者问题限制为经典公共硬币则塌缩到 BQP（Beigi-Shor-Watrous）。论文旨在系统刻画"证明者响应长度 ℓ 有限"这一介于二者之间的量子交互证明类的计算能力与结构。

Method: 定义类 QIP_{ℓ-bit}(2)（ℓ 为证明者响应长度）及其带间隙版本 QIP_{ℓ-bit}[2,c,s]；引入 Multi-State Distinguishability 作为完全问题刻画该类；通过从 QIP_{ℓ-bit}[2,c,s] 到 QIP_bit 的答案压缩、以及改进的极化（polarization）技术分析易情形；并研究验证者问题恰为 EPR 对之半的 qc-QAM[ℓ] 子类。

Result: (1) 用 Multi-State Distinguishability 完全刻画 QIP_{ℓ-bit}(2)，QSD 为 QIP_bit-完全且 QSZK-难，故 ℓ≥2 时该类位于"略高于 QSZK"的位置；(2) 给出塌缩为 QSZK 的易情形：当 a(n)-b(n)≥1/O(log n) 时 QSD[a,b]（及 QIP_bit[a,b]）∈QSZK，并结合答案压缩得到 2c>(1+2^{ℓ/2})s 时的另一易情形；改进的极化同时适用于 SD 与 SZK，解决 Sahai-Vadhan 的公开问题；(3) 常数间隙下 qc-QAM[O(√log n)]⊆BQP，说明量子公共硬币使交互失效。

Conclusion: 简明证明者的量子两消息交互证明系统由多状态可区分性刻画，ℓ≥2 时严格位于 QSZK 之上；多数参数区间塌缩回 QSZK，且改进极化统一了 QSD/SD 与 QSZK/SZK 的分析，推进了经典公开问题；量子公共硬币（EPR 半对问题）进一步使交互无效，使对应类落入 BQP。

Abstract: Interactive proof systems with a laconic prover, studied by Goldreich, Vadhan, and Wigderson (CC, 2002), capture problems verifiable with logarithmic prover communication in the classical setting. For two-message quantum analogs, even a single-bit prover response contains quantum statistical zero-knowledge ($\sf QSZK$), introduced by Watrous (FOCS 2002). However, restricting the verifier's question to classical public coins collapses the corresponding class to $\sf BQP$, as shown by Beigi, Shor, and Watrous (ToC, 2011).
  We further study two-message quantum interactive proof systems with a laconic prover. To this end, we introduce the class ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$, where $\ell$ is the length of the prover's response, and establish:
  1. A natural complete characterization of ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$ by Multi-State Distinguishability. In particular, Quantum State Distinguishability (QSD) is ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}$-complete. Since QSD is $\sf QSZK$-hard, our result places ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$, for $\ell\geq 2$, in a landscape "just above" $\sf QSZK$.
  2. Easy regimes for ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$ collapsing to $\sf QSZK$. We prove that QSD$[a,b]$ (and thus ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}[a,b]$) is in $\sf QSZK$ when $a(n)-b(n)\geq 1/O(\log n)$, and combine this with an answer compression from ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}[2,c,s]$ to ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}$ to obtain another easy regime when $2c>(1+2^{\ell/2})s$. Remarkably, our improved polarization applies to SD and $\sf SZK$, resolving an open problem in Sahai and Vadhan (JACM, 2003).
  3. Quantum public coins also make the interaction useless: ${\sf qc}\text{-}{\sf QAM}[O(\sqrt{\log{n}})]$ with constant gap is in $\sf BQP$, where ${\sf qc}\text{-}{\sf QAM}[\ell]$ is a subclass of ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$ in which the verifier's question is exactly halves of EPR pairs.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum double lock-in detection via sequential orthogonal quantum mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39498)
*Min Zhuang,Sijie Chen,Jiahao Huang,Chaohong Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种仅用单次量子干涉仪即可实现量子双锁相检测的通用协议，基于顺序正交周期多脉冲序列，可从振荡信号中提取振幅、频率和初相位的完整信息；当输入为GHZ态并施以两种相互作用操作时，三者的测量精度均可逼近海森堡极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度振荡信号测量是基础科学与实用技术中的普遍需求。常规量子双锁相检测需两次独立的量子干涉测量，每次均须进行态制备与读出，而实际实验中态制备与读出耗时不可忽略（态制备甚至长于传感时间）。因此，如何在仅用单次量子干涉仪的条件下仍能提取振幅、频率与初相位的完整信息，以节省实验资源，是一个具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出一种通用协议：在顺序正交周期多脉冲序列下，仅通过单次量子干涉仪实现量子双锁相检测。特别地，以Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger（GHZ）态作为输入态，并在干涉测量过程中施加两种基于相互作用的操作。

Result: 在GHZ输入态与两种相互作用操作的条件下，频率、振幅和初相位的测量精度均可逼近海森堡极限。

Conclusion: 该研究为以单次量子干涉仪测量振荡信号开辟了新途径，并为实现交替信号的海森堡极限探测提供了可行方法。

Abstract: High-precision measurement of oscillating signal is a ubiquitous issue in fundamental science and a critical task in practical technologies. In quantum metrology, quantum lock-in detection provide an efficient method for measuring such signal. In general, when the initial phase of the oscillating signal is unknown,quantum double lock-in detection can effectively extract complete information about the signal's amplitude, frequency, and initial phase. Conventional quantum double lock-in detection requires two individual quantum interferometry, each of which must undergo state preparation and readout. However, the time for state preparation and readout need not be negligible in practical experiments. In particular, the time for state preparation is longer than the time for sensing. To save experimental resources, it is challenging to achieve quantum double lock-in detection just via a single quantum interferometry while still extracting complete information about the signal's amplitude, frequency, and initial phase. Here, we present a general protocol for achieving a quantum double lock-in detection just via a single quantum interferometry under a sequential orthogonal periodic multipulse sequences. In particular, if the input state is a Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger state and two interaction-based operations are applied during interferometry, the measurement precisions for frequency, amplitude, and initial phase can both approach the Heisenberg limit. Our study paves a new way for measuring oscillating signals with a single quantum interferometry, and provides a feasible method for achieving Heisenberg-limited detection of alternating signals.

</details>


### [99] [Layer codes as quantum memories: syndrome extraction, thresholds and idle robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39522)
*Zhu Sun,Zhenyu Cai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在电路级噪声下将层码（layer code）作为主动存储进行评估：提出免解码器的调度器生成综合征提取电路，在十八种层码上与同距离表面码对比，发现层码在空闲鲁棒性与阈值上具优势，但开销更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层码是三维持局CSS码（校验权重至多六），由CSS输入码与未旋转表面码片准级联得到。已有结论仅描述码本身，而非测量它的电路。作者希望评估层码在电路级噪声下作为主动存储（active memory）的真实性能，这需要先构造综合征提取电路。

Method: 耦合各片块的结稳定子（junction stabilizer）排除了借用表面码片CNOT调度的可能。作者提出一种免解码器调度器：先约束钩子传播（hook propagation），再压缩深度；据此为四种输入码、共十八种层码生成电路，并与同距离表面码在相同仿真与解码方式下比较。此外对超出二维的跨平面CNOT代价进行建模分析。

Result: 在[[4,2,2]]输入码上，凡能承担电路距离证明之处，电路均不因钩子错误损失距离；相较通用CSS码调度器，逻辑错误率更低，而每轮CNOT深度约为其一半。主要族（[[4,2,2]]输入码）达到电路级阈值5.06(7)×10⁻³。空闲鲁棒性上，同距离层码可容忍的综合征提取间隔比旋转表面码长1.7–3.0倍，代价是单位时间操作数高1.7–5.0倍、每逻辑比特物理比特数约多五倍。跨平面CNOT建模显示其速度远比保真度重要。

Conclusion: 层码作为主动存储相较表面码在空闲容忍度与阈值上更优，但以显著的操作率与量子比特开销为代价；跨平面CNOT的速度而非保真度是决定其成本的关键。

Abstract: Layer codes are three-dimensional local CSS codes of check weight at most six, obtained by quasi-concatenating a CSS input code with unrotated surface code patches. What is established about them describes the code, not the circuit that measures it. In this work, we evaluate them as active memories under circuit-level noise. Doing so first requires a syndrome extraction circuit, and the junction stabilizers that couple the patches rule out borrowing a surface code patch's CNOT schedule. We present a decoder-free scheduler that constrains hook propagation first and compresses depth afterwards, generate circuits for eighteen layer codes across four input codes, and compare them with surface codes of the same distance, simulated and decoded in the same way. The circuits lose no distance to hook errors on [[4,2,2]]-input codes wherever a circuit-distance proof is affordable, and reach a lower logical error rate than those of a general-purpose scheduler for CSS codes at roughly half its CNOT depth per round. Our primary family built from a [[4,2,2]] input code reaches a circuit-level threshold of $5.06(7)\times 10^{-3}$. The layer code outperforms the surface code in terms of idle robustness: at the same distance it tolerates an interval between syndrome extraction rounds 1.7-3.0 times longer than the rotated surface code. This comes at the cost of 1.7-5.0 times more operations per unit time and approximately five times more physical qubits, both per logical qubit. Finally, we model the cost of the beyond-2D cross-plane CNOTs that the construction demands, and find that their speed matters far more than their fidelity.

</details>


### [100] [Optimal local recovery cannot alter critical orthogonality exponents in quantum spin environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39528)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Jun Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了正基态局域振幅比界与有限区域擦除下保真度的显式反向比较；对连通的临界Ising链给出精确格点计算，得到振幅指数1/8，并研究了最优控制、Cauchy乘积渐近、双自旋优化与干涉测量方案，另给出有限中介定理、基态制备Ramsey界及反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限区域擦除下量子基态信息的恢复能力，刻画正基态振幅比与保真度的界，并厘清最优控制与投影态推断的适用范围，为临界Ising链等模型的局部信息压缩与恢复提供精确基准。

Method: 证明局域振幅比界与保真度反向比较；对连通临界Ising链进行精确格点计算；分析任意最优控制下振幅指数的保持性及o(log N)自旋上的对数指数；推导有限Cauchy乘积及均匀条带渐近；给出精确双自旋优化与干涉测量恢复方案；建立有限中介定理以量化局部压缩与解码器；补充基态制备Ramsey界、投影态推断反例及完整哈密顿量独立计算。

Result: 临界Ising链的振幅指数为1/8，且在任意固定自旋集上的最优控制下保持不变，在o(log N)自旋上对数指数层面亦保持；给出有限Cauchy乘积的均匀条带渐近、精确双自旋优化结果及可测恢复的干涉协议；有限中介定理实现局部压缩并精确保留环境其余部分；同时得到基态制备Ramsey界和更强投影态推断的反例。

Conclusion: 结果给出了擦除恢复与振幅比之间的定性与定量联系，确立了临界Ising链振幅指数1/8的稳健性，并通过有限中介定理、Ramsey界与反例界定了相关推断的边界。

Abstract: We prove a local amplitude-ratio bound for positive ground states and an explicit reverse comparison for fidelity under finite-region erasure. An exact lattice calculation for a connected critical Ising chain gives amplitude exponent $1/8$, preserved by arbitrary optimal control on any fixed spin set and, at the level of logarithmic exponents, on $o(\log N)$ spins. We derive the finite Cauchy product and its uniform strip asymptotics, and document exact two-spin optimization and an interferometric protocol for measuring the recovery. A separate finite-mediator theorem quantifies local compression and a decoder while retaining the rest of the environment exactly. We also give a ground-preparation Ramsey bound, counterexamples to stronger projected-state inferences, and independent full-Hamiltonian calculations.

</details>


### [101] [Boron vacancies in bulk h-BN created by high-energy He+ irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39535)
*P. G. Vilyuzhanina,S. V. Bolshedvorskii,R. Isayev,A. S. Doroshkevich,A. A. Tatarinova,V. V. Soshenko,I. S. Cojocaru,A. M. Kozodaev,S. M. Drofa,A. Chernyavskiy,N. I. Salangin,A. N. Smolyaninov,A. Y. Kuntsevich,A. V. Akimov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作通过约1 MeV氦离子注入，在块体六方氮化硼中成功制备出带负电的硼空位色心，并用光致发光与光探测磁共振予以确认，同时测量了色心的深度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方氮化硼中的带负电硼空位色心在磁测量等方面具有潜力，但可靠地产生硼空位仍具挑战，因此需要一种可控、可重复的制备方法。

Method: 采用约1 MeV的氦离子注入块体h-BN以产生带负电硼空位色心；通过特征光致发光和光探测磁共振进行表征；并沿c轴测量色心深度，与SRIM（Stopping and Range of Ions in Matter）模拟预测进行比较。

Result: 观测到特征光致发光和光探测磁共振信号，共振线宽为180 MHz，并呈现符合预期的塞曼频移；实测色心深度与SRIM预测在报道的不确定度范围内一致（1σ区间重叠），但实验值深度更大、分布更宽。

Conclusion: 氦离子注入可作为一种在块体h-BN中可靠制备带负电硼空位色心的有效途径，其磁共振特性与深度分布均得到验证，为二维材料色心在传感等领域的应用提供了基础。

Abstract: While color centers in diamond and other three-dimensional crystals are nowadays key elements of several types of sensors, color centers in two-dimensional materials are rapidly developing and promise high-tech applications. One of the interesting centers in 2D materials is the negatively charged boron vacancy in hexagonal boron nitride (h-BN), which has already shown some potential for magnetometry, but the reliable creation of boron vacancies remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate the fabrication of negatively charged boron vacancy color centers in bulk h-BN via implantation of helium ions at ~1 MeV, as confirmed by characteristic photoluminescence and optically detected magnetic resonance. The resonance has a width of 180 MHz and exhibits the expected Zeeman shift of the resonance lines. The depth of the color center along the c-axis of the h-BN crystal was measured and compared with predictions from Stopping and Range of Ions in Matter modeling. The experimental value and the modeled prediction are consistent within the reported uncertainties, as their 1σ intervals overlap, although the experiment shows a greater depth and a wider distribution.

</details>


### [102] [Exact Maximum Likelihood Decoding beyond Treewidth via Rank-Decomposition Dynamic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39556)
*Bin Cheng,Feng Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于秩分解动态规划（Rank DP）的量子纠错最大似然解码算法，将解码配分函数表示为独立局部故障因子的二次幂和，复杂度对输入规模多项式、对Tanner图秩宽度指数，对特定码族实现多项式时间解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然解码在随机Pauli噪声下对量子纠错是渐进最优策略，但计算逻辑类概率困难，主流精确张量网络收缩方法时间复杂度随树宽度指数增长，亟需更高效的精确解码途径。

Method: 将解码配分函数表达为独立局部故障因子的二次幂和，并应用秩分解动态规划（Rank DP）；经高斯消元后，得到关于输入规模多项式时间、关于故障划分下Tanner图秩宽度指数的精确求值器（含分解构造）；引入非负实现以给出浮点相对误差界。

Result: 在独立单量子比特Pauli噪声下，对打孔量子Reed-Muller码和距离增长的Steane级联族实现多项式时间解码，而对应张量网络标准收缩需超多项式时间；数值实验在码容量与电路级实例上相对所测张量网络实现具有运行时间优势，可对最多1,023量子比特的Reed-Muller码完成完整似然评估，并用于从校验子学习电路噪声参数、评估稀有后选择概率与量化解码器最优性差距。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用代数结构进行量子解码与噪声刻画开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Maximum-likelihood decoding provides an optimal decoding strategy for quantum error correction under stochastic Pauli noise. However, computing logical-class probabilities is challenging, and the leading exact tensor-network contraction requires time exponential in treewidth. In this work, we introduce a new decoding algorithm based on rank-decomposition dynamic programming (Rank DP). We express decoding partition functions with independent local fault factors as quadratic sums of powers and apply Rank DP. The resulting exact evaluator has arithmetic complexity polynomial in the input size and exponential in the Tanner graph's rank-width with respect to the fault partition, including decomposition construction. After Gaussian elimination, it gives polynomial-time decoding for punctured quantum Reed-Muller codes and a Steane-concatenated family with growing distance under independent single-qubit Pauli noise. Standard contraction of the corresponding tensor networks requires superpolynomial time. A nonnegative realization gives relative floating-point error bounds under explicit arithmetic assumptions. Numerical experiments demonstrate runtime advantages over the tested tensor-network implementations on selected code-capacity and circuit-level instances, with full likelihood evaluation for Reed-Muller codes up to $1{,}023$ qubits. We further use these likelihoods to learn circuit noise parameters from syndromes, evaluate rare postselection probabilities, and quantify decoder optimality gaps. Our work opens new avenues for exploiting algebraic structure in quantum decoding and noise characterization.

</details>


### [103] [Rate 1/5 Non-Malleable Codes against Entangled Split-State Tampering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39589)
*Naresh Goud Boddu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了针对经典消息的高效信息论不可延展码，可抵抗两方不通信、且可预共享纠缠的局部量子篡改；在任意足够小的固定 ξ>0 下，当第一份额长度 n 充分大时码率至少为 1/5-ξ，具完美正确性与 2^{-n^{Ω(1)}} 误差，对每条消息均安全且每次攻击存在单一与消息无关的模拟器，解决了纠缠二分态模型中最坏情形经典消息的常数率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在纠缠二分态（two-split-state）模型下，Batra、Boddu 与 Jain 的基于置换的构造只能对均匀随机消息达到接近 1/5 的码率；对最坏情形经典消息能否实现常数率尚属未解问题，因此本文旨在解决该常数率问题。

Method: 沿用 Batra-Boddu-Jain 的置换型二分构造架构，将其置换的均匀消息输入替换为“指定消息拼接新鲜均匀填充”；核心贡献是针对该修改给出最坏情形安全归约。

Result: 对任意足够小的固定 ξ>0 及所有充分大的第一份额长度 n，码率至少 1/5-ξ，完美正确，误差 2^{-n^{Ω(1)}}；安全性对每条消息成立，且每次攻击存在单一与消息无关的模拟器。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了纠缠二分态模型中最坏情形经典消息的常数率问题，表明所提出的拼接填充修改可在保持原架构与码率的同时，将安全保证从均匀随机消息推广到最坏情形经典消息。

Abstract: We construct efficient information-theoretic non-malleable codes for classical messages that are secure against two noncommunicating local quantum tampering operations with arbitrary pre-shared entanglement. For every sufficiently small fixed $ξ>0$ and all sufficiently large first-share lengths $n$, the codes have rate at least $1/5-ξ$, perfect correctness, and error $2^{-n^{Ω(1)}}$. Security holds for every message, with a single message-independent simulator for each attack. This resolves the constant-rate question for worst-case classical messages in the entangled two-split-state model.
  Our construction builds on the permutation-based two-split construction of Batra, Boddu, and Jain, which achieves rate approaching $1/5$ for uniformly random messages. We retain their architecture but replace the uniform message input to the permutation with a prescribed message concatenated with fresh uniform padding. Our main contribution is a worst-case security reduction for this modification.

</details>


### [104] [The Complexity of Single-Interaction Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39592)
*Elad Hecker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将任意有限局部相互作用字母表精确编译为单一固定相互作用，建立SIH模型，证明其可再现原哈密顿量的低能谱，并给出从k-LH到SIH_{3k+3}的通用多项式归约，进而得到SIH_3的QMA完全性及若干经典变体的硬度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Local Hamiltonian问题在更严格限制下的复杂度：每个局部项都是同一固定相互作用的无权出现。核心疑问是，独立选取的局部矩阵和独立可调的耦合强度对于广泛的哈密顿量复杂度现象是否必要。

Method: 构造一个精确编译器，将任意有限局部相互作用字母表转换为单一相互作用；在指定辅助子空间上精确复现源哈密顿量，并在选定分离尺度下保持完整低能谱及其重数。结合有限字母表归一化，给出从k-Local Hamiltonian到SIH_{3k+3}的多项式时间归约（允许已知全局能量尺度与逆多项式近似）；并通过更精细的构造处理几何局域、有界度与有界相互作用范围，以及stoquastic情形。

Result: 得到SIH_3的QMA完全性，以及几何局域、有界度、有界范围的SIH_8的QMA完全性；在stoquastic情形下证明Stoq-SIH_3的StoqMA完全性与1-Pinned Stoq-SIH_4的QMA完全性；还给出依赖于系统规模的一致相互作用硬度结果，并导出Hamiltonian量子PCP猜想、无阻挫、指数精度与引导Local Hamiltonian问题的单一相互作用表述。

Conclusion: 单一固定相互作用即可承载广泛的哈密顿量复杂度现象；独立选择的局部矩阵与独立可调耦合强度并非这些现象的必要条件。

Abstract: We study the complexity of Local Hamiltonian problems under the restriction that every local term is an unweighted occurrence of the same fixed interaction. In the resulting Single-Interaction Hamiltonian (SIH) model, once the interaction is fixed, the Hamiltonian is specified entirely by its ordered interaction hypergraph.
  Our main technical tool is an exact compiler that converts any fixed finite alphabet of local interactions into a single interaction. On a designated auxiliary subspace, the compiled Hamiltonian reproduces the source Hamiltonian exactly, while the complete spectrum below a chosen separation scale is preserved with multiplicity. Combined with a finite-alphabet normalization, this gives a universal polynomial-time reduction from $k$-Local Hamiltonian to SIH$_{3k+3}$ up to a known global energy scale and inverse-polynomial approximation.
  Sharper constructions give QMA-completeness of SIH$_3$ and of geometrically local SIH$_8$ with bounded degree and interaction range. In the stoquastic setting, we obtain StoqMA-completeness of Stoq-SIH$_3$ and QMA-completeness of 1-Pinned Stoq-SIH$_4$. We also establish hardness results for uniformly system-size-dependent interactions and derive single-interaction formulations of the Hamiltonian quantum PCP conjecture, frustration-free, exponentially precise, and guided Local Hamiltonian problems. These results show that independently chosen local matrices and independently tunable coupling strengths are not necessary for a broad range of Hamiltonian-complexity phenomena.

</details>


### [105] [Thermodynamics of Ahn--Doherty--Landahl Continuous Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39597)
*Juan Garcia-Nila,Pedro B. Melo,Lucas Johns*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将基于测量的连续量子纠错（CQEC）建模为信息引擎，区分系统侧与控制器侧的功率消耗，并在单比特ADL协议及三比特重复码中揭示逻辑稳定与能量资源之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续量子纠错用连续综合征提取与实时哈密顿反馈取代离散测量与恢复操作，但其所需的热力学资源尚不明确，本文旨在量化该过程的能量代价。

Method: 将基于测量的连续量子纠错表述为信息引擎，区分系统侧功率（反馈场与被保护系统间能量转移）与控制器侧功率（反馈哈密顿量）。框架先在单比特Ahn-Doherty-Landahl（ADL）协议中建立，再扩展到连续稳定子监测下的三比特重复码，并进行数值模拟。

Result: 随反馈强度增大，稳态保真度提高、条件态熵降低；但在保真度开始饱和后，两项能量贡献的量值仍持续增大。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了逻辑稳定与连续反馈所需能量资源之间的直接权衡，与量子测量和量子Zeno稳定中已有的能量—精度权衡密切相关。

Abstract: Continuous quantum error correction (CQEC) replaces discrete syndrome measurements and recovery operations with continuous syndrome extraction and real-time Hamiltonian feedback. Here we investigate the thermodynamic resources required by this process by formulating measurement-based continuous quantum error correction as an information engine. We distinguish the system-side power associated with energy transfer between the feedback field and the protected system from the controller-side power associated with the feedback Hamiltonian. The framework is first developed for the one-qubit Ahn-Doherty-Landahl (ADL) protocol and subsequently extended to the three-qubit repetition code under continuous stabilizer monitoring. Numerical simulations show that increasing the feedback strength improves the steady-state fidelity and reduces the conditional-state entropy, while both energetic contributions continue to increase in magnitude after the fidelity begins to saturate. These results expose a direct tradeoff between logical stabilization and the energetic resources required for continuous feedback, closely related to previously established energy--precision tradeoffs in quantum measurement and quantum-Zeno stabilization.

</details>


### [106] [Warm-Start Iterative QITE for Distribution Network Reconfiguration via Branch-Exchange Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39602)
*Noah Crum,Pete Pritchard,Tanner Rase,Phillip C. Lotshaw,Shaked Regev,Claudio Girotto,Ananth Kaushik,Paul Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子-经典算法用于配电网重构，结合径向保持的分支交换编码与迭代热启动量子虚时演化，以最小化有功线损，并在10个测试用例上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网重构是配电网上的组合优化问题，目标是最小化有功线损。传统方法面对大规模问题存在求解困难，需要借助量子-经典混合方法提升求解能力。

Method: 采用固定宽度二进制表示顺序分支交换动作的径向保持编码，确保每个寄存器结果都解码为径向配置；量子子程序由拟合到经典求解的交流潮流（ACPF）标签的代理模型提供信息，并使用迭代热启动量子虚时演化。还提供两种缩减问题规模的方法以扩展至更大网络。在预算内的ACPF求解和电路采样耗尽后，返回已见过的最低损耗配置。

Result: 在十个测试用例（含七个常用基准案例及三个修改案例）上进行实验，六个案例成功达到最小损耗配置，其余四个案例的损耗比参考最小损耗高0.2%至2.18%。

Conclusion: 该混合量子-经典算法结合径向保持编码与热启动量子虚时演化，能有效求解配电网重构问题，并通过问题规模缩减方法具备扩展到更大网络的潜力。

Abstract: We present a hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for distribution network reconfiguration, a combinatorial optimization problem on power distribution networks, that combines a radiality preserving branch-exchange encoding with iterative warm-start quantum imaginary-time evolution to minimize active line losses. The encoding uses a fixed-width binary representation of sequential branch-exchange actions, ensuring that every register outcome decodes to a radial configuration. The quantum subroutine is informed by a surrogate model fit to classically-solved alternating current power flow (ACPF) labels. After a set budget of ACPF solves and circuit samples is exhausted, the algorithm returns the lowest-loss configuration seen. We employ our algorithm on ten test cases, including seven commonly-used benchmark cases and three cases we modified from these and similar standard systems. We include two methods to reduce problem size, which enable extensions of the algorithm to larger networks. We successfully reach minimal-loss configurations for six cases and find configurations with losses from 0.2 percent to 2.18 percent above the reported reference minimal loss for the other four.

</details>


### [107] [Weakly Measured Loops for Quantum Amplitude Amplification: Oracle Savings and Adaptive Search with Unknown Target Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39621)
*Wang Fang,Chris Heunen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一族将标准Grover旋转与可调弱测量交替循环执行的量子振幅放大算法。失败后不重启，而是从测量后状态继续迭代，从而在保持Θ(1/√p)搜索规模的同时获得可证明的oracle开销节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Grover算法及优化重启Grover在高迭代次数下存在固定常数开销，作者希望引入弱测量作为显式定点控制机制，在不改变Θ(1/√p)搜索规模的前提下减少期望Grover迭代次数，并系统分析已知p、仅知下界p0以及完全未知p三种情形。

Method: 将普通Grover旋转与可调弱测量交错置于循环中：成功测量直接输出目标态并终止；失败则从测量后状态继续循环。针对角度接近π/4的状态给出精确弱测量条件循环；在仅知0 < p0 ≤ p < 1/2时，用离散Lyapunov方程求固定测量强度下的精确期望oracle成本及闭式最优强度；对完全未知p，识别κ(t)=Θ(1/t)为临界尺度，并分析κ_b(t)=min{1/2, b/t}，对每个固定b>2严格推导闭式Gamma函数表达式C(b)。

Result: 已知目标概率p且状态接近π/4时，精确弱测量条件循环的期望Grover迭代次数为(π/8+1/4+o(1))/√p，优于标准Grover常数π/4及优化重启Grover，并达到连续角度分析的下确界。仅知下界p0时，使用由p0选出的最优强度，期望迭代次数至多为(1/(2√2))(1/√p+1/√p0)，且在承诺边界p=p0处给出主导常数1/√2。完全未知p时，每个固定b>2对应期望迭代(C(b)/2+o(1))/√p；数值最小化得b≈5.2、C(b)/2≈1.01。

Conclusion: 弱测量可在保持Θ(1/√p)量子搜索规模的同时，提供显式定点控制机制并带来可证明的oracle节省。结果为已知、部分已知和完全未知目标概率三类场景给出了精确或闭式的最优测量强度与期望迭代开销。

Abstract: We present a family of quantum amplitude amplification algorithms that interleave ordinary Grover rotations with tunable weak measurements in loops. A successful measurement yields the target state and stops the algorithm, while after a failure, the loop resumes from the post-measurement state without restarting. We express the leading costs in expected Grover iterations as $p \to 0$. For a known target probability $p$, an exact weak measurement-conditioned loop for states near the angle $π/4$ uses $(π/8+1/4+o(1))/\sqrt{p}$ iterations, improving on the standard Grover constant $π/4$ and on optimised restart Grover, and matching the corresponding infimum in our continuous-angle analysis. When only a lower bound $0 < p_0 \leq p < 1/2$ is available, a discrete Lyapunov equation gives the exact expected oracle cost for every fixed measurement strength and a closed-form optimal strength. Using the strength selected from $p_0$, the expected number of Grover iterations is at most $\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_0}})$, where the expected cost decreases as the actual $p$ increases, and gives the leading constant $1/\sqrt{2}$ at the promise boundary $p = p_0$. For completely unknown $p$, we identify measurement strength $κ(t)=Θ(1/t)$ as the critical scale within the regular schedules considered here and analyse $κ_b(t)=\min\{1/2,b/t\}$. For each fixed $b > 2$, we rigorously derive a closed-form Gamma-function expression $C(b)$ giving $(C(b)/2+o(1))/\sqrt{p}$ expected iterations. Numerical minimisation of this explicit expression gives $b \approx 5.2$ and $C(b)/2 \approx 1.01$. The results show that weak measurements preserve the $Θ(1/\sqrt{p})$ search scale while adding an explicit fixed-point control mechanism and provable oracle savings.

</details>


### [108] [Phase-sensitive avalanche quantum sensing of sub-shot-noise fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39650)
*Nikolai D. Klimkin,Misha Ivanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出并数值验证了一种基于原子云“雪崩式”放大机制，可对低于量子散粒噪声极限的微弱光场实现非微扰探测，高达两个数量级的放大且保持相位信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密测量中基于雪崩的探测（如盖革计数器、单光子雪崩探测器）已很成熟，但如何将此类放大原理拓展到低于量子散粒噪声极限的光波探测，尤其是兼顾灵敏度与相位保持，仍是挑战。

Method: 将原子云囚禁于光子腔中，把腔模调谐至宇称禁戒跃迁；在连续波激光泵浦下，数值模拟原子云发射对初始亚散粒噪声涨落的放大行为。

Result: 该机制将初始亚散粒噪声涨落放大约两个数量级；放大对频率可宽范围调谐，且不依赖原子能级结构；同时保留了原始超弱光波施加于原子的相位。

Conclusion: 该发现对量子态表征与弱经典信号探测均具意义，相较常规方案在相位保持方面有定性提升。

Abstract: Avalanche-based detection of small perturbations is commonplace in precision measurement devices from Geiger counters to single-photon avalanche detectors. Here, we expand this principle to sensing of light waves below the quantum shot noise limit, demonstrating numerically how atomic clouds trapped in photonic cavities can exhibit non-perturbative sensitivity to changes in the cavity field when the cavity mode is tuned to a parity-prohibited transition. We find that, when pumped by a continuous-wave laser, the atomic cloud creates emissions which amplify the initially sub-shot-noise fluctuation by two orders of magnitude. Crucially, this amplification is broadly tunable with respect to frequency and independent of atomic energy structure. Even more strikingly, our amplification mechanism preserves the phase imparted on the atoms by the original ultra-weak light wave, marking a qualitative improvement over conventional protocols. Our finding has implications for both quantum state characterization and detection of weak classical signals.

</details>


### [109] [QMA(2) with Limited Shared Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39668)
*Alex Della Schiava,Ranitha Mataraarachchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明 QMA(2) 在最多对数个共享 EPR 对时功率不变，即 QMA(2;h)=QMA(2) (h=O(log n))，并给出单调性、padding 推论及 LOCC 模型扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson 等人提出 QMA(2;h) 模型（两证明者可共享 h 个 EPR 对），并留下开放问题：纠缠共享如何影响 QMA(2) 的计算能力。本文旨在刻画共享 EPR 对数目的作用范围，并确定何时纠缠共享会改变模型能力。

Method: 主要通过对四个非纠缠见证者的模拟，结合 Harrow-Montanaro 等式 QMA(4)=QMA(2)（FOCS 2010）来证明 h=O(log n) 时 QMA(2;h)=QMA(2)。进一步证明 EPR 预算的单调性 QMA(2;h)⊆QMA(2;H)（h≤H，保持完备性与可靠性），并结合输入 padding 推导多项式预算的等价性。最后将结果推广到证明者可在见证准备阶段使用 LOCC 的模型变体。

Result: 证明了 h=O(log n) 时 QMA(2;h)=QMA(2)；证明了 EPR 预算的单调性 QMA(2;h)⊆QMA(2;H)；结合 padding 表明，若对任意固定 ε>0 有 QMA(2;n^ε)=QMA(2)，则可推出所有多项式预算下相等，从而解决 Aaronson 等人的开放问题。在 LOCC 模型下，对对数规模见证和逆多项式间隙，(h=O(log n)) 时两模型均等价于非纠缠对应模型；并证明若在 LOCC 模型中将该等价性推广到任意超对数 EPR 预算，将蕴含 NP⊆BQP。

Conclusion: 共享对数数量 EPR 对不增加 QMA(2) 的能力；EPR 预算具有单调性，任意多项式预算的等价性可由单个 n^ε 情形经 padding 推出。LOCC 准备模型在超对数 EPR 预算下的等价性将导致 NP⊆BQP，说明该推广极不可能成立，界定了纠缠共享增强计算能力的界限。

Abstract: A $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ protocol involves two provers submitting unentangled witnesses to a polynomial-time quantum verifier. In The Power of Unentanglement (ToC, 2009), Aaronson et al. proposed $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)$, a variant of $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ in which the two provers may share $h$ EPR pairs. Our main result shows that the power of $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ remains unchanged for up to logarithmically many shared EPR pairs: $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ for $h=O(\log n)$, where $n$ is the input length. The result follows from a simulation using four unentangled witnesses, combined with the Harrow-Montanaro equality $\mathsf{QMA}(4)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ (FOCS, 2010).
  We also prove monotonicity in the EPR budget: $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)\subseteq\mathsf{QMA}(2;H)$ for $h\le H$, preserving completeness and soundness. Combined with input padding, this shows that establishing $\mathsf{QMA}(2;n^\varepsilon)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ for any fixed $\varepsilon>0$ would imply equality for every polynomially bounded budget, resolving the open problem raised by Aaronson et al.
  Finally, we extend these results to a variant of the model in which the provers may use local operations and classical communication (LOCC) during witness preparation. For logarithmic-size witnesses and inverse-polynomial gaps, both models remain equivalent to their unentangled counterpart when $h=O(\log n)$. We show that extending this equivalence to any superlogarithmic EPR budget in the LOCC model would imply $\mathsf{NP}\subseteq\mathsf{BQP}$.

</details>


### [110] [Separable decompositions of 2xn states with operator Schmidt rank three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39669)
*Perrine Vantalon,Nicolas Macris*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了算子Schmidt秩为3的量子比特-n能级二分态可分解为rank(ρ)个纯乘积态，以及至多n+1个混合乘积态，后者是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二分量子态的乘积态分解长度问题，尤其关注算子Schmidt秩为3的情形，并试图统一处理纯乘积态与混合乘积态两种分解。

Method: 对由态显式确定的算子的合适酉扩张进行特征分解，构造性地给出分解方法。

Result: 任何qubit-n能级且算子Schmidt秩为3的二分态可写成rank(ρ)个纯乘积态之和，或至多n+1个混合乘积态之和；n+1这一上界是紧的，存在不能用更少项分解的态。该框架也重现了纯乘积态分解长度的已有结果，且对纯乘积态给出的分解与现有分解不同。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了秩3情形下乘积态分解的长度，兼具构造性，并统一了纯乘积态与混合乘积态分解，同时改进了纯乘积态的分解构造。

Abstract: We prove that every bipartite state $ρ$ of a qubit coupled to an $n$-level system whose operator Schmidt rank equals three can be written as a mixture of $\text{rank}(ρ)$ pure product states, and as a mixture of at most $n+1$ mixed product states in general. The second result is tight in the sense that there exist states that cannot be decomposed with a smaller number of terms. Our proof gives constructive methods to obtain the decompositions. It involves eigendecompositions of suitable unitary dilations of an operator explicitly determined by the state. Our framework recovers previously known results for the length of decompositions into pure product states and is also able to deal with decompositions into mixed states. For pure product states, our decomposition is different from existing ones.

</details>


### [111] [Polylog-depth Quantum Thermal Simulation via Local Recovery Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39674)
*Hideaki Hakoshima,Atsushi Iwaki,Nobuyuki Yoshioka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在任意固定空间维度下，对非对易局域哈密顿量的量子热态，可在polylog电路深度内制备，其门与比特数为 N·polylog(N/ε)。条件耦合了约化密度矩阵有效相互作用的局域性与稳定性、高温条件以及粗粒化经典参考哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联系两个基本问题：热平衡中关联如何组织，以及相应量子态能否由量子力学允许的操作高效实现；为平衡态局域性赋予构造性的计算解释，并使其在量子算法设计中具有物理依据。

Method: 在固定空间维度下，对非对易局域哈密顿量的Gibbs态，结合约化密度矩阵有效相互作用的局域性与稳定性界、定量高温条件，以及可访问的粗粒化经典参考哈密顿量；由空间局域Petz恢复映射构造制备电路，用微观哈密顿量导出的量子修正实现精确恢复，而经典参考保持粗粒化；若参考可局域生成，则经典预处理代价为 N^{1+o(1)}。

Result: 在逆多项式全局精度下，经典预处理成本为 N^{1+o(1)}；预处理后电路以 N·polylog(N/ε) 个门和比特制备正则纯化，其中 ε 为制备误差。

Conclusion: 将静态平衡结构转化为显式制备电路，为平衡态局域性提供了构造性计算解释，并在量子算法设计中赋予其物理基础角色。

Abstract: We establish sufficient conditions for preparing quantum thermal states of noncommuting local Hamiltonians with polylogarithmic circuit depth in arbitrary fixed spatial dimension. Our conditions combine locality and stability bounds on the effective interactions of reduced density matrices of a Gibbs state with a quantitative high-temperature condition and access to coarse classical reference Hamiltonians. When these references can be generated locally, the classical preprocessing cost is $N^{1+o(1)}$ for $N$ sites at inverse-polynomial global accuracy. We construct the preparation circuit from spatially localized Petz recovery maps. Quantum corrections derived from the microscopic Hamiltonian enable accurate recovery while the classical references remain coarse. After preprocessing, the resulting circuit prepares the canonical purification using $N\operatorname{polylog}(N/\varepsilon)$ gates and qubits, where $\varepsilon$ is the preparation error. These results link two fundamental questions: how correlations are organized in thermal equilibrium, and how efficiently the corresponding states can be realized through operations allowed by quantum mechanics. By translating static equilibrium structure into explicit preparation circuits, they give equilibrium locality a constructive computational interpretation and a physically grounded role in quantum algorithm design.

</details>


### [112] [Scaling Laws of Quantum Networks: An Entanglement Transport Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39690)
*Jaemin Kim,Petar Popovski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"纠缠输运"指标与双层框架（资源层／物理层），刻画多用户量子网络中纠缠分发性能如何随节点数 N 与用户数 M 的标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络走向多用户架构时，纠缠分发性能随 N 与 M 的标度关系是核心问题，但既有研究目标函数、资源假设与归一化方式各异，结果缺乏可比性。

Method: 定义纠缠输运：将满足固定保真度阈值的已交付 Bell 对按端点距离加权。构建两层框架：(i) 资源层在给定聚合方式下测量每个激活时隙的容量；(ii) 物理层在实现约束下测量每秒服务量。并在蜂窝晶格与均匀链等场景中比较固定窗口纠缠渗流构造与渐近聚合容量。

Result: 在单次随机会话的扩张蜂窝晶格上，固定窗口纠缠渗流构造在阈值以下可达 Θ(N^{-1}) 的每激活时隙纠缠输运，阈值以上为 Θ(√N)。均匀链上渐近聚合容量相对固定窗口容量高出 Θ(N²/M)；空间扩张下含噪物理实现可达 Θ(M/N) 的每秒服务量。

Conclusion: 纠缠输运的标度不仅取决于网络连通性，还取决于资源聚合方式与物理实现，该框架为建立量子网络标度律提供了统一基准。

Abstract: As quantum networks move toward multi-user architectures, a central question is how the performance of entanglement distribution scales with the numbers of network nodes $N$ and active users $M$, yet existing studies use different objectives, resource assumptions, and normalizations. To account for delivery distance under multi-user demand, we define entanglement transport, which weights each delivered Bell pair meeting a fixed fidelity threshold by its endpoint separation. In a two-layer framework, (i) a resource layer measures capacity per activation slot under different aggregation regimes, and (ii) a physical layer measures service per second under implementation constraints. For matched implementations with a bounded number of activation slots per second, asymptotic-aggregation capacity sets a service ceiling, whereas achievable service must be established separately. On expanding honeycomb lattices with one random session, fixed-window entanglement-percolation constructions achieve $Θ(N^{-1})$ entanglement transport per activation slot below their thresholds and $Θ(\sqrt N)$ above them. For homogeneous chains, asymptotic aggregation exceeds fixed-window capacity by a factor $Θ(N^2/M)$, and in spatial expansion a noisy physical implementation achieves $Θ(M/N)$ service per second. These examples show that entanglement-transport scaling depends not only on connectivity but also on resource aggregation and physical implementation. The framework provides a common basis on which quantum-network scaling laws can be established.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum--classical break-even in electronic dynamics of macrocyclic molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39693)
*Shu Kanno,Kenji Sugisaki,Takashi Imamichi,Toshinari Itoko,Rei Sakuma,Hajime Nakamura,Qi Gao,Naoki Yamamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作展示了化学动力学应用中的量子-经典盈亏平衡：基于张量网络电路压缩构建电子动力学量子工作流，并将其应用于12量子比特苯和120量子比特[60]环烯，实现QPU墙钟时间与经典方法估计相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索量子硬件在化学相关电子动力学中能否达到与实用经典计算资源相比有意义的盈亏平衡，从而判断量子硬件是否可成为现实的计算选项。

Method: 构建基于张量网络电路压缩、利用空间局域性的电子动力学量子工作流，将电路压缩扩展到非一维开放边界系统；针对一维周期哈密顿量，仅对短时小空间区域经典优化电路，再在空间和时间上复制以构造全系统长时动力学，而无需经典模拟对应的大规模演化。将其应用于具有旋转周期性的环状共轭大环：12量子比特苯和120量子比特[60]环烯，嵌入IBM量子处理器环上。

Result: 对于[60]环烯，空穴掺杂非平衡动力学的QPU墙钟时间与两种经典方法（Majorana传播和基于含时变分原理的矩阵乘积态时间演化）的估计墙钟时间相当；视任务不同对应数十到数百个计算节点（每节点36个CPU核心，假设近似理想并行扩展）。还展示了混合量子-经典计算在精度和计算时间上均为合理选择的情形。

Conclusion: 量子硬件可成为化学相关电子动力学的一种现实计算选项。

Abstract: Here we demonstrate quantum--classical break-even for chemical dynamics applications, reaching useful accuracy with a quantum-hardware wall-clock time comparable to that estimated for practical classical computing resources. Specifically, we construct a quantum workflow for electronic dynamics based on tensor-network circuit compression leveraging spatial locality, extending circuit compression beyond one-dimensional open-boundary systems. This enables application of the workflow to problems that are hardware-native but classically hard. The workflow targets one-dimensional periodic Hamiltonians: circuits classically optimized only for short-time evolution within a small spatial region can be replicated in both space and time to construct long-time dynamics of the full system without classically simulating the corresponding large-scale evolution. We apply the workflow to cyclic conjugated macrocycles with rotational periodicity, 12-qubit benzene and 120-qubit [60]annulene, by embedding their electronic structure onto loops of IBM quantum processors. For the annulene, the quantum-processing-unit (QPU) wall-clock times for hole-doped nonequilibrium dynamics became comparable to the estimated wall-clock time of two classical methods, Majorana propagation and matrix-product-state time evolution based on the time-dependent variational principle; this corresponds to tens to hundreds of compute nodes depending on the task (36 CPU cores per node), assuming near-ideal parallel scaling, bringing the quantum calculation into a wall-clock-time regime comparable to conventional parallel high-performance-computing resources. We also show a situation in which a hybrid quantum--classical calculation is a reasonable choice in terms of both accuracy and computational time. These results show that quantum hardware can become a realistic computational option for chemically relevant electronic dynamics.

</details>


### [114] [The Intrinsic Cost of Quantum Syndrome Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39705)
*Chia-Tung Chu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接从稳定子群定义的、与生成元选取无关的相互作用内在度量，用以刻画综合征提取所需的硬件交互量，并据此给出任意稳定子码精确综合征提取深度的硬件相关下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件连通性受限，稳定子码的综合征提取时间受局域性约束。既有工作（DBT21、BFS23）已建立综合征提取与局域性约束之间的联系，但需要一个不依赖于码的特定生成元描述、从稳定子群本身出发的内在度量，以得到更本质、更普适的深度下界。

Method: 首先从稳定子群直接定义综合征提取所需相互作用的内在度量（独立于描述码所用的生成元集合）；随后利用该度量推导任意稳定子码精确综合征提取深度的硬件相关下界；并以此为指导构造环面/环形表面码的常深度局域综合征提取实例。

Result: 该度量给出任意稳定子码的硬件相关精确综合征提取深度下界；构造的环形表面码常深度局域综合征提取实例构成对 DBT21 推论1 的反例；对某固定量子 Tanner 族，该度量强化了 BFS23 中显式给出的深度界，并结合匹配构造建立了渐近紧的空间—深度权衡。

Conclusion: 理论上，结果将综合征提取所需信息与设备的通信容量联系起来；实践上，为在确定硬件布局前评估不可避免的电路开销提供了方法。

Abstract: How much time is fundamentally required to extract the syndrome of a stabilizer code on hardware with a given connectivity graph? Previous work by Delfosse--Beverland--Tremblay (2021) and Baspin--Fawzi--Shayeghi (2023) established bounds connecting syndrome extraction to locality constraints on quantum hardware. Here, we address this question by first formulating an intrinsic measure of the interaction required for syndrome extraction directly from the stabilizer group, independent of the generating set used to describe the code. We then show that this measure yields hardware-dependent lower bounds on exact syndrome-extraction depth for arbitrary stabilizer codes. Guided by this measure, we also construct annular surface-code examples with constant-depth local syndrome extraction that provide a counterexample to Corollary 1 of DBT21. Furthermore, for a fixed quantum Tanner family, this measure strengthens the depth bound explicitly stated in BFS23 and, together with our matching construction, establishes an asymptotically tight space--depth tradeoff. In theory, our results connect the information required by syndrome extraction to the communication capacity of a device; while in practice, they provide a way to assess unavoidable circuit costs before committing to a hardware layout.

</details>


### [115] [Complex Quantum Dynamics Versus Classical Simulability of Noisy Random Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39734)
*Anjali Waghmare,Sergii Strelchuk,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示含噪量子线路中动态诊断量（magic、scrambling等）与经典可模拟性可双向脱钩，诊断量不能作为经典硬度证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势主张依赖量子线路经典模拟的硬度，magic、operator scrambling、anticoncentration、非高斯性等常被当作复杂量子动力学的诊断量。对纯态，部分诊断量已被严格关联到经典可模拟性，但在噪声下它们是否能可靠刻画高效经典模拟的极限仍不清楚。

Method: 在含噪 Clifford+T 线路与最近邻 matchgate+SWAP 线路中，考察动态诊断量与经典可模拟性的关系，并将二者的失配追溯到所探测统计性质的不同：magic 与 scrambling 依赖 Pauli 谱的四阶统计量，而模拟算法主要依赖二阶矩，局域噪声对二者的抑制速率不同。

Result: 在含噪 Clifford+T 与最近邻 matchgate+SWAP 线路中，动态诊断量与经典可模拟性可双向分离：经典模拟已变得高效后诊断量仍可保持非平凡，或在已知高效经典算法适用前诊断量已变得平凡。

Conclusion: 在噪声量子设备上测得的动态诊断量本身并不构成经典硬度的证据。

Abstract: Claims of quantum advantage rest on the classical hardness of simulating quantum circuits. Magic, operator scrambling, anticoncentration, and non-Gaussianity for fermionic circuits are standard diagnostics of complex quantum dynamics. For pure states, some of these have been rigorously connected to classical simulability. Whether these diagnostics reliably track the limits of efficient classical simulation under noise remains unclear. Here, we show that in noisy Clifford+$T$ and nearest-neighbour matchgate+SWAP circuits, dynamical diagnostics and classical simulability can separate in both directions. In particular, the diagnostics can remain nontrivial after classical simulation becomes efficient, or become trivial before known efficient classical algorithms apply. We trace this mismatch to the different statistical properties they probe: magic and scrambling depend on fourth-order statistics of the Pauli spectrum, whereas the simulation algorithms depend primarily on second-order moments, which local noise suppresses at different rates. Thus, dynamical diagnostics measured on a noisy quantum device do not by themselves constitute evidence for classical hardness.

</details>


### [116] [What makes a causal loop consistent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39735)
*Hippolyte Dourdent,Andreas Leitherer,Emanuel-Cristian Boghiu,Kyrylo Simonov,Ravi Kunjwal,Antonio Acín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明确定性经典过程逻辑自洽当且仅当不含信号回路（允许因果回路但禁止信号回路），并给出事件级、无需干预、非递归的自洽刻画：事件列表两两互斥且完备；指出这与Specker原理的多体形式等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果回路是否会导致逻辑悖论长期存在争议；已有"无干预判据"会遗漏全局回路，因此需要更精确、不依赖干预、非递归的自洽性刻画。

Method: 从"在任意独立局域干预下能生成有效概率"出发，分析确定性经典过程的逻辑自洽性，将其与信号回路的存在性建立等价关系，并用事件列表的互斥性与完备性刻画自洽性。

Result: 确定性经典过程逻辑自洽 ⇔ 不含信号回路；循环信号被禁止而循环因果被允许，从而容许不定因果序；自洽性恰对应事件两两互斥且完备；修复了遗漏全局回路的旧判据；揭示其根源是Specker原理的多体形式。

Conclusion: 因果回路在无信号回路时可保持逻辑自洽并支持不定因果序；所给事件级判据为一致性因果回路提供了首个非递归、无干预刻画，并与多体Specker原理相联系。

Abstract: Causal loops escape paradoxes if they generate valid probabilities under arbitrary independent local interventions. We show that a deterministic classical process is logically consistent if and only if it contains no signaling loops: cyclic signaling is forbidden but cyclic causation is not, allowing indefinite causal order. Logical consistency is then found to hold exactly when the list of events of a process is pairwise exclusive and complete, providing the first event-based, intervention-free, non-recursive characterization of consistent causal loops. Our results repair a previously introduced intervention-free criterion that misses global loops and reveal that what makes deterministic classical communications without causal order consistent is a multipartite form of Specker's principle: if any two of several questions can be answered jointly, so can all of them.

</details>


### [117] [A depolarizing choir sings in Gaussian harmony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39747)
*Rabsan Galib Ahmed,Sujeet Bhalerao,Sungjai Lee,Felix Leditzky,Debbie Leung,Luke Schaeffer,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子比特去极化信道正量子容量的噪声阈值，揭示了去极化信道在对称子空间渐近极限下涌现玻色高斯信道的机制，并通过码的翻译改进阈值下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子比特去极化信道的正量子容量噪声阈值是量子信息理论中的核心开放问题，精确阈值尚未确定，已有下界仍有改进空间，作者希望借助新视角提升下界并加深对对称码的理解。

Method: 解析研究去极化信道在输入量子比特对称子空间上的作用，取信道的渐近多次使用极限，发现玻色高斯信道的涌现；将已有的去极化信道码翻译为涌現高斯信道的码，在更简单的高斯信道上优化后再翻译回去，构造相干信息的输入态。

Result: 将优化后的码翻译回去极化信道后，得到极好的相干信息输入态，产生了正容量噪声阈值的新的、更优的下界。

Conclusion: 去极化噪声与高斯信道之间新建立的联系不仅改进了阈值下界，还为理解这些对称码提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the noise threshold for positive quantum capacity for the qubit depolarizing channel. We explore analytically the action of the qubit depolarizing channel on the symmetric subspaces of the input qubits, in the limit of asymptotically many uses of the channel. We observe the emergence of a bosonic Gaussian channel. Furthermore, the codes previously developed for the depolarizing channel can be translated to codes for the emergent Gaussian channel, and it is easier to further optimize these codes for the simpler emergent Gaussian channel. Translating these codes back to the depolarizing channel leads to extremely good input states for the coherent information of the depolarizing channel producing new lower bounds on the noise threshold for positive capacity. In addition to improved lower bounds on the threshold, this newly found link between depolarizing noise and Gaussian channels offers a novel perspective contributing to our understanding of these symmetric codes.

</details>


### [118] [Theory of spacetime quantum fault tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39758)
*Yijia Xu,Yixu Wang,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的Clifford时空量子容错代数理论，用时空链复形统一多种容错框架并连接时空距离与gadget正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子容错存在多种分散的表述（时空码、Gottesman gadget框架、故障复形、ZX演算等），缺乏统一代数基础，且现象学与电路级故障模型通常被分开处理。

Method: 从电路的张量网络表示构造时空链复形，利用其规范与稳定子结构编码故障传播、探测器辛ndrome与逻辑作用。

Result: 该理论统一了多种容错表述；将时空距离与Gottesman形式中的gadget级正确性性质相联系；通过不同张量分解与故障分配容纳现象学和电路级故障模型；并恢复重复辛ndrome提取、横截与折叠横截门的关联探测器、Steane型和Knill型提取gadget、单次纠错等已知构造与结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为Clifford时空量子容错提供了统一代数框架，可望作为通用容错量子计算的通用理论基础。

Abstract: We present a unified algebraic theory of Clifford spacetime quantum fault tolerance, which underpins universal fault-tolerant quantum computation. We construct a spacetime chain complex from a tensor network representation of a circuit that encodes fault propagation, detector syndromes, and logical actions through the associated gauge and stabilizer structure. We show that this theory (1) unifies various formulations of quantum fault tolerance including spacetime codes, Gottesman's gadget framework, fault complexes, and ZX calculus; (2) connects spacetime distances to the gadget-level correctness properties in Gottesman's formalism; (3) naturally accommodates a range of fault models, in particular phenomenological and circuit-level models, bringing these often separately formulated descriptions into a common algebraic framework through different tensor decompositions and fault assignments; and (4) recovers many known fault tolerance constructions and results as special cases, including repeated syndrome extraction, correlated detectors for transversal and fold-transversal gates, Steane- and Knill-type syndrome extraction gadgets, and single-shot error correction.

</details>


### [119] [Measurement and feedforward circuits from quantum error correcting codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39764)
*Georgios Styliaris,Rahul Trivedi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文建立了"测量+前馈"浅层量子电路协议与量子纠错码之间的一般对应关系，将态制备与全局酉操作的实现统一转化为编码设计问题，并阐明了非稳定子性的来源及突破途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层量子电路借助测量与前馈可确定性地实现全局酉操作、制备长程纠缠态，但此前缺乏统一的理论框架来解释这些协议的共性，也缺乏对非稳定子性来源的清晰刻画，因此作者试图建立与纠错码的对应以统一并推广此类协议。

Method: 建立协议与量子纠错码的对应：将测量前的电路视为编码器，证明当且仅当测量投影算符是码空间上的可探测错误时，酉前馈才能消除后选择。据此把态制备与全局酉实现统一为码设计问题，并针对稳定子码加泡利测量的情形分析非稳定子性的来源，进而采用非泡利测量或非加性码来突破限制。

Result: 对于稳定子码配合泡利测量，尽管所得操作可以是非Clifford的，其非稳定子性完全来源于编码器；使用非泡利测量或非加性码可以克服这一限制，构造出仅需单量子比特酉修正即可生成长程非稳定子性的协议。

Conclusion: 该对应关系为测量与前馈协议提供了统一框架，将态制备和全局酉实现归约为编码设计问题；同时揭示了稳定子码情形下非稳定子性的编码器起源，并给出通过非泡利测量或非加性码生成长程非稳定子性的可行路径。

Abstract: Measurements and feedforward enhance the power of shallow quantum circuits, enabling the deterministic implementation of global unitary operations and the preparation of long-range entangled states. We establish a general correspondence between all such protocols and quantum error-correcting codes: the circuit preceding the measurements acts as an encoder, and unitary feedforward can eliminate post-selection if and only if the measurement projectors are detectable errors on the codespace. This correspondence provides a common framework for state preparation and implementation of global unitaries with measurements and feedforward, turning both into a code-design problem. For stabilizer codes with Pauli measurements, although the resulting operation can be non-Clifford, we show that its nonstabilizerness originates entirely from the encoder. We overcome this restriction using non-Pauli measurements or non-additive codes, constructing protocols that generate long-range nonstabilizerness while requiring only single-qubit unitary corrections.

</details>


### [120] [Double Localization for Quantum Gibbs Sampler Gaps: From an Abstract Framework to Finite-Group Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39802)
*Ryu Hayakawa,Angus Southwell,Caesnan M. G. Leditto,Kuo-Chin Chen,Min-Hsiu Hsieh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"双重局域化"框架，通过几何局域化与界面局域化两类互补操作证明量子Gibbs采样的谱隙，并应用于Kitaev有限群量子双模型的局域Davies动力学，得到与图规模无关的一致谱隙下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Gibbs采样的收敛速度由生成元的谱隙控制，但全局量子动力学（尤其含非对易项）的谱隙估计困难，需要在界面子空间上做全局估计与局部量子方向控制之间的分离，以获得不依赖系统规模的谱隙保证。

Method: 提出双重局域化框架：(1)几何局域化，选取支撑在小空间区域上的更新；(2)界面局域化，聚焦于模型定义的观测量子空间（界面）但保持几何全局性。抽象谱隙定理将界面上的全局界与在定量耦合及几何装配条件下的局部量子谱隙估计相结合，给出全动力学的显式下界，且不要求界面在生成元下不变。用于Kitaev有限群量子双构造的顶点项哈密顿量（无面项），定义在有限简单三正则、围长至少6的图上，研究指定的局域Davies动力学。

Result: 对每个固定非平凡有限群及固定逆温度β（0≤βJ<log(5/3)，J>0为耦合强度），证明了与图规模无关的正数谱隙下界。对最小非阿贝尔群S₃，双重局域化给出在整个区间0≤βJ≤log 3上对图规模一致的正下界，该保证无法直接由已有结果得到。

Conclusion: 该框架将全局估计与局部量子控制分离，使经典比较等全局方法可与剩余量子方向的局域控制结合；模型相关的有限计算可扩展全量子动力学的谱隙保证。

Abstract: We introduce double localization, a framework for proving spectral gaps of quantum Gibbs samplers through two complementary operations: geometric localization, which selects updates supported in small spatial regions, and interface localization, which focuses on a model-defined subspace of observables while remaining geometrically global. Our abstract gap theorem combines a global bound on this subspace, called the interface, with local quantum gap estimates under quantitative coupling and geometric assembly conditions. The resulting explicit lower bound controls the gap of the full dynamics without requiring the interface to be invariant under the generator. This separation allows global estimates, including classical comparison, to be combined with local control of the remaining quantum directions.
  We apply the framework to Hamiltonians built from the vertex terms of Kitaev's finite-group quantum double construction, without plaquette terms, on finite simple three-regular graphs of girth at least six. For the specified local Davies dynamics, we prove an unconditional spectral-gap lower bound by a positive constant independent of graph size for every fixed nontrivial finite group and each fixed inverse temperature $β$ with $0\leβJ<\log(5/3)$, where $J>0$ is the coupling strength. For the smallest non-Abelian group $S_3$, double localization yields a positive lower bound uniform in both graph size and the entire interval $0\leβJ\le\log 3$, providing a guarantee that does not follow directly from existing results. This demonstrates how model-specific finite calculations can extend spectral-gap guarantees for the full quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [121] [Sharp Quantum Entropy Mixing Rates and the Operator Layer Cake Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39814)
*Alexander Stottmeister*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bravyi关于二元量子系综混合速率的small incremental mixing猜想，最优常数为c=1，即矩阵不等式‖[A,log B]‖₁≤h₂(p)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Hamiltonian演化下量子态系综的von Neumann熵变化速率，验证Bravyi提出的small incremental mixing猜想及其后续由Mariën等人归结的矩阵不等式最优常数问题。

Method: 利用Cheng和Liu发展的算子layer cake定理，导出交换子[A,log B]的精确积分表示，从而证明该矩阵不等式。

Result: 证明了该猜想，最优常数为c=1；由此得混合速率满足small incremental mixing且常数为最优，二分Hamiltonian的纠缠速率被(2log d+1)‖H‖所界定；并修正了Lieb和Vershynina关于一般系综混合速率的猜想。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了不依赖于维数的尖锐界限，刻画了二元量子系综中单一组分酉演化改变熵的最大速率，并修正了相关猜想。

Abstract: At what rate does the von Neumann entropy of an ensemble of quantum states change under Hamiltonian evolution of its constituents? Bravyi proposed the small incremental mixing conjecture controlling the mixing rate of a binary ensemble $\{(1\!-\!p,ρ_1),(p,ρ_2)\}$ by $c\,\|{H}\|h_{2}(p)$ (with the binary entropy $h_{2}$). The proof of Bravyi's conjecture was subsequently reduced to the matrix inequality $\|[A,\log B]\|_{1}\!\leq\! c\,h_{2}(p)$ for positive trace-class operators $A\!\leq\! B$ with $\text{Tr} A\!=\!p$ and $\text{Tr} B\!=\!1$ on separable Hilbert spaces by Mariën, Audenaert, Van Acoleyen, and Verstraete, and they conjectured the optimal constant to be $c\!=\!1$. Here, I prove the latter conjecture using an exact integral representation of the commutator $[A,\log B]$ obtained from the operator layer cake theorem, due to Cheng and Liu. The result gives a sharp dimension-independent limit on how rapidly unitary evolution of one component can change the entropy of a binary quantum ensemble. It follows that mixing rates satisfy small incremental mixing with the optimal constant, and entangling rates of bipartite Hamiltonians are bounded by $(2\log d\!+\!1)\|H\|$. Moreover, the result corrects a conjecture by Lieb and Vershynina for mixing rates of general ensembles.

</details>


### [122] [Learning Random Quantum Circuits and the Emergence of Pseudorandomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39821)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Qizhao Huang,Makrand Sinha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效算法，仅利用输出态副本即可学习k维砖砌随机量子电路，时间复杂度为poly(n,2^{ℓd})，当ℓd=O(log n)时为多项式时间，并给出维度无关的Carbery-Wright型反集中不等式作为核心技术工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子电路的可学习性与伪随机性之间存在自然边界：伪随机态需要ℓd=ω(log n)。此前方法存在学习整个反向光锥的瓶颈，难以高效识别门。本文旨在突破该瓶颈，明确伪随机性出现的条件边界。

Method: 核心思想是局部关联判据，无需学习完整反向光锥即可识别最后一层的门。关键技术工具是面向Haar随机酉矩阵低次多项式的Carbery-Wright型反集中不等式，其小球指数与矩阵维度无关，从而可处理增长局域性的门。

Result: 对深度d、n个位点、随机2ℓ量子比特门的电路，算法以高概率在poly(n,2^{ℓd})时间内学习原电路U，适用于任意常数维格点。当ℓd=O(log n)时为多项式时间。在一维情形下达到伪随机性所暗示的自然边界（伪随机态需ℓd=ω(log n)）；高维情形下同一尺度仍可能是无辅助比特伪随机性的阈值。

Conclusion: 该工作通过局部关联判据和维度无关反集中不等式，实现了随机量子电路的高效学习，厘清了伪随机性在该设定下出现的条件，并提供了可能具有独立价值的技术工具。

Abstract: We give an efficient algorithm for learning $k$-dimensional brickwork random quantum circuits using only copies of the output state obtained by applying $U$ to the all-zero input. For a depth-$d$ circuit on $n$ sites with random $2\ell$-qubit gates, the algorithm learns the original circuit $U$ with high probability in $\text{poly}(n,2^{\ell d})$ time for every constant dimensional lattice. In particular, the algorithm is polynomial time as long as $\ell d = O(\log n)$. In one dimension, this reaches the natural boundary suggested by pseudorandomness: pseudorandom states require $\ell d=ω(\log n)$, and structured cryptographic constructions suggest that this scale may be achievable from above. In higher dimensions, it remains plausible that the same $\ell d=ω(\log n)$ scale remains the threshold for ancilla-free pseudorandomness, and our results help clarify the conditions under which pseudorandomness can arise in this setting.
  The main idea behind our algorithm is a local correlation criterion that identifies gates in the final layer without learning their entire backward light cones, avoiding a bottleneck in the previous approaches. A key technical ingredient is a Carbery-Wright type anticoncentration inequality for low-degree polynomials of Haar random unitaries whose small-ball exponent is independent of the matrix dimension. The dimension-independent exponent is crucial for handling gates of growing locality. This anticoncentration result may also be of independent interest.

</details>


### [123] [Improved Quantum Random Self-Reduction for Linear Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39823)
*Vahid R. Asadi,Shuichi Hirahara,Nobutaka Shimizu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上线性问题的量子随机自归约。给定与线性映射 x↦Mx 在 ε 比例输入上一致的预言机 O，以及矩阵 M 的相干元素访问，提出一种均匀量子归约，在 T=Õ(n) 时以 Õ(n^{4/3}) 时间计算 Mx，优于此前的 Õ(n^{3/2}+T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 Asadi 等人 (SODA 2024) 的 Õ(n^{3/2}+T) 量子归约。先前归约需显式学习加法组合学保证的 Bogolyubov–Ruzsa 子空间（及其正交补的基），该步骤计算代价高昂，因此希望避免显式学习并降低总体复杂度。

Method: 利用加法组合学保证的 Bogolyubov–Ruzsa 子空间，但不显式学习它，也不恢复其正交补的基；核心步骤是将输入分解为稀疏片段，并借助基于振幅放大的量子搜索找出位于该子空间之外的向量，从而在查询平均情形预言机的代价与验证矩阵-向量乘积的代价之间实现可调权衡。

Result: 给出均匀量子归约，对任意指定输入 x，以至少 2/3 概率在时间 Õ(nT^{1/3}) 内计算 Mx，其中 n≤T≤n^{3/2}，域大小与 ε 为常数，T 为一次相干查询 O 的代价。特别地，当 T=Õ(n) 时运行时间为 Õ(n^{4/3})，改进了此前 Õ(n^{3/2}+T) 的结果。

Conclusion: 通过避免显式学习 Bogolyubov–Ruzsa 子空间，并采用稀疏分解加量子搜索定位子空间外向量，所提归约在查询代价与验证代价之间取得可调权衡，并在 T=Õ(n) 区间将量子随机自归约的时间复杂度从 Õ(n^{3/2}) 降至 Õ(n^{4/3})。

Abstract: We study quantum random self-reductions for linear problems over finite fields. Let $M\in\mathbb{F}^{n\times n}$ be an arbitrary matrix, and let $\mathcal{O}$ be an oracle that agrees with the linear map $x\mapsto Mx$ on an $\varepsilon$-fraction of inputs $x\sim\mathbb{F}^n$. Given coherent access to $\mathcal{O}$ and coherent entry access to $M$, we give a uniform quantum reduction that computes $Mx$ on any prescribed input $x$ with probability at least $2/3$ in time $\widetilde{O}(nT^{1/3})$, for $n\le T\le n^{3/2}$ and constant field size and $\varepsilon$, where $T$ is the cost of one coherent query to $\mathcal{O}$. In particular, when $T=\widetilde{O}(n)$, the reduction runs in time $\widetilde{O}(n^{4/3})$, improving the $\widetilde{O}(n^{3/2}+T)$ reduction of Asadi, Golovnev, Gur, Shinkar, and Subramanian (SODA 2024).
  Our reduction uses the Bogolyubov--Ruzsa subspace guaranteed by additive combinatorics, but it avoids learning this subspace explicitly, which was computationally expensive for the previous reduction; in particular, it does not recover a basis for its orthogonal complement. The main technical step is to decompose the inputs into sparse pieces and find a vector that lies outside the Bogolyubov--Ruzsa subspace via a quantum search based on amplitude amplification. This yields a tunable tradeoff between the cost of querying the average-case oracle and the cost of verifying matrix-vector products.

</details>


### [124] [Dynamic distributed quantum sensing of radio-frequency fields via time-bin entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39831)
*Vedansh Nehra,Richard Birrittella,Benjamin Malia,Nicholas J. Barton,Christopher Tison,James Schneeloch,David Hucul,Benjamin Kyle,Erin Sheridan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于时间仓纠缠量子比特的离散变量分布式量子传感框架，利用时间仓贝尔态的内禀时间结构作为可调内置双时间差分采样参考，实现对射频场的动态相位感知，理论证明灵敏度提升6 dB并保持每对3 dB纠缠优势，蒙特卡洛仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏振贝尔态的协议仅适用于静态测量，无法对动态射频相位信号实现相干映射；亟需一种可将动态RF相位信号转换为静态量子光学相位、并兼容离散变量光子量子网络的传感方案。

Method: 以时间仓纠缠量子比特替代传统偏振探针，利用时间仓贝尔态内禀时间结构构成可调的内置双时间差分采样参考；通过将时间仓间隔匹配到射频或中频的半周期（反足采样），实现动态RF相位信号向静态量子光学相位的相干映射。

Result: 理论上相对于单次采样静态偏振编码方案获得6 dB灵敏度增强，同时保持相对于可分离标准量子极限基准的每对3 dB纠缠优势；蒙特卡洛仿真验证了性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方案为离散变量光子量子网络中的射频传感提供可行路径，有助于实现量子增强、频率捷变的分布式传感阵列，并拓展时域量子探针编码的应用范围。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for discrete-variable (DV) distributed quantum sensing, wherein traditional polarization-based probes are replaced with time-bin entangled qubits. We show that the intrinsic temporal structure of time-bin Bell states can be utilized as a tunable, built-in two-time differential sampling reference for sensing radio-frequency (RF) fields. Unlike existing protocols based on polarization Bell states, which are limited to static measurements, our proposed architecture enables the coherent mapping of dynamic RF phase signals onto static quantum optical phases. By matching the time-bin separation to the RF or intermediate frequency (IF) half-period (antipodal sampling), we theoretically demonstrate that our protocol provides a 6 dB sensitivity enhancement over one-sample static polarization encodings while preserving the 3 dB per-pair entanglement advantage over the separable standard quantum limit benchmark. The performance enhancement is validated with Monte Carlo simulations. This approach provides a solution for RF sensing in DV photonic quantum networks, offering a clear path toward quantum-enhanced and frequency-agile distributed sensor arrays and broader utilization of time-domain quantum probe encodings.

</details>


### [125] [All-Microwave Multiqubit Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39834)
*Guanqi Wang,Yao Song,Peng-Bo Li,Xiu-Hao Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种适用于频率可调超导transmon网络的全微波方案，通过交叉共振驱动实现含n个控制比特和m个目标比特的单步多比特门，理论推导有效哈密顿量并结合脉冲优化，三比特门保真度超99.9%（60 ns），并扩展到五比特架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多比特门对降低近中期量子处理器和容错架构的电路深度至关重要，但实现多比特相互作用与纠缠仍是关键挑战，限制了多比特门的实现。

Method: 提出全微波方案实现含n个控制和m个目标比特的单步多比特门；利用交叉共振（CR）驱动诱导有效两体ZX相互作用，无需可调耦合器；通过quad frame变换与严格块对角化解析推导系统有效哈密顿量，识别同时增强目标耦合、抑制ZXX等寄生高阶项的最优参数区间；结合数值模拟与基于梯度的脉冲整形优化。

Result: 三比特门在60 ns门时间内保真度超过99.9%；将优化扩展到五比特CXXXX架构，验证了多目标驱动方案的物理可行性。

Conclusion: 由于这些多比特操作天然模拟稳定子测量，该架构提供了一种硬件高效策略：用单步多比特门替代顺序两比特门分解，从而降低稳定子型操作的电路深度。

Abstract: Implementing high-fidelity multiqubit gates is critical for reducing circuit depth in near-term quantum processors and fault-tolerant architectures. However, realizing multiqubit interactions and entanglement remains a critical challenge that limits the implementation of multiqubit gates. Here we propose an all-microwave scheme to realize single-step multiqubit gates incorporating n control and m target qubits, tailored for frequency-tunable superconducting transmon networks. By leveraging cross-resonance (CR) drives, this approach induces effective two-body ZX interactions that are significantly stronger than those achieved in conventional resonant regimes, circumventing the need for tunable couplers. The system's effective Hamiltonian is analytically derived using both quad frame rotation and rigorous block diagonalization. These combined theoretical methods facilitate the identification of optimal parameter regimes that simultaneously enhance desired target couplings and suppress parasitic higher-order terms, such as ZXX interactions. Through numerical simulations and gradient-based pulse-shape optimization, we demonstrate three-qubit gates achieving fidelities exceeding 99.9% within a 60 ns gate duration. Furthermore, we evaluate the fundamental scalability of this framework by extending the optimization to a five-qubit CXXXX architecture, confirming the physical viability of the multi-target driving scheme. Because these multiqubit operations naturally emulate stabilizer measurements, this architecture provides a hardware-efficient strategy for reducing the circuit depth of stabilizer-type operations by replacing sequential two-qubit gate decompositions with single-step multiqubit gates.

</details>


### [126] [A self-tallying quantum anonymous voting protocol for multiple-selection elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39835)
*Guang-Bao Xu,Meng-Na Li,Yu-Guang Yang,Dong-Huan Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出首个支持多选投票的自计票量子匿名投票（SQAV）协议，利用纠缠态分组测量与编码实现多候选人投票，满足自计票、不可重复、可验证与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SQAV协议受设计挑战限制，仅支持单选投票，无法满足多选投票需求。

Method: 将$nm$个$n$粒子纠缠态均分为$n$组，每组纠缠态的$n$个粒子分发给$n$位选民（每人一粒子）。每位选民依次测量其属于不同组的全部粒子，得到$n$个投票向量，并将投票信息编码到秘密索引对应的向量中。基于纠缠关联，选民可通过计算验证选票正确性，任意参与者可通过计算获得各候选人得票数。

Result: 该协议实现多选投票功能，并通过纠缠相关性支持选票验证与计票计算。

Conclusion: 该协议满足自计票、不可重复、可验证与公平性，突破了现有SQAV仅支持单选投票的局限。

Abstract: Self-tallying quantum anonymous voting (SQAV) has drawn considerable attention since it was proposed. However, constrained by design challenges, all existing protocols only accommodate single-selection voting and cannot support multi-selection voting. In this paper, we propose a SQAV protocol with multi-selection voting functionality. In our protocol, $nm$ $n$-particle entangled states are equally divided into $n$ groups, and the $n$ particles in each entangled state are delivered to $n$ voters, one particle per voter. Each voter obtains $n$ voting vectors by sequentially measuring all his (or her) particles that belong to $n$ different group, and then encodes his or her voting information into the vector corresponding to the secret index. Due to entanglement correlation, each voter can verify whether his or her ballot is correct through computation, while any participant can obtain the vote count for each candidate through computation. Our protocol satisfies self-tallying, nonreusability, verifiability, and fairness.

</details>


### [127] [Natural Barriers to Quantum Extraction: On the Post-Quantum (In)security of (O)EKE and Masny-Rindal OT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39844)
*James Bartusek,Jake Januzelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明 EKE/（O）EKE 与 Masny-Rindal OT 这类依赖理想对称原语的编译器在量子多项式时间敌手下不具备 UC 安全性，即使底层 KEM 是后量子安全的；同时给出它们在基于游戏的安全性下的正面结果，并提出一个新的 advantage-tight one-way to hiding 引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EKE 和 Masny-Rindal OT 仅依赖理想对称原语，可将任意 KEM 编译为 PAKE 和 OT，具备简单、即插即用、可灵活选择 KEM 以及经典模型下已有 UC 安全证明等优点。随着后量子 KEM 标准化进展，这些编译器自然被视为高效后量子 PAKE/OT 的候选。因此需要回答其 UC 安全性是否能延伸到量子敌手模型。

Method: 作者研究一般族（O）EKE 协议以及 Masny-Rindal OT，在量子多项式时间敌手模型下分析其 UC 安全性。为证明 UC 不安全，构造一种敌手策略，证明其能够阻止模拟器提取模拟器输入（PAKE 中的口令，OT 中的接收方选择比特）。同时分析两个编译器在 game-based 安全定义下的安全性，并建立一个新的 advantage-tight one-way to hiding 引理。

Result: 证明一般族（O）EKE 协议与 Masny-Rindal OT 在量子多项式时间敌手下均不是 UC 安全的，即使使用后量子 KEM 实例化也是如此。与此相对，证明两个编译器至少能实现某些 game-based 安全概念。并建立了一个可能具有独立价值的新 one-way to hiding 引理。

Conclusion: 这些编译器的经典 UC 安全性不能自动推广到量子敌手模型；后量子 KEM 的替换不足以使其获得量子 UC 安全性。尽管如此，它们仍能提供一定程度的 game-based 安全性。该负结果对后量子 PAKE 和 OT 的构造与安全证明提出了重要警示：不能仅依赖经典 UC 证明来论证量子安全性。

Abstract: Encrypted key exchange (EKE), introduced by Bellovin and Merritt (IEEE S\&P 1992), and Masny-Rindal OT, introduced by Masny and Rindal (ACM CCS 2019), are highly-efficient methods for compiling essentially any KEM into advanced cryptographic protocols, namely password-authenticated key exchange (PAKE) and oblivious transfer (OT), by relying only on idealized symmetric-key primitives. They have become leading candidates for practically-implementable PAKE and OT due to (1) their simplicity, (2) their plug-and-play nature, allowing for flexibility in the choice of KEM, and (3) existing proofs of UC-security (in the classical adversarial model).
  Due to point (2) above, these compilers yield attractive candidates for efficient \emph{post-quantum} PAKE and OT, especially given the recent post-quantum KEM standardization efforts. This motivates the question of whether the (UC-)security of these compilers translates to the quantum adversarial model. In this work, we show that it does not. In particular, we prove that a general family of (O)EKE protocols, as well as Masny-Rindal OT, are \emph{not} UC-secure against quantum polynomial-time adversaries, even when instantiated with a post-quantum KEM. To establish UC-insecurity, we devise an adversarial strategy that provably thwarts any attempt by the simulator to extract its input (the password in the case of PAKE, and the receiver's choice bit in the case of OT).
  To complement these negative results, we establish that both compilers yield certain notions of \emph{game-based} security. Along the way, we establish a novel ``advantage-tight'' one-way to hiding lemma that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [128] [Reverse quantum state diffusion from differential geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39861)
*Dinh-Long Vu,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将量子态扩散的Lindblad方程解绑为纯态随机轨迹，推导其在流形上的时间反演（后向随机微分方程），并借鉴score-based生成模型，从先验分布生成接近原始系综的新系综，给出误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态扩散将Lindblad主方程展开为纯态轨迹，其轨迹系综在纯态流形上的概率密度比密度矩阵包含更多信息。作者希望刻画这一扩散的时间反演，并利用它实现对逆Lindbladian的物理、非线性解绑，从而为量子系综生成提供理论基础。

Method: 先在流形上以Stratonovich形式写出一般扩散的时间反演，再过渡到纯态流形，得到关于态矢量的后向随机微分方程，适用于任意Hilbert空间维度和任意Lindbladian。随后按score-based生成模型，用该后向方程生成新系综。对退极化信道，证明其解绑为复射影空间上的布朗运动，均匀纯态分布为其任意维度的平稳分布，并以此为前验出发，在精确与学习score、连续与离散时间等设定下给出分布误差界。

Result: 导出适用于任意维度与任意Lindbladian的后向随机微分方程；该方程给出仅对构造所用系综成立的逆Lindbladian非线性解绑；退极化信道的解绑是复射影空间上的布朗运动，均匀纯态分布为平稳律；在精确/学习score及连续/离散时间下得到生成分布与原始分布的误差上界。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子态扩散时间反演的一般框架，将逆Lindbladian的解绑与score-based生成模型联系起来，并在退极化信道下给出可分析的平稳结构与分布误差界，为量子系综生成与量子机器学习提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Quantum state diffusion unravels a Lindblad master equation into stochastic trajectories of pure states. The ensemble of trajectories is described by a probability density on the manifold of pure states, which contains much more information than the density matrix, its first moment. We derive the time reversal of this diffusion. Writing the reversal of a general diffusion on a manifold in Stratonovich form, and passing through the manifold of pure states, we obtain a backward stochastic differential equation for the state vector, valid for any Hilbert space dimension and any Lindbladian. The backward stochastic equation provides a physical, nonlinear unravelling of the inverse Lindbladian that holds only for the ensemble from which it was constructed. Following score-based generative models, we then use the backward equation to generate a new ensemble close to the original. For the depolarizing channel the unravelling is a Brownian motion on complex projective space, so the uniform distribution of pure states is its stationary law in any dimension. Starting the backward equation from that prior, we bound the error of the resulting distribution compared to the original one in different settings: exact and learned scores, continuous and discrete time.

</details>


### [129] [Reducing the Entanglement Cost of Distributed Bipartite Quantum Computation with Constant Qubit Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39865)
*Kosuke Matsui,Jun-Yi Wu,Min-Hsiu Hsieh,Mio Murao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究纠缠辅助模型下的分布式量子计算，证明任意两体Clifford酉算符可用每QPU至多2个辅助量子比特达到算子Schmidt秩下界，并对T-count为t的Clifford+T酉算符构造了纠缠代价至多高于下界2t个Bell对的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算通过量子通信连接多个量子处理单元以完成大规模量子计算，但每个QPU可用量子比特数有限，因此需要在保持少量量子比特开销的同时降低量子通信量。已有理论表明，在量子比特开销不受限时，目标两体酉算符的算子Schmidt秩给出了纠缠代价的一般下界，但该下界能否在常数量子比特开销下逼近尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用纠缠辅助模型，以纠缠消耗作为量子通信代价的度量。针对精确确定性实现，分析目标两体酉算符的算子Schmidt秩下界与量子比特开销之间的关系，并构造具体实现方案：对任意两体Clifford酉算符给出每QPU至多2个辅助量子比特的实现；对由精确Clifford+T分解指定、T-count为t的非Clifford酉算符，构造具有同样量子比特开销的实现。

Result: 证明算子Schmidt秩下界对任意两体Clifford酉算符均可在每QPU至多2个辅助量子比特的常数开销下达到。对于Clifford+T分解指定的非Clifford酉算符，所构造实现的纠缠代价至多高于该下界2t个Bell对，其中t为T-count。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在常数量子比特开销下，算子Schmidt秩下界对Clifford酉算符是可达到的，对一般Clifford+T酉算符也仅需付出与T-count成正比的额外纠缠代价，从而明确了分布式量子计算中通信代价与量子比特开销之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computation connects multiple quantum processing units (QPUs) through quantum communication to jointly perform large-scale quantum computations. Since the number of qubits available at each QPU is limited, it is important to reduce quantum communication while keeping the qubit overhead small. To this end, we study an entanglement-assisted model, in which entanglement consumption serves as a measure of quantum communication cost. For exact deterministic implementations in this model, the operator Schmidt rank of the target bipartite unitary provides a general lower bound on entanglement cost when qubit overhead is unrestricted. However, it has remained unclear how closely this bound can be approached with constant qubit overhead. In this work, we show that this lower bound is attainable for every bipartite Clifford unitary using at most two auxiliary qubits per QPU. In addition, for non-Clifford unitaries specified by exact Clifford+$T$ decompositions with $T$-count $t$, we construct implementations with the same qubit overhead whose entanglement cost is at most $2t$ Bell pairs above this lower bound.

</details>


### [130] [Parallel algebraic surgery for qLDPC codes with constant shuttling depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39874)
*Tian-Gang Zhou,Boren Gu,Jens Eisert,Chen Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出面向提升乘积码（lifted product codes）的"代数手术"（algebraic surgery）方案，通过逻辑测量、辅助码与原子迁移（shuttling）的协同设计，实现硬件高效的泡利积测量，并在中性原子处理器上以恒定迁移深度支持大规模并行逻辑测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构原子阵列是实现高速率量子低密度奇偶校验（qLDPC）码的潜力平台，但其计算优势取决于能否在不引入过高迁移开销与空间开销的前提下实现逻辑操作。传统基于图的手术方案在多逻辑测量并行时效率受限，因此需要新的协议设计。

Method: 提出针对提升乘积码的"代数手术"（algebraic surgery）方法：将逻辑测量、辅助码（auxiliary codes）与原子迁移进行协同设计，以支持硬件高效的泡利积（Pauli-product）测量。协议在循环对称性约束下提供灵活的逻辑可寻址性，可支持包括物理支撑高度重叠的规范逻辑算符、同一码块内或不同提升规模（lift size）码块之间的联合测量等并行逻辑测量。在明确的布局假设下，于中性原子处理器上实现恒定迁移深度。

Result: 在代表性硬件模型基准测试中，一个支持33个并行规范逻辑测量的周期，比所对比的单可观测量的基于图手术调度方案快约2倍，且量子比特开销具有竞争力。迁移与电路级仿真结果得到验证。

Conclusion: qLDPC码的代数结构不仅可用于降低空间开销，还可用于简化物理实现并扩展中性原子处理器上可高效访问的逻辑操作集合，为硬件高效的逻辑测量与并行逻辑操作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reconfigurable atom arrays provide a promising platform for high-rate quantum low-density parity-check codes, but their computational advantage depends on whether logical operations can be implemented without incurring prohibitive shuttling and space overheads. We introduce algebraic surgery for lifted product codes, co-designing the logical measurements, auxiliary codes, and atom shuttling to enable hardware-efficient Pauli-product measurements. These protocols provide flexible logical addressability subject to the cyclic-symmetry constraint, enabling a broad class of parallel logical measurements, including canonical logical operators with heavily overlapping physical supports and joint measurements within or between code blocks even with different lift sizes, while achieving constant shuttling depth on neutral-atom processors under explicit layout assumptions. In a representative hardware-model benchmark, a cycle supporting 33 parallel canonical logical measurements is about $2\times$ faster than the compared single-observable graph-based surgery schedules, with competitive qubit overhead. These shuttling and circuit-level simulations confirm that the algebraic structure of quantum low-density parity-check codes can be used not only to reduce space overhead, but also to simplify physical implementation and expand the set of efficiently accessible logical operations on neutral-atom processors.

</details>


### [131] [Fault Tolerant Quantum Phases of Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39879)
*Colin V. Coane,Shouzhen Gu,Aleksander Kubica*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息保护与容错传输能力的混合态量子相分类，用带测量与经典通信的浅层局域信道电路定义相等价，证明容错相的整体性与约化子空间码分类，并揭示综合征结构对容错性的决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子相分类多针对纯态与基态，缺乏对混合态、特别是编码逻辑量子信息的态集在噪声下相变的系统刻画；同时子系统码分类依赖规范子系统的特定状态，且综合征结构如何影响测量电路的容错性尚不清楚。

Method: 将相等价定义为编码逻辑量子信息的态集之间，由浅层局域信道电路连接，并辅以局域测量与测量结果的全局经典通信，且所有量子操作（含测量）须对噪声鲁棒；以局域噪声恢复阈值的存在性作为相的整体性质，通过有限深度局域信道电路关联两组非零阈值态集，并分析以扩展综合征与点状综合征为特征的稳定子/量子位稳定子码电路。
