Result: 证明非零恢复阈值是容错相的整体属性；任意两组经有限深度局域信道电路关联且具非零阈值的态集属于同一容错相；给出不偏向规范子系统任意状态的子系统码分类，并利用3D规范色码中的维度跳跃证明2D与3D色码相等价；发现具扩展综合征的qudit稳定子码可单次态制备，而测量点状综合征的广泛浅层电路（含2D拓扑码态制备及可解任意子理论的非阿贝尔拓扑序）不具容错性。

Conclusion: 容错性可作为混合态量子相的分类不变量，相等价由浅层局域信道电路结合测量与经典通信刻画；子系统码可无偏分类，且色码跨维度相等价；综合征结构（扩展 vs. 点状）是决定测量电路能否容错的关键因素。

Abstract: We introduce a classification of quantum phases of mixed states based on the ability to preserve and fault tolerantly transfer information. We formulate phase equivalence between sets of states that encode logical quantum information and are connected by shallow local channel circuits assisted by local measurements and global classical communication of measurement outcomes, where all quantum operations, including measurements, must be robust to noise. We show that the existence of a recovery threshold for local noise is a property of an entire phase. We prove that any two sets of states with nonzero thresholds that are related by finite-depth local channel circuits belong to the same fault tolerant phase. Our approach leads to a classification of subsystem codes without favoring any state of the gauge subsystem, and we demonstrate phase equivalence between 2D and 3D color codes using dimensional jumps within the 3D gauge color code. Lastly, we study how syndrome structure affects fault tolerance in circuits with measurements. We show that qudit stabilizer codes with extended syndromes permit single-shot state preparation, whereas a broad class of shallow circuits measuring point-like syndromes, including state preparation for 2D topological codes and non-Abelian topological orders with solvable anyon theories, is not fault tolerant.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum State Routing and Perfect State Transfer on Signed Graphs under Environmental Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39890)
*Nur Mohammad Sanfui,Supriyo Dutta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于边重复符号图上硬币式Szegedy量子行走的确定性拓扑量子路由架构。通过将边符号视为平衡硬币反射中的局域相移，实现路由节点处精确零背散射条件，在符号哑铃开关与可扩展胶合二叉树等原型上实现单位保真度的确定性完美态传输，并分析了开放系统振幅与相位阻尼噪声下的路由性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式通信网络中路由未知量子信息需要自主、无需测量的机制以避免波函数坍缩。Szegedy量子行走虽可提供空间态传输机制，但传统无权图上的行走存在严重的背反射、空间色散与信道串扰问题，限制了其路由可靠性。

Method: 采用边重复符号图（edge-duplicated signed graphs）上的硬币式Szegedy量子行走构建确定性拓扑量子路由架构；将边符号建模为平衡硬币反射中的局域相移，从而在路由节点处强制满足精确的零背散射条件。

Result: 在所考察的基本原型（符号哑铃开关D_{2m,0,2n}与可扩展胶合二叉树）上，实现了在精确到达时刻具有单位保真度的确定性完美态传输（PST）；并在现实的开放系统振幅阻尼与相位阻尼噪声信道下评估了路由性能。

Conclusion: 边重复符号图上的硬币式Szegedy量子行走可提供一种无需测量、抑制背反射的确定性拓扑量子路由方案，适用于分布式量子通信网络中的未知量子信息传输，并具备在噪声环境下的性能可分析性。

Abstract: Routing unknown quantum information across distributed communication networks requires autonomous, measurement-free mechanisms to prevent wave-function collapse. Szegedy quantum walks provide a mechanism for spatial state transport. The conventional walks on unweighted graphs suffer from severe back-reflection, spatial dispersion, and channel crosstalk. In this paper, we introduce a deterministic topological quantum routing architecture based on coined Szegedy quantum walks on edge-duplicated signed graphs. By treating edge signs as localized phase shifts within a balanced coin reflection, we enforce an exact zero back-scattering condition across routing nodes. We demonstrate deterministic Perfect State Transfer (PST) with unit fidelity at exact arrival times across fundamental archetypes, including the signed dumbbell switch $D_{2m,0,2n}$ and scalable glued binary trees. Furthermore, we analyse routing performance under realistic open-system amplitude and phase damping noise channels.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum Sensing and Hamiltonian Learning under Stochastic Parameter Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39908)
*Kelvin Koor,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"随机演化哈密顿量"(SEH)概念，即系数由随机微分方程(SDE)驱动的局域哈密顿量，并研究其哈密顿量学习任务，分析海森堡极限的可保持与不可保持区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习研究通常假设系数是确定性的，而实际量子信息处理中控制参数可能受随机过程驱动。作者希望将随机微分方程引入量子信息处理场景，拓展理论框架。

Method: 定义SEH（系数服从SDE的局域哈密顿量），建立其理论性质；重新分析与扩展Huang-Tong-Fang-Su协议（首个实现哈密顿量系数学习海森堡极限的方案），刻画可保留海森堡极限的理论区间；并发展量子传感与哈密顿量模拟的新工具。

Result: 在SEH情形下对Huang-Tong-Fang-Su协议进行了必要扩展，理论上明确划分了海森堡极限能够保持和无法保持的参数区间，并给出了若干可用于量子传感和哈密顿量模拟的工具。

Conclusion: 研究为控制参数由SDE驱动的量子信息处理设定开辟了方向，鼓励探索此类随机演化哈密顿量下的更多量子信息处理问题。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of stochastically evolving Hamiltonians (SEHs), which are local Hamiltonians whose coefficients are governed by stochastic differential equations (SDEs). After developing some theoretical aspects of these objects, we consider the task of Hamiltonian learning for SEHs. We reanalyse and make necessary extensions to the Huang-Tong-Fang-Su protocol, which first achieved the Heisenberg limit for Hamiltonian coefficient learning, under these circumstances. We delineate theoretically the regimes where the Heisenberg limit can and cannot be retained. Along the way we develop a few tools in quantum sensing and Hamiltonian simulation that may be of independent interest under similar settings in quantum information processing. The broad aim is to encourage the exploration of various settings in quantum information processing in which the control parameters are governed by SDEs.

</details>


### [134] [Distillation of N-Qubit Stabilizer States on a Star Network Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39916)
*Theodore M. Mahaffey,Chaohan Cui,Saikat Guha,Murphy Yuezhen Niu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种利用任意[[n,k,d]]稳定子码将N量子比特GHZ态副本蒸馏为逻辑副本的纠缠蒸馏协议，适用于星型网络，抗去极化噪声能力强，并推广至任意CSS稳定子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子网络中，噪声会使GHZ态等纠缠资源退化，需要高效的纠缠蒸馏方案以在多个量子处理器间分发高保真纠缠态，并突破以往仅针对Bell对或GHZ态的蒸馏局限。

Method: 基于任意[[n,k,d]]稳定子码设计纠缠蒸馏协议，在星型网络拓扑上给出显式构造，仅需局部操作，可扩展至任意N，并最小化空闲物理比特受误差影响；利用稳定子形式化证明协议可推广到任意N量子比特CSS稳定子态。

Result: 在5-qubit码、toric码和[[144,12,12]]双变量自行车码上的数值实验表明，针对独立单比特去极化噪声，在去极化率高达或超过6%时，编码方案相对单裸GHZ态仍提高最终GHZ态保真度；并证明该协议可应用于任意CSS稳定子态，某些稳定子码可蒸馏任意稳定子态。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了以往基于稳定子的Bell对与GHZ态蒸馏方案的工作原理，并将其推广到更一般的稳定子态，为多处理器量子网络中的高保真纠缠分发提供了可扩展的通用框架。

Abstract: We introduce an entanglement distillation protocol that utilizes an arbitrary $[[n,k,d]]$ stabilizer code to convert $n$ raw copies of an $N$-qubit Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) state into $k$ logical copies in the presence of Pauli noise. Our explicit formulation of the scheme on a star network topology is efficiently scalable to arbitrary $N$, requires only local operations, and minimizes the impact of errors on idle physical qubits, enabling the distribution of high-fidelity entangled logical states between any number of quantum processors. We report the results of numerical experiments using the 5-qubit code, toric code, and $[[144,12,12]]$ bivariate bicyle code to protect against independent single-qubit depolarizing noise on all qubits. Our encoding scheme improves the final GHZ state fidelity relative to a single bare GHZ state sent over the same noisy channel for depolarizing rates up to or above $6 \%$ for all codes tested. Additionally, we use the stabilizer formalism to prove that our protocol can be applied to any $N$-qubit Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) stabilizer state, from which the GHZ state emerges as a special case. This result explicitly establishes the working principle of previous stabilizer-based Bell pair and GHZ state distillation schemes, and generalizes such protocols beyond the traditionally considered states. We also show that some stabilizer codes can distill any stabilizer state with our protocol.

</details>


### [135] [Verifiable quantum advantage based on polynomials with planted structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39918)
*Markus Bläser,Michael Gullans,Dominik Hangleiter,Yuxuan Liu,Youming Qiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"模拟秘密(simulation secrets)"思想用于可验证量子优势,基于含植入独立空间的立方多项式IQP电路,在两条猜想下实现验证时间与经典对手通过协议时间之间的多项式级差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势理论的核心问题:是否存在既具备与随机电路采样相近资源需求、又能仅凭量子计算经典输出进行验证的量子优势协议。

Method: 提出用于可验证优势的"模拟秘密"概念:验证者可利用模拟秘密以快于经典对手通过交叉熵检验的速度完成评估。用含植入独立空间的立方多项式描述IQP电路加以实例化;这些独立空间对应多项式在一般线性群轨道下的最大独立集,并产生对应态的低秩稳定子分解。

Result: 在两条猜想下——大独立空间对计算受限对手不可见、以及对均匀随机多项式通过交叉熵检验的细粒度复杂性假设——该方案在验证时间与经典对手通过协议所需时间之间给出多项式级差距(二者均为指数级)。输出分布具有高最小熵,潜在可用于生成经典可认证的随机性;估计该植入多项式方案可在约10^{-6}逻辑错误率下用100个逻辑量子比特实现。

Conclusion: 该工作为可验证量子优势提供了新范式,并将模拟秘密与IQP电路结构、细粒度复杂性猜想相结合,同时指向经典可认证随机性生成及可行的实验实现规模。

Abstract: A central question in the theory of quantum advantage is whether there are quantum advantage protocols with similar resource requirements as random circuit sampling that are also verifiable just from the classical outputs of the quantum computation. Here, we develop the idea of simulation secrets for verifiable advantage. A verifier can use a simulation secret to evaluate a cross-entropy test faster than it would take a classical adversary to pass the test. We instantiate this idea using IQP circuits described by cubic polynomials with planted independent spaces. These correspond to the largest independent set in the orbit of a polynomial under the general linear group and yield a low-rank stabilizer decomposition of the corresponding state. We conjecture that large independent spaces are invisible to a computationally bounded adversary, and therefore they cannot exploit them to pass the protocol. A second conjecture regards the fine-grained complexity of producing samples that pass the cross-entropy test for uniformly random polynomials. Under these conjectures, our scheme results in a polynomial gap between the verification time and the time a classical adversary would need to pass the protocol---both are exponential. It has a potential application to generating classically certifiable randomness, since the output distributions have high min-entropy. We estimate that the planted polynomial scheme is implementable using 100 logical qubits at logical error rates around $10^{-6}$.

</details>


### [136] [Fault-tolerant cost of shallow QAOA on near-symmetric optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39922)
*Jernej Rudi Finžgar,Martin Leib,Elisabeth Wybo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究深度一QAOA在容错量子计算下的实际成本，证明通过小角度Clifford+T旋转合成可将每电路非Clifford代价降至Õ(n²)，并分析了电路编译所需的成本函数Fourier系数泄漏问题，构造了非Clifford二次缩放与困难精确优化共存的实例族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Montanaro与Zhou已证明深度一QAOA能以Ω(1)概率求解某类近对称约束满足问题的planted解，并在显式实例上观察到经典通用求解器的指数级运行时间，被视为低深度QAOA经验性指数加速的强证据。本文追问：若将该电路容错实现，其代价究竟如何？重点关注非Clifford门成本以及编译所需的显式子句列表是否会泄漏planted解。

Method: 1) 分析深度一QAOA成本哈密顿量：其包含Θ(n^ℓ)个子句，但成功所需的旋转角随n^{1-ℓ}缩小；2) 采用小角度Clifford+T旋转合成技术，对任意子句局域性ℓ控制非Clifford门数量；3) 研究编译电路所需的显式子句列表：指出使QAOA恒定成功率的内在机制固定了成本函数的一次Fourier系数，其符号可揭示planted解；4) 设计特定问题族，使二次非Clifford缩放与困难精确优化可以共存。

Result: 对任意子句局域性ℓ，小角度Clifford+T旋转合成将每电路的非Clifford代价降至Õ(n²)；同时发现恒定QAOA成功机制所固定的一次Fourier系数符号会泄漏planted解信息，但该泄漏并不必然使问题变易，可构造出非Clifford二次缩放与困难精确优化并存的实例族。

Conclusion: 深度一QAOA在容错实现下具有Õ(n²)的非Clifford门代价，且这一成本与经典困难性可以兼容。然而编译阶段对显式子句列表的依赖引入了Fourier系数泄漏，虽不一定破坏经典困难性，但构成需关注的实现约束。

Abstract: Montanaro and Zhou proved that depth-one Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) finds the planted solution of certain near-symmetric constraint satisfaction problems with probability $Ω(1)$, and observed exponential runtimes for general-purpose classical solvers on explicit realizations, providing strong evidence of an empirical exponential speedup of low-depth QAOA. We ask what such a circuit costs fault-tolerantly. Although the cost Hamiltonian carries $Θ(n^\ell)$ clauses, the rotation angle at which QAOA succeeds shrinks as $n^{1-\ell}$, and we show that small-angle Clifford$+T$ rotation synthesis reduces the non-Clifford cost per circuit to $\widetilde O(n^2)$ for every clause locality $\ell$. Compiling the circuit, however, requires the explicit clause list. We find that the mechanism underlying constant QAOA success fixes the degree-one Fourier coefficients of the cost, whose signs reveal the planted solution. However, this leakage need not reveal the solution: we design families in which the quadratic non-Clifford scaling and hard exact optimization coexist.

</details>


### [137] [A Few Constrain Many: Correlation-Enhanced Learning of Many-Body Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39935)
*Matteo Tedde,Davide Girolami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文表明量子观测量之间的关联可降低量子学习复杂度：少量测量即可约束指数级依赖但未测量的量，从而高效估计量子态的一般函数，并在100量子比特系统上实现了对纠缠、magic等量子资源的更优估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定多体量子系统的性质因需测定参数数量庞大而极具挑战，作者希望利用量子观测量之间的关联来降低量子学习的复杂度。

Method: 利用多项式数量的选定测量来约束指数级依赖但未测量量的取值，进而设计"相关性信息学习"(correlation-informed learning)算法，估计纠缠、magic等关键量子资源；方法基于多项式数量的Pauli string测量信息，且无需测量前纠缠门。

Result: 在100量子比特系统上，所提算法的相对误差低于shadow tomography与neural-state方法。

Conclusion: 量子约束本身即为探索大规模量子系统的资源，且该协议可在当今量子计算机上直接测试。

Abstract: Discovering properties of many-body quantum systems is challenging because of the large number of parameters to determine. Here, we show that correlations among quantum observables help reduce the complexity of quantum learning. A polynomial number of selected measurements can bound the values of exponentially many dependent yet unmeasured quantities, enabling efficient estimation of general functions of quantum states. We leverage this result to design correlation-informed learning algorithms that estimate key quantum resources, such as entanglement and magic, in systems of 100 qubits. They achieve a lower relative error than shadow tomography and neural-state methods by using information about a polynomial number of measured Pauli strings. These protocols are readily testable with today's quantum computers, as they do not require premeasurement entangling gates. Quantum constraints themselves are therefore a resource for exploring large quantum systems.

</details>


### [138] [Fast and Sure-ious Quantum Process Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39941)
*A. Afham,Sayantan Sen,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明partial normalization是量子信道Choi态在fidelity（等价于Bures或purified距离）下的精确投影，从而打通QST到QPT的闭式提升路径，并给出保持Choi秩、以Kraus形式计算、无需Choi维特征分解的同余变换，将两个单拷贝协议提升为QPT算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子过程层析（QPT）常通过Choi-Jamiolkowski同构由量子态层析（QST）提升得到，但需额外施加保迹（TP）约束。现有方法分为两类：folklore partial normalization虽有闭式但仅被当作启发式使用，其分析是渐近的且丢失维度多项式因子；基于投影的方法有原理保障却无闭式，且随维度扩展性差。论文旨在解决这一二分困境。

Method: 证明partial normalization恰为量子信道Choi态集在fidelity（等价Bures或purified距离）下的精确投影；由此得到单一闭式同余变换，保持Choi秩并以Kraus形式计算，无需对Choi维度做特征分解；据此将两个单拷贝QST协议提升为QPT协议，其中一个为Fidelity Projected Least Squares（非自适应）。

Result: 证明非自适应的Fidelity Projected Least Squares能恢复Choi秩，并以与1/ε成正比的信道使用次数达到infidelity ε。数值实验中，对低Choi秩信道，其精度优于state-of-the-art projected least squares，且在TP正则化步骤及返回估计的时间与内存上改进了若干数量级。

Conclusion: partial normalization的投影解释消除了QPT中原理性与可计算性之间的二分：任何在fidelity下有保证的QST算法都可经一次闭式同余提升为具有相同样本复杂度的QPT算法，兼顾理论保证、闭式表达与良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Quantum process tomography (QPT) protocols are often designed by lifting quantum state tomography (QST) protocols through the Choi-Jamiolkowski isomorphism, at the cost of an extra step enforcing the trace-preserving (TP) constraint. Existing approaches fall into two classes. The folklore partial normalization, which sandwiches the density estimate between inverse square roots of its input marginal, has a closed form but is used as a heuristic, its only analyses being asymptotic and losing a factor polynomial in the dimension. Projection-based methods are principled but have no closed forms and scale poorly with dimension. We resolve this dichotomy by showing that the partial normalization is the exact projection onto the Choi states of quantum channels in fidelity (equivalently in Bures or purified distance). Consequently, any QST algorithm with guarantees in fidelity lifts to a QPT algorithm that inherits its sample complexity, through a single closed-form congruence that preserves the Choi rank and is computed in Kraus form without any eigendecomposition of Choi dimension. We lift two single-copy protocols and prove that the non-adaptive one (Fidelity Projected Least Squares) recovers the Choi rank and reaches infidelity $\varepsilon$ from a number of channel uses proportional to $1/\varepsilon$. Numerically, for channels of low Choi rank, it improves on the state-of-the-art projected least squares in accuracy, and by orders of magnitude in the time and memory of the TP-regularization step and of the returned estimate.

</details>


### [139] [Scaling law of variational quantum algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39949)
*Ningfeng Wang,Zhengwei Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过大规模数值实验研究变分量子算法在组合优化问题上的标度律，在最多 n=5000 量子比特、O(log n) 电路深度下，收敛能量与基准最优/参考能量的偏差不超过 0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究变分量子算法在组合优化任务中的可扩展性（标度律），即在量子比特数增长时其性能是否仍能保持，从而判断其实际可用性。

Method: 开展大规模数值实验，采用经典可模拟的拟设（classically simulable ansatz），电路深度为 O(log n)，量子比特规模扩展至 n = 5000。

Result: 在最大 n = 5000 量子比特的实验规模下，算法收敛能量与基准报告的最优能量或参考能量之间的偏差始终不超过 0.5%。

Conclusion: 在大规模、浅层（O(log n) 深度）且经典可模拟的设定下，变分量子算法能在组合优化问题上稳定逼近最优解，偏差极小，显示出良好的标度行为。

Abstract: We study scaling law of variational quantum algorithms by large-scale numerical experiments on combinatorial optimizations. On experiments up to n = 5000 qubits with O( log(n)) circuit depth with a classically simulable ansatz, the converged energies consistently differ by no more than 0.5% from the optimal or reference energies reported in the benchmarks.

</details>


### [140] [Haah's 3D cubic code thermalizes rapidly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39959)
*Sebastian Stengele,Libor Caha,Ángela Capel,Simone Warzel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明Haah三维立方码在任意正温度下都快速热化，从而否定其作为自纠错量子记忆体的可能性；这是首个针对任意分形子码在所有正温度下快速混合的解析证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Haah三维立方码因缺乏弦算符被视为三维自纠错量子记忆体的候选，但其是否真正自纠错长期悬而未决；此前仅有数值探索记忆时间，缺乏解析上界。

Method: 借助[Commun. Math. Phys. 407, 195 (2026)]的框架，将关联衰减条件转化为Davies生成元的修正对数Sobolev不等式，从而给出严格的热化分析。

Result: 证明立方码在每个正温度下都快速热化，给出严格的解析上界，解决了这一长期开放问题。

Conclusion: 立方码不是自纠错量子记忆体；该工作首次对任意分形子码在所有正温度下给出快速混合的解析证明。

Abstract: Haah's 3D cubic code was introduced as a candidate for a self-correcting quantum memory in three dimensions, using the absence of string logical operators to obstruct thermal errors. It has been a long-standing open question whether it is a self-correcting quantum memory. Even though the memory time was explored numerically, no analytical upper bound was known. Here, we settle this question rigorously and show that the cubic code thermalizes rapidly at every positive temperature. Our proof relies on the framework of [Commun. Math. Phys. 407, 195 (2026)] to turn a decay-of-correlations condition into a modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality for the Davies generator. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first analytic proof of rapid mixing for all positive temperatures for any fracton code.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum Algorithms for Computational Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39962)
*Mario Guillaume Cecile,Nis-Luca van Hülst,Tomohiro Hashizume,Pia Siegl,Abhishek Setty,José Diogo da Costa Jesus,Paul Over,Sergio Bengoechea,Muhammad Umer,Spyros Tserkis,Eleftherios Mastorakis,Tristan Kraft,Francisco Cárdenas-López,Leonardo Scandurra,Thomas Rung,Felix Motzoi,Belda Yesil,Barbara Kraus,Martin Kiffner,Dimitris G. Angelakis,Eugene de Villiers,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向CFD中偏微分方程的量子求解方法，涵盖全量子算法（QLSA/HHL、QSVT、哈密顿模拟、QLBM）与混合量子-经典方法（QPINN、振幅编码变分PDE求解器），并引入张量网络形式化连接CFD离散化与量子态/算子/线路，进而转化为张量可编程变分量子算法（TP-VQA），最后结合基准问题评估量子优势的条件依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD中PDE求解计算成本高昂，量子计算有望提供加速潜力；但现有研究分散于不同算法框架，且当前处于含噪中型量子（NISQ）与初现容错架构的过渡期，亟需系统梳理硬件无关算法、数学表述、算法结构及其局限，明确量子优势的适用边界。

Method: 采用综述与比较分析方法：按全量子与混合量子-经典两类框架系统梳理QLSA（HHL至QSVT）、哈密顿模拟、QLBM、QPINN及振幅编码变分PDE求解器；对每种框架分析数学形式化、算法结构与主要限制；引入张量网络（TN）表示，将CFD场、微分算子与几何信息以低秩形式编码，并把紧凑表示映射为TP-VQA；结合Poisson、反应、扩散及非线性模型方程等基准问题进行评估。

Result: TN形式化有效桥接了CFD离散化与量子态、算子及量子线路，使低秩紧凑表示可翻译为张量可编程变分量子算法；量子算法对流体动力学关键特征的捕捉能力因问题而异，潜在量子优势高度依赖条件数、表示复杂度、态制备与测量约束。

Conclusion: 面向CFD的可扩展量子算法仍面临条件数、态制备、测量与表示复杂度等瓶颈；量子优势是问题依赖的，需针对具体PDE与硬件条件选择算法框架。论文勾勒了全量子与混合方法的现有能力、局限及未来挑战。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive review of quantum approaches for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) arising in computational fluid dynamics (CFD). We examine fully quantum approaches, including quantum linear system algorithms (QLSAs), ranging from the Harrow--Hassidim--Lloyd (HHL) algorithm to quantum singular value transformation (QSVT), Hamiltonian simulation, and quantum lattice Boltzmann methods (QLBMs), while emphasizing hybrid quantum--classical approaches, including quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) and amplitude-encoded variational PDE solvers. We focus on hardware-agnostic algorithms compatible with present noisy processors and emerging fault-tolerant architectures. For each framework, we analyze the mathematical formulation, algorithmic structure, and principal limitations. We also examine tensor-network (TN) representations, since CFD fields, differential operators, and geometrical information can often be encoded efficiently in low-rank form. The TN formalism bridges CFD discretizations and quantum states, operators, and circuits, enabling compact representations to be translated into tensor-programmable variational quantum algorithms (TP-VQAs). We further review benchmark problems, including Poisson, reaction, diffusion, and nonlinear model equations, and assess how well quantum algorithms capture key features of fluid dynamics. Our analysis highlights that potential quantum advantage is highly problem dependent and governed by condition number, representational complexity, state preparation, and measurement constraints. We outline capabilities, limitations, and challenges toward scalable quantum algorithms for CFD.

</details>


### [142] [Single-shot state preparation threshold: rigorous theorem and statistical mechanical mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39965)
*Yuanchen Zhao,Yi Yuan,Zhengyi Han,Dong E. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明线性confinement即可保证CSS量子LDPC码的单次态制备阈值，挑战了声度（soundness）必要性的传统观点，并通过统计力学映射与3D环面码数值模拟探讨了超越该假设的阈值条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮带噪syndrome测量制备逻辑码字可降低容错量子计算的时间开销，但哪些码的结构性质足以保证单次态制备（single-shot state preparation）存在阈值尚不清楚。传统观点认为soundness是必要条件，本文旨在澄清充分条件并探索更弱的阈值保证。

Method: 理论证明：对CSS量子低密度奇偶校验（qLDPC）码，证明线性confinement足以给出非零的单次态制备阈值；在距离至少为对数增长时，证明低于非零局部随机读出与数据错误阈值下，形式理想最终恢复后的逻辑错误概率指数小并随码尺寸趋于零；在更强线性soundness假设下，证明制备协议的残余数据错误是局部随机的，可直接与其他容错gadget组合。进一步建立统计力学映射，将最终逻辑错误率表示为经典配分函数，并对三维环面码进行Monte Carlo模拟。

Result: 线性confinement对CSS qLDPC码足以保证非零单次态制备阈值；在对数距离下，逻辑错误概率在非零局部随机读出/数据错误阈值以下指数小且随码尺寸趋于零；线性soundness下残余数据错误为局部随机，可实现容错组合。3D环面码数值模拟表明亚线性soundness也可能支持单次态制备阈值。

Conclusion: 线性confinement是单次态制备阈值的充分结构条件，而非soundness必要；线性soundness进一步保证残余错误的局部随机性以支持模块化容错组合；数值证据提示亚线性soundness亦可能足够，为更广泛码族的单次容错量子计算提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Preparing logical codewords with a single round of noisy syndrome measurements can reduce the time overhead of fault-tolerant quantum computation. It remains unclear which structural properties of the code suffice to guarantee a threshold for single-shot state preparation. Here we prove that linear confinement suffices for a nonzero single-shot state-preparation threshold for CSS quantum low-density parity-check codes, in contrast to the common belief that soundness is necessary. When the distance is at least logarithmic, we prove that below nonzero local stochastic readout and data error thresholds, the logical error probability after a formal ideal final recovery is exponentially small and approaches zero when the code size approaches infinity. Under the stronger assumption of linear soundness, we show that the residual data error of the preparation protocol is local stochastic, directly allowing composition with other fault-tolerant gadgets. To investigate single-shot state-preparation threshold beyond the above assumptions, we further develop a statistical mechanical mapping that expresses the final logical error rate in terms of a classical partition function. We perform Monte Carlo simulations of this model for the three-dimensional toric code. The numerical results suggest that sublinear soundness can also support a single-shot state-preparation threshold.

</details>


### [143] [The quantum query complexity of the semigroup product problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39974)
*Troy Lee,Miklos Santha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究半群乘积 $x_1\cdots x_n$ 的量子查询复杂度，引入积宽度 $\beta$ 刻画代数结构，给出交换非周期幺半群紧界 $\Theta(\min\{n,\sqrt{n\beta}\})$，并改进一般有限非周期半群的查询上界，同时用 Dyck 下界说明对半群规模的依赖近乎最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Aaronson、Grier、Schaeffer）对大小为 $N-1$ 的有限非周期半群给出 $\sqrt n\,(N\log(nN+2))^{O(N)}$ 的量子查询上界，但该界未能反映半群的代数结构。作者希望获得能体现代数结构（如交换性、非周期性指标、偏序）的查询复杂度刻画与更紧的界。

Method: 引入“积宽度” $\beta$：使每个输入词都存在至多 $\beta$ 个字母的子序列具有相同乘积的最小界。针对不同代数结构分别分析：非平凡有限交换非周期幺半群；具有以单位元为最小元的稳定偏序的幺半群；一般有限非周期半群。并将 $\beta$ 与非周期性指标 $k$ 及规模 $|M|$ 建立关系。

Result: 对非平凡有限交换非周期幺半群，有界错误量子查询复杂度为 $\Theta(\min\{n,\sqrt{n\beta}\})$，完全由积宽度刻画；若其非周期性指标为 $k$，则 $\beta=O(k\log(|M|+1)\log\log(|M|+2))$。对含稳定偏序（单位元为最小元）的幺半群，上界为 $\sqrt{n+1}((\beta+2)\log(n+2))^{O(\log(\beta+2))}$。对任意 $N-1$ 阶有限非周期半群，上界改进为 $\min\{n,\sqrt n\,\log^{O((N\log(N+2))^{1/3})}(n+2)\}$。下界方面，基于 Ambainis 等人的有界深度 Dyck 下界，存在需要 $\sqrt n\,2^{\Omega(N^{1/3})}$ 次量子查询的非周期幺半群，说明对半群规模的依赖近乎紧。

Conclusion: 积宽度 $\beta$ 是刻画半群乘积量子查询复杂度的有效代数参数，尤其对交换非周期幺半群给出紧的 $\Theta(\min\{n,\sqrt{n\beta}\})$ 界。作者将一般有限非周期半群的上界从已有工作的 $N$ 指数级依赖改进为 $(N\log N)^{1/3}$ 的指数依赖，并通过下界证明该规模依赖近乎最优。

Abstract: We study the quantum query complexity of computing a semigroup product $x_1\cdots x_n$, when one query reveals one input element and the multiplication table is given. For a finite aperiodic semigroup of size $N-1$, the argument of Aaronson, Grier, and Schaeffer gives an upper bound of $\sqrt n\,(N\log(nN+2))^{O(N)}$ queries.
  To obtain query bounds that reflect algebraic structure, we study the product breadth $β$: the smallest bound such that every input word has a subsequence of at most $β$ letters with the same product.
  For nontrivial finite commutative aperiodic monoids, the bounded-error quantum query complexity is $Θ(\min\{n,\sqrt{nβ}\})$, and is thus characterized by product breadth. We further show that if such a monoid $M$ has aperiodicity index $k$ (the least positive integer satisfying $x^k=x^{k+1}$ for every $x\in M$), then $β=O(k\log(|M|+1)\log\log(|M|+2))$.
  For monoids with a stable partial order in which the identity is the minimum element, we prove that the bounded-error quantum query complexity is at most $\sqrt{n+1}((β+2)\log(n+2))^{O(\log(β+2))}$.
  For arbitrary finite aperiodic semigroups of order $N-1$, we improve the bound of Aaronson, Grier, and Schaeffer, obtaining a bounded-error quantum query complexity of at most \[ \min\left\{n,\sqrt n\, \log^{O((N\log(N+2))^{1/3})}(n+2)\right\}. \] The dependence on semigroup size is nearly tight: the bounded-depth Dyck lower bound of Ambainis et al. yields aperiodic monoids requiring $\sqrt n\,2^{Ω(N^{1/3})}$ quantum queries in the relevant parameter range.

</details>


### [144] [Logical information localisation in stabiliser codes via single-qubit measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39980)
*Jelena Mackeprang,Hemant Sharma,Jonas Helsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作对稳定子路径查找（SPF）进行系统的解析与计算研究，提出g-SPF并证明其定位阈值，同时给出精确与启发式两类求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPF可在单轮单量子比特测量下，将发生丢失的稳定子码中的逻辑信息定位到预先指定的目标量子比特上，这对资源受限的容错通信（如飞行量子比特）具有重要意义。但已有研究规模有限，缺乏对定位阈值的理论刻画与高效求解算法。

Method: 提出g-SPF：将逻辑信息定位到至多g个未指定目标量子比特的集合上。基于渗流理论与稳定子码的不相交性进行解析论证，证明在i.i.d.量子比特丢失概率p<1/2且平面表面码足够大时，常数g的g-SPF定位成功率趋近于1，从而建立定位阈值。算法上，一是将SPF精确建模为整数线性规划，二是将其建模为解码问题并给出启发式算法。

Result: 数值实验复现了表面码的定位阈值，验证了两种算法的有效性；启发式算法显著更快。相比当前最优算法，求解SPF所需时间大幅降低，使研究规模远大于以往文献的稳定子码成为可能。

Conclusion: 理论证明了g-SPF的定位阈值，算法上实现了高效求解，二者共同为容错量子隐形传态与高效逻辑融合等应用开辟了道路。

Abstract: Stabiliser path finding (SPF) has previously been introduced as a method to localise logical information in a stabiliser code undergoing loss onto a single pre-specified target qubit, using only one round of single-qubit measurements. When working with limited resources and flying qubits, this fast read-out of logical information is a helpful tool for fault-tolerant communication. In this work, we provide a broad analytical and computational study of localisation via SPF. We introduce $g$-SPF, where the task is to localise the logical information onto a set of at most $g$ unspecified target qubits. Through analytical arguments based on percolation theory and the disjointness of stabiliser codes, we prove that for i.i.d. qubit loss with probability $p<1/2$ and sufficiently large planar surface codes, localisation via $g$-SPF for constant $g$ succeeds with a probability converging to one, which establishes a localisation threshold. Furthermore, we propose and implement two algorithms to solve SPF. The first is exact and formulates SPF as an integer linear program, whereas the second is heuristic and formulates SPF as a decoding problem. We validate both algorithms by numerically reproducing the localisation threshold for the surface code and demonstrate that the heuristic algorithm is considerably faster. Our work significantly reduces the time required to solve SPF compared to current state-of-the-art algorithms, allowing us to study localisation in substantially larger stabiliser codes than previously considered in the literature. Together, these theoretical and computational results open the door to various applications, such as fault-tolerant teleportation and efficient logical fusion.

</details>


### [145] [Predicting properties of Scrooge ensembles with high accuracy and low sample complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39992)
*Yue Wu,Yuzhi Tong,Helen Propson,Yihui Quek,Liang Mao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出对Scrooge系综k阶矩的多项式近似，误差阈值由多项式级提升至指数级；据此给出首个高效量子算法，用Õ(k³/ε⁵)份ρ估计可观测期望，并给出k阶矩的块编码构造及对称子空间投影的子程序，避免k!后选择开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scrooge系综是平均密度矩阵为ρ的最大随机系综，在量子多体动力学与量子信息中有应用。其高阶矩因含非多项式Haar被积函数而难以解析；现有采样机制需昂贵后选择，大系统下低效。作者希望发展高效且精确的分析与实现方法。

Method: 1) 对Scrooge第k阶矩引入多项式近似，误差可调，阈值误差从关于1/||ρ||∞的逆多项式改进为指数级；2) 基于该近似给出首个高效量子算法，用Õ(k³/ε⁵)份ρ估计可观测期望值；3) 已知ρ制备线路时，构造Scrooge第k阶矩的块编码；4) 关键子程序：用O(k³/ε²)份ρ高效实现ρ^{⊗k}到k重对称子空间的投影，避免k!后选择代价。

Result: 得到可调误差的多项式近似，误差阈值指数级改进；首个估计可观测期望的高效量子算法，副本复杂度Õ(k³/ε⁵)；Scrooge k阶矩的块编码构造；对称子空间投影子程序仅需O(k³/ε²)份ρ，避免k!后选择成本。

Conclusion: 结果为Scrooge系综的理论分析与实际预测提供新工具，有望应用于量子多体动力学与量子信息处理。

Abstract: The Scrooge ensemble associated with a density matrix $ρ$ is the maximally random ensemble whose average density matrix is $ρ$. It has attracted growing interest for its applications to quantum many-body dynamics and quantum information. The intrinsic statistical properties of the Scrooge ensembles are encoded in their higher-order moments, yet they are nontrivial to analyze and prepare for they feature a non-polynomial Haar integrand, blocking direct use of Haar integration machinery. Existing physical mechanisms to sample multiple copies of states from the Scrooge ensembles, meanwhile, typically require costly postselection, rendering them inefficient for large system size. In this work, we develop efficient and accurate methods for analyzing and implementing Scrooge moments. We first introduce a polynomial approximation to the Scrooge $k$-th moments whose error is tunable down to a threshold that improves from inverse polynomial to exponential in $1/||ρ||_\infty$ over previous results. Building on our approximation, we then give the first efficient quantum algorithm that estimates observable expectation values using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(k^3/ε^5)$ copies of $ρ$ for any target error $ε$ above this threshold. We further provide a construction of a block encoding of the Scrooge $k$-th moment when the preparation circuit of $ρ$ is known. Of independent interest could be a key subroutine in our algorithms, which efficiently implements projection onto the $k$-fold symmetric subspace for $ρ^{\otimes k}$ using $\mathcal{O}(k^3/ε^2)$ samples of $ρ$, avoiding the naive $k!$ postselection cost of such a projection. These results provide new tools for both the theoretical analysis and practical prediction of properties of Scrooge ensembles, with potential applications to quantum many-body dynamics and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [146] [Quantum state learning beyond approximate unitary designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39994)
*Gyungmin Cho,Changhun Oh,Dohun Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文质疑近似酉设计能否普适替代精确设计，指出指数小的设计误差未必保持学习保证；转而从测量电路结构直接推导学习保证，证明对数深度两层Clifford电路在经典阴影观测估计中达到全局Clifford方差缩放，并给出逆阴影信道的紧致张量网络表示，同时揭示复用测量基时近似设计需近线性深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作用对数深度电路实现近似酉设计，从而为量子态学习任务提供浅层测量协议并保持性能。这引出核心问题：近似设计能否在更一般意义上替代精确设计？作者关注"近似Haar随机性"是否等价于"复现其学习保证"。

Method: 构造反例说明指数小的设计误差不保持精确设计的学习保证；随后直接从测量电路结构推导学习保证，分析对数深度两层Clifford电路用于经典阴影的观测估计；给出逆阴影信道的紧致精确张量网络表示；并研究测量基复用情形下近似酉设计的多发极限表现，针对全互联随机两局域电路分析所需深度。

Result: 证明对数深度两层Clifford电路提供无偏估计量，且对任意态和任意Hermitian可观测量均匹配全局Clifford方差缩放；该界使浅层测量在设置高效层析、混合态计量、稳定子结构学习等任务中保留全局Clifford保证；给出逆阴影信道的紧致精确张量网络表示；发现复用基时任意高阶近似设计未必统一匹配Haar方差，全互联随机两局域电路需近线性深度。

Conclusion: 浅层量子态学习兼具能力与局限：近似Haar随机性与复现其学习保证是两回事，近似设计不能普遍替代精确设计；应基于测量电路结构直接建立学习保证。

Abstract: Approximate unitary designs reproduce the statistics of Haar-random unitaries to a given order and accuracy. Recent constructions realize such designs with logarithmic-depth circuits, enabling shallow measurement protocols for various quantum state-learning tasks while preserving performance. These results raise the question of whether approximate designs can replace exact designs more generally. We show that even exponentially small design error need not preserve the learning guarantees of exact designs. This motivates deriving learning guarantees directly from the measurement circuit structure. For observable estimation with classical shadows, we prove that logarithmic-depth two-layer Clifford circuits yield an unbiased estimator matching the global Clifford variance scaling for every state and Hermitian observable. Beyond observable estimation, this bound allows shallow measurements to retain global Clifford guarantees for other tasks, including setting-efficient tomography, mixed-state metrology, and stabilizer structure learning. We complement these statistical guarantees with a compact, exact tensor-network representation of the inverse shadow channel. When measurement bases are reused, approximate unitary designs of arbitrarily high order need not uniformly match the Haar variance in the many-shot limit. For the all-to-all random two-local circuits considered, doing so requires nearly linear depth. Together, our results reveal the capabilities and limitations of shallow quantum state learning, highlighting the distinction between approximating Haar randomness and reproducing its learning guarantees.

</details>


### [147] [An exponential query advantage from access to Stinespring dilation unitaries in quantum channel learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40001)
*Manaki Arihara,Satoshi Yoshida,Philip Taranto,Mio Murao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个量子信道的学习任务，证明了黑盒信道访问与Stinespring膨胀酉算子（及其逆）访问两种模型间查询复杂度存在指数级分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同量子信道访问模型（黑盒 vs. Stinespring膨胀酉算子及逆）在估计信道内在性质时的能力差异，探索系统-环境联合演化访问能否带来指数级优势。

Method: 以估计最优平均门保真度（OAGF）为学习任务，比较两种访问模型的查询复杂度。对常数Kraus秩、膨胀环境维度为polylog(d)的情形，设计使用polylog(d)次查询的算法；对黑盒模型给出Ω(√d)的查询下界。

Result: Stinespring膨胀访问下可用polylog(d)次查询以固定加性精度估计OAGF；黑盒访问下即使Kraus秩为常数、精度为常数，也至少需要Ω(√d)次查询。作为推论，排除了仅用poly(log d, q)次查询的通用模拟器。

Conclusion: 访问系统-环境联合演化及其逆可在估计量子信道内在性质上提供指数级优势，两种访问模型存在本质性复杂度分离。

Abstract: We present a learning task for quantum channels that exhibits an exponential separation in query complexity (in terms of the number of qubits) between two access models: i) black-box channel access and ii) access to a Stinespring dilation unitary of the channel along with its inverse. We consider the task of learning how close the unknown channel is to the set of unitary channels. This is characterized by the \textit{optimal average gate fidelity (OAGF)}, defined as the average gate fidelity maximized over all unitary channels. For constant Kraus rank and a dilation environment of dimension $\operatorname{polylog}(d)$, we provide an algorithm that estimates the OAGF to fixed additive accuracy using $\operatorname{polylog}(d)$ queries to the Stinespring dilation unitary and its inverse. In contradistinction, under the black-box access model, this estimation requires at least $Ω(\sqrt{d})$ queries, even for channels of constant Kraus rank and constant additive accuracy. As a corollary, we rule out a universal simulator that uses only $\operatorname{poly}(\log d,q)$ queries to the unknown channel to approximate the output of arbitrary $q$-query algorithms with access to a randomly-sampled Stinespring dilation unitary and its inverse, even to sufficiently small constant error. These results demonstrate that access to the system-environment joint evolution and its inverse can provide an exponential advantage in estimating an intrinsic property of a quantum channel.

</details>


### [148] [Technical analysis of the Resource-efficient Quantum Walkers Quantum Random Access Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40020)
*Giuseppe De Riso,Giuseppe Catalano,Seth Lloyd,Vittorio Giovannetti,Dario De Santis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是量子随机存取存储器(qRAM)架构的技术性后续论文，基于离散时间量子行走者模型，提供完整数学推导、协议规范与资源分析，并新增技术以提升实现可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有qRAM方案存在两大缺陷："Bucket Brigade"架构需要指数级O(2^n)活跃节点，而先前的量子行走者方案存在空间瓶颈(需多个并行树)。本文旨在解决这些问题，使qRAM实现更接近现实。

Method: 采用离散时间量子行走者构建资源高效的qRAM架构，量子行走者数量随地址大小n与消息大小m线性增长，无需并行树。提出长程与短程两种算法范式：长程方案使用多目标受控门，短程方案将相互作用分解为2体和3体局部门序列。形式化路由、消息复制与行走者检索三个阶段，并探索玻色子、双轨量子比特和四能级qudit等不同行走者类型。

Result: 短程方案实现了最优的O(n+m)电路深度，将多目标受控门分解为局部2体、3体门序列，提升了近实验实现的可行性。

Conclusion: 该模型避免了Bucket Brigade的指数级节点需求，克服了先前量子行走者方案的空间瓶颈。短程方案达到最优O(n+m)电路深度，更适合近期实验实现。

Abstract: Quantum Random Access Memory (qRAM) is a critical component for achieving quantum advantage in algorithms ranging from database search to quantum machine learning. In a recently introduced model [arXiv:2508.02855], we proposed a resource-efficient qRAM architecture based on discrete-time quantum walkers. This article serves as a comprehensive technical follow-up, providing the full mathematical derivations, detailed protocol specifications, and in-depth resource analysis. Moreover, we extend the original proposal with novel techniques for the purpose of making the qRAM implementation more realistic. Our model resolves the primary drawbacks of leading qRAM proposals: it avoids the exponential number of active nodes $\mathcal{O}(2^n)$ required by the "Bucket Brigade" architecture by employing a number of quantum walkers that scales linearly with the address and message sizes, $n$ and $m$, respectively. Simultaneously, it overcomes the spatial bottlenecks of previous quantum-walker schemes by eliminating the need for multiple parallel trees. We propose two algorithmic paradigms: the long- and the short-range approaches, which differ by the length of interaction of the main routing gates employed in the qRAM. We formalize the routing, message-copy, and walker retrieval phases for both variants and we show how, while the long-range scheme employs controlled gates having a multitude of target systems, the short-range approach decomposes these interactions into sequences of 2- and 3-body local gates, which improves the architecture's feasibility for near-term experimental implementation. Finally, our comprehensive resource analysis confirms that the short-range approach achieves the optimal $\mathcal{O}(n+m)$ circuit depth. Within these two paradigms, we explore the potential of different types of quantum walkers, namely bosons, dual-rail qubits and four-level qudits.

</details>


### [149] [CSS codes for Quantum Metrology with Discrete-time Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40022)
*Ugnė Liaubaitė,Debora Ramacciotti,Robert Raußendorf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种基于CSS码的量子度量协议，证明对垂直于探测哈密顿量的噪声，离散时间纠错可在有限探测时间窗口内保持类海森堡时间标度，并给出Steane码与Shor码的解析与数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度量旨在以超越经典精度估计未知参数并达到海森堡标度，但实际噪声会显著削弱这一优势并常使其退化为标准量子极限（SQL）性能；因此需要借助量子纠错实现噪声鲁棒的度量策略。

Method: 构建基于Calderbank-Shor-Steane（CSS）码的量子度量协议，发展适用于任意CSS码的解析框架，刻画离散时间纠错下的动力学，并将其应用于Steane码和Shor码。

Result: 对垂直于探测哈密顿量的噪声，离散时间纠错在时长依赖于纠错频率的有限探测窗口内保持类海森堡时间标度；仅在探测演化后施加恢复操作，相比未纠错的噪声协议无度量优势；确定了Steane与Shor码的类海森堡到类SQL的转变及最优探测时间，并进行了数值分析。

Conclusion: 量子纠错（尤其是离散时间纠错）能在特定噪声方向下于有限窗口内保留量子度量增益，但其有效性依赖于纠错时机与频率；仅在演化后恢复无益，需在探测过程中进行纠错。

Abstract: The goal of quantum metrology is to estimate an unknown parameter with better-than-classical precision and, ideally, to attain Heisenberg scaling. In realistic settings, however, noise can substantially reduce this advantage and, in many cases, restore standard-quantum-limit-like performance. Preserving the quantum enhancement therefore requires noise-robust strategies, which can be implemented using quantum error correction. In this work, we develop and analyze a quantum-metrology protocol based on Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes. The main result is that, for noise perpendicular to the sensing Hamiltonian, discrete-time error correction preserves Heisenberg-like temporal scaling over a finite interrogation-time window whose duration depends on the correction frequency. It is further shown that recovery applied only after the sensing evolution provides no metrological advantage over the corresponding uncorrected noisy protocol in the setup considered here. An analytical framework is developed for arbitrary CSS codes and applied to the Steane and Shor codes, for which the discrete-time corrected dynamics, the Heisenberg-like-to-SQL-like crossover, and the optimal interrogation time are determined and numerically analysed.

</details>


### [150] [Measurement-induced dynamics and emergent symmetries of particles moving in a one-dimensional lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40026)
*Salvatore Di Lorenzo,Klaus Mølmer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过弱连续测量单格点占据数，可在无初始对称性的一维晶格多粒子系统中诱导纠缠并稳定地筛选出玻色、费米及仲统计对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续测量既揭示又改变量子动力学;本文探究弱连续探测如何影响晶格隧穿粒子的纠缠与对称性质选择。

Method: 对一维晶格中粒子的隧穿运动进行数值模拟，对单个格点的占据数施加弱连续探测（弱测量）。

Result: 弱连续探测可诱导纠缠，并为波函数宇称、置换对称性等系统性质选定确定值。

Conclusion: 对于两个和三个无先验置换对称性的粒子，连续测量可将其引导至稳定的玻色型、费米型与仲统计对称性扇区。

Abstract: Continuous measurements simultaneously reveal and modify the dynamics of a quantum system. In this work we consider the tunneling motion of particles between the sites of a one-dimensional lattice. We investigate how weak continuous probing of the occupation of a single lattice site induces entanglement and selects definite values for system properties such as wave function parity and permutation symmetry. Our simulations show how continuous measurement can steer systems of two and three particles, with no prior permutation symmetry, into stable bosonic, fermionic and parastatistical symmetry sectors.

</details>


### [151] [Pauli instability in arbitrary states: detecting magic in physical correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40032)
*Tanner Jackson,Alexey Milekhin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用非时序关联函数（OTOC）度量量子演化中的非稳定子性（magic），并证明该量对满秩态（如吉布斯态）为Clifford演化当且仅当为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非稳定子性（magic）刻画量子演化在经典计算机上模拟的难度。一个自然的问题是：如何从物理关联函数中（例如有限温度或基态下）提取有关magic的信息。为此需要建立可物理测量的magic度量。

Method: 作者构造了在任意态下求值的非时序关联函数（OTOC）来度量magic。对每个满秩态（特别是每个吉布斯态），该量在演化是Clifford时为零且仅当此时为零。作者还将其与Choi态的稳定子熵（stabilizer entropy）建立联系。

Result: 该量满足基本性质；与Choi态的稳定子熵相关；并且即使仅使用单个固定探针算符，它也能给出在任何线路架构下实现该动力学所需T门数量的下界。

Conclusion: 基于OTOC的magic度量提供了一种可从物理关联函数中提取演化非稳定子性的方法，且能通过单个探针算符给出T门复杂度的下界。

Abstract: Measures of non-stabilizerness (``magic'') of a quantum evolution quantify how hard it is to simulate on a classical computer. A natural question is how to extract information about magic from physical correlators, for example at finite temperature or in the ground state. In this paper we show how to measure magic using out-of-time-order correlators evaluated in an arbitrary state. For every full-rank state, and in particular for every Gibbs state, the resulting quantity vanishes if and only if the evolution is Clifford. We establish its basic properties, relate it to the stabilizer entropy of the Choi state, and prove that, even with a single fixed probe operator, it gives a lower bound on the number of $T$ gates needed to implement the dynamics in any circuit architecture.

</details>


### [152] [Experimentally realized local-global tradeoff in quantum resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40033)
*Yue Dai,Zhiyu Wang,Xinzhi Zhao,Xinglei Yu,Chengjie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了局域量子资源（局域最大相干性）与全局量子资源（量子互信息）之间的权衡不等式，二者之和存在确定上界，揭示了局域与全局量子资源互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同量子资源之间的相互转换（如相干性与纠缠的权衡）已受广泛关注，但局域量子资源与全局量子资源之间的关系仍鲜有探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以局域最大相干性刻画局域量子资源、以量子互信息刻画全局量子资源，提出局域-全局权衡关系，并通过实验进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，局域最大相干性与量子互信息之和受到一个确定上界的约束，为局域与全局量子资源的互补性提供了直接的实验证据。

Conclusion: 研究确立了基本的权衡不等式，并揭示量子网络中的重要约束：各节点可用的局域量子资源与网络的全局量子资源相互排斥，增强其一必然限制其二。

Abstract: The interconversion among different quantum resources has attracted considerable attention, exemplified by the tradeoff between coherence and entanglement. However, the relationship between local and global quantum resources remains largely unexplored. Here, we propose and experimentally demonstrate a local global tradeoff in quantum resources, quantified respectively by the local maximum coherence and the quantum mutual information. Our experiments show that the sum of the local maximum coherence and the quantum mutual information is bounded by a definitive upper limit. These results not only establish a fundamental tradeoff inequality, but also provide direct experimental evidence of the complementarity between local and global quantum resources. Moreover, our findings reveal an important constraint in quantum networks: the local quantum resources available at individual nodes and the global quantum resources of the network are mutually exclusive, implying that enhancing one necessarily limits the other.

</details>


### [153] [Cycle Codes and Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40036)
*Anuj Apte,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Andi Gu,Stephen P. Jordan,Ojas Parekh,Ruslan Shaydulin,Jacob Watkins,Noureldin Yosri,Adam Zalcman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析DQI将双变量约束优化问题归约为解码循环码的性能，通过考虑不完美解码、推广随机实例结果、研究有限域上同调循环码及Linial--Simkin图族，给出DQI在经典解码器限制下可实现近似最优的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明DQI仅在MaxCut经典易解的线性围长图上具有非平凡满足比保证，且其no-go结果依赖最小距离假设，而该假设并不代表常见噪声信道的真实可解码阈值，从而低估了DQI实际性能。因此需要估计DQI的真实性能。

Method: 推导固定实例下存在不完美解码时的DQI满足保证，并推广随机实例的先前结果；研究任意有限域上的同调循环码，证明q>2时最小权解码为NP难；在Linial--Simkin正则图族上证明最大似然恢复界及非均匀加性噪声信道族的强逆定理；利用LP解码器实现多项式时间恢复。

Result: 任意q>2有限域上同调循环码的最小权解码均为NP难；在Linial--Simkin对数围长正则图上获得最大似然恢复界与强逆；LP解码器可多项式时间恢复正比例的随机位置、任意值错误；据此给出DQI限制于经典解码器时可实现近似最优的上下界。

Conclusion: 这些界在所分析区域内排除了量子优势，同时识别出一类正则Max-k-Cut实例，DQI可在其上高效实现非平凡的满足比保证。

Abstract: Decoded Quantum Interferometry (DQI) reduces optimization problems with two-variable constraints to decoding cycle codes. For one such problem, namely MaxCut, prior work showed that DQI achieves a nontrivial satisfaction fraction guarantee only on linear-girth graphs, for which MaxCut is classically easy. However, these no-go results rely on minimum distance assumptions that do not represent true decodability thresholds for common noise channels, thus underestimating actual DQI performance. To estimate the true performance, we derive DQI satisfaction guarantees in the presence of imperfect decoding for fixed instances and generalize prior results for random instances. We then study homological cycle codes over arbitrary finite fields, and show that minimum-weight decoding is NP-hard for every field of size $q>2$, in contrast with known results for binary cycle codes. On the Linial--Simkin ensemble of regular graphs with logarithmic girth, we prove maximum-likelihood recovery bounds and a strong converse for a family of non-uniform additive noise channels. Moreover, LP decoders provide polynomial-time recovery of a positive fraction of randomly located errors with arbitrary values. These results allow us to prove upper and lower bounds on the approximate optima achievable by DQI when restricted to classical decoders. These bounds rule out quantum advantage in the regimes we analyze but also identify a family of regular Max-$k$-Cut instances on which DQI efficiently achieves a nontrivial satisfaction fraction guarantee.

</details>


### [154] [Finite-Bandwidth Protection of a Three-Level Quantum Heat Engine Against Parasitic Heat Leaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40038)
*Gilberto Aparecido Prataviera,Marcos César de Oliveira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究有限带宽环境工程中"抑制寄生跃迁"与"滤波响应时间"之间的竞争，针对连续三能级热机（热环境寄生耦合到冷跃迁）进行精确求解与多种主方程建模，揭示了谱选择性保护热力学循环的同时存在动态通量成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限带宽的"库工程"可抑制量子热机中不需要的跃迁（如热环境寄生耦合到冷跃迁导致的短路），但物理滤波器必然引入有限响应时间，两者存在本质竞争关系，需系统研究其权衡。

Method: 1) 建立连续三能级热机的三态速率网络并精确求解；2) 显式引入阻尼辅助模式作为物理谱滤波器（洛伦兹响应）；3) 采用独立局部GKSL与非久期Bloch–Redfield计算；4) 对比频率分辨的洛伦兹速率约化模型。

Result: 寄生跃迁形成热→冷的热力学短路，得到正功率运行失效的闭合阈值。洛伦兹响应可抑制失谐的寄生跃迁，但过度窄化限制有效能量通量。局部GKSL与非久期Bloch–Redfield在弱耦合极限均回到无泄漏马尔可夫热机，且预测存在有限的最大功率带宽，但具体位置依赖模型；而频率分辨洛伦兹速率约化则无内部最优点。

Conclusion: 谱选择性可保护有用的热力学循环，但真实滤波器无法在不付出动态通量代价的情况下任意窄化；带宽存在与模型相关的有限最优值，设计需权衡滤波选择性与能量通量。

Abstract: Finite-bandwidth reservoir engineering can suppress unwanted transitions in a quantum thermal machine, but a physical filter also introduces a finite response time. We study this competition in a continuous three-level heat engine whose hot environment couples parasitically to the cold transition. The corresponding three-state rate network is solved exactly, showing that the parasitic transition produces a hot-to-cold thermodynamic short circuit and yielding a closed threshold for the loss of positive-power operation. We then retain a damped auxiliary mode explicitly as a physical spectral filter. Its Lorentzian response suppresses the detuned parasitic transition, whereas excessive narrowing limits the useful energy throughput. Independent local-GKSL and nonsecular Bloch--Redfield calculations both recover the no-leak Markovian engine in the weak-coupling limit and predict a finite maximum-power bandwidth, although its precise location is model dependent. A frequency-resolved Lorentzian-rate reduction, by contrast, has no interior optimum. The resulting design principle is that spectral selectivity can protect the useful thermodynamic cycle, but a real filter cannot be narrowed without a dynamical throughput cost.

</details>


### [155] [Unconditional quantum advantage with noisy planar architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40050)
*Libor Caha,Robert Koenig,Louis Paletta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在恒定阈值以下的局部随机噪声下，限于2D局部操作的量子电路在计算能力上强于AC0电路，给出了首个适用于平面量子器件、具备噪声鲁棒性且无条件的复杂度分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势的实验演示需贴近现实条件，即器件受限于2D局部操作并存在噪声。现有复杂度分离结果大多依赖理想无噪声假设或依赖复杂度理论假设，缺乏适用于平面量子器件且无条件、噪声鲁棒的分离。

Method: 构造一个计算问题：一方面，某个几何2D局部量子电路即使实现不完美，也能以高概率正确求解该问题的任意实例；另一方面，任何多项式规模AC0电路都会以常数概率在部分实例上失败。通过这一问题的构造来建立分离。

Result: 证明了受局部随机噪声（低于恒定阈值）影响的2D局部量子电路在计算上强于AC0电路，即不存在多项式规模AC0电路能求解上述问题，而噪声下的2D局部量子电路可以。

Conclusion: 这是首个适用于平面量子器件、融合噪声鲁棒性且无条件的复杂度理论分离，使无条件量子优势的实验演示更接近实验现实。

Abstract: We consider quantum devices restricted to local operations in 2D and subject to local stochastic noise below a constant threshold. We show that such circuits are computationally more powerful than AC0-circuits, i.e., noise-free, geometrically-unconstrained constant-depth classical circuits with unbounded fan-in AND, OR and NOT gates. To this end, we exhibit a computational problem with the following properties: (i) Any instance of the problem is correctly solved with high probability by a certain geometrically 2D-local quantum circuit even if the latter is imperfectly implemented, but (ii) any polynomial-size AC0-circuit fails to solve certain instances of the problem with constant probability. To our knowledge, this isthe first complexity-theoretic separation which applies to planar quantum devices, incorporates noise-resilience and is unconditional, i.e., does not rely on complexity-theoretic assumptions. This brings the experimental demonstration of an unconditional quantum advantage closer to experimental realities.

</details>


### [156] [Transmitting algebras through quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40054)
*Robert Salzmann,Satvik Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立有限维 C*-代数通过量子信道精确传输的理论，引入"支配代数"概念，发现支配可失败并需要混合容量与禁代数刻画，揭示了张量积下的超可加性及代数类型的双指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维 C*-代数可描述经典-量子混合信息，其中量子系统维度可依赖于经典消息。作者希望将零误差信息论与算子代数纠错统一起来，研究所有可传输代数类型（按嵌入排序）是否总存在一个能容纳所有可传输代数的"支配代数"。

Method: 允许任意编码与解码，得到按嵌入排序的代数类型传输集合。提出支配代数存在性问题，引入混合容量重构唯一可能的支配代数类型，并用极小禁代数类型（minimal forbidden algebra types）给出支配性的完整有限刻画。研究图同构于 *-代数的算子系统信道、高可除信道、单次零误差量子容量为零的信道，以及张量积下的联合编码。

Result: 支配可失败，即使在小维度；此时存在若干互不可比的最大代数，对应同一信道的不同最优用途。对某些信道（算子系统图同构于 *-代数、高可除、零单次零误差量子容量）给出支配代数。张量积下联合编码能产生新的代数类型，构成香农理论超可加性的代数类比；两信道各自有支配代数时联合使用仍可能失败。构造出 n 重张量幂具有双指数多个最大代数类型的信道，达到有限维信道的最大可能增长规模。

Conclusion: 精确传输结构不能由任何有限块长编码集合生成；新的可传输代数类型可在任意大块长处出现。用户需根据操作任务与待保护信息类型选择不同最大代数，支配性的缺失是本质的。

Abstract: We develop a theory of exact transmission of finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebras through quantum channels. These algebras describe hybrid classical-quantum information, allowing the dimension of the quantum system to depend on the classical message. Allowing arbitrary encodings and decodings yields a transmission set of algebra types, ordered by embedding, that unifies zero-error information theory with operator-algebraic error correction. We ask if this set admits a dominating algebra into which every transmittable algebra embeds. Our central finding is that domination can fail even in small dimensions. In its absence, several incomparable maximal algebras can describe different optimal uses of the same channel, forcing the user to select one depending on the operational task and the type of information to be preserved. We introduce hybrid capacities that reconstruct the only possible dominating algebra type and present a complete finite characterization of domination using minimal forbidden algebra types. We identify dominating algebras for channels whose operator systems are graph-isomorphic to $*$-algebras, including highly divisible channels, and for channels with zero one-shot zero-error quantum capacity. Under tensor products, we prove that joint coding can produce new algebra types, giving an algebraic analogue of superadditivity from Shannon theory. Domination can fail for the joint use of two channels, even when both channels separately admit dominating algebras. Finally, we construct a channel whose $n$-fold tensor powers have doubly exponentially many maximal algebra types, attaining the largest possible growth scaling for finite-dimensional channels. Consequently, new transmittable algebra types appear at arbitrarily large block-lengths for this channel, so its full transmission structure cannot be generated from any finite collection of bounded-block-length codes.

</details>


### [157] [Equality in the Bosonic Quantum Entropy Power Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40061)
*Yonglong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文完整刻画了玻色量子熵功率不等式（线性与指数形式）在有限平均光子数独立输入下的等号成立条件：当且仅当两输入为具有相同协方差矩阵的高斯态（位移可任意）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色量子熵功率不等式给出了分束器输出熵关于输入熵的界，但其等号成立类（equality class）此前尚不完整，作者希望确定独立输入情形下等号何时成立。

Method: 针对任意模式数与任意透射率（严格介于0与1）：先将等号通过热辅助态转化为固定噪声信道下互信息的保持问题，用两结果测量仪器（two-outcome instrument）证明互信息保持要求输入与参考态为乘积态；再借助量子Darmois–Skitovich定理推出高斯性，并用中心极限论证把指数等号归约为线性等号。

Result: 在有限平均光子数的独立输入下，线性与指数形式的等号成立当且仅当输入为具有相同协方差矩阵的高斯态，且位移可任意。

Conclusion: 论文给出了玻色量子熵功率不等式完整的等号成立类，刻画了达到该信息论极限的唯一态结构，并提供了基于互信息、量子Darmois–Skitovich定理与中心极限论证的证明框架。

Abstract: The bosonic quantum entropy power inequality bounds the entropy of a beam-splitter output in terms of the input entropies. We determine its complete equality class among independent inputs of finite mean photon number: for any number of modes and any transmissivity strictly between zero and one, equality in either the linear or the exponential form holds if and only if the inputs are Gaussian with the same covariance matrix, allowing arbitrary displacements. The main step shows that equality for one output forces the two outputs to be independent: a thermal auxiliary converts equality into preservation of mutual information by a fixed noisy channel, and a two-outcome instrument shows that preservation of mutual information requires a product state with the reference. The quantum Darmois--Skitovich theorem then gives Gaussianity, while a central-limit argument reduces exponential equality to linear equality.

</details>


### [158] [A Fourier-Label Information-Loss Barrier for Dihedral Coset Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40062)
*Aparna Gupte,Seyoon Ragavan,Mark Zhandry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为DCP的一类量子算法建立了no-go定理：在Regev的傅里叶采样与子集和测量模板下，若测得子集和的低n-1位后，算法丢弃每个傅里叶标签中任意ω(log n)位，则算法无法求解DCP。作为主要应用，作者证明Simon（IACR ePrint:2026/1591）的近期算法并不求解DCP，因为它实际上仅依赖傅里叶标签的最高三分之一位，因而落入该no-go定理的适用范围。作者还发布了Lean 4形式化代码以增强可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCP（二面体陪集问题）是量子算法领域的重要问题，Regev提出的傅里叶采样与子集和测量模板是求解DCP的主要途径之一。该模板中，测量后如何处理傅里叶标签（丢弃多少位、保留多少信息）对算法能力的影响此前缺乏严格刻画。本文的动机在于：给出此类模板算法的能力上界，明确"必须大量使用傅里叶标签"这一必要条件，从而为DCP算法设计提供指导；同时据此检验近期声称求解DCP的算法（Simon 2026）是否真正有效。

Method: 作者针对Regev模板（Fourier-sampling与subset-sum-measurement）定义了一个广义的量子算法类：算法测量子集和的低n-1位，随后从每个傅里叶标签中丢弃任意ω(log n)位。在此设定下证明no-go定理，即该类算法均不能成功求解DCP。作为应用，对Simon的算法进行分析：证明在子集和测量之后，该算法（在指数级小误差意义下）仅使用傅里叶标签的最高三分之一位即可实现，因此属于上述no-go定理所覆盖的算法类。为增强可验证性，作者使用Lean 4对相关结果进行了形式化验证。

Result: 建立了DCP的no-go定理：在Regev模板中，若测量子集和低n-1位后丢弃每个傅里叶标签的任意ω(log n)位，则算法无法求解DCP。由此推出，任何遵循该模板的算法都必须大量使用傅里叶标签。作为主要应用，证明Simon（IACR ePrint:2026/1591）的算法因仅需最高三分之一傅里叶标签（指数小误差下）而受该no-go定理约束，故不求解DCP。相关结果附带Lean 4形式化代码。

Conclusion: 本文的no-go定理刻画了Regev型DCP算法的一个根本性限制：丢弃过多傅里叶标签信息（超过ω(log n)位）将导致算法失效，凸显傅里叶标签的充分使用是此类算法的必要条件，为DCP算法设计提供指引。同时该结果否定了Simon近期算法求解DCP的主张，并借助Lean 4形式化提升了结论的可信度与可检验性。

Abstract: We establish a no-go theorem for a broad class of quantum algorithms for the dihedral coset problem (DCP). We consider the Fourier-sampling and subset-sum-measurement template proposed by Regev (SIAM Journal on Computing, 2004), which is one of the main approaches to solving DCP. Suppose that, after measuring the lower $n-1$ bits of the subset sum, the algorithm discards any $ω(\log n)$ bits from each of the Fourier labels. Then we prove that the algorithm cannot succeed in solving DCP. This shows that any algorithm following this template must make extensive use of the Fourier labels, and thus serves as a useful guide for developing algorithms for DCP.
  As a main application, we show that the recent algorithm by Simon (IACR ePrint:2026/1591, August 11 2026) does not solve DCP. We show that after the subset-sum measurement, this algorithm can be implemented (up to exponentially-small error) using only the most-significant third of the Fourier labels, and is therefore subject to our general no-go theorem. To help with verifiability, we release Lean 4 code for our results.

</details>


### [159] [Transducer-based linear combination of unitaries: theory and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40073)
*Dong An,Dekuan Dong,Changpeng Shao,Yuxin Zhang,Chenhao Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于传感器的LCU框架，将量子线性组合（LCU）的代价从最昂贵酉算子的最坏情形依赖降为加权平均查询复杂度，并将传感器作用以对数精度转换为块编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LCU的查询复杂度由组合中最昂贵的酉算子主导，当高代价酉算子权重较小、且缩放因子λ/a较大时这种最坏情形依赖过于保守，存在显著改进空间。

Method: 基于Belovs、Jeffery与Yolcu的transducer框架，发展面向块编码的互补算子级理论；引入resolvent norm K作为除catalyst complexity外的新复杂度度量，并给出将transducer作用转化为ε-近似块编码的构造。

Result: 对A=Σ_j c_j U_j（‖A‖≤a），以α=O(a)的缩放因子实现A/α的块编码，仅需Õ(C_max + C̄·λ/a)次原始查询，优于标准LCU的Õ(C_max·λ/a)；同时将transducer到块编码的精度依赖由多项式改进为对数，仅需O(K·log(1/ε))次查询。

Conclusion: 该框架在昂贵酉算子权重小、λ/a较大时可显著降低查询复杂度，并应用于稀疏矩阵块编码，得到稀疏哈密顿量模拟与量子线性系统求解的近最优（至polylog因子）查询复杂度算法。

Abstract: Linear combination of unitaries (LCU) is a fundamental primitive in quantum algorithms, whose cost is typically governed by the most expensive unitary appearing in the combination. We develop a transducer-based LCU framework that reduces this worst-case dependence to a weighted average query complexity, when the constituent unitaries share access to a common set of primitive oracles.
  Consider $A=\sum_j c_j U_j$, where $c_j>0$ and each unitary $U_j$ can be implemented using $C_j$ primitive queries. Given an upper bound $a\geq \|A\|$, our algorithm implements a block-encoding of $A/α$ with rescaling factor $α=\mathcal{O}(a)$, using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}} (C_{\max}+\overline{C} λ/{a} )$ primitive queries, where $λ=\sum_j c_j$, $C_{\max}=\max_j C_j$, and $\overline{C}= {\sum_j c_j C_j}/λ$. By comparison, the standard LCU construction requires $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}} (C_{\max} λ/{a} )$ primitive queries. The improvement can therefore be substantial when costly unitaries have small weights and $λ/a$ is large. As applications, we obtain improved block-encodings of sparse matrices, leading to quantum algorithms for sparse Hamiltonian simulation and quantum linear systems with near-optimal query complexity up to polylogarithmic factors in all relevant parameters.
  Our main technique is the transducer framework developed by Belovs, Jeffery, and Yolcu [Quantum, 8:1444 (2024)]. Here we develop a complementary operator-level theory tailored to block-encodings. We identify the resolvent norm $K$ as a key complexity measure for transducer implementation besides the existing catalyst complexity. We show that the transducer action can be converted into an $ε$-approximate block-encoding using only $\mathcal{O}\left(K \log(1/ε)\right)$ queries to the transducer, improving the precision dependence from polynomial to logarithmic.

</details>


### [160] [Oscillator-Qubit Primitives for a Molecular Quantum Dynamics Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40078)
*Jungsoo Hong,Joonsuk Huh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出近最优量子原语，用广义Jacobi-Anger展开合成非线性及多模玻色相位门，实现分子量子动力学模拟，电路长度较直接QSP缩短约三分之一，并在简化CO2模型上完成振动能量交换模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子与核同时量子力学处理的分子动力学模拟对化学反应预测至关重要，但经典计算所需波函数资源随粒子数指数增长；振荡器-量子比特处理器（囚禁离子、电路QED）天然提供线性振动相互作用，可构建可编程非谐相互作用，然而乘积公式法需重复非对易相位步、累加成本与误差限制了动力学可模拟的时长，因此需要更低成本、近最优的量子原语。

Method: 在量子信号处理（QSP）框架内，利用广义Jacobi-Anger（GJA）展开合成非线性及多模玻色相位门，分别对电子（量子比特）与核振动（振荡器）两类粒子及其相互作用进行表示；相互作用模型与门合成各自设置独立精度参数，二者联合调优以降低整体成本。

Result: 门合成成本在相互作用强度与精度两方面均近最优增长；多模基准电路比此前直接QSP方法缩短约三分之一。对简化二氧化碳模型模拟伸缩与弯曲振动间的能量交换，在电路QED噪声下，当量子比特与振荡器寿命接近2 ms与5 ms时，短时模拟勉强未达精度标准。

Conclusion: 所提近最优原语与联合精度设置显著压缩电路规模，2 ms（量子比特）与5 ms（振荡器）寿命构成迈向近期振荡器-量子比特处理器上非谐多模分子量子动力学模拟的具体里程碑。

Abstract: Molecular quantum dynamics simulations that treat both electrons and nuclei quantum mechanically are crucial for predicting chemical reactions. With classical computation, a full wave-function representation of both types of particles requires resources that grow exponentially with their number. Oscillator-qubit processors, including trapped-ion and circuit QED devices, represent electronic states with qubits and nuclear vibrations with oscillators. Their native operations produce linear vibronic interactions, from which programmable anharmonic interactions can be built. Product-formula approaches build the corresponding phase gates by repeating small noncommuting phase steps, and quantum signal processing (QSP) approaches approximate each gate directly with a Fourier series. Simulations apply many such gates in sequence, so accumulated cost and error limit how long the dynamics can be followed. We propose near-optimal quantum primitives for molecular quantum dynamics simulation, covering the representations of both types of particles and their interactions. We synthesize nonlinear and multimode bosonic phase gates using generalized Jacobi-Anger (GJA) expansions within QSP, with a cost that grows near-optimally in both interaction strength and precision. Our interaction model and gate synthesis each have their own accuracy setting. Chosen together, these settings make our multimode benchmark circuits about one-third shorter than those of the previous direct QSP approach. We then simulate energy exchange between the stretching and bending vibrations of a simplified carbon dioxide model. With circuit-QED noise, a short simulation narrowly misses our accuracy criteria at qubit and oscillator lifetimes near 2 ms and 5 ms. These lifetimes mark a concrete checkpoint on the way to simulating anharmonic multimode molecular quantum dynamics on near-term oscillator-qubit processors.

</details>


### [161] [Universal Entanglement Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40080)
*Salvatore Tirone,Francesco Anna Mele,Vittorio Giovannetti,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用纠缠蒸馏协议，可在未知有限维两体态上渐近达到其可蒸馏纠缠，且无速率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠蒸馏通常假设量子态已知，而实际中态往往未知。本文旨在探究：在态未知时，是否会造成渐近可蒸馏速率损失。

Method: 结合基于LOCC的层析（tomography）与自适应蒸馏流程，并利用局部鲁棒性引理控制有限样本估计误差，构造通用协议；同时分析一族态的最坏情形蒸馏。

Result: 该协议的ebit产率可达到任意低于已知态最优值的速率，且迹距离误差趋于零；族态的最优统一速率等于其闭包上可蒸馏纠缠的最小值。

Conclusion: 对量子态的未知性不会带来渐近速率惩罚，通用协议在渐近意义下可达最优。

Abstract: We present a universal entanglement distillation protocol that asymptotically achieves the distillable entanglement of any unknown bipartite state in a fixed finite dimension. That is, its ebit yield attains every rate below the optimum that would be available if the state were known, with vanishing trace-distance error. Our approach combines tomography by local operations and classical communication with an adaptive distillation routine, using a local robustness lemma to control finite-sample estimation errors. We also characterize worst-case distillation from a family of states: the optimal uniform rate is the minimum distillable entanglement on the family's closure. These results show that ignorance of the state need not incur an asymptotic rate penalty.

</details>


### [162] [Optimal Resource Scaling for Early Fault Tolerant Iterative Quantum Phase Estimation under Cost Error Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40128)
*Mrudula A Mahindrakar,Avhishek Chatterjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究迭代量子相位估计算法(IPEA)在资源受限下各迭代位最优重复次数分配问题,通过构造上、下代理问题并利用集中/反集中不等式得到闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPEA更适合NISQ及早期容错硬件,但误差缓解与校正导致酉算符不完美,引发相位反馈错误。通过重复第k次迭代并多数表决可提高相位比特可靠性,但不同迭代线路复杂度不同,资源代价w_k与单次错误概率q_k各异,在紧资源约束下需确定每位最优测量次数。

Method: 将问题建模为在总资源约束∑w_k N_k ≤ W下最大化全部L位正确的概率。通过紧集中与反集中界构造可处理的上、下代理问题,并证明当q_k≪1时二者解收敛,从而得到N_k*的闭式表达式。

Result: 在W≫∑w_k、w_k随k增大、q_k充分小且L较大的条件下得到闭式解:当W≫∑w_k ln(∑w_k)时,N_k*∝ln(∑w_k)/c_k,与单个w_k无关,其中c_k=-ln(2√((1-q_k/2)q_k/2))∝ln(1/q_k);当∑w_k≪W≪∑w_k ln(∑w_k)时,分配还受(1/c_k)ln(1/w_k)的加性影响,且对较大k更显著。

Conclusion: 研究给出了IPEA资源分配的简单经验法则,可为实际系统设计提供指导。

Abstract: The iterative quantum phase estimation algorithm (IPEA) is better suited for NISQ and early fault-tolerant hardware compared to the original algorithm, which requires the inverse quantum Fourier transform. However, current error mitigation and correction implementations lead to imperfect unitaries, causing erroneous phase feedback in IPEA. To improve the reliability of phase bits is to repeat the k^th iteration for the 2^k-th power of the unitary $N_k^*$ times, followed by a majority decision. However, due to variations in their circuit complexities, different iterations incur varying resource costs ($w_k$) and different per-shot error probabilities ($q_k$). Given tight resource constraints, we ask what the optimal number $\{N_k^*: 1\le k \le L\}$ of shots per bit is: $$\arg \max_{\{N_k\}} \mathbb{P}(\text{all } L \text{ bits correct}) \text{ s.t.} \sum_{k=1}^L w_k N_k \le W$$ We obtain closed-form expressions for $N^*_k$ under the conditions: $W \gg \sum_k w_k $(enough for at least one shot per iteration), $w_k$ increases with $k$, $q_k$ are small enough and $L$ is large. We do so by analyzing tractable upper and lower surrogate problems derived via tight concentration and anti-concentration bounds and showing that their solutions converge when $q_k\ll 1$.
  We observe that when $W\gg \sum_k w_k \ln\! \left(\sum_k w_k\right)$, the optimal $N_k^*$ is proportional to $\frac{\ln\! \left(\sum_k w_k\right)}{c_k}$ and does not depend on individual $w_k$, where $c_k=-\ln\left(2\sqrt{\left(1-\frac{q_k}{2} \right) \frac{q_k}{2}}\right) \propto \ln\frac{1}{q_k}$. However, when $\sum_k w_k \ll W\ll\sum_k w_k \ln\! \left(\sum_k w_k\right)$, the allocation is also (additively) influenced by $\frac{1}{c_k}\ln\frac{1}{w_k}$ for larger values of $k$, i.e., higher powers of unitary. These results lead to simple rules of thumb for resource allocation that may benefit the design of practical systems.

</details>


### [163] [Fast Cliffords When Your Quantum Memory Is Full](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40144)
*Marten Folkertsma,Ian Mertz,Sergii Strelchuk,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究量子电路的催化实现：在保证脏量子比特（含纠缠）在计算结束时精确还原的前提下，证明任意n量子比特Clifford电路可用O(n²/log²n)个催化量子比特、无需干净量子比特实现深度O(log n)的电路，并将方法推广至Clifford层级固定层级C_k的对角元素及半Clifford门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增加量子比特可为并行计算提供工作空间从而降低量子电路深度，但标准构造假设工作空间被初始化在已知状态。论文旨在探讨能否改用脏量子比特，只要在计算结束时将其联合状态（含与其他寄存器的纠缠）精确还原，即催化实现。

Method: 采用催化实现（catalytic implementation）框架，允许使用脏量子比特作为工作空间，但要求计算结束时其联合状态（包括与其他寄存器的纠缠）被精确恢复。针对n量子比特Clifford电路构造深度O(log n)、使用O(n²/log²n)个催化量子比特且无需干净量子比特的方案；并将该构造推广到Clifford层级任意固定层级C_k的对角元素以及半Clifford门。

Result: 每个n量子比特Clifford电路都存在使用O(n²/log²n)个催化量子比特、无干净量子比特、深度为O(log n)的催化实现，与有干净工作空间时可达到的渐近深度相当。对Clifford层级固定层级C_k的对角元素，可实现深度O(log(n+1))、O(n^k/log(n))个门、O(n^k/log²(n))个催化量子比特的催化实现；该结果也适用于半Clifford门。

Conclusion: 脏量子比特可被有效用作催化工作空间：在不消耗或改变其状态的前提下，Clifford电路及Clifford层级特定门类可获得与使用干净工作空间相当的渐近深度，从而扩展了低深度量子电路构造的适用范围。

Abstract: Additional qubits can reduce the depth of a quantum circuit by providing workspace for parallel computation, but standard constructions assume that this workspace is initialized in a known state. In this work we study catalytic implementations, i.e. asking whether dirty qubits can instead be used provided that their joint state including any entanglement with other registers is restored exactly at the end of the computation. We show that every $n$-qubit Clifford circuit has a catalytic implementation of depth $O(\log n)$ using $O(n^2/\log^2 n)$ catalytic qubits and no clean qubits, matching the asymptotic depth achievable when clean workspace is available. We extend this approach to diagonal elements of any fixed level $C_k$ of the Clifford hierarchy, which admit catalytic implementations of depth $O(\log(n+1))$ with $O(n^k/\log(n))$ gates and $O(n^k/\log^2(n))$ catalytic qubits, as well as to semi-Clifford Gates.

</details>


### [164] [QuLoC: Photonic Quantum-Assisted Low-Rank LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40146)
*Xiao-Hui Ni,Yu-Han Yao,Yu-Ze Zhu,Hang Song,Xiang Zhao,Lin Yang,Xian-Min Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 QuLoC，一种光子量子辅助的 LLM 压缩算法，利用量子电路输出对保留的低秩分量进行门控，在训练后可将门控系数吸收进低秩因子，从而在经典硬件上推理。在 Qwen3.5-4B 上平均准确率相对 SOTA 提升 9.73%，并在 LLaMA-7B 多压缩比及真实光子量子硬件上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 规模增长，高效部署需要压缩。基于 SVD 的低秩压缩虽能减少参数量，但会导致下游性能下降。因此需要一种在压缩后仍能恢复性能的方法，并探索量子计算在其中的辅助作用。

Method: 提出 QuLoC：使用量子电路输出对训练中保留的低秩分量进行门控（gating）；通过局部函数重构与端到端知识蒸馏恢复模型性能；训练后将门控系数吸收进低秩因子，使压缩模型可在经典硬件上推理，无需在推理时执行量子电路。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-4B 上，多个下游任务的平均准确率相对 SOTA 基线提升 9.73%；在 LLaMA-7B 的不同参数压缩比下，平均下游准确率持续优于 SOTA 基线；使用光子量子硬件的实验在相对仿真仅有小幅精度损失的情况下保持收益，表明对硬件噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: QuLoC 有效提升压缩后 LLM 的下游性能，并具备跨模型、跨压缩比的适用性及对量子硬件噪声的鲁棒性，为光子量子辅助压缩在更大模型和更复杂智能体任务上的进一步探索提供依据。

Abstract: As LLMs grow in size, compression becomes increasingly important for efficient deployment. SVD-based low-rank compression reduces parameter counts but can degrade downstream performance. To improve performance after compression, we introduce QuLoC, a photonic quantum-assisted LLM compression algorithm that uses quantum circuit outputs to gate the retained low-rank components during training. Model performance is recovered through local functional reconstruction followed by end-to-end knowledge distillation. After training, the gating coefficients are absorbed into the low-rank factors, allowing the compressed model to run on classical hardware without executing quantum circuits during inference. Experiments on Qwen3.5-4B show that QuLoC achieves a 9.73\% relative improvement in average accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines across multiple downstream tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness. We further evaluate QuLoC on LLaMA-7B at different parameter compression ratios. It consistently achieves higher average downstream accuracy than state-of-the-art baselines, supporting its applicability to a larger model across different compression settings. Notably, experiments using the photonic quantum hardware retain these benefits with a small accuracy loss relative to simulation, suggesting robustness to hardware noise. These results motivate further exploration of photonic quantum-assisted compression for larger models and more complex agentic tasks.

</details>


### [165] [Quantum Černý complexity of binary words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40154)
*Pui Hang Lee,Pui-Yee Lee,Bjørn Kjos-Hanssen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入二元词w的量子Černý复杂度qc(w)，定义为使w成为唯一最短同步词所需的最小量子信道维数，证明其上下界、常值词极值性质，并研究纯态目标变体qcp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Černý猜想涉及同步自动机最短同步词长度，本文旨在研究量子类比对这一复杂度的影响，探究量子信道能否带来二次节省，以及是否存在经典Černý函数的量子对应。

Method: 定义量子Černý复杂度qc(w)：存在d×d密度矩阵上的量子信道A_0,A_1与初始态ρ_0，使w为在可达集上信道唯一的常值最短词的最小维数d。进一步定义纯态目标变体qcp。通过构造显式信道实例（如结合相干旋转与测量漏斗信道）进行分析，并给出qc可计算性的归约。

Result: 证明对任意非空w有2≤qc(w)≤⌈√(|w|+1)⌉，相比经典情形获得二次节省；常值词为极值qc(0^m)=⌈√(m+1)⌉；而qc(01^n0)=2对所有n≥1成立，由单量子比特实现。证明维数2下长度≥2的词不能成为纯态目标的唯一最短同步词；给出qutrit实例达到qcp(01^n0)=3且同步对所有输入态普遍成立，纯态目标恰好多耗一维。并观察到qc可通过归约到实数一阶理论计算。

Conclusion: 量子情形不存在经典Černý函数的类比，qc与直观的描述复杂度强烈反相关；纯态目标在特定族上恰好多耗一维；qc是可计算的。

Abstract: We introduce the quantum Černý complexity $\mathrm{qc}(w)$ of a binary word $w$: the least dimension $d$ for which there exist quantum channels $A_0,A_1$ on $d\times d$ density matrices and a start state $ρ_0$ such that $w$ is the unique shortest word whose associated channel is constant on the reachable set. We show that $2\le\mathrm{qc}(w)\le\lceil\sqrt{|w|+1}\,\rceil$ for every nonempty $w$, a quadratic saving over the classical analogue, and that constant words are extremal: $\mathrm{qc}(0^m)=\lceil\sqrt{m+1}\,\rceil$. In contrast, $\mathrm{qc}(01^n0)=2$ for every $n\ge 1$, realized by a single qubit whose rotation angle acts as a counter; consequently there is no quantum analogue of the Černý function, and $\mathrm{qc}$ is strongly anti-correlated with intuitive notions of descriptive complexity. We further study the variant $\mathrm{qcp}$ in which the synchronization target is required to be a pure state. We prove that in dimension $2$ no word of length at least $2$ can be a unique shortest synchronizing word with pure target, and we exhibit an explicit qutrit instance, combining a coherent rotation with a measure-and-funnel channel, achieving $\mathrm{qcp}(01^n0)=3$ with target a computational basis state and with synchronization holding universally over all input states. Thus purity of the reset state costs exactly one dimension on this family. We also observe that $\mathrm{qc}$ is computable, by reduction to the first-order theory of the reals.

</details>


### [166] [Classical and Quantum Simulation of All-to-All Quantum Dynamics in Logarithmic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40162)
*Maximilian Lutz,Rahul Trivedi,J. Ignacio Cirac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究全连接自旋哈密顿量（量子Hopfield、SK、Dicke型模型）无序平均淬火动力学的经典与量子复杂度，提出一种在多项式时间和O(log N)量子/经典空间下模拟置换不变k-局域动力学的量子算法，并给出短时经典算法，指出Hopfield模型长时动力学可能成为同时时间-空间量子优势的候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序平均恢复置换不变性，使N个自旋态可存于O(log N)量子比特。已知对数空间量子计算可被经典多项式时间或polylog空间模拟，但是否存在单一算法同时实现两者尚属开放问题，这暗示了同时量子优势的可能性。作者因此希望在时间与空间两维度同时探索量子优势。

Method: 针对无无序的置换不变k-局域动力学，设计多项式时间且仅需O(log N)量子与经典空间的量子算法；证明若经典算法达到同等资源则蕴含BQL⊆SC；对短时情形给出满足该资源的经典算法（含SK模型）；对Hopfield模型，通过将无序扩张至辅助量子比特，把问题归约到扩大系统上的无无序情形，从而推广经典与量子结果。

Result: 给出了置换不变k-局域动力学的多项式时间、O(log N)量子与经典空间量子算法；证明匹配该资源的经典算法将蕴含BQL⊆SC；提供了短时经典算法，覆盖SK模型；通过辅助比特扩张将结果推广至Hopfield模型。

Conclusion: Hopfield模型的长时动力学呈现非平凡性，因而成为量子模拟中同时实现时间-空间量子优势的候选问题。

Abstract: We study both the classical and the quantum complexity of the problem of estimating disorder-averaged quench dynamics for all-to-all spin Hamiltonians such as quantum Hopfield, Sherrington--Kirkpatrick (SK) or Dicke-type models. Disorder averaging restores permutation invariance in this setting, so states of $N$ spins can be stored in $\mathcal O(\log N)$ qubits. While logarithmic-space quantum computations are known to be simulatable classically in polynomial time or in polylogarithmic space, whether a single algorithm can achieve both is open, raising the possibility of simultaneous quantum advantage. To this end, we give a quantum algorithm for permutation-invariant $k$-local dynamics without disorder using polynomial time and $\mathcal O(\log N)$ quantum and classical space. We show that any classical algorithm matching these resources would imply $\mathsf{BQL}\subseteq \mathsf{SC}$. For short times, however, we provide such algorithms, including for SK models. For Hopfield models, we discuss how dilating the disorder to ancilla qubits reduces the problem to the disorder-free case on an enlarged system, thus extending both classical and quantum results to them. We give some evidence that their long-time dynamics are non-trivial. They thus emerge as a candidate for a simultaneous time--space advantage in quantum simulation.

</details>


### [167] [Exact spectral gaps for random Pauli rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40164)
*Ziyuan Dong,Xiang Fan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了Baer和Haah关于随机Pauli旋转量子游走谱隙的猜想：在SU(d)上谱隙为(d-8)/(8(d-1))（n≥4），推翻了原猜想公式；在PU(d)上谱隙恰为猜想的d(d-3)/(8(d^2-1))（n≥3），并给出解析证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Baer和Haah提出了随机Pauli旋转量子游走的谱隙问题并给出猜想公式。该问题关乎随机量子游走的混合性质与表示论检测能力，但原猜想在完整特殊酉群上的正确性未被验证，且平衡张量表示无法检测更小的谱隙，需要精确刻画不同群上的谱隙值。

Method: 考虑由独立均匀选取的非恒等Pauli算子P与角度θ构成的随机游走，步骤为e^{iθP}。在SU(d)（d=2^n）上证明谱隙为(d-8)/(8(d-1))；在PU(d)上证明谱隙为d(d-3)/(8(d^2-1))。上界由显式向量给出：SU(d)情形构造于∧^8 C^d中，源自F_2^n的仿射三维子空间；PU(d)情形在U↦U^{⊗4}⊗\bar U^{⊗4}中达到。下界对所有有限维酉表示一致成立，综合运用反交换Pauli算子图的谱、二元多项式的最小权重界以及单量子比特Clifford固定子空间上的局部不等式，证明为解析的，无需计算验证。

Result: SU(d)上谱隙为(d-8)/(8(d-1))（n≥4），推翻了Baer-Haah猜想的完整特殊酉群公式；PU(d)上谱隙恰为猜想值d(d-3)/(8(d^2-1))（n≥3）。SU情形的谱隙由∧^8 C^d中的显式向量达到，其非平凡中心作用解释了平衡张量表示为何无法检测该更小的谱隙。

Conclusion: 随机Pauli旋转游走在SU(d)与PU(d)上的谱隙被精确确定，纠正了原猜想在SU(d)上的错误，同时验证了其在PU(d)上的正确性；下界对所有有限维酉表示一致成立，且证明完全解析、无需计算辅助。

Abstract: We resolve the spectral-gap problem posed by Baer and Haah for random Pauli rotations. In this walk, a nonidentity $n$-qubit Pauli operator $P$ and an angle $θ$ modulo $2π$ are chosen independently and uniformly, and the step is $e^{\mathrm{i}θP}$. Writing $d=2^n$, we prove that the gap on $\mathsf{SU}(d)$ is $(d-8)/(8(d-1))$ for $n\ge4$. This disproves their conjectured formula on the full special unitary group. We also prove that the conjectured value, $d(d-3)/(8(d^2-1))$, is exactly the gap on $\mathsf{PU}(d)$ for $n\ge3$. The special-unitary gap is attained by an explicit vector in $\bigwedge^8\mathbb C^d$, constructed from affine three-dimensional subspaces of $\mathbb F_2^n$. Its nontrivial central action explains why balanced tensor representations do not detect this smaller gap. On the projective group, the gap is attained in $U\mapsto U^{\otimes4}\otimes\bar U^{\otimes4}$. The matching lower bounds hold uniformly over all finite-dimensional unitary representations. They combine the spectrum of the graph of anticommuting Pauli operators, a minimum-weight bound for binary polynomials, and a local inequality on a single-qubit Clifford-fixed subspace. The proof is analytic and requires no computational verification.

</details>


### [168] [Shadow Quantum Singular Value Transformation with Shallow Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40167)
*Nai-Hui Chia,Hyunseong Kim,Chia-Ying Lin*
