Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Shadow QSVT框架：给定初态|ψ⟩、厄米矩阵H、多项式f与一组观测量{O_j}，目标是一次性估计⟨ψ|f(H)†O_j f(H)|ψ⟩，通过利用初态与观测量中的结构及"仅需观测量估计而非合成完整酉算子"的事实，在三种算法下降低标准QSVT的电路深度与量子资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准QSVT需要构造f(H)的酉块编码（block-encoding），电路深度与量子资源开销大；而许多应用实际只需观测量期望估计，并不需要显式合成完整酉算子。因此希望借助初态与观测量中的结构来系统性削减所需量子资源。

Method: 提出三种算法：(1) 状态感知QSVT（state-aware QSVT），当H与|ψ⟩对应的Krylov子空间维数低或存在精确低维近似时，以低电路深度制备目标态；(2) 观测量感知Shadow QSVT（observable-aware），引入新的观测量感知Krylov子空间并结合history states，进一步降低电路深度与门复杂度；(3) Classical Shadow QSVT，从H、f、|ψ⟩构造经典表示，无需事先知道观测量、也无需显式制备与f(H)|ψ⟩成比例的目标态。

Result: 状态感知算法借助低维（或可低维近似）Krylov子空间实现低深度目标态制备；观测量感知算法通过观测量感知Krylov子空间与history states联合降低电路深度与门复杂度；Classical Shadow QSVT构建的经典表示支持在量子计算之后指定的观测量，仍可估计目标量。

Conclusion: 三种算法共同构成在多种设置下降低QSVT类计算电路深度的工具集，把QSVT从"合成酉算子"范式拓展到"直接估计观测量"范式，并支持观测量后置（late-binding）的经典表示。

Abstract: We introduce shadow quantum singular value transformation (Shadow QSVT): given an initial state $|ψ\rangle$, a Hermitian matrix $H$, a polynomial $f$, and a set of observables $\{O_1,\dots,O_m\}$, the goal is to estimate $\langleψ|f(H)^{\dagger}O_j f(H)|ψ\rangle$ for all $j\in\{1,\dots,m\}$. Shadow QSVT provides a systematic route to reduce the quantum resources required by standard QSVT, which constructs a unitary block-encoding of $f(H)$. It uses structure in the input state and observables, together with the fact that many applications require only observable estimates rather than synthesizing the full unitary.
  We present three algorithms that exploit structure in the initial state and observables to reduce quantum circuit depth. First, we develop a state-aware QSVT algorithm that prepares the target state with low circuit depth when the Krylov subspace associated with $H$ and $|ψ\rangle$ is low-dimensional or admits an accurate low-dimensional approximation. Second, we introduce an observable-aware Shadow QSVT algorithm that combines a new observable-aware Krylov subspace with history states to further reduce circuit depth and gate complexity. Finally, we develop Classical Shadow QSVT, which constructs a classical representation from $H$, $f$, and $|ψ\rangle$ without prior knowledge of the observables or explicit preparation of the target state proportional to $f(H)|ψ\rangle$. This representation enables estimation of the target quantities for observables specified after the quantum computation. Together, these three algorithms provide tools for reducing the circuit depth of QSVT-based computations across a range of settings.

</details>


### [169] [Non-Markovian dissipation as a resource for quantum reservoir computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40172)
*Luca Nigro,Francesco Monzani,Enrico Prati*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出以分数阶导数显式建模与调控非马尔可夫性的量子储备池计算框架，利用分数阶时间从属生成可调重尾弛豫动力学，从而通过非马尔可夫信息回流优化储备池的短期记忆与非线性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储备池计算通常依赖马尔可夫耗散处理序列数据，而非马尔可夫记忆效应在其中的计算作用尚不明确，因此需要建立一个能够显式刻画并调控非马尔可夫性的理论框架。

Method: 采用分数阶导数显式建模非马尔可夫性，并通过分数阶时间从属（fractional time subordination）生成可调的重尾弛豫动力学，用以调控系统与环境之间的信息回流；随后分析该信息回流对系统时间保持能力及储备池记忆分布的影响。

Result: 非马尔可夫信息回流调节系统的整体时间保持，使短期线性记忆容量最大化；在最优工作区间内，分数阶非马尔可夫性将储备池记忆重新分配至近期输入，在短延迟下同时提升线性记忆容量与非线性预测精度，但以牺牲长程时间保持为代价。

Conclusion: 系统—环境相互作用不仅是量子储备池计算的必要条件，更是一种主动资源，可将记忆保持直接嵌入量子演化过程之中。

Abstract: The control of open-system dynamics provides a powerful mechanism for using quantum information to process sequential tasks. While quantum reservoir computing typically relies on Markovian dissipation to process sequential data, the computational role of non-Markovian memory effects remains largely unexplored. We introduce a framework for quantum reservoir computing where non-Markovianity is explicitly modeled and regulated using fractional derivatives. By employing fractional time subordination, we generate tunable, heavy-tailed relaxation dynamics that govern the information backflow between the system and its environment. Non-Markovian information backflow regulates the overall temporal retention of the system, maximizing short-term linear memory capacity. Within an optimal operating regime, fractional non-Markovianity redistributes the reservoir memory towards recent inputs, improving both linear memory capacity and nonlinear prediction accuracy at short delays, at the expense of long-range temporal retention. System-environment interaction proves being not merely a requirement for quantum reservoir computing, but an active resource that embeds memory retention directly into the quantum evolution.

</details>


### [170] [Practical fermionic shadows enabled by improved sample-complexity bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40173)
*Maxwell West,Su Yeon Chang,Luke Coffman,Martin Larocca,M. Cerezo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对费米子（matchgate）阴影层析，将任意Majorana度为2k的观测量的样本复杂度从O(n^{2k}||O||_∞^2)改进为渐近紧的O(n^k||O||_∞^2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典阴影层析虽具有多项式级样本复杂度，但在仅数百量子比特的现阶段，多项式缩放仍可能过于昂贵，因此迫切需要尽可能紧的样本复杂度上界。作者针对费米子（matchgate）阴影的样本复杂度展开研究。

Method: 作者分析费米子（matchgate）阴影，针对Majorana度为2k的任意可观测量O，推导并证明其样本复杂度上界，得到O(n^k||O||_∞^2)的渐近紧界。

Result: 样本复杂度由O(n^{2k}||O||_∞^2)改进为O(n^k||O||_∞^2)，达到渐近紧。以开放费米子50格点Hubbard链（近邻跃迁t=1、在位相互作用V=4）每模能量的估计、目标加性精度0.1为例，所需测量次数从约10^9降至约10^5，减少约99.98%。

Conclusion: 更紧的样本复杂度界显著降低了费米子阴影层析的测量开销，在含噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）时代具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: Classical shadow tomography is widely touted as supplying a family of methods for extracting information from quantum systems with polynomially scaling sample-complexities. In the current era of quantum computers possessing on the order of hundreds of qubits, however, polynomial scaling can nonetheless be prohibitive. Thus, there is a strong practical need for obtaining sample-complexity bounds which are as tight as possible. Here we address this in the case of fermionic (matchgate) shadows. For an arbitrary observable $O$ of Majorana degree $2k$, we improve the previously known sample-complexity bound of $\mathcal{O}(n^{2k}\|O\|_\infty^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(n^{k}\|O\|_\infty^2)$, which is asymptotically tight. For example, for the estimation of the energy per mode of an open fermionic 50-site Hubbard chain with hopping and on site strenghts respectively given by $t=1$, $V=4$, and for a target additive precision of 0.1, this reduces the number of required shots from $\sim 10^9$ to $\sim 10^5$. That is, the new bound reduces the required number of shots by approximately $99.98\%$.

</details>


### [171] [Dissipation accelerates quantum and classical simulation of open-system dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40174)
*Armando Angrisani,Ricard Puig,Yanting Teng,Zoë Holmes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明，一类广泛的Pauli噪声（含去极化）能指数压低Heisenberg演化可观测量中的高权重分量，从而使含噪开放量子系统的量子与经典模拟都变容易：估计局部可观测量的期望值可用与系统规模n无关的电路深度，且稀疏Pauli传播具有多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统中环境耦合塑造了弛豫、激发输运及量子关联动力学。在量子硬件上，耗散通道通常被视为额外操作、会增加模拟成本；作者希望反驳这一直觉，说明适当噪声反而能降低模拟复杂度，并厘清量子与经典模拟在此情形下的成本边界。

Method: 针对有界度Lindblad动力学，利用Pauli噪声（如去极化）对Heisenberg演化可观测量高权重Pauli分量的指数压制（压缩）。将该压缩与Richardson外推结合，给出相对于传播可观测量2-范数的经典与量子误差界，并据此分析随机输入态或随机Hamiltonian下的期望值保证及OTOC（乱序关联函数）。同时分析稀疏Pauli传播算法的经典复杂度。

Result: 估计时刻t局部可观测量的期望值达到精度ε，电路最大深度为O([1+(t/γ)^{3/2}]log²(1/ε))，与系统规模n无关。经典方面，稀疏Pauli传播在任意固定γ>0时运行时间为n、t、1/ε的多项式，在弱耗散极限下多项式次数为O(1/γ)。量子与经典上界的差距表明，随耗散减弱可能存在实质性的多项式量子优势，数值结果进一步支持这一观察。

Conclusion: Pauli型噪声非但不是模拟的负担，反而通过指数压缩高权重分量显著降低开销，使含噪有界度Lindblad动力学的局部期望值估计可在系统规模无关的深度内完成；稀疏Pauli传播的经典多项式时间与量子界的差距在弱耗散下暗示潜在的多项式量子优势。

Abstract: Simulating open quantum systems reveals how environmental coupling shapes relaxation, excitation transport, and the dynamics of quantum correlations. On quantum hardware, dissipative channels add operations and might seem to increase cost. However, we show that a broad class of Pauli noise, including depolarization, can ease quantum and classical simulation by exponentially suppressing high-weight components of Heisenberg-evolved observables. For bounded-degree Lindblad dynamics, this permits system-size-independent Trotter steps when estimating local observables. Combining this compression with Richardson extrapolation, we provide classical and quantum error bounds with respect to the 2-norm of the propagated observable. These give high-probability expectation-value guarantees for random input states or random Hamiltonians acting on a fixed state and control (out-of-time-order) correlators. Concretely, we show that expectation values of local observables at time $t$ can be estimated to accuracy $\varepsilon$ using circuits of maximum depth $O\!\left(\left[1+\left(t/γ\right)^{3/2}\right]\log^2(1/\varepsilon)\right)$, independent of the system size $n$. Classically, we show that sparse Pauli propagation runs in time polynomial in $n$, $t$ and $1/\varepsilon$ for every fixed $γ>0$, with polynomial degree $O(1/γ)$ in the weak dissipation limit. The gap between our quantum and classical upper bounds leaves room for a substantial polynomial quantum advantage as dissipation weakens, an observation further supported by our numerical results.

</details>


### [172] [Breaking the Multiplicative Overhead in Quantum Entropy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40179)
*Junxiang Huang,Chenyang Li,Lu-Fan Zhang,Yusen Wu,Yukun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出多级量子算法（局部归一化与精度分配、可变时间估计），将Tsallis、von Neumann与Rényi熵估计的查询复杂度降至近最优：Tsallis熵在1<α<2达Õ(ε^{-1/(α-1)})、α≥2达Õ(1/ε)，von Neumann熵由Õ(R/ε²)改进至Õ(R/ε)，非整数Rényi熵达Õ(R/ε)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统熵估计策略将谱变换与统计读取的最坏代价相乘，导致查询复杂度高于下界，存在优化空间。作者希望缩小这一差距，并为非整数阶及更广泛的密度矩阵泛函提供统一的近最优估计框架。

Method: 采用多级算法：利用局部归一化与精度分配降低开销；引入可变时间估计以计入到达昂贵谱测试的概率。在受控纯化访问及其逆操作下，针对加性误差ε、秩上界R与固定阶α建立估计方案，并证明函数估计定理——将全局乘积替换为由函数幅度与谱质量加权的局部代价之和。

Result: Tsallis熵在1<α<2时达到近最优、秩无关的查询复杂度Õ(ε^{-1/(α-1)})（当维度与秩容纳下界实例），在α≥2时为Õ(1/ε)：前者节省1/ε因子，后者将整数阶结论推广至非整数阶。von Neumann熵从Õ(R/ε²)改进为Õ(R/ε)；非整数Rényi阶（1<α<3）获得Õ(R/ε)，其他阶亦有相应界。此外以Õ(R/ε)查询估计固定对数矩与熵方差。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅改进了熵估计的查询复杂度，还高效给出进入有限块长量子压缩与纯态纠缠转换的波动参数；并可推广至更广泛的密度矩阵泛函，为量子谱估计的最优查询复杂度提供系统化途径。

Abstract: The von Neumann, Tsallis, and Rényi entropies are fundamental measures of quantum information. A common estimation strategy multiplies the worst-case costs of spectral transformation and statistical readout, leaving gaps to query lower bounds. We reduce this overhead with multi-level algorithms that use local normalization and precision allocation, while variable-time estimation accounts for the probability of reaching expensive spectral tests. With controlled purified access and its inverse, we obtain bounds for additive error $\varepsilon$, rank upper bound $R$, and fixed order $α$. Tsallis entropy estimation has near-optimal rank-independent query complexity $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-1/(α-1)})$ for $1<α<2$, when the allowed dimension and rank accommodate the lower-bound instances, and $\widetilde O(1/\varepsilon)$ for $α\ge2$, where $\widetilde O$ suppresses logarithmic factors. The first saves a factor $1/\varepsilon$; the second extends known integer-order scaling to noninteger orders. We also improve the von Neumann entropy bound from $\widetilde O(R/\varepsilon^2)$ to $\widetilde O(R/\varepsilon)$ and obtain $\widetilde O(R/\varepsilon)$ for noninteger Rényi orders $1<α<3$, with further bounds at other orders. Our functional-estimation theorem replaces the global product by a sum of local costs weighted by function magnitudes and spectral masses. We further estimate fixed logarithmic moments and entropy variance using $\widetilde O(R/\varepsilon)$ queries, providing efficient access to fluctuation parameters that enter finite-blocklength quantum compression and pure-state entanglement conversion. More broadly, our framework extends beyond entropy to a broad class of density-matrix functionals, offering a systematic approach toward optimal query complexity in quantum spectral estimation.

</details>


### [173] [Improved Quantum Query Bounds for Boolean Matrix Product Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40180)
*Amin Shiraz Gilani,François Le Gall,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出布尔矩阵乘积验证（BMPV）量子查询复杂度的非平凡上界 $\widetilde O(n^{17/12})$，同时证明 $\Omega(n^{5/4})$ 下界，并建立其与正交向量问题（OV）的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BMPV 的量子查询复杂度长期仅有基于 Grover 搜索的平凡上界 $O(n^{3/2})$（Buhrman-Špalek, SODA 2006），下界仅为 $\widetilde\Omega(n^{19/18})$（Childs-Kimmel-Kothari, ESA 2012），上下界之间存在显著间隙，是一个悬而未决的公开问题。

Method: 以正交向量问题（OV）为核心，证明 OV 与 BMPV 之间的等价性，并据此为 OV 建立上述上、下界；同时对 BMPV 的一个变体（判断乘积中是否含给定行向量）给出紧确的 $\widetilde\Theta(n^{3/2})$ 界，并利用这些结果推导图半径与直径判定问题的多项式分离。

Result: 对 $n\times n$ 矩阵，BMPV 上界提升至 $\widetilde O(n^{17/12})$，优于此前的 $O(n^{3/2})$；下界提升至 $\Omega(n^{5/4})$，优于此前的 $\widetilde\Omega(n^{19/18})$。变体问题获得紧确的 $\widetilde\Theta(n^{3/2})$ 界。由此得到判定图半径至多为 2 与直径至多为 2 二者量子查询复杂度之间的多项式分离。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实质性缩小了 BMPV 量子查询复杂度的上下界间隙，并通过与 OV 的等价性统一处理了相关问题的复杂度，同时给出图半径与直径判定问题间的多项式分离。

Abstract: We prove the first non-trivial upper bound for the quantum query complexity of Boolean Matrix Product Verification ($\mathsf{BMPV}$), answering a longstanding open question in quantum query complexity. For $n\times n$ matrices, our upper bound is $\widetilde O(n^{17/12})$, improving on the standard $O(n^{3/2})$ bound obtained using Grover search by Buhrman and Špalek (SODA 2006). We complement this result by showing an $Ω(n^{5/4})$ lower bound, which improves over the previous best known lower bound of $\widetildeΩ(n^{19/18})$ by Childs, Kimmel, and Kothari (ESA 2012).
  Our approach centers on a connection with Orthogonal Vectors ($\mathsf{OV}$), which asks whether an indexed list of $n$ Boolean vectors of dimension $n$ contains two vectors with disjoint supports. In particular, we prove equivalences between $\mathsf{OV}$ and $\mathsf{BMPV}$ and establish the above bounds for $\mathsf{OV}$. We also prove a tight $\widetilde Θ(n^{3/2})$ bound for a variant of $\mathsf{BMPV}$ that asks whether the product contains a given row vector. Together, these results imply a polynomial separation between the quantum query complexities of deciding whether a graph has radius at most two and whether it has diameter at most two.

</details>


### [174] [A mixing time method for estimating the sample complexity of quantum state discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40182)
*Juntai Zhou,Felix Leditzky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合时间（mixing time）方法，用于估计量子态判别（quantum state discrimination）的样本复杂度。对几何均匀纯态集合的最小误差判别，样本复杂度可由量子齐次混合时间与量子广义Dobrushin系数给出紧估计；当生成群G与稳定子群H构成Gelfand对时，量子混合时间约化为经典混合时间，广义Dobrushin系数可用交换Hecke代数的表示论量完整表达。该框架解决了若干学习相位态的公开问题，并推广至混合态集合、极小极大判别及强化数据处理不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态判别的样本复杂度通常依赖具体态的最坏情况分析，缺乏统一的、可利用群对称性与表示论结构的估计工具。作者希望借鉴经典马尔可夫链混合时间与Dobrushin系数理论，建立一套能处理几何均匀纯态集合、相位态、超图态等结构化量子态的样本复杂度分析框架，并解决若干公开问题。

Method: 1) 针对几何均匀纯态集合的最小误差判别，引入量子齐次混合时间与量子广义Dobrushin系数（Wolfer, 2020）给出样本复杂度紧估计；2) 当生成群G与稳定子群H构成Gelfand对时，证明量子混合时间约化为经典混合时间，并用交换Hecke代数的表示论量完整刻画广义Dobrushin系数；3) 将该框架应用于F_q上d次相位态、Z_q上广义布尔相位态及超图态集合；4) 推广至均匀先验的任意混合态集合，用弱混合时间证明最小误差判别样本复杂度的夹逼界，并用Dobrushin型系数给出极小极大判别样本复杂度的紧估计；5) 讨论强化数据处理不等式方法并给出基于其常数的上界。

Result: 1) 几何均匀纯态集合最小误差判别的样本复杂度获得由量子齐次混合时间与量子广义Dobrushin系数给出的紧估计；2) 在Gelfand对情形下，该估计完全由交换Hecke代数End_G(C[G/H])的表示论量表达；3) 框架将学习量子优惠券收集态与相位态的样本复杂度估计约化为经典混合时间问题；4) 解决了F_q上d次相位态（Alrabiah等, 2026）与Z_q上广义布尔相位态（Arunachalam等, 2023）的公开问题；5) 对超图态集合给出完全由超图数据表达的估计，并恢复图态集合的结果（Montanaro和Shao, 2022）；6) 对任意混合态集合，用量子弱混合时间给出最小误差判别样本复杂度的夹逼界，并用Dobrushin型系数给出极小极大判别样本复杂度的紧估计；7) 给出基于强化数据处理不等式常数的上界。

Conclusion: 混合时间方法为量子态判别样本复杂度提供了统一且紧致的分析框架，将群论与表示论结构、经典混合时间理论及数据处理不等式联系起来。该框架不仅约化并解决了一系列相位态与超图态学习的公开问题，还推广至混合态集合与极小极大判别，表明混合时间与Dobrushin型系数是刻画量子态判别复杂度的有力工具。

Abstract: We develop a mixing time method for estimating the sample complexity of quantum state discrimination. We start with considering the minimum-error discrimination of geometrically uniform pure state ensembles, and prove that its sample complexity has a tight estimate given by a quantum homogeneous mixing time [George et al., 2026] and a quantum version of the generalized Dobrushin coefficient [Wolfer, 2020]. This quantum mixing time further reduces to a classical one when the generating group $G$ forms a Gelfand pair with the stabilizer subgroup $H$ of the generator state. In this case the generalized Dobrushin coefficient can be fully expressed by representation-theoretic quantities of the commutative Hecke algebra $\operatorname{End}_G(\mathbb C[G/H])$. In particular, this method reduces the sample complexity estimation of learning quantum coupon collector states [Arunachalam et al., 2020] and learning phase states to classical mixing time problems. We apply this framework to answer the open problems of learning degree-$d$ phase states over $\mathbb F_q$ in [Alrabiah et al., 2026] and generalized Boolean phase states over $\mathbb Z_q$ [Arunachalam et al., 2023]. The framework also applies to hypergraph state ensembles, giving estimates expressed fully in terms of hypergraph data and recovering estimates for graph state ensembles in [Montanaro and Shao, 2022]. Finally, we extend the discussion to arbitrary mixed state ensembles with uniform priors, prove a sandwiched bound for minimum-error discrimination sample complexity by a quantum weakly mixing time, and provide a tight estimate for the minimax discrimination sample complexity from [D'Ariano et al., 2005] by a Dobrushin-type coefficient. We also discuss the method of strengthened data processing inequality [Gao and Rouz{é}, 2022] and give an upper bound in terms of a strengthened data processing inequality constant.

</details>


### [175] [More is Less:Optimal Security for Haar Quantum Money and More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40184)
*Zihan Hao,Xingjian Li,Qipeng Liu,Wei Zhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子货币的安全性，证明基于 n-量子比特 Haar 随机态与反射预言机的构造具有最优查询安全性：只要用户持有的钞票数低于 Ω(2^n)，其已有钞票无法渐近加速伪造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子货币安全界随用户持有钞票数量增加而变弱，缺乏在钞票数低于态层析所需规模时仍保持安全性的构造与分析工具。

Method: 基于压缩预言机技术建立框架，用于定义并分析 Haar 态克隆等量子任务的进展度量，并证明生成 r 个额外副本的紧下界及给出匹配攻击。

Result: 证明了 n-量子比特 Haar 随机态与反射预言机构造达到最优查询安全性，用户持有少于 Ω(2^n) 张钞票时最佳攻击与无钞票时相同，并获得 r 个额外副本生成的紧下界与匹配攻击，结果可应用于量子复制保护。

Conclusion: 该构造在用户钞票数低于态层析尺度时查询安全性不随钞票数增加而退化，为量子货币及量子复制保护提供了更优的安全保障。

Abstract: Quantum cryptography leverages unclonability to enable a wide range of cryptographic applications that are impossible classically, such as digital currency protected against counterfeiting by quantum mechanics. Security requires that no efficient user can produce even one additional valid banknote beyond those already in their possession. However, existing security bounds weaken as the number of banknotes available to a user increases.
  In this paper, we study a construction of quantum money whose asymptotic query security does not deteriorate as long as the number of banknotes available to a user remains below the scale required for state tomography. In particular, we show that the construction based on an $n$-qubit Haar-random state and a reflection oracle achieves optimal query security: unless a user holds $Ω(2^n)$ banknotes, their existing banknotes cannot asymptotically speed up counterfeiting --- their best possible attack is the same as if they do not have any banknotes. To establish this result, we develop a framework based on the compressed-oracle technique for defining and analyzing progress measures for quantum tasks such as Haar state cloning. Furthermore, we prove a tight lower bound for generating $r$ additional copies, give a matching attack, and apply our results to quantum copy-protection.

</details>


### [176] [The Structure of Higher-Order Quantum Resources with an Application to Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40186)
*Felix Ahnefeld,Thomas Theurer,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一处理量子态、信道和超映射（过程）资源性的高阶量子资源通用框架，引入超映射资源单调量及蒸馏/稀释概念，并应用于动力学相干资源理论，分析相容超映射集合的刻画及其在信道蒸馏与稀释中的操作差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源理论多分别针对量子态或信道，缺乏将量子态、信道与超映射（过程）的高阶资源性统一处理的框架，因此需要建立一般性理论来刻画高阶资源的量化、转换与操作意义。

Method: 推广态与信道资源理论中的既有概念，定义超映射资源单调量以及超映射蒸馏和稀释；将自由超映射分为自由网络、相容超映射和最大自由超信道三类；在近似信道到信道转换中分析钻石距离转换误差与最坏情况不保真度的关系；并将框架应用于动力学相干资源理论，用半定约束刻画最大相容超映射集合。

Result: 在温和假设下，关于钻石距离的转换误差与最坏情况不保真度重合；在动力学相干资源理论中证明了最大相容超映射集合的存在性，并通过半定约束刻画表明其严格大于对应自由网络但不包含所有最大自由超信道；进一步给出平行设置下单次信道蒸馏与稀释的解，以及自适应设置下稀释的解。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了统一的高阶量子资源理论框架，为超映射层面的资源量化与转换提供了系统工具，并揭示相容超映射、自由网络与最大自由超信道在动力学相干资源理论中的操作差异。

Abstract: We develop a general framework for higher-order quantum resources that treats the resourcefulness of quantum states, channels, and supermaps (also called processes) in a unified manner. By generalizing well-established concepts from resource theories of states and channels, we introduce and study supermap resource monotones and supermap distillation and dilution. As free supermaps, we consider free networks (supermaps that can be implemented with free channels), compatible supermaps (sets of supermaps closed under arbitrary composition), and maximally free superchannels. Specializing to approximate channel-to-channel conversions, we show that under mild assumptions, the conversion error with respect to the diamond distance and the worst-case infidelity coincide. We then apply the general framework to resource theories of dynamical coherence, where we establish the existence of largest sets of compatible supermaps. By characterizing these sets with semidefinite constraints, we show that they are strictly larger than the corresponding free networks but do not contain all maximally free superchannels. We finish by investigating the operational differences of these sets in one-shot channel distillation and dilution, providing solutions for both tasks in the parallel setting and for dilution in the adaptive setting.

</details>


### [177] [Fast classical simulation algorithms for free-fermion dynamics with magic input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40187)
*Jiwon Heo,Oliver Reardon-Smith,Michał Oszmaniec,Zoltán Zimborás,Changhun Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对非高斯输入的自由费米子（matchgate）电路，发展了精确采样、加性误差估计与精确期望值估计的经典算法，在若干输入模型下达到 $O(2^n)$ 采样成本或多项式时间，为费米子量子模拟提供经典基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟自由费米子（matchgate）电路的经典代价同时取决于输入态与计算任务；已有工作多假设高斯输入，对非高斯输入的经典可模拟性认识不足，因此需要针对非高斯输入发展采样与期望值估计的经典算法。

Method: 1) 对由 $n$ 个四模 magic state 拷贝构成的输入，推广 Clifford–Clifford 玻色采样算法，给出无源与有源自由费米子动力学的精确采样算法，最坏情况每样本算术代价 $O(2^n)$ 且无多项式乘性前因子；2) 将数关联子等可观测量的加性误差估计从无源推广到有源动力学，运行时间关于 $n$ 与加性误差倒数多项式，采用适配每个采样态的高斯态分解控制二阶矩；3) 对常数块大小的偶宇称乘积输入，利用动态规划在 Majorana 因子的子集间共享计算，实现对数权重 Majorana 单项式期望值的精确多项式时间计算。

Result: 给出了非高斯输入下精确采样的 $O(2^n)$ 每样本算法（含无多项式前因子），可用于精确模拟近期囚禁离子实验的非相互作用区域；有源动力学下加性误差估计达到多项式时间，并可估计单个输出概率；偶宇称乘积输入的常数块情形下，对数权重 Majorana 单项式期望值可在多项式时间内精确计算，优于先前准多项式时间的 Majorana 传播类算法。

Conclusion: 本研究为非高斯输入的自由费米子电路在采样、加性误差估计与精确期望值计算等多类任务上建立了经典算法与复杂度基准，扩展了费米子量子模拟可经典验证的范围。

Abstract: The classical cost of simulating free-fermion (matchgate) circuits depends on both the input state and the computational task. We develop classical algorithms for circuits with non-Gaussian inputs, addressing exact sampling and both additive-error and exact estimation of expectation values. For inputs consisting of $n$ copies of the four-mode magic state, we give exact sampling algorithms, generalizing the Clifford and Clifford algorithm for Boson sampling, for passive and active free-fermion dynamics with worst-case arithmetic cost $O(2^n)$ per sample, without any multiplicative polynomial prefactor. The passive algorithm enables an exact simulation of the non-interacting regime of a recent trapped-ion experiment. We also extend recently developed additive-error estimation of number correlators and related observables from passive to active dynamics with runtime polynomial in $n$ and the inverse additive-error, for input states formed by products of $n$ four-mode even parity states. Our estimator uses a Gaussian-state decomposition adapted to each sampled state to control its second moment. This includes estimation of individual output probabilities. Finally, we show that for even-parity product inputs composed of constant-size blocks, expectation values of Majorana monomials of logarithmic weight can be computed exactly in polynomial time, improving on prior Majorana propagation-style algorithms with quasi-polynomial runtime. The exact algorithm processes the input blocks by dynamic programming, sharing calculations across subsets of Majorana factors. Our suite of algorithms provide classical benchmarks for fermionic quantum simulations across a broad spectrum of computational tasks.

</details>


### [178] [Query-Limited RAM Programs and their Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40194)
*Jiahui Liu,Justin Raizes,Bhaskar Roberts,Omri Shmueli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"查询受限RAM程序"(QLP)，将量子一次性程序(QOTP)推广为有状态、结构化、策略驱动的受控访问范式，可防止对抗性分叉与状态回滚，并给出基于"一次性程序"(统一一次性签名与单次有效查询范式)的通用构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子一次性程序(QOTP)只能将功能编码为量子令牌并在单个选定输入上求值一次，本质上无状态，且每次电路求值都需要重新签发并分发令牌，难以支持更丰富的受控访问场景。因此需要探讨一次性计算范式能否扩展到有状态、受限次数的受控访问形式，以及该扩展是否带来超越标准OTP的固有优势。

Method: 引入查询受限RAM程序(QLP)，作为QOTP的RAM推广，允许受控的求值序列同时阻止对抗性分叉或计算状态回滚；提出"一次性程序"(one-shot programs)概念，统一一次性签名(Amos等，STOC 2020)与单次有效查询范式(Gupte等，STOC 2025)；并证明一次性程序可通用地推出查询受限程序。

Result: 证明一次性程序泛型蕴含查询受限程序，说明一次性签名增强的不可克隆性保证可转化为更强的功能；厘清签名令牌原语与量子一次性程序之间的关系，本质上表明一次性签名程序蕴含一次性通用计算。新应用包括：令牌规模仅依赖代码长度(与运行时间无关)的图灵机量子令牌、可转移的k次或预算受限程序、以及适用于SaaS等委托计算场景的低通信机制。

Conclusion: 该工作将一次性计算从无状态扩展至有状态、策略驱动的受控访问，建立了签名令牌原语与量子一次性程序之间的通用联系，并展示了一次性签名程序对一次性通用计算的蕴含关系，为可转移、预算受限及低通信委托计算开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum one-time programs (Broadbent, Gutoski and Stebila, CRYPTO 2013) or OTPs for short, enable a functionality to be encoded into a quantum token that can be evaluated on a single chosen input and then becomes unusable. While powerful, this primitive is inherently stateless and tied to a setting in which a quantum token needs to be issued and distributed for every single evaluation of a circuit. This raises a natural question: can the one-time computation paradigm be extended to richer, stateful forms of controlled access, and would such an extension offer inherent advantages beyond standard OTPs?
  We introduce $\textit{query-limited RAM programs}$ (QLPs), a RAM-generalization of QOTPs that supports structured, stateful computation under bounded or policy-driven access. QLPs allow controlled sequences of evaluations while preventing adversarial forking or rollback of computational state. This enables new applications beyond stateless one-time programs, including quantum tokens for Turing Machines whose size depends only on $\textit{code length}$ (and not runtime), transferable $k$-time or budget-limited programs, and low-communication mechanisms for delegating computation in settings such as Software-as-a-Service.
  To construct QLPs, we introduce $\textit{one-shot programs}$, unifying one-shot signatures (Amos, Georgiou, Kiayias and Zhandry, STOC 2020) with the single effective query paradigm (Gupte, Liu, Raizes, Roberts and Vaikuntanathan, STOC 2025). We prove that one-shot programs generically imply query-limited programs, demonstrating that the strengthened unclonability guarantees of one-shot signatures translate into enhanced functionality. Along the way, we clarify the relationship between signature-token primitives and quantum one-time programs via generic constructions, essentially showing that one-time signing programs imply one-time general computation.

</details>


### [179] [Generalized Reimpell-Werner Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40203)
*Shihao Ru,Bikun Li,Weibo Gao,Liang Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将Reimpell–Werner迭代推广至任意Hermitian代价矩阵的线性目标，证明在合适支撑重叠初始化下收敛到全局最优，并给出O(1/ε)渐近迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量与信道决定量子协议中信息的提取、编码与传输，其性能优化常需随Hilbert空间维度增长仍实用的数值方法。Reimpell–Werner迭代通过尊重约束的重复矩阵更新提供了实用途径，但其收敛保证适用范围有限（如仅面向特定目标与初始化）。本文旨在为该迭代奠定严格理论基础并扩大其收敛保证可覆盖的优化问题类别。

Method: 将Reimpell–Werner迭代推广到具有任意Hermitian代价矩阵的线性目标函数；分析迭代的矩阵更新与约束保持性质；在初始化满足合适的支撑重叠（support overlap）条件时证明迭代点收敛到全局最优；对固定问题、给定迭代参数与可允许初始化，建立达到ε最优目标值的渐近迭代次数上界。

Result: 证明当初始化满足合适支撑重叠条件时，迭代收敛到全局最优；对每个固定问题、迭代参数选择及可允许初始化，渐近地O(1/ε)次迭代即可使目标值进入最优值ε范围内。

Conclusion: 该工作为Reimpell–Werner迭代提供了严格收敛基础，并将其收敛保证推广到更广的一类优化问题（任意Hermitian代价矩阵的线性目标），同时给出明确的渐近复杂度界。

Abstract: Quantum measurements and channels determine how information is extracted, encoded, and transmitted in quantum protocols. Optimizing their performance often requires numerical methods that remain practical as Hilbert space dimensions increase. The Reimpell-Werner iteration offers a practical approach to these tasks through repeated matrix updates that respect the constraints. Here, we generalize this iteration to linear objectives with arbitrary Hermitian cost matrices. We prove that the iterates converge to a global optimum whenever the initialization satisfies suitable support overlap conditions. For each fixed problem, choice of iteration parameters, and admissible initialization, $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon)$ iterations suffice asymptotically to bring the objective value within $\varepsilon$ of the optimum. These results provide a rigorous foundation for the iteration and broaden the class of optimization problems to which its convergence guarantees apply.

</details>


### [180] [Submodularity of entropy under quantum convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40211)
*Milad M. Goodarzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为离散量子卷积的冯·诺依曼熵建立了次模框架，给出了熵加性组合学直接方向的非交换对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵加性组合学中直接方向依赖次模性方法，而量子卷积熵缺乏相应的次模几何基础，需要建立非交换对应理论。

Method: 引入全局加权量子卷积，形成由固定输入集合的容许子集所索引的相容族；证明其熵增长背后存在多拟阵几何，即相对于任一固定容许输入块，熵增益可规范化、单调、次模地延拓到其余输入的所有子集。

Result: 该定理导出卷积强次可加性、量子Ruzsa三角不等式及任意输入态的量子熵Plünnecke–Ruzsa不等式；对重复输入给出各容许尺度下熵增长的紧比较，量子倍增常数以最优指数控制所有更高容许卷积熵。

Conclusion: 上述结果将加性组合学中的次模方法引入量子场景，为广泛的卷积熵不等式族提供了系统化路径。

Abstract: We develop a submodular framework for the von Neumann entropy of discrete quantum convolutions, providing a noncommutative counterpart to the direct side of entropic additive combinatorics. We first introduce globally weighted quantum convolutions, which form compatible families indexed by admissible subsets of a fixed collection of inputs. Our main theorem reveals a polymatroidal geometry underlying their entropy growth: relative to any fixed admissible input block, the entropy gains admit a normalized, monotone, submodular extension to all subsets of the remaining inputs. The theorem yields convolutional strong subadditivity, quantum Ruzsa triangle inequality, and quantum entropic Plünnecke--Ruzsa inequalities for arbitrary input states. For repeated inputs, it gives sharp comparisons of entropy growth across admissible scales; in particular, the quantum doubling constant controls all higher admissible convolution entropies with optimal exponents. Together, these results bring submodular methods from additive combinatorics into the quantum setting and provide a systematic route to broad families of convolutional entropy inequalities.

</details>


### [181] [Quantum Markov State Models for Metastable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40214)
*Hao-En Li,Lin Lin,Michael Ragone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构建量子马尔可夫状态模型（QMSM），在保持量子相干性的同时刻画开放系统的亚稳态动力学，并给出最优舍入误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MSM仅用相标签描述亚稳态，无法捕捉亚稳态间可能持续的量子相干性；且即使慢模式已知，其谱投影未必产生合法量子态。本文旨在构建能在小物理状态空间（含经典扇区与量子矩阵块）上描述幸存信息及其演化的QMSM，并回答Kitaev的精确舍入问题。

Method: 假设演化信道C二次作用变化很小，缺陷η=‖C²−C‖_◇足够小，且慢自由度数目与全系统尺寸无关。在此假设下构造压缩与重构信道，使两者复合对每个约化态精确恢复，反向复合给出精确幂等信道逼近C。

Result: 构造对所有输入态给出最优菱形范数界O(η^{1/3})，对由C制备的亚稳态给出改进界O(η^{1/2})，常数仅依赖慢维度。约化转移信道可迭代以可控误差预测微观动力学。以弱驱动耗散自旋链为例，展示其支持亚稳逻辑量子比特或长寿命经典相。

Conclusion: QMSM在经典MSM框架中融入量子相干性，通过压缩-重构实现精确恢复与幂等逼近，并给出最优误差界，为开放系统亚稳态动力学提供了可迭代预测的量子化建模工具。

Abstract: Open quantum systems can rapidly lose most microscopic information, leaving only a few degrees of freedom to govern their long-time dynamics. Classical Markov state models (MSMs) describe such metastable dynamics as transitions between a few representative phases and are a well-established tool in condensed matter and chemical physics. However, phase labels alone cannot capture quantum coherence that may persist between metastable states. Even when the slow modes are known, their spectral projection need not produce valid quantum states. We construct quantum Markov state models (QMSMs) that describe the surviving information and its evolution on a small physical state space containing classical sectors and quantum matrix blocks. We assume that the evolution channel $\mathcal C$ changes little when applied a second time, with sufficiently small defect $η=\|\mathcal C^2-\mathcal C\|_\diamond$, and that the number of slow degrees of freedom is bounded independently of the full system size. Under these assumptions, we construct compression and reconstruction channels whose composition recovers every reduced state exactly, while the reverse composition gives an exactly idempotent channel approximating $\mathcal C$, answering Kitaev's exact-rounding question \cite{Kitaev2025}. Our constructions give an optimal diamond-norm bound of $\mathcal{O}(η^{1/3})$ on all input states, as well as an improved bound of $\mathcal{O}(η^{1/2})$ on metastable states prepared by $\mathcal C$, with constants depending only on the slow dimension. The reduced transition channel can be iterated to predict the microscopic dynamics with controlled error. We illustrate the QMSM through a weakly driven dissipative spin chain supporting either a metastable logical qubit or long-lived classical phases.

</details>


### [182] [Universal distillation of quantum entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40215)
*Ryuji Takagi,Kaito Watanabe,Takaya Matsuura,Hayato Arai,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明可提纯纠缠（distillable entanglement）在不预先知道输入态描述的条件下仍是可达的基本操作量：作者构造了一个通用LOCC协议，其描述与输入态无关，对任意未知输入态都能达到其可提纯纠缠率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可提纯纠缠（可经LOCC从含噪纠缠态中提取Bell态的最大速率）一直被视为刻画纠缠态操作价值的核心量，但这一操作意义仅在双方已知输入态描述、并可据此定制协议的假设下成立。该假设未必符合自然操作场景，因此需要澄清：若缺乏输入态知识，可提纯纠缠是否仍具操作意义。

Method: 作者提出一个通用LOCC协议，协议本身与输入态无关（universal），因此对任意输入均可适用。技术路线基于复合态（compound states）的态合并（state merging），并结合有限块量向可提纯纠缠的均匀收敛性质，从而证明该协议对任意未知输入态都能达到其可提纯纠缠率。

Result: 该通用协议对任意未知输入态均可实现其可提纯纠缠，即无需输入态知识即可达到纠缠提纯的最优速率。由此，可提纯纠缠在没有事先态描述假设的情形下依然是可达的基本操作量。

Conclusion: 研究将可提纯纠缠提升为具有鲁棒性的、操作上有意义的量，并明确说明：要达到纠缠提纯的最优速率，并不需要关于输入态的知识。

Abstract: Distillable entanglement---the maximum rate of Bell states extractable from the given noisy entangled states via local operations and classical communication (LOCC)---has been a central quantity to characterize the operational value of entangled states. However, this operational meaning had been known to be valid only under a crucial assumption: both parties know the description of the input state and can choose the protocol tailored to it, which does not necessarily reflect the natural operational setting. Here, we show that the distillable entanglement is the fundamental operational quantity even without this assumption. We present a universal LOCC protocol---whose description does not depend on the input state and thus works universally for any input---that achieves the distillable entanglement of an arbitrary unknown input state. Our result therefore lifts the distillable entanglement to a robust notion of an operationally meaningful quantity and clarifies that input-state knowledge is not necessary to achieve the optimal rate of entanglement distillation. Our protocol is based on state merging for compound states, together with the uniform convergence property of the finite-block quantity toward distillable entanglement.

</details>


### [183] [Quantum Log-Determinant Methods for Torsion-Sensitive Topological Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40216)
*Dimitrios Thanos,Caesnan Leditto,Adam Wesolowski,Andreea-Iulia Lefterovici,Mahtab Yaghubi Rad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出首个可证明正确的积分同调挠阶量子估计器，通过对数伪行列式的交替组合恢复高阶临界群大小及同调挠子群阶，并给出带谱隙、查询与精度显式依赖的量子算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Betti数无法刻画积分同调中的挠（torsion）信息，而挠可区分具有相同Betti数的拓扑结构，并在拓扑数据分析与图机器学习（含生物医学应用）中具有实用价值。因此需要研究量子谱方法能否获取这类额外的挠敏感信息。

Method: 以边界拉普拉斯算子的非零特征值对数之和定义对数伪行列式，用于概括单纯复形的谱信息；在明确拓扑假设下，用这些量的交替组合得到高阶临界群的大小（图沙堆群的高维类比），并在更强认证条件下通过相关构造恢复同调中挠子群的阶。随后给出新的量子算法估计这些量，显式依赖输入查询方式、所需精度及谱隙，并利用平衡多部团复形的精确谱获得有利谱隙，构造显式含挠基准；算法采用紧凑查询访问，无需显式构造指数大的边界矩阵，对受限含挠族用定制振幅估计实现查询优势。

Result: 据作者所知，这是首个可证明正确的积分同调挠阶量子估计器。在估计对数伪行列式时，对受限含挠族，定制振幅估计例程所用查询数比任何在可比访问下的随机经典算法二次方地更少。但对于将完整、未归一化量估计到固定加性误差，其代价仍与候选空间维度D成正比。

Conclusion: 量子谱方法能够访问Betti数之外的挠信息，并可估计积分同调挠阶，但完整未归一化量的估计成本仍受候选空间维度D限制，构成该类方法的固有瓶颈。

Abstract: Betti numbers are central to topological data analysis but fail to capture torsion in integral homology. Torsion can distinguish topological structures with identical Betti numbers, and examples from classical topological data analysis show that it can affect the topology inferred from data. Related torsion-sensitive quantities are also known to have practical applications in graph-based machine learning, including biomedical applications. We investigate how quantum spectral methods can access this additional information.
  Log pseudodeterminants, obtained by summing the logarithms of the nonzero boundary-Laplacian eigenvalues, summarize spectral information about a simplicial complex. Under explicit topological assumptions, an alternating combination of these quantities gives the size of a higher critical group, the higher-dimensional analogue of a graph sandpile group. With stronger certification, a related construction recovers the size of the torsion subgroup in homology itself. This is, to our knowledge, the first provably correct quantum estimator of integral-homology torsion order.
  We give a new quantum algorithm for estimating these quantities, with explicit dependence on how the input is queried, the required accuracy, and the spectral gaps. Exact spectra for balanced multipartite clique complexes provide favorable gaps, and a related construction gives an explicit torsion-bearing benchmark. With compact query access, the algorithm need not construct the exponentially large boundary matrices explicitly. For a restricted torsion-bearing family, a tailored amplitude-estimation routine uses quadratically fewer queries than any randomized classical algorithm given comparable access when estimating the log pseudodeterminant. Estimating the full, unnormalized quantity to fixed additive error still costs in proportion to the candidate-space dimension $D$.

</details>


### [184] [Improved quantum volume estimation with transducers and amortized quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40217)
*Arjan Cornelissen,Simon Apers,Sander Gribling*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对经典体积估计算法进行量子化，将其查询复杂度从 Õ(d^3.5 + d^2.25/ε) 改进为 Õ(d^3.5 + d^1.75/ε)，核心技术是摊销量子游走代价的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积估计是计算几何中的经典问题，其随机算法推动了 MCMC 与模拟退火等重要技术发展。现有的最优随机算法查询复杂度为 Õ(d^3.5 + d^3/ε^2)，而其量子版本的最佳界为 Õ(d^3.5 + d^2.25/ε)，仍有改进空间。

Method: 作者对 Cousins 与 Vempala 提出的最先进随机算法进行量子化，提出一个摊销量子游走代价的框架，该框架基于 Belovs、Jeffery 和 Yolcu 近期引入的 transducer 工具包。

Result: 获得了查询复杂度为 Õ(d^3.5 + d^1.75/ε) 的量子算法，优于当前最优界 Õ(d^3.5 + d^2.25/ε)。

Conclusion: 该摊销量子游走框架使作者能够利用 Cousins 与 Vempala 球游走的摊销分析，从而克服了此前阻碍其量子实现的关键障碍，实现了体积估计量子算法的改进。

Abstract: The volume estimation problem is a classic task in computational geometry. The development of randomized algorithms for this problem spurred the development of many influential algorithmic techniques related to Markov Chain Monte Carlo and simulated annealing, and the problem connects to several important geometrical results, like the recently-resolved KLS conjecture. In this work, we quantize the state-of-the-art $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$-query randomized algorithm developed by Cousins and Vempala, and obtain a $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5} + d^{1.75}/\varepsilon)$-query quantum algorithm, improving over the $\widetilde{O}(d^{3.5} + d^{2.25}/\varepsilon)$ state-of-the-art bound. Our key technical contribution is a framework for amortizing the cost of a quantum walk. The framework is based on the recent transducer toolkit introduced by Belovs, Jeffery and Yolcu. It is this amortized quantum walk framework that allows us to exploit the amortized analysis of the ball walk by Cousins and Vempala, thus overcoming the key barrier that previously barred its quantum implementation.

</details>


### [185] [Purification brings advantages in sequential quantum channel discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40218)
*Zoe G. del Toro,Marco Túlio Quintino,Jessica Bavaresco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子信道序贯判别中纯化（purification）作为操作资源的作用，证明在环境参考系未知或平均化时，纯化访问相比裸信道访问具有严格优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯化通常被视为量子态与信道的便捷数学表示，暗示纯化自由度及其与环境的相关性只是表示变换。作者希望探究这些自由度能否被用作实际的操作性资源，特别是在序贯量子信道判别任务中。

Method: 作者在序贯信道判别框架下对比"裸信道访问"与"平均化纯化访问"。通过构造两族qubit-qubit信道，分析两查询情形下的成功概率差异；并构造另一对信道，其纯化形式在三次查询下可完美判别而任意有限次裸查询均无法完美判别。

Result: 在状态判别与并行信道判别中裸访问与平均化纯化访问等价，但在序贯访问下二者出现严格分离：两查询情形存在成功概率的严格优势；三查询情形下纯化可达完美判别，而裸查询无论多少次都无法完美判别。

Conclusion: 纯化自由度可作为操作资源带来序贯判别的优势，同时证明无法通过允许查询间任意干预的变换将裸信道查询转化为平均化纯化查询，强化了此前的不可转化性结论。

Abstract: Purifications are often used as a convenient mathematical representation of quantum states and channels when constructing protocols for quantum information processing tasks. Although powerful, this perspective can suggest that the purifying degrees of freedom and its correlations with an environment are just a useful change of representation. Here we show how they can instead be exploited as an operational resource in sequential quantum channel discrimination, even when the environmental reference frame is unknown or averaged. In contrast to state discrimination and parallel channel discrimination, for which bare and averaged-purification access are equivalent, sequential access to purified resources exhibits a strict advantage in probability of success. We prove this strict separation via two main results. In the first, we construct a pair of families of qubit-qubit channels with a strict separation in the two-query case, and in the second, we show an example of a pair of qubit-qubit channels whose purifications are perfectly distinguishable with three queries while no finite number of bare (non-purified) queries suffices for perfect discrimination. These separations imply an advantage of sequential over parallel strategies for channel discrimination and establish the impossibility of converting bare channel queries into averaged-purification queries via transformations that allow arbitrary interventions between queries, strengthening previous results.

</details>


### [186] [NLTM Hamiltonians from gauged sheaf quantum locally testable codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40220)
*Fuchuan Wei,Zhengyi Han,Zimu Li,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了"无低能平凡魔法"(NLTM)猜想，构造了一类局域哈密顿量，其正能密度窗口内的任意态从任意稳定子态制备都需要 Ω(log n) 的电路深度，且该结论对任意有界局部维度的 qudit 稳定子输入均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能量子态的复杂度是量子复杂度理论与多体物理的核心问题。NLTS 猜想（无低能平凡态）虽已被解决，但仍允许用浅层稳定子线路制备低能态。本文旨在强化该结论，排除浅层稳定子类经典见证，从而更深入地刻画与量子 PCP 相关的低能复杂度。

Method: 构造基于非阿贝尔规范化的层叠码（gauged sheaf codes），利用良好量子局部可测码上的 cup product 结构实现；为关联哈密顿量建立常数算符可靠性（constant operator soundness），使受保护的逻辑结构能够约束所有足够低能密度下的态。

Result: 证明了 NLTM 猜想：构造出一族局域哈密顿量，任意处于正能密度窗口内的态，从任意稳定子态（可高度纠缠）制备均需 Ω(log n) 电路深度。结果具有内在性——同一哈密顿量族与能量密度阈值适用于任意有界局部维度的 qudit 稳定子输入。

Conclusion: 本文显著强化了 NLTS，排除了浅层稳定子基的经典见证，推进了对与量子 PCP 相关的低能复杂度的理解。

Abstract: Understanding the complexity of low-energy quantum states is a central problem in quantum complexity theory and many-body physics. Here we prove the no low-energy trivial magic (NLTM) conjecture, constructing a family of local Hamiltonians for which every state within a positive energy density window requires circuit depth $Ω(\log n)$ to prepare from arbitrary stabilizer state that may themselves be highly entangled. Notably, our result is intrinsic in the sense that the same Hamiltonian family and energy density threshold apply to arbitrary qudit stabilizer inputs with any bounded local dimensions. Our construction builds on non-Abelian gauged sheaf codes obtained using the cup product structure on good quantum locally testable codes. We establish constant operator soundness for the associated Hamiltonians, enabling their protected logical structure to constrain all states at sufficiently low energy density. By excluding shallow stabilizer-based classical witnesses, Our result substantially strengthens NLTS and advances our understanding of low-energy complexity relevant to quantum PCP.

</details>


### [187] [Causal inequalities witness non-stabilizerness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40223)
*Leonardo Vaglini,Nasra Daher Ahmed,Ravi Kunjwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文刻画了"无魔法的非稳定子性"(NSWM)现象，给出稳定子基能否仅用稳定子操作完美区分的判据，并证明乘积稳定子基需要非稳定子性的充要条件是其对应过程函数违反因果不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子操作(SO)可被经典高效模拟，故通用量子计算需要魔法态等非稳定子资源。然而资源理论的"操作"与"公理"路径存在差异：SO严格小于完全稳定子保持操作(CSPO)，出现三量子比特稳定子乘积基在SO下不可完美区分、在CSPO下可完美区分的NSWM现象。作者希望为这一现象建立原理性理解，并揭示其与因果结构的联系。

Method: 首先推导一个简洁判据，判定给定稳定子基能否仅用稳定子操作完美区分；随后聚焦仅含乘积态的稳定子基，将其与"过程函数"（无悖论因果环的经典模型）建立联系，据此证明充要条件。

Result: 得到稳定子基在SO下可完美区分的判据；并证明：稳定子乘积基中的态需要非稳定子性才能完美区分，当且仅当其对应的过程函数违反因果不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作为NSWM提供了原理性理解，并赋予因果不等式违反新的操作意义——作为非稳定子性（一种计算非经典性）的见证。

Abstract: Stabilizer operations describe a fragment of quantum theory that is known to be efficiently classically simulable, thanks to the Gottesman-Knill theorem. For this reason, nonstabilizer resources such as magic states are necessary for universal quantum computation. Interestingly, the operational and axiomatic approaches to the resource theory of magic differ: the set of free operations in the former, namely, stabilizer operations (SO), is strictly smaller than that in the latter, namely, completely stabilizer preserving operations (CSPO). A simple example showing the separation is given by a three-qubit stabilizer product basis whose states cannot be perfectly discriminated using SO, but which do admit perfect discrimination using CSPO. Such an ensemble of states is said to exhibit nonstabilizerness without magic (NSWM). Here we obtain a principled understanding of this phenomenon, proving necessary and sufficient conditions for its existence. We first derive a simple criterion to decide whether, given a stabilizer basis, its states can be perfectly discriminated using stabilizer operations alone. We then consider the case where the stabilizer basis contains only product states and use its link with process functions---classical models of paradox-free causal loops---to prove the following: the states in a stabilizer product basis require nonstabilizerness for perfect discrimination if and only if the corresponding process function violates a causal inequality. This provides a new operational meaning to causal inequality violations as witnesses of nonstabilizerness, a form of computational nonclassicality.

</details>


### [188] [A physical and universal model of bosonic computations with Solovay-Kitaev theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40226)
*Dorian Rudolph,Arsalan Motamedi,Dhruva Sambrani,Hamid Reza Naeij,Ulysse Chabaud,Sevag Gharibian,Saeed Mehraban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出玻色能量守恒量子计算（BEQC）新模型，将能量视为计算资源，仅通过输入相干态供能、门由能量守恒哈密顿量生成，从而杜绝能量暴涨；并证明其在多项式能量下精确恢复BQP，具备通用门集、Solovay-Kitaev定理及GKP态制备、Fock态制备等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有玻色量子计算模型（Lloyd-Braunstein模型、混合振子-量子比特模型）允许能量剧烈增长，导致非物理的计算能力，并使通用高效编译等基本算法工具失效。因此需要一个物理上自洽、能量受限的玻色量子计算模型。

Method: 提出BEQC模型：能量仅由输入相干态提供，所有量子门由能量守恒哈密顿量生成，从构造上排除能量暴涨。随后分析其计算能力（模拟现有模型中所有多项式能量计算、复杂度上界）与通用性（基于线性光学和Kerr相互作用的通用门集、态合成及Solovay-Kitaev定理）。

Result: 1）计算能力：BEQC高效模拟现有模型中所有多项式能量计算，在多项式能量设定下精确恢复BQP，并在能量、精度、空间参数变化时给出若干复杂度上界；2）通用门集与态合成：获得规避此前no-go结果的Solovay-Kitaev定理，并给出（a）具严格制备保证的GKP态工程协议、（b）指数精度Fock态制备、（c）任意量子比特酉操作的Fock空间原生模拟等应用。

Conclusion: BEQC是一个物理上有据、计算上稳健且通用的玻色量子计算模型，通过将能量作为计算资源，既避免了非物理的能量增长，又在多项式能量下保持与BQP等价的计算能力，并提供通用门集与态合成工具。

Abstract: Bosonic quantum systems are among the leading architectures for quantum information processing, offering continuous-variable degrees of freedom with strong error-correction capabilities. However, standard bosonic quantum computation models such as the Lloyd-Braunstein [Lloyd and Braunstein, 1999] and hybrid oscillator-qubit models [Brenner, Dias, and Koenig, 2025; Liu et al., 2026] permit dramatic energy growth, leading to unphysical computational power and the breakdown of fundamental algorithmic tools such as universal and efficient compilation [Brenner et al., 2026; Rudolph et al., 2025]. To address this, we introduce a new model of bosonic quantum computation, Bosonic Energy-Preserving Quantum Computation (BEQC), in which energy is treated as a computational resource. Namely, energy is supplied solely through input coherent states, and all gates are generated by energy-preserving Hamiltonians. Thus, by construction, dramatic energy growth is impossible, making the model physically grounded.
  We next show that BEQC is a computationally robust and universal model in many respects, including: (1. Computational power) BEQC efficiently simulates all polynomial-energy computations in existing models, and exactly recovers BQP in the polynomial energy setting. It further admits several complexity-theoretic upper bounds when varying the energy, precision, and space parameters of the model. (2. Universal gate sets and state synthesis) BEQC has natural universal gate sets based on linear optics and Kerr interactions. In particular, we obtain a Solovay-Kitaev theorem which circumvents previous no-go results. We give various applications, including (a) a protocol for engineering GKP states with rigorous preparation guarantees, (b) Fock state preparation to exponential precision, and (c) native Fock space simulation of any qubit-based unitary.

</details>


### [189] [Randomness of exact unitary designs under symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40227)
*Christopher Vairogs,Felix Leditzky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了酉设计强度与对称设计强度，证明在高维及一大类对称性（含全局U(1)、SU(d)）下存在对称设计强度严格高于酉设计强度的有限酉系综，并给出群结构系综的若干界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理对称性约束下，只有对称兼容算符才可用，因此标准酉设计强度不足以刻画系综在对称约束下的随机化能力；需要新指标并厘清其与酉设计强度的大小关系。

Method: 定义酉设计强度（t阶矩与酉群相同的最大t）与对称设计强度（对称兼容子系综t阶矩与对称兼容酉群相同的最大t）；在高维及包括全局on-site U(1)与SU(d)的广义对称类中构造有限酉系综；并针对构成群的算符系综推导强度界的证明。

Result: 在足够高维与广义对称下，存在任意高酉设计强度且对称设计强度严格更高的有限酉系综；对任意构成有限群的系综，在全局on-site U(1)与SU(d)下对称设计强度至多为2；并给出任意维数下任意高对称设计强度的均匀加权群之存在性及其结构分析。

Conclusion: 对称约束下系综可具备比无对称时更高的随机化效能；结果推广并补充了Clifford群对称设计强度受限的先前工作，并给出群结构系综的统一界与构造。

Abstract: We define the unitary design strength of a finite unitary ensemble to be the maximal integer $t$ for which its $t$-th statistical moment is identical to that of the unitary group. In the presence of physical symmetry, not all operators of an ensemble are symmetry-compatible. This motivates us to define the symmetric design strength of a finite unitary ensemble to be the maximal integer $t$ for which the $t$-th moments of its symmetry-compatible sub-ensemble match those of the group of symmetry-compatible unitaries. We show that in sufficiently high dimension and for a broad family of symmetries, which includes the global on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(d)$ symmetries, there exist finite unitary ensembles of arbitrarily high design strength whose symmetric design strength is strictly higher. Ensembles with this property may be more versatile under symmetry constraints for protocols that rely on unitary randomization than they are without symmetry. Our results extend the previous work [Mitsuhashi and Yoshioka, PRX Quantum 4.4 (Nov. 2023)] showing that the symmetric design strength of the Clifford group is strictly upper bounded by its unitary design strength for symmetries that do not trivialize the symmetry-compatible unitaries. Due to the special significance of unitary designs with a group structure, we also derive a variety of bounds on the unitary and symmetric design strengths of finite unitary ensembles whose operators form a group. In particular, we provide a simple proof that an arbitrary ensemble of operators forming a finite group must have a symmetric design strength of at most two under the global on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(d)$ symmetries. Along the way, we show that there exist uniformly weighted groups of arbitrarily high symmetric design strength in arbitrary dimension and analyze their structure.

</details>


### [190] [Perfect non-local quantum computation is impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40228)
*Marten Folkertsma,Dmitry Grinko,Gina Muuss,Florian Speelman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了即使在两量子比特情形下，Haar-几乎每个酉操作都不存在使用有限维资源的精确非局域量子计算（NLQC）协议；同时给出了无精确协议的显式目标，并推广到可局域化量子测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLQC要求两方借助纠缠资源态与一轮同时量子通信实现联合操作，其在量子位置验证攻击、通信复杂度和量子引力等领域有应用。已知每个双体酉操作都有带有限纠缠的近似NLQC协议，且若干结构化族存在精确协议，但"是否每个固定酉操作都存在有限维资源的精确协议"这一根本问题始终未决。本文旨在解决该存在性问题。

Method: 证明分两步：首先证明精确协议的平滑形变只能通过局域酉操作改变其目标；其次，对于固定架构，利用半代数几何（semialgebraic geometry）将精确协议限制为有限多个连通族，因此每个架构只能覆盖有限多个局域酉轨道。对所有架构取并集，可精确实现的酉操作构成可数多个局域酉轨道，而非空开集性质的Haar测度论证表明其测度为零。

Result: 即使对两量子比特，Haar-几乎每个酉操作都不允许有限维资源的精确协议，即便资源态与局域操作可针对目标定制。可精确实现的酉操作仅构成可数多个局域酉轨道。此外，受控相位 diag(1,1,1,e^{iθ}) 当 e^{iθ} 为超越数（如 θ=1）时无有限维精确协议。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了精确NLQC的稀缺性：可精确实现的目标在Haar测度下为零测集，且仅覆盖可数多个局域酉轨道。结论同样适用于可局域化量子测量：Haar-随机基下的秩一投影测量几乎必然不能在有限维资源态与有限多个局域输出下被局域化。

Abstract: Non-local quantum computation (NLQC) asks two parties to apply a joint operation to their quantum inputs using an entangled resource state and one round of simultaneous quantum communication. NLQC has applications across quantum information, including attacks on quantum position verification, communication complexity, and quantum gravity.
  Every bipartite unitary has an approximate NLQC protocol with finite entanglement, and exact protocols are known for several structured families. However, it has remained open whether every fixed unitary admits an exact protocol with finite-dimensional resources. We show that, even for two qubits, Haar-almost every unitary does not admit such a protocol, even when the resource state and local operations are tailored to the target.
  The proof first shows that smooth deformations of an exact protocol can only change its target by local unitaries. For a fixed architecture, semialgebraic geometry limits the exact protocols to finitely many connected families, so each architecture reaches only finitely many local-unitary orbits. Taking the union over all architectures, the exactly implementable unitaries form countably many local-unitary orbits. We also show that some natural targets do not admit a protocol: the controlled phase $\mathrm{diag}(1,1,1,e^{iθ})$ has no exact protocol with finite-dimensional resources whenever $e^{iθ}$ is transcendental, for instance for $θ= 1$.
  Our results also apply to localizable quantum measurements, whose outcome is obtained from local measurements on inputs and a shared entangled resource state without communication. A rank-one projective measurement in a Haar-random basis is almost surely not localizable with a finite-dimensional resource state and finitely many local outcomes.

</details>


### [191] [A Quantum Algorithm for $st$-Transport on Flat Connection Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40231)
*Stacey Jeffery,Tobias J. Osborne,Galina Pass*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将无权无向 st-连通性推广到边带量子操作（酉矩阵）的图，提出 st-传输问题，并给出时间 $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$、空间 $O(\log n+\log k+\log(1/\varepsilon))$ 的有界误差量子算法，同时证明 $\Omega(n)$ 量子查询下界，表明算法在常数 $\varepsilon$ 下近似最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典无向 st-连通性是基础图问题，本文希望将其推广到边携带量子操作（酉）的图，以刻画谱图论中的一致连接图与群同步的无噪声实例。在该设定下，从 s 到 t 的态传输由唯一酉 $U_s(t)$ 定义，因此自然的问题是在给定相干预言机下判定连通性并估计传输后态与目标态的平方重叠。

Method: 作者考虑在每条边 $\{u,v\}$ 上标注酉 $U_{uv}\in\mathbb{C}^{k\times k}$（$U_{vu}=U_{uv}^\dagger$）的无向图，并假设标注构成平坦连接（纯规范），即任意路径上的有序乘积与路径无关。给定态 $|\psi_s\rangle,|\psi_t\rangle$ 以及返回邻居并相干施加对应边酉的预言机，定义 st-传输问题。算法通过设计一个 transducer，并对输入图应用 Metropolis-Hastings 重加权来实现。

Result: 算法为有界误差量子算法，运行时间为 $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$，空间为 $O(\log n+\log k+\log(1/\varepsilon))$。同时证明了一个 $\Omega(n)$ 的量子查询下界，该下界即使在 s 与 t 被承诺连通时仍成立。

Conclusion: 对于常数 $\varepsilon$，所提算法在多项式对数因子意义下达到最优，是 st-传输问题的一个近似最优量子算法；当 $k=1$ 且所有标注平凡时，该问题退化为经典无向 st-连通性。

Abstract: We study a generalization of undirected $st$-connectivity to graphs whose edges carry quantum operations. Let $G=(V,E)$ be an undirected graph on $n$ vertices in which each edge $\{u,v\}$ is labeled by a unitary $U_{uv}\in\mathbb{C}^{k\times k}$, with $U_{vu}=U_{uv}^\dagger$. We assume the labels form a \emph{flat} connection: the ordered product of labels along any path between a pair of vertices $u$ and $v$ is independent of the path. Equivalently, the connection is pure gauge, i.e., gauge-equivalent to the trivial connection; such graphs are exactly the consistent connection graphs of spectral graph theory and the noiseless instances of group synchronization. Consequently, whenever $s$ and $t$ are connected, transporting a state from $s$ to $t$ defines a unique unitary $U_s(t)$. Given states $|ψ_s\rangle,|ψ_t\rangle\in\mathbb{C}^k$ and an oracle that returns the neighbours of a vertex while coherently applying the corresponding edge unitaries, the \emph{$st$-transport problem} is to decide whether $s$ and $t$ are connected and, if so, to estimate the squared overlap between $U_s(t)|ψ_s\rangle$ and $|ψ_t\rangle$ to additive error $\varepsilon$. When $k=1$ and all labels are trivial, this is exactly undirected $st$-connectivity. We give a bounded-error quantum algorithm for $st$-transport that runs in time $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$ and uses $O(\log n+\log k+\log(1/\varepsilon))$ space. We do this by designing a transducer and applying a Metropolis-Hastings reweighting to the input graph. We also prove an $Ω(n)$ quantum query lower bound that holds even when $s$ and $t$ are promised to be connected, so for constant $\varepsilon$ our algorithm is optimal up to polylogarithmic factors.

</details>


### [192] [Optimal Purity Estimation with Incoherent Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40248)
*Junseo Lee,Chirag Wadhwa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非相干测量下量子态纯度估计的副本复杂度，给出改进的非自适应算法与自适应估计器，并刻画了该问题的整体最优副本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计未知量子态的纯度至乘性误差ε是基本任务。允许一般集体测量时已有最优副本复杂度Θ(√d/ε²+d/ε)，但集体测量在大量副本上实验实现困难，因此需要刻画非相干测量的副本复杂度。然而该设定下此前仅有非自适应算法达到O(d/ε²+d²/ε)，比已知下界多项式级更大，存在明显差距。

Method: 提出一种新的非自适应非相干测量算法；并设计一种新的自适应纯度估计器，针对高精度区间ε=o(1/d)改善复杂度。

Result: 新的非自适应算法使用O(d/ε²+d^{3/2}/ε)个副本，改进了后一项；证明该复杂度对任意非自适应算法最优。结合自适应估计器，得到非相干纯度估计的最优复杂度Θ(min{d/ε²+d^{3/2}/ε, d²/ε+√d/ε²})。

Conclusion: 在非相干测量设定下，非自适应算法达到O(d/ε²+d^{3/2}/ε)且为最优；自适应估计器在高精度区间更优；二者结合刻画了该问题的整体最优副本复杂度。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the fundamental task of estimating the purity of an unknown state to within $\textit{multiplicative}$ error $\varepsilon$. When one can perform general collective measurements, $Θ\left(\frac{\sqrt{d}}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d}{\varepsilon}\right)$ copies are known to be necessary and sufficient for this problem [AISW20]. However, implementing collective measurements on such a large number of copies can be experimentally demanding, and we thus aim to characterize the copy complexity of this problem with incoherent measurements.
  In this setting, the only non-trivial result is a non-adaptive algorithm that uses $O\left(
  \frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^2}{\varepsilon}
  \right)$ copies [PTTW26], which is polynomially larger than the best-known lower bound. Our first result is a new algorithm performing non-adaptive incoherent measurements that succeeds using $O\left(
  \frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}
  \right)$ copies, improving on the latter term in the copy complexity. Moreover, we show that for any algorithm restricted to non-adaptive measurements, the above copy complexity is optimal.
  We also develop a new adaptive estimator for the purity of a state that improves on the above complexity in the high-precision regime, i.e., for $\varepsilon = o(1/d)$. We also show that the non-adaptive and adaptive estimators together yield the optimal complexity for incoherent purity estimation; in particular, we show that the copy complexity of this problem is $$
  Θ\left(\min\left\{
  \frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}, \frac{d^2}{\varepsilon} + \frac{\sqrt{d}}{\varepsilon^2}\right\}
  \right).
  $$

</details>


### [193] [An (almost) efficient classical algorithm for sampling from typical Gibbs states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40250)
*Saleh Naghdi,Eric R. Anschuetz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子 p-spin 模型的 Gibbs 采样，给出反对指数级量子优势的强证据：提出基于算法随机局部化（ASL）的经典采样算法，可在多项式小全变差误差下从 Gibbs 态采样，并给出该算法可工作至自旋玻璃转变温度的物理论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs 态制备被认为是量子优势的潜在来源，且最坏情形下已已知优势；但在平均情形局域系统中，凡量子可高效制备 Gibbs 态的温度区间，经典准多项式算法也能计算局域期望值。然而在更强的“采样”模拟意义下，量子是否仍有优势尚不清楚。Gibbs 采样支撑玻尔兹曼机量子学习与量子霸权方案，但物理相关模型的经典平均情形复杂度仍悬而未决，本文即针对该空白展开。

Method: 提出一种针对量子 p-spin 模型系综的经典 Gibbs 采样算法，核心为元算法“算法随机局部化”（ASL）。主要技术结果是 ASL 的广义收敛定理：将超立方体上任一分布的近似采样归约为对相关“倾斜分布”均值的低加性误差估计。随后将已有的、用于计算相关无序模型局域期望值的准多项式时间算法推广至量子 p-spin 模型的倾斜均值计算，并给出该均值估计算法可下探至自旋玻璃转变温度的非严格物理argument。

Result: 得到可在任意多项式小全变差距离误差下从 Gibbs 态采样的经典算法；证明广义 ASL 收敛定理，实现采样到均值估计的归约；将准多项式局域期望值算法推广用于估计量子 p-spin 模型的倾斜均值；并论证该均值估计可工作至自旋玻璃转变温度。

Conclusion: 由于高效量子算法在自旋玻璃转变温度处同样失效，本文结果表明：在量子算法亦高效的温度区间内，经典算法也（几乎）高效地完成量子 p-spin 模型的 Gibbs 采样，从而对 Gibbs 采样中指数级量子优势给出强反证。

Abstract: Gibbs state preparation has emerged as a potential avenue for quantum advantage in the study of physical systems. Although there is known advantage for computing local properties of worst-case Gibbs states, recent results suggest a different picture for average-case local systems: at temperatures where their Gibbs states can be efficiently prepared quantumly, quasi-polynomial time classical algorithms can also compute local expectation values. Yet this leaves open the possibility of an advantage under a stronger notion of simulation: sampling. Gibbs sampling underlies Boltzmann machine-based quantum learning algorithms and quantum supremacy proposals, but its classical average-case complexity for physically relevant models remains unresolved. Here, we give strong evidence against an exponential quantum advantage in Gibbs sampling for an ensemble of local systems known as the quantum $p$-spin model. We describe a classical algorithm for sampling from the Gibbs state to any polynomially small error in total variation distance. Our algorithm is based on a meta-algorithm known as algorithmic stochastic localization (ASL). Our main technical result is a generalized convergence theorem for ASL which reduces approximate sampling from any distribution on the hypercube to estimating the mean of a related "tilted distribution" to sufficiently low additive error. We then extend known quasi-polynomial time algorithms for computing local expectation values of a related disordered model to compute these "tilted means" for the quantum $p$-spin model. Finally, we give a non-rigorous physics argument that this mean-estimation algorithm works down to the spin glass transition temperature. As efficient quantum algorithms also fail at this transition, this suggests they cannot Gibbs sample the quantum $p$-spin model at temperatures below that for which classical algorithms are also (almost) efficient.

</details>


### [194] [Quantum oblique eigenprojection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40254)
*Alexander M. Dalzell,Yuan Su*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子计算机可在给定输入矩阵的块编码访问下，高效实现一般（非正规）方阵的斜本征投影，查询复杂度近线性于逆谱隙，归一化因子接近投影范数，并给出本征态制备、连续时间代数Riccati方程与Sylvester方程的量子加速应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般方阵的谱分解对应非正交的广义本征子空间，其斜本征投影通常是非厄米、非酉的，超出以往仅处理正交（厄米）本征投影的量子算法框架。将量子奇异值/本征值变换从实谱、正规矩阵推广到可对角化或具复特征值的矩阵，是扩展量子线性代数能力的关键缺口。

Method: 以块编码方式访问输入矩阵，通过矩阵预解式的离散傅里叶变换构造双侧块预条件处理（two-sided block preconditioning），从而在量子线路上实现斜本征投影 Π 的块编码。该方法依赖输入满足的谱集条件，覆盖数值范围或可对角化等常见假设；查询复杂度近线性于逆谱隙，归一化因子接近 ‖Π‖。

Result: 在谱集条件下，算法以近线性于 1/gap 的查询复杂度实现斜本征投影，归一化代价接近 ‖Π‖，与已知正交本征投影结果相匹配。应用上：(i) 制备复特征值矩阵的本征态，将 Low–Su 量子本征值变换推广到非实谱；(ii) 求解连续时间代数Riccati方程，相较 Rodenas-Ruiz、Zhao 与 Lee 的先前求解器达到三次加速；(iii) 求解一般Sylvester方程，相较 Wang 与 Liu 的增广直接法获得二次加速。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一般方阵斜本征投影的量子算法，突破了正交投影与实谱的限制，为在量子计算机上应用非解析矩阵函数（如投影、Riccati 与 Sylvester 解算子）提供了可行路径，并给出了多个具有多项式加速的具体应用。

Abstract: Every square matrix decomposes its underlying Hilbert space into generalized, nonorthogonal eigensubspaces. We show that a quantum computer can perform such an oblique eigenprojection $Π$ given block encoding access to the input matrix. Our approach has a query complexity nearly linear in the inverse gap and a normalization factor close to $\lVertΠ\rVert$ under a spectral-set condition on the input. This covers common assumptions on the numerical range or diagonalizability and matches known results for orthogonal eigenprojections. We achieve this with a two-sided block preconditioning that uses a discrete Fourier transform of the matrix resolvent. We describe applications to: (i) preparing eigenstates of matrices with complex eigenvalues, extending the quantum eigenvalue transformation algorithm of Low and Su beyond real spectra; (ii) solving continuous-time algebraic Riccati equations, cubically speeding up a prior solver of Rodenas-Ruiz, Zhao, and Lee; and (iii) solving ordinary Sylvester equations, quadratically improving a direct augmented method of Wang and Liu. Our result suggests a promising route to applying nonanalytic matrix functions on quantum computers.

</details>


### [195] [A General Theory of Multi-Resource Theories Involving Finite-Group Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40255)
*Yosuke Mitsuhashi,Hiroyasu Tajima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了统一理论，研究有限群不对称性与多种量子资源理论组合后的转换速率，核心取决于对称信息的可获取性：可渐近无误获取时组合速率等于两子理论速率较小者，否则出现额外兼容性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实量子场景通常同时施加多种约束，因此需要研究多资源理论；其中与不对称性的组合尤为自然，因为对称性是物理中基础且普遍存在的。

Method: 提出统一框架，将有限群不对称性与广泛资源理论的组合一并处理；关键观测量是底层资源理论下对称信息的可获取性，即转换所需信息能否从输入中提取并用于控制自由操作；并分析对称性施加于基本操作而非仅通道的情形。

Result: 若对称信息可以渐近消失误差提取，则组合理论的转换率等于两构成理论速率的较小者，组合不引入额外代价。该条件对任意混合多体态上的LOCC、Clifford对称下多比特魔态转换、以及Gibbs保持操作均成立。热操作因时间平移协变只能获取有限对称信息，产生两对称性间的额外兼容条件。对LOCC与热操作在适当假设下、以及Pauli算符表示对称性的魔资源建立了类似结果。

Conclusion: 有限群对称性不降低每副本的渐近遍历性，因此不引入新的完全被动态。

Abstract: Resource theories provide a universal framework for quantifying properties of quantum states, such as entanglement, magic, athermality, and asymmetry, as resources under various constraints encountered in quantum science. Since realistic settings typically impose several constraints at once, it is important to study multi-resource theories that combine them. Among these, combinations with asymmetry are especially natural, as symmetry is fundamental and ubiquitous in physics. Here we develop a general theory that treats combinations of finite-group asymmetry with a broad range of resource theories in a unified manner. Our key observation is that the effect of combining the two constraints is governed by the accessibility of symmetry information under the underlying resource theory: whether the information needed for the conversion can be extracted from the input and used to control free operations. If this can be done with asymptotically vanishing error, we show that the conversion rate of the combined theory equals the smaller of the rates of the two constituent theories, so combining them incurs no additional cost. This condition holds for LOCC on arbitrary mixed multipartite states, magic-state conversion of multiqubit systems under Clifford symmetries, and Gibbs-preserving operations. Thermal operations, being time-translation covariant, can access only limited symmetry information, giving an additional compatibility condition between the two symmetries. We also analyze symmetry imposed on elementary operations rather than only on the resulting channel. We establish analogous results for LOCC and thermal operations under suitable assumptions, and for magic when the symmetry is represented by Pauli operators. Finally, we show that finite-group symmetry does not reduce the asymptotic ergotropy per copy and hence introduces no new completely passive states.

</details>


### [196] [Self-testing the toric code against classical communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40259)
*Gustavo Fróes,Amanda Wei,Carlos de Gois,Denis Rochette,Marc-Olivier Renou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在量子网络中，即使不可信设备在有限网络距离内秘密交换经典信息，环面码的码空间仍可被噪声鲁棒地自检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数现有设备无关自检验在各方可交换信息时失效。本文将场景放宽到量子网络：诚实协议中各方不通信，但需在不可信设备秘密进行有界距离经典通信时仍保证认证有效性，从而填补带通信条件下的自检验空白。

Method: 将环面码码空间分布在环面几何拓扑的网络上，构造对该网络上传播的经典通信具有鲁棒性的自检验协议；提出构建此类协议的算法化方法；并将结果推广至表面码与簇态。

Result: 环面码码空间可在该设定下被噪声鲁棒自检验，可抵御与网络直径成比例的通信距离；算法化构造方法有效；结果可扩展至表面码与簇态，并对其鲁棒至一轮最近邻经典通信。

Conclusion: 量子网络中的拓扑码与簇态可实现对抗有界经典通信的设备无关自检验，为带通信鲁棒性认证提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Device-independent self-testing certifies a multipartite quantum state from the correlations it produces alone, without assumptions on the inner workings of the devices. Most existing self-tests, however, break down as soon as the parties can exchange messages. We consider a relaxed setting in a quantum network, where in the honest version of the protocol, the parties never communicate, yet certification remains sound even in an untrusted case where the untrusted devices secretly exchange classical messages within a bounded distance in the network. We show that the code space of the toric code, distributed over a network with the geometry of a torus, can be self-tested in this setting in a noise-robust way, against communication up to distances proportional to the network diameter. We also develop an algorithmic method for constructing such protocols and extend our results to the surface code and cluster states, robust to one round of nearest-neighbor classical communication

</details>


### [197] [Quantum Algorithms for Minimum Generating Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40260)
*Bireswar Das,Udit Kumar,Kavita Samant,Dhara Thakkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出求解可解黑箱群最小生成集的量子多项式时间算法，并针对群类Γ_d设计计算主序列与最小生成集的量子算法，同时证明一般黑箱群最小生成集问题属于NP ∩ coAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱群的最小生成集问题是计算群论中的核心难题，经典方法缺乏多项式时间算法。作者借助量子计算能力，试图在可解群及更广的群类上突破该问题的复杂度瓶颈，并刻画一般情形下的复杂度上界。

Method: 首先给出可解黑箱群最小生成集的量子多项式时间算法；随后定义群类Γ_d（其非交换合成因子同构于固定d的对称群S_d的子群），设计多项式时间量子算法计算交换因子群的直积分解并求解因子群的构造性成员问题；在此基础上设计计算Γ_d黑箱群主序列的量子算法，并利用主序列构造最小生成集算法；最后通过复杂度类归约证明一般黑箱群最小生成集问题属于NP ∩ coAM。

Result: 得到可解黑箱群最小生成集的量子多项式时间算法；实现对Γ_d群交换因子群直积分解与构造性成员问题的多项式时间量子求解；构造出Γ_d黑箱群主序列及最小生成集的量子多项式时间算法；证明一般黑箱群最小生成集问题∈ NP ∩ coAM。

Conclusion: 量子算法可有效求解可解群及Γ_d群的最小生成集问题，主序列是关键工具；一般黑箱群情形虽未给出多项式时间算法，但其判定问题被定位在NP ∩ coAM内，为后续研究提供了复杂度边界。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a polynomial-time quantum algorithm for computing a minimum-sized generating set of solvable black-box groups. Next, we consider the class $Γ_d$ of black-box groups, where every non-abelian composition factor is isomorphic to a subgroup of the symmetric group $S_d$ for a fixed $d$. We design polynomial-time quantum algorithms to compute the direct product decomposition of abelian factor groups and solve the constructive membership problem for factor groups of groups from $Γ_d$. With the help of these algorithms, we design a quantum algorithm for computing a chief series of black-box groups from $Γ_d$. Using the chief series, we construct a polynomial-time quantum algorithm for computing minimum generating sets of black-box groups from $Γ_d$. Finally, we show that the minimum generating set problem for general black-box groups is in $\textrm{NP} \cap \textrm{coAM}$.

</details>


### [198] [Quantum List Recovery and Decoding: Achievability and Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40262)
*Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Xiaojuan Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子列表恢复（QLR）与量子列表译码（QLD）的组合上下界，针对平衡折叠量子Reed-Solomon（FQRS）码与平衡随机CSS码，给出了接近量子Singleton界时列表规模的精确渐近刻画与半径权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLR与QLD需要给出与给定伴随式及误差约束一致的、逻辑上不同的Pauli修正的短列表。对CSS码而言存在两个问题：分别处理X与Z扇区会使输出列表规模相乘放大；而不同的经典候选在稳定子商化后可能坍缩为同一类。因此需要能同时控制列表规模并保持稳定子可区分性的组合界。

Method: 面向平衡FQRS码与平衡随机CSS码建立组合上界与下界。可达性方面，基于Brakensiek、Chen、Dhar、Zhang（STOC 2026）的工作，建立联合X/Z候选列表的配对引理，保持单扇区系数并避免列表规模的乘积损失。QLD逆命题方面，基于经典投影-拼接论证并引入稳定子可区分性，证明量子广义Singleton界。QLR下界方面，改造Chen与Zhang（STOC 2025）的折叠Reed-Solomon构造，使候选保持稳定子可区分。随机CSS码方面，证明经典坏列表以高概率在稳定子商化后仍存活。

Result: 令量子码率R∈(0,1)，公共分量码率R1=(1+R)/2。当半径ρ=(1-R)/2-γ趋近量子Singleton界时，对FQRS码确定性成立、对平衡随机CSS码以高概率成立：L*_QLR=ℓ^{Θ(R1/γ)}，L*_QLD=Θ((1-R)/γ)（γ↓0）。渐近精确的QLD半径权衡为ρ_L*=(L/(L+1))·(1-R)/2。上述结论可推广到平均半径情形。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了平衡FQRS码与平衡随机CSS码在接近量子Singleton界时QLR与QLD列表规模的渐近精确界及QLD半径权衡，并通过配对引理、量子广义Singleton界、改造的折叠Reed-Solomon构造与随机CSS码商化存活性分析，解决了X/Z分扇区乘积放大与稳定子商化坍缩两个问题。

Abstract: Quantum list recovery (QLR) and quantum list decoding (QLD) seek short lists of logically distinct Pauli corrections consistent with a syndrome and prescribed error constraints. For CSS codes, two issues arise: treating the \(X\)- and \(Z\)-sectors separately can multiply their output list sizes, while distinct classical candidates can collapse after stabilizer quotienting.
  We study combinatorial upper and lower bounds for balanced folded quantum Reed--Solomon (FQRS) codes and balanced random CSS codes, both studied by Bergamaschi, Golowich, and Gunn (STOC 24). Let \(R\in(0,1)\) be the quantum rate and \(R_1=(1+R)/2\) the common component rate. As the radius \(ρ=(1-R)/2-γ\) approaches the quantum Singleton bound, we have, deterministically for FQRS codes and w.h.p. for balanced random CSS codes, \[
  L^\star_{\rm QLR}
  =
  \ell^{Θ(R_1/γ)},
  \qquad
  L^\star_{\rm QLD}
  =
  Θ\!\left(\frac{1-R}γ\right)
  \qquad (γ\downarrow0). \] The asymptotically exact QLD radius tradeoff is \[
  ρ_L^\star
  =
  \frac{L}{L+1}\frac{1-R}{2}. \] These conclusions extend to the average-radius setting.
  For achievability, building on the work of Brakensiek, Chen, Dhar, and Zhang (STOC 2026), we establish a pairing lemma for joint \(X/Z\) candidate lists that preserves the one-sector coefficient and avoids a product loss in list size. For the QLD converse, we prove a quantum generalized Singleton bound based on the classical projection-and-patching argument with stabilizer distinctness. For the QLR lower bounds, we adapt the folded Reed--Solomon construction of Chen and Zhang (STOC 2025) so that the candidates remain stabilizer distinct. For random CSS codes, we show classical bad lists survive the stabilizer quotient with high probability.

</details>


### [199] [Superlinear Quantum Query Lower Bounds for Subgraph Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40263)
*Amin Shiraz Gilani,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了子图检测问题在量子查询复杂度上的首个无条件超线性下界，回答了长期未决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子图检测中，H的副本是常数大小证书，非负权重的对手方法无法证明超线性下界，因此需要新的技术来突破这一障碍。

Method: 核心技术结果是：当输入比特独立采样时，从已知族中寻找全1证书的下界。证明结合了Zhandry的压缩oracle与随机植入证书的条件化，改进了Belovs的论证。困难实例由图构造，包含大量目标子图副本且重叠受限；团使用Gowers-Janzer构造，完全二部图使用随机构造。

Result: 对每个固定r≥4，检测K_r需n^{λ_r-o(1)}次查询，λ_4=19/18且随r严格递增，λ_r≥2-4√(2/r)+O(1/r)。对每个色数c≥4的固定连通图H均给出超线性下界，c大时趋于二次：n^{2-O(√(loglog c/c))-o(1)}。检测K_{r,r}需n^{β_r-o(1)}，β_10=181/180，β_r≥2-O(1/√r)。

Conclusion: 色数本身不能刻画子图检测的量子查询复杂度，因为K_{r,r}的表现不同。该工作首次给出此类问题的无条件超线性下界，并推动了相关量子查询复杂度的理解。

Abstract: Subgraph detection asks whether an $n$-vertex graph, accessed through queries to its adjacency matrix, contains a copy of a fixed graph $H$. We prove the first unconditional superlinear lower bounds on the bounded-error quantum query complexity of this problem, answering a longstanding open question. A copy of $H$ is a certificate of constant size, so the adversary method with nonnegative weights cannot prove superlinear lower bounds.
  For every fixed $r\ge 4$, detecting the clique $K_r$ requires $n^{λ_r-o(1)}$ queries, where $λ_4=19/18$, the exponents $λ_r$ increase strictly with $r$, and $λ_r\ge 2-4\sqrt{2/r}+O(1/r)$. More generally, we prove superlinear lower bounds for detecting every fixed connected graph $H$ with chromatic number $c\ge 4$. These bounds approach quadratic as $c$ grows: for sufficiently large $c$, detection requires $n^{2-O(\sqrt{\log\log c/c})-o(1)}$ queries. Chromatic number alone does not characterize the quantum query complexity of subgraph detection: we show that detecting the complete bipartite graph $K_{r,r}$ requires $n^{β_r-o(1)}$ queries, where $β_{10}=181/180$ and $β_r\ge 2-O(1/\sqrt{r})$.
  Our main technical result is a lower bound for finding an all-ones certificate from a known family when the input bits are sampled independently. Its proof combines Zhandry's compressed oracle (CRYPTO 2019) with conditioning on a randomly planted certificate, adapting an argument of Belovs (FOCS 2026). Our hard instances are built from graphs containing many copies of the desired subgraph with limited overlap. For cliques, we use a construction of Gowers and Janzer (CPC 2021); for complete bipartite graphs, we use a random construction.

</details>


### [200] [Two-Stage Quantum-Classical Distribution Network Reconfiguration via Cycle-Edge Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40264)
*Nowar Alashkar,Cade Kennedy,Phillip C. Lotshaw,Shaked Regev,Miguel Angel Lopez-Ruiz,Ananth Kaushik,Paul Smith,Claudio Girotto,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段量子-经典配电网重构方法：先用带周期-边编码的LR-QAOA搜索拓扑可行子空间，再以所得采样分布引导MISOCP求解器，并在33至417节点的六个网络上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网重构（DNR）是受拓扑与电气运行约束的组合优化问题，目标为寻找低损网络拓扑，传统求解方法在规模增大时效率受限，需要借助量子-经典混合手段缩小搜索空间并加速求解。

Method: 第一阶段采用迭代线性斜坡量子交替算子拟设（LR-QAOA），在基于新型周期-边编码构造的拓扑可行子空间中搜索；该编码保证每个被表示的配置均为生成树，且子空间大小与覆盖范围可依可用量子比特预算调节。第二阶段用第一阶段得到的采样分布引导混合整数二阶锥规划（MISOCP）求解器。

Result: 在33至417母线的六个配电网系统上，通过仿真及在囚禁离子量子硬件上执行进行评估；在所有六个测试系统中，由硬件所得分布引导的MISOCP求解器均能在中位求解时间内获得与已知最优解差距在1%以内的可行解，且中位求解时间少于无引导的对应求解器。

Conclusion: 该两阶段量子-经典框架结合周期-边编码与分布引导，可在受量子比特预算限制的条件下有效缩小DNR搜索空间，并在多规模网络上稳定提升MISOCP求解效率与解质量。

Abstract: Distribution network reconfiguration (DNR) is a combinatorial optimization problem that seeks a low-loss network topology subject to topological and electrical operating constraints. In this work, we propose a two-stage quantum-classical method for DNR. First, an iterative linear-ramp Quantum Alternating Operator Ansatz (LR-QAOA) searches topologically feasible subspaces constructed using a novel cycle-edge encoding. The encoding guarantees that every represented configuration is a spanning tree while allowing the size and coverage of each subspace to be adjusted to the available qubit budget. Second, the resulting sample distribution is used to guide a mixed-integer second-order-cone programming (MISOCP) solver. The method is evaluated on six distribution networks ranging from 33 to 417 buses through simulation and execution on trapped-ion quantum hardware. Across all six test systems, the MISOCP solver guided by hardware-derived distributions reaches an incumbent within 1\% of the best-known solution in less median solver time than the corresponding unguided solver.

</details>


### [201] [Compatibility of quantum instruments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40266)
*Chloe Kim,Yujie Zhang,Eric Chitambar,Marius Junge,Felix Leditzky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将测量兼容性推广为 $d$-兼容性，用算子空间/算子系统的公共线性分解刻画，定义兼容性泛函以证明 $d$-不兼容性，并应用于信道、仪器、Choi 集合及记忆受限制备博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典测量兼容性要求一族量子测量可由单一父测量加经典后处理模拟。本文希望允许父测量传递有限维量子系统（维度 $d$）及无限经典信息，从而刻画更强的资源受限兼容性，并用于认证 $d$-不兼容性与相关资源。

Method: 将 $d$-兼容性形式化为通过 $d$ 维寄存器的算子空间与算子系统的公共线性分解；证明 $d$-兼容族在无限设置与可分离无限维输入下仍为闭集；定义兼容性泛函为公共分解中完全有界范数乘积的最小值；使用算子空间分解理论分析 measure-and-prepare 信道与 pinching 信道；通过 channel-state duality 联系 multi-instruments 的 $d$-兼容性与 Choi assemblages 的可制备性。

Result: $d$-兼容族构成闭集；基于互无偏基的 measure-and-prepare 信道和基于 Heisenberg-Weyl 算子的 pinching 信道在某些 $d$ 范围内是 $d$-不兼容的；多仪器 $d$-兼容性可联系 Choi assemblage 可制备性，并可认证二分态的 Schmidt 数及仪器的一向 LOCC 可实现性；兼容性泛函给出兼容性广义鲁棒性的上界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了测量、信道与仪器 $d$-兼容性的统一算子空间框架，提供 $d$-不兼容性认证工具，并揭示其与 Choi 集合、Schmidt 数、LOCC 可实现性及记忆受限非瞬态制备博弈的联系。

Abstract: Measurement compatibility asks whether a family of quantum measurements can be simulated by one parent measurement followed by classical post-processing.
