We extend this measurement compatibility to the notion of \emph{$d$-compatibility} for measurements, channels, and instruments whose parent passes a $d$-dimensional quantum system and unlimited classical information to the post-processing. We formulate $d$-compatibility as a common linear factorization of operator spaces and operator systems and show that $d$-compatible families form a closed set, even with infinitely many settings and a separable infinite-dimensional input. To certify $d$-incompatibility, we define the compatibility functional as the smallest product of completely bounded norms over common factorizations through a $d$-dimensional register. Using factorization theory of operator spaces, we show that measure-and-prepare channels defined in terms of mutually unbiased bases and pinching channels defined in terms of Heisenberg-Weyl operators are $d$-incompatible in certain regimes of $d$. We also link $d$-compatibility of multi-instruments to the preparability of Choi assemblages using channel-state duality, and use this connection to show that for bipartite states the (in)compatibility of instruments can be used to certify Schmidt numbers and the implementability of the instrument using one-way LOCC protocols. Finally, our compatibility functional also bounds the generalized robustness of compatibility, which we interpret through a memory-bounded nontransient preparation game.

</details>


### [202] [Testing quantum Gaussianity with constant sample complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40270)
*Mahtab Yaghubi Rad,Ricard Puig,Saksham Hassanandani,Luke Coffman,Jens Eisert,Carlos Bravo-Prieto,Antonio Anna Mele*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文给出了费米子与玻色子高斯态的最优且鲁棒的检验算法，样本复杂度与系统尺寸、粒子数、能量均无关，并提出统一的"梯度流方法"作为分析核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯态是量子光学与多体物理中的核心结构化态，也定义了高效的经典模拟范式，但检验量子态是否具有高斯性的最优算法此前仍未知。

Method: 提出统一的梯度流方法（gradient-flow method）：利用表示论将定义对称性的近似满足转化为对拒绝概率梯度的定量控制，从而实现从输入态到目标族（高斯态）的连续下降，下降路径长度可界定其距离。协议采用实验可行的测量：费米子使用两副本上的Bell采样，玻色子使用三副本上的被动干涉测量与光子计数；容错检验器需在更多但仍是常数个副本上进行联合测量。

Result: 获得费米子与玻色子高斯态的最优且鲁棒检验算法，样本复杂度与系统尺寸、粒子数、能量无关；协议仅需实验可行的测量；并进一步给出容错检验器，所需副本数为更大但仍为常数。

Conclusion: 梯度流方法适用于本文考虑的所有结构化族，构成最优检验器的分析基础，并对高斯态检验问题给出了最优复杂度刻画。

Abstract: Efficiently testing whether a quantum state possesses a given structure is both a fundamental and a practical task in quantum information. Among the most important structured families are Gaussian states, which underpin quantum optics and many-body physics while defining paradigmatic regimes of efficient classical simulation. Despite the central role of Gaussian states, optimal tests of Gaussianity have remained unknown. Here we give optimal and robust algorithms for testing fermionic and bosonic Gaussian states, with sample complexity independent of system size, particle number, and energy. Remarkably, the protocols implement experimentally feasible measurements associated with known exact symmetry characterizations of Gaussianity, using Bell sampling on two copies for fermions and passive interferometry with photon counting on three copies for bosons. We further obtain tolerant testers requiring joint measurements on a larger, but still constant, number of copies. At the heart of our analysis is a unified approach---which we call the gradient-flow method---that applies across all structured families considered here and underlies our optimal testers: representation theory turns approximate satisfaction of the defining symmetry into quantitative control of the gradient of the rejection probability, allowing a continuous descent from the input state to the target family whose length bounds their distance.

</details>


### [203] [Refined sample complexities from the tomographic rate function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40271)
*Arick Grootveld,Alexander Maloney,Jason Pollack,Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了协变量子态层析协议的速率函数分析框架，比较不同协议在大偏差意义下的性能，证明速率函数比样本复杂度更能细粒度区分协议优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析协议通常以样本复杂度衡量，但样本复杂度可能无法区分性能不同的协议。作者希望用控制误差估计大偏差行为的速率函数来更精细地刻画协议性能，并比较这两种度量。

Method: 建立协变协议的分析框架，推导多种协议（包括随机纯化协议与Haah等人的样本最优协议）的速率函数。这些速率函数用一类新的散度表示，涵盖反向夹层Rényi散度和Keyl退火量子相对熵作为特例。进一步引入基于Wasserstein距离而非迹距离的样本复杂度变体，并将速率函数排序与其联系起来。

Result: 推导出多个协议的速率函数；发现尽管各协议样本复杂度均为阶最优，其速率函数仍满足严格排序；基于Wasserstein距离的样本复杂度也呈现相应的严格不等式。

Conclusion: 层析速率函数是比通常样本复杂度更细粒度的协议性能指标，可作为协变量子态层析协议比较与设计的有力工具。

Abstract: Protocols for quantum state tomography can be characterized either by their sample complexity or by the tomographic rate function that governs the large deviation behaviour of error estimates. We establish a framework for the analysis of covariant protocols which allows us to compare these measures. First, we derive the rate functions for several protocols, including random purification-based protocols and the sample-optimal protocol of Haah et al. These rate functions are expressed in terms of a new family of divergences which includes the reverse sandwiched Rényi divergence and Keyl's annealed quantum relative entropy as special cases. We show that these rate functions obey a strict ordering, even though the sample complexity of each protocol is order optimal. We consider a different version of sample complexity based on Wasserstein distance rather than trace distance, and show that the ordering of the rate functions implies analogous strict inequalities for the Wasserstein-type sample complexity. Thus the tomographic rate function is a more fine-grained indicator of the performance of a tomography protocol than the usual sample complexity.

</details>


### [204] [Conditioning-Free Non-Uniform Quantum Fourier and Chebyshev Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40277)
*Chaowen Guan,Akshit Katiyar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的非均匀Chebyshev变换量子算法，基于去除节点采样条件化的改进NUQFT，实现O(1)归一化、O(L)量子比特与Õ(L²)门的ε精度块编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于QFT的量子Chebyshev变换无法处理在x∈[-1,1]上均匀、而在角度θ=arccos x上非均匀的采样节点，因此需要专门的非均匀Chebyshev变换算法。此外，先前工作的误差界依赖几何相关参数κ，且行访问预言机被假设而非给出构造。

Method: 改进现有非均匀量子Fourier变换（NUQFT），消除非均匀节点采样中的条件化，使误差界不再依赖几何参数κ。利用Chebyshev变换矩阵是两个Type-II非均匀DFT的平均这一事实，用单个受控NUQFT电路实现。给出显式预言机构造，包括此前仅作假设的行访问预言机。

Result: 得到ε精度的块编码，归一化为O(1)，使用O(L)个量子比特和Õ(L²)个门，其中L=log N+log(1/ε)。提供端到端实现及复杂度分析，涵盖成功概率与输出态误差。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了非均匀Chebyshev变换的高效量子算法，去除了对几何参数的依赖并补齐了预言机构造，为相关量子数值算法提供了完整的复杂度保障。

Abstract: We present an efficient quantum algorithm for the non-uniform Chebyshev transform. It is defined as the projection of a function onto Chebyshev polynomials sampled at given nodes that are uniform in $x\in[-1,1]$, and hence non-uniform in the angle $θ=\arccos x$, a setting that QFT-based quantum Chebyshev transforms cannot handle. Our construction is based on an improvement of an existing Non-uniform Quantum Fourier Transform (NUQFT) whereby we remove the conditioning from non-uniform node sampling. Hence, error bounds are independent of the geometry-dependent parameter $κ$ of prior work. We use the fact that Chebyshev transform matrix is the average of two Type-II non-uniform DFTs, which we implement with a single controlled NUQFT circuit. We provide explicit oracle constructions, including the row-access oracle previously left as an assumption. The resulting $\varepsilon$-accurate block encoding has $O(1)$ normalization and uses $O(L)$ qubits and $\widetilde O(L^2)$ gates, where $L=\log N+\log(1/\varepsilon)$. We give an end-to-end implementation with complexity analysis, including the success probability and output-state error.

</details>


### [205] [Computational Bounds for $f$-Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40278)
*Oren Renard,Nicholas Spooner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $f$-routing 协议针对一致攻击者的无条件资源下界：若能以少量量子比特和简短描述高概率成功，则函数 $f$ 必属于特定受限复杂度类（QSZK(poly(nq))、DTIME(poly(nqs))、DTIME(poly(nq2^t))），由此给出在相应计算限制下可抵御多项式乃至拟多项式量子比特的显式安全函数，突破了先前 $q\le\log n$ 的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $f$-routing 是量子位置验证（QPV）的主要候选协议，但针对显式函数的安全性保证仍然有限。先前工作的核心技术依赖通信复杂度下界，而这类下界本质上最多随输入长度线性增长，形成了难以逾越的障碍。

Method: 绕过以往工作所依赖的通信复杂度下界，转而采用新的技术手段，对一致生成的攻击策略建立无条件资源下界。具体地，考虑输入长度 $n$、足够小的常数 $ε>0$，假设存在使用 $q$ 个量子比特、描述长度为 $\mathrm{poly}(q)$、且对每个输入成功概率至少 $1-ε$ 的一致生成策略，据此分别推导函数的计算复杂性上限：(1) 策略为任意量子信道；(2) 策略为显式 Pauli-sparse 酉算子；(3) 策略为 Clifford+T 电路。

Result: 证明了三条计算界：(1) 任意量子信道策略下 $f\in\mathrm{QSZK}(\mathrm{poly}(nq))$；(2) 至多 $s$ 个非零 Pauli 系数的显式 Pauli-sparse 酉算子下 $f\in\mathrm{DTIME}(\mathrm{poly}(nqs))$；(3) 至多 $t$ 个 magic 门的 Clifford+T 电路下 $f\in\mathrm{DTIME}(\mathrm{poly}(nq2^t))$。结合时间与空间层级定理，得到在多/拟多项式量子比特下、于相应计算限制内安全的显式函数。这些界超越了 Bluhm、Christandl 和 Speelman（2022）对內积函数 $f=\mathrm{IP}$ 给出的 $q\le\log n$ 界，代价是限制对抗方计算能力并提高诚实评估复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文在不依赖通信复杂度下界的新框架下，为 $f$-routing 协议建立了针对一致攻击者的无条件资源下界，将其转化为对函数 $f$ 在受限计算模型下的复杂度类约束。由此可在多项式乃至拟多项式量子比特规模下构造安全显式函数，拓展了此前 $q\le\log n$ 的结果；但这一提升以限制对抗方计算、增加诚实评估复杂度为代价。

Abstract: The $f$-routing protocol is a leading candidate for quantum position verification (Kent, Munro, and Spiller, 2011), but security guarantees for explicit functions remain limited. We prove unconditional resource lower bounds for uniform attackers; our new techniques bypass communication-complexity bounds central to previous works, which are inherently at most linear in the input length.
  We show that, for input length $n$ and sufficiently small constant $ε>0$, a uniformly generated strategy using $q$ qubits and having description length $\mathrm{poly}(q)$, with success probability at least $1-ε$ on every input, implies the following computational bounds on $f$:
  1. If the strategies are arbitrary quantum channels, then $f\in\mathrm{QSZK}(\mathrm{poly}(nq))$, where $\mathrm{QSZK}(T)$ is the class of languages having quantum statistical zero knowledge proofs in which the verifier runs in time $T$ (and the simulator in time $\mathrm{poly}(T)$).
  2. If the strategies are explicit Pauli-sparse unitaries on $q$ qubits that have at most $s$ nonzero Pauli coefficients, then $f\in\mathrm{DTIME}(\mathrm{poly}(nqs))$.
  3. If the strategies are Clifford+T circuits using at most $t$ magic gates, then $f\in\mathrm{DTIME}(\mathrm{poly}(nq2^t))$.
  Time and space hierarchies then yield explicit functions secure against polynomial and even quasipolynomial qubits $q$ under our computational restrictions. These bounds exceed the $q\le\log n$ bound of Bluhm, Christandl, and Speelman (2022) for inner product function $f=\mathrm{IP}$, at the cost of restricting adversarial computation and increasing honest evaluation complexity.

</details>


### [206] [Classical simulation of coherent crosstalk in surface codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40279)
*Andrew S. Darmawan,Yelyzaveta Kolesnyk,Robert Koenig*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了表面码在相干噪声下的经典可模拟性：给出针对相干近邻ZZ串扰的syndrome分布采样的多项式时间经典算法，但证明当单比特相干噪声与相干串扰同时存在时，不存在高效经典采样算法（除非多项式层级坍塌）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干噪声是量子纠错中实际存在且可能破坏容错阈值的噪声类型，而已有工作仅对表面码中的相干单比特错误给出高效模拟算法，对相干串扰的可模拟性尚不清楚，因此需要界定表面码相干噪声经典可模拟与不可模拟的边界。

Method: 对相干近邻ZZ串扰情形，设计多项式时间经典算法，从被串扰污染的surface code态的syndrome分布中采样；对单比特相干噪声与相干串扰同时存在的情形，将相干噪声下的syndrome分布与一个非平面图相关的IQP电路输出分布建立联系，由此推出高效经典采样的不存在性。

Result: 针对相干近邻ZZ串扰，得到多项式时间经典采样算法，补充了单比特相干错误的高效模拟结果；在两种相干噪声同时存在时，证明除非多项式层级坍塌，否则不存在高效经典算法可从syndrome分布中采样，即使允许常数乘性误差。

Conclusion: 表面码中相干单比特噪声与相干ZZ串扰各自可高效经典模拟，但二者结合会产生计算复杂度上的障碍，该障碍通过syndrome分布与非平面图关联IQP电路输出分布的联系来刻画。

Abstract: We give a polynomial-time classical algorithm which samples from the syndrome distribution of a surface code state corrupted by coherent nearest-neighbor $ZZ$ crosstalk. Our algorithm complements the known efficient simulation algorithms for coherent single-qubit errors in the surface code. When both single-qubit coherent noise and coherent crosstalk are present, we find a complexity-theoretic obstruction to efficient simulation: unless the polynomial hierarchy collapses, there is no efficient classical algorithm for sampling from the syndrome distribution, even up to a constant multiplicative error. This is obtained by connecting the syndrome distribution under coherent noise to the output distribution of certain IQP circuits associated with a non-planar graph.

</details>


### [207] [Memory dimension detection in open quantum dynamics via pseudo-control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40281)
*Chang Liu,Alexander Yosifov,Ximing Wang,Zhenhuan Liu,Jinzhao Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种伪控制协议，将记忆维度编码于可实验获取的干涉矩阵中，通过其秩认证记忆维度的下界，无需直接访问环境或完整过程张量层析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画开放量子动力学需识别塑造系统演化的环境自由度；记忆维度（复现环境影响所需最小有效记忆的大小）可量化动态相关的环境资源，并为比较不同多体环境提供统一基准，但现有方法难以在实验中高效获取。

Method: 引入伪控制协议（pseudo-control protocol），将记忆维度信息编码进一个可实验访问的干涉矩阵；通过该矩阵的秩认证记忆维度的下界，避免直接访问环境或进行完整的过程张量层析。将该协议应用于量子多体环境，并可灵活调节相互作用时间与间隔时间。

Result: 干涉矩阵的秩可给出记忆维度的经认证下界；应用于多体环境时，通过调节相互作用与间隔时间，揭示了这些参数、环境动力学以及系统—环境相互作用如何共同塑造可探测的记忆。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种刻画开放量子动力学的新操作性技术，并可通过记忆特征探测系统—环境相互作用的结构。

Abstract: Characterising open quantum dynamics requires identifying the environmental degrees of freedom that shape the system's evolution. The memory dimension, defined as the size of the smallest effective memory needed to reproduce this influence, quantifies the dynamically relevant environmental resources and provides a common basis for comparing different many-body environments. Here we introduce a pseudo-control protocol that encodes information about the memory dimension in an experimentally accessible interference matrix, whose rank certifies a lower bound on this dimension without direct access to the environment or full process-tensor tomography. Applied to quantum many-body environments, the protocol allows flexible adjustment of the interaction and interval times, revealing how these parameters, environmental dynamics, and system-environment interactions shape detectable memory. Our approach provides a new operational technique for characterising open quantum dynamics and probing the structure of system-environment interactions through their memory signatures.

</details>


### [208] [Distinguishing Coherent Crosstalk from Calibration Drift via Pauli-Transfer Signatures and Quantum Edge Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40283)
*Syed Emad Uddin Shubha,Tasnuva Farheen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于残差Pauli转移矩阵（PTM）的结构化验证与检测框架，用于识别多租户量子处理器中脉冲级相干串扰；证明局部信道不混合权重、相互作用方向局部可观测，并给出反称交叉权重检测器及结构对抗策略，仿真验证检测阈值与M^{-1/2}标定规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多租户量子处理器在脉冲层面暴露攻击面，其中相干串扰在平均门失真度匹配时可伪装成良性校准漂移，常规指标难以区分，因此需要一种结构性验证与检测方法。

Method: 以残差Pauli转移矩阵（PTM）为核心：证明局部unital保迹信道乘积在任意强度/轴相干漂移下不能混合权重-1与权重-2 Pauli算符；借助PTM-哈密顿量关系，证明任意大系统中指定量子比特对的九个相互作用系数到反称交叉权重特征的一阶映射在尺度意义下为等距；针对校准小局部旋转，采用共享随机Pauli测量与反称检测器进行周期性验证；并分析大局部旋转下的特征抵消与交叉权重范数在局部酉复合下的不变性。

Result: 仿真中16,384个随机构型在5%校准假阳性目标下给出λ_min≈0.13的检测阈值，且随设置数M呈约M^{-1/2}缩放；实验进一步刻画了弛豫、去极化、原生ZZ涨落与时间依赖脉冲动力学下的检测表现；FRQI编码配合Pauli-graph量子Hadamard边缘检测以数值精度复现经典结构边缘分数。

Conclusion: 残差PTM交叉权重特征可作为多租户量子处理器脉冲级相干串扰的结构化、局部可观测检测量，兼具理论保证与随机化测量可实现性，并为局部酉复合提供结构性对抗思路。

Abstract: Multi-tenant quantum processors expose a pulse-level attack surface in which coherent crosstalk can mimic benign calibration drift at matched average gate infidelity. We present a structural verification and detection framework based on the residual Pauli transfer matrix (PTM). We prove that products of local unital, trace-preserving channels cannot mix weight-1 and weight-2 Pauli operators, even under coherent drift of arbitrary strength or axis. Building on established PTM-to-Hamiltonian relations, we prove that, for any specified qubit pair in an arbitrarily large system, the first-order map from its nine interaction coefficients to the antisymmetric cross-weight feature is an isometry up to scale. Thus, every interaction direction is locally observable near the identity. We also exhibit cancellation of this feature under large local rotations and prove that the full cross-weight norm is invariant under local unitary composition, providing a structural countermeasure. For calibrated small local rotations, shared randomized-Pauli measurements support periodic verification using the evaluated antisymmetric detector. In simulation, $16{,}384$ randomized settings yield a detection threshold of $λ_{\min}\approx0.13$ at a $5\%$ calibrated false-positive target, with approximately $M^{-1/2}$ scaling in the number of settings $M$. Further experiments characterize detection under relaxation, depolarization, native ZZ fluctuations, and time-dependent pulse dynamics. Finally, an FRQI encoding with Pauli-graph quantum Hadamard edge detection reproduces the classical structural edge score to numerical precision.

</details>


### [209] [Verifiable Quantum Advantage and Computation via Quantum Circuit Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40289)
*Alexandru Gheorghiu,Aparna Gupte,Vojtěch Havlíček,Yunchao Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于量子不可区分混淆(qiO)，构建了可经典验证的量子优势协议及 BQP 计算的经典验证协议；结果在仅对无辅助比特幺正电路存在 qiO 时亦成立，并证明了相应的最坏到平均归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为启发式的量子优势方案（如Aaronson-Zhang的峰值随机电路采样）提供严格的密码学基础，并探索经典验证BQP计算这一难题，同时寻找不依赖额外计算硬度假设的(量子)iO非平凡密码学应用。

Method: 以量子不可区分混淆(qiO)为核心工具，假设BQP≠BPP的稍强版本：(1)构造两消息、高效且公开可验证的量子优势协议；(2)构造两类经典验证任意BQP计算的协议——私有可验证（仅需qiO）与公开可验证（额外假设后量子单向函数）；(3)将qiO假设弱化到仅无辅助比特幺正电路，并证明其最坏到平均情形归约，扩展Canetti等(TCC 2024)的局部混合框架至量子类比假设。

Result: 1) 在qiO与稍强BQP≠BPP假设下得到两消息、高效公开可验证的量子优势协议，为峰值随机电路采样方案提供严格密码学基础；2) 私有可验证BQP经典验证协议仅依赖qiO，是(量子)iO少见的不附加计算硬度假设的非平凡应用；3) 公开可验证协议额外需后量子单向函数，据称是标准模型下基于计算假设的首个公开可验证BQP经典验证协议；4) 所有结果在仅对无辅助比特幺正电路假设qiO时仍成立，并证明该情形下的最坏到平均归约。

Conclusion: qiO可作为构建可经典验证量子优势与BQP经典验证协议的通用密码学基础，且相关假设可弱化至无辅助比特幺正电路并通过最坏到平均归约获得支持，拓展了量子局部混合框架的应用范围。

Abstract: We construct protocols for classically verifiable quantum advantage and classical verification of $\mathsf{BQP}$ computations using \emph{quantum indistinguishability obfuscation} (qiO). Specifically, given qiO and assuming a slightly stronger version of $\mathsf{BQP}\neq\mathsf{BPP}$, we construct a two-message quantum-advantage protocol that is efficiently and publicly verifiable. Our result can be viewed as a rigorous cryptographic foundation for the heuristic quantum advantage proposals based on \emph{peaked random circuit sampling} of Aaronson and Zhang (arXiv:2404.14493).
  We also construct two simple protocols for classically verifying arbitrary $\mathsf{BQP}$ computations. The first protocol is privately verifiable and assumes only the existence of qiO. This gives a rare example of a nontrivial cryptographic application of (quantum) iO that does not make additional computational hardness assumptions. The second protocol additionally assumes post-quantum one-way functions and is \emph{publicly verifiable}. To our knowledge, this is the first publicly verifiable protocol for classical verification of $\mathsf{BQP}$ computations under computational assumptions in the standard model.
  We show that all our results hold when qiO is assumed only for ancilla-free unitary circuits. As evidence supporting this assumption, we prove a worst-to-average-case reduction for obfuscating such circuits. This reduction extends the local-mixing framework of Canetti, Chamon, Mucciolo and Ruckenstein (TCC 2024) under quantum analogues of their assumptions.

</details>


### [210] [The power of constant-depth quantum circuits of unbounded size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40294)
*Sergii Strelchuk,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian,Máté Weisz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无界扇入门量子电路能否在常数深度内实现任意酉操作，给出了置换、对角酉和任意纯态准备的精确常数深度构造，并将任意酉实现归约到无迹对合酉，最后用端口基隐形传态给出深度 O(√d) 的近似方案，但任意酉的常数深度实现仍开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典无界扇入电路在规模不受限时可用常数深度计算任意布尔函数。作者希望探究量子情形：若同时取消电路规模和辅助量子比特的限制，由任意单量子比特门和广义 Toffoli 门构成的量子电路是否也能在常数深度内实现任意酉操作。

Method: 作者给出计算基态任意置换、对角酉和任意纯态准备的精确常数深度构造，将其与可逆经典计算及概率分布准备相联系；在门集中含 fanout 时使用指数级门和辅助比特并全部归零，用精确电路替代 fanout 后保持常数深度但规模可能变为双指数。对任意酉实现给出等价刻画（复制指定正交基向量、提取标签、实现受限酉族），并通过额外一个干净量子比特将任意酉归约到无迹对合酉。采用端口基隐形传态，在输入维度 d、端口数 M≥d²−1 时构造深度 O(√d) 的酉电路。

Result: 成功构造了置换、对角酉和任意纯态准备的精确常数深度电路；将任意酉实现归约为无迹对合酉；端口基隐形传态方案深度为 O(√d)，独立于 M，含资源准备与端口选择，纠缠保真度至少为 (1−(d²−1)/(2M))²，故固定 d 时可在不增加深度的情况下任意逼近。带自适应测量时，门隐形传态深度与门在 Clifford 层级中的级别成正比。

Conclusion: 任意酉操作的常数深度实现仍为开放问题；能否消除端口基隐形传态中对 d 的依赖也仍待解决。

Abstract: Classical circuits with unbounded fan-in can compute any Boolean function in constant depth when their size is unrestricted. We ask whether removing the restrictions on circuit size and ancillary qubits also allows quantum circuits built from arbitrary single-qubit gates and generalised Toffoli gates to implement every unitary in constant depth.
  We give exact constant-depth constructions for arbitrary permutations of computational basis states, diagonal unitaries and the preparation of arbitrary pure states. These connect quantum state preparation to reversible classical computation and the preparation of probability distributions. With fanout in the gate set, they use exponentially many gates and ancillary qubits and return all ancillary qubits to zero. Replacing fanout by an exact circuit over the original gate set preserves constant depth, although the size bounds can become doubly exponential.
  The implementation of arbitrary unitaries in constant depth remains open. We give equivalent formulations in terms of copying the vectors of a specified orthonormal basis, extracting their labels and implementing restricted families of unitaries. We also reduce arbitrary unitary implementation to that of traceless unitary involutions using one additional clean qubit. With adaptive measurements, gate teleportation gives depth proportional to the level of a gate in the Clifford hierarchy.
  Towards arbitrary unitary implementation in constant depth, we use port-based teleportation: for input dimension $d$ and $M\geq d^2-1$ ports, we construct a unitary circuit of depth $O(\sqrt d)$, independent of $M$ and including resource preparation and port selection, with entanglement fidelity at least $(1-(d^2-1)/(2M))^2$. Thus, at fixed $d$, the approximation can be made arbitrarily accurate without increasing depth. Whether the dependence on $d$ can also be removed remains open.

</details>


### [211] [Need for Coherent Access in Constructing Quantum Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40301)
*Minki Hhan,Changhun Oh,Vaughn Sohn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造经典可访问的随机预言机，证明了量子安全单向函数存在但超对数输出长度的伪随机态不存在，揭示了构造PRS需要相干访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有黑盒构造表明量子安全单向函数（OWF）可以构造伪随机态（PRS）生成器，这似乎与本文的分离结果矛盾。作者希望厘清这一表面矛盾，并探究访问模型（经典访问 vs 相干访问）在PRS构造中的根本作用。

Method: 构造量子预言机：使用经典可访问的随机预言机（即使量子算法也只能经典访问），同时保持另一预言机分量不变。在此模型下证明量子安全OWF存在，但超对数输出长度的PRS不存在；并证明该PRS不可能性适用于任何替换随机预言的经典可访问经典预言机。进一步证明在对数输出长度下PRFS存在。

Result: 相对于所构造的预言机：量子安全OWF存在，但超对数输出长度的PRS不存在；而经典可访问的对数长度PRFS存在。由此得到经典可访问对数长度PRFS与超对数长度PRS之间的预言机分离。

Conclusion: PRS构造中对预言机的相干访问是必要的；从对数到超对数输出长度的完全黑盒PRS长度扩展必须使用对底层短PRS的相干访问。表面的矛盾源于访问模型差异，而非黑盒构造本身的问题。

Abstract: We construct quantum oracles relative to which quantum-secure one-way functions (OWFs) exist but pseudorandom states (PRSs) with superlogarithmic output length do not. At first glance, this appears to contradict the known black-box constructions of PRS generators from quantum-secure OWFs. The distinction lies in the access model to the oracles; our oracle separation uses \emph{classical-accessible} random oracles that can be accessed only classically even by quantum algorithms. In fact, our impossibility of PRSs applies to \emph{any} classically accessible classical oracle in place of the random oracle, while keeping the other oracle component unchanged, showing the need for coherent access in constructing PRSs.
  We further show that logarithmic output length pseudorandom function-like states (PRFSs) exist relative to our oracles, giving an oracle separation between classically accessible logarithmic length PRFSs and superlogarithmic length PRSs. This shows that fully black-box PRS length extension from logarithmic to superlogarithmic output length must use coherent access to the underlying short PRS.

</details>


### [212] [Solving Sparse SDPs in Sublinear Time: A Classical Algorithm Inspired by the Quantum OR Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40302)
*Fernando G. S. L. Brandão,Alexander M. Dalzell,András Gilyén,Francisca Vasconcelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出首个稀疏半定规划（SDP）在有界半径区域下的次线性时间经典求解器，不依赖低秩假设或约束矩阵的Frobenius范数依赖。核心是通过随机Lánczos滤波与基于采样的估计器，经典地实现量子OR引理中的样本复用机制，并对大量Gibbs态期望值进行同步估计，同时提出随机在线学习框架改进精度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brandão等（2019）与van Apeldoorn和Gilyén（2019）的量子算法在常数精度、有界原始/对偶半径下达到对维度n和约束数m的O(√n+√m)依赖，相较经典O(mn)存在四次量子加速。该加速不仅来自Grover搜索，更依赖量子OR引理的样本复用机制，将Gibbs态制备成本与约束搜索解耦。作者旨在探究该复用机制是否可以在经典上实现。

Method: 1) 提出经典过程：结合随机Lánczos滤波与高效采样估计器，同步估计稀疏Hamiltonian的Gibbs态下多个期望值；2) 引入随机在线学习框架用于SDP求解，相比标准基于oracle的MMWU方法显著改善精度依赖；3) 用s表示输入矩阵稀疏度，γ:=Rr/ε刻画原始半径R、对偶半径r与目标精度ε的依赖。

Result: 当γ²≤min{m, n/s}时，求解器运行时间为Õ(nsγ^{4.5}+msγ²)；当γ=O(1)时为Õ((n+m)s)，对O(mns)输入规模次线性。与量子算法类似，结果在m和n上匹配已知下界（至对数因子）。这意味着就维度m和n而言，通用稀疏SDP求解不存在超二次量子优势。

Conclusion: 量子OR引理的样本复用机制可经典实现，从而给出稀疏SDP的首个次线性经典算法，并在有界半径、常数精度下匹配已知下界。该结果表明，通用稀疏SDP求解在维度上不具备超二次量子优势，量子加速的边界被重新界定。

Abstract: We give the first sublinear-time classical solvers for sparse semidefinite programs in the bounded-radius regime, without low-rank assumptions or Frobenius norm dependence on the constraint matrices. For constant precision and bounded primal and dual radii, prior quantum algorithms of Brandão et al. (2019) and van Apeldoorn and Gilyén (2019) achieved $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{m})$ dependence on matrix dimension $n$ and constraint number $m$. Compared with the $\widetilde{O}(mn)$ runtime of existing classical methods, this suggests a quartic quantum speedup when $m \approx n$. Beyond a usual Grover speedup, this separation relies on the Quantum OR lemma, whose sample-reuse mechanism decouples the cost of Gibbs-state preparation from constraint search. We show that this reuse mechanism is classically realizable for sparse SDPs.
  Our main technical contribution is a classical procedure for simultaneously estimating many expectation values with respect to a sparse Hamiltonian's Gibbs state. This combines randomized Lánczos filtering with an efficient sampling-based estimator. We also introduce a stochastic online-learning framework for SDP solving, substantially improving accuracy-dependence over standard oracle-based MMWU approaches. Let $s$ denote the the input matrix sparsity and $γ:=Rr/\varepsilon$ capture dependence on the primal $(R)$ and dual $(r)$ radii as well as target accuracy $(\varepsilon)$. When $γ^2\leq\min\{m,n/s\}$, our solver runs in time $\widetilde{O}\left(nsγ^{4.5}+msγ^2\right)$. For $γ=O(1)$, this is $\widetilde{O}\left((n+m)s\right)$ and sublinear in the $O(mns)$ input size. Similar to the quantum algorithms, this matches known lower bounds with respect to $m$ and $n$, up to logarithmic factors. This implies that, with respect to dimensions $m$ and $n$, there is no super-quadratic quantum advantage for generic sparse SDP solving.

</details>


### [213] [A fully Gaussian quantum Stein's lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40307)
*Filippo Girardi,Jacopo Rizzo,Leah Turner,Gerardo Adesso,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究多模玻色高斯态在任意集体高斯测量限制下的渐近非对称假设检验，为多对状态建立了Stein指数的单字母公式，并揭示其非可加性仅导致一阶与二阶矩渐近解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息中，受限测量下量子态的渐近可区分性是核心但神秘的问题。经典Stein引理已有单字母刻画，但在高斯态与集体高斯测量约束下，Stein指数的单字母表达与可加性尚不明确。

Method: 针对一般多模玻色高斯态，在任意集体高斯测量约束下构建渐近非对称假设检验；通过新的对数行列式矩阵不等式，给出二阶矩项可加性的充分条件，并分析高斯测量相对熵的非可加性。

Result: 为多对状态建立了Stein指数的单字母公式，包含分别对应一阶矩与二阶矩的两项贡献；证明高斯测量相对熵存在真正的非可加性，但非可加性违背的唯一渐近效果是使一阶与二阶矩解耦；对中心高斯态，排除了集体高斯测量带来任何优势的可能性。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅在实际广泛情形下给出高斯Stein指数的单字母表达式，还表明集体高斯测量对中心高斯态无渐近优势，厘清了非可加性在高斯假设检验中的渐近作用。

Abstract: A central yet mysterious problem in quantum information is the asymptotic distinguishability of quantum states under restricted measurements. Here we look at asymptotic asymmetric hypothesis testing for general multi-mode bosonic Gaussian states restricted to arbitrary collective Gaussian measurements. We establish a single-letter formula for the Stein exponent for many pairs of states, featuring two distinct contributions, corresponding to first and second moments, respectively. Remarkably, the Gaussian-measured relative entropy exhibits genuine non-additivity, but the only effect of additivity violations is to asymptotically decouple first and second moments. We prove this by establishing sufficient conditions for additivity of the second-moment term via novel log-determinant matrix inequalities. Our results not only identify a single-letter expression for the Gaussian Stein exponent in a wide range of practical situations, but also rule out any possible advantage from collective Gaussian measurements when considering centred Gaussian states.

</details>


### [214] [Decoupling of the QAOA into independent spin-boson systems and high-depth performance on pure and mixed spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40309)
*Sami Boulebnane,Abid Khan,Pragna Subrahmanya,Dylan Herman,Edward Farhi,Benjamin Villalonga,Ruslan Shaydulin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出自旋-玻色子映射框架，证明在混合稠密自旋玻璃上计算QAOA能量时自旋近似解耦，无穷大尺寸极限下严格成立，并借助张量网络以低代价递归计算QAOA能量，从而将QAOA研究推进到先前技术无法处理的高深度区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA被视为近期组合优化量子优势的有力候选，但缺乏大规模研究手段。已有的精确递推公式虽可预测大尺寸自旋玻璃上的QAOA性能，其计算代价随QAOA层数指数增长，无法研究前景可期的高深度区间。因此需要一个能在保持大尺寸的同时突破深度瓶颈的高效评估方法。

Method: 作者证明在任意混合稠密自旋玻璃问题上计算QAOA能量时，自旋近似解耦为独立的spin-boson系统，且在无穷尺寸极限下解耦严格成立，从而建立QAOA多体物理的spin-boson映射框架。基于该广义spin-boson映射，给出可用张量网络以适中代价执行的递归程序来计算QAOA能量，并据此在纯与混合自旋玻璃上优化QAOA。

Result: 该递归程序使QAOA优化达到先前技术不可处理的深度。数值实验表明：高阶问题要达到可比近似比需要更多层，同时角度优化变得更困难；解耦虽支持大尺寸、大深度的QAOA能量评估，但并不能实现QAOA的强模拟——仍需量子计算机采样对应于预测能量的比特串。

Conclusion: spin-boson映射为QAOA的许多体物理提供了自然框架，将可评估范围扩展到高深度区，但仍不构成对QAOA的经典强模拟，量子采样环节不可替代。

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) is regarded as a promising candidate for near-term quantum advantage in combinatorial optimization, yet our ability to study it at scale is limited. Exact recursive formulas have been introduced for predicting QAOA performance on large spin glasses, but the computational cost of evaluating them grows exponentially with the number of QAOA layers, preventing the study of QAOA in the promising high-depth regime. In this work, we show that for the task of computing QAOA energy on any mixed dense spin glass problem, spins approximately decouple into independent spin-boson systems. In the infinite-size limit, the decoupling becomes exact, establishing the spin-boson mapping as a natural framework for the many-body physics of QAOA. This generalized spin-boson mapping gives a recursive procedure for computing QAOA energies that can be executed at modest cost using tensor networks. As a numerical application, we optimize QAOA on pure and mixed spin glasses at depths intractable for prior techniques, observing that higher-degree problems require more layers for a comparable approximation ratio while angle optimization becomes more challenging. While decoupling enables the evaluations of QAOA energies at large size and depth, it does not enable strong simulation of QAOA; a quantum computer is required to sample the bitstring corresponding to the predicted energy.

</details>


### [215] [Planted Cliques and Quantum Symmetry-Adapted Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40310)
*Vojtech Havlicek,Jordan Docter,Subhash Khot*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从信息论与结构角度研究植入团问题（planted clique）的两种量子编码，证明二值相位态编码需要大量副本才能达到常数优势检测，而基于Schur变换的对称适配测量保留足够信息以区分图分布，并给出单量子样本可实现高效量子区分器的条件性计算分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植入团问题具有显著的计算-统计鸿沟及经典硬度证据，被视为量子优势的候选问题。作者希望探究经典样本的量子编码是否保留足够信息用于植入团检测，并评估其算法效率潜力。

Method: 研究两种经典样本的量子编码：（1）自然的二值相位态编码；（2）由Schur变换自然产生的对称适配测量（含弱Schur采样、保留表示标签与Specht寄存器的变体），并进行副本复杂度下界与分布区分距离分析。

Result: 二值相位态编码下，常数优势检测需Ω(n^{1+2ε}ln²n)副本（即使允许任意联合测量）；测量约Õ(n²)副本可统计性超过对数团阈值。弱Schur采样结果分布仅依赖边数，无法区分；保留表示标签与Specht寄存器可保持距离1-o(1)，近乎完美区分性在丢弃标签后仍存。给出保留态的具体形式作为高效测量目标。一个相干量子样本即可实现高效量子区分器，在对量子植入团硬度的假设下给出相对于单经典样本的条件性计算分离。

Conclusion: 结果属于结构性与信息论层面；在猜想困难区间内从单个经典图进行高效检测仍是开放问题。

Abstract: The planted clique problem is a promising candidate for quantum advantage with a wide computational-statistical gap and substantial evidence for classical hardness. We study two quantum encodings of classical samples, a natural binary phase state encoding and symmetry-adapted measurements, and determine if they preserve enough information for planted-clique detection, as well as discuss their potential towards algorithmic efficiency. For the binary phase state encoding, we show that constant-advantage detection requires $Ω(n^{1+2\varepsilon}\ln^2 n)$ copies, even under arbitrary joint measurements. Measurements on $\tilde{O}(n^2)$ copies suffice statistically above the logarithmic clique threshold. The symmetry-adapted measurements on the full graph register arise naturally from the Schur transform. We show that the outcome distribution of weak Schur sampling depends on the sampled graph only through its edge count and fails to distinguish the distributions; whereas retaining the representation label and Specht register after discarding multiplicity preserves distance $1-o(1)$. Near-perfect distinguishability survives even if the label is also discarded. We calculate the retained states, providing concrete targets for efficient measurement. Finally, we show that one supplied coherent quantum sample enables an efficient quantum distinguisher, which yields a conditional computational separation from one classical sample under quantum planted-clique hardness. Our results are structural and information-theoretic; efficient detection from one classical graph in the conjectured hard regime remains open.

</details>


### [216] [More mutually unbiased bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40311)
*Mateo Cárdenes Wuttig,Joseph Tindall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构造互不偏基（MUBs）的新拟设，在多个非素数幂维度中构造出比以往更多的MUBs，并在每第18维中渐近超越张量积上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MUBs的最大数目在非素数幂维度中仍未知，现有构造方法（如张量积）在这些维度上给出的数量有限，亟需新的构造途径以逼近甚至超越已知上界。

Method: 引入一种拟设：每个基由一个对角相位矩阵作用于固定酉矩阵构成，该酉矩阵是傅里叶矩阵的张量积，并可选地包含任意实Hadamard矩阵；结合Paley实Hadamard矩阵构造维度 d=q(q+1)（q为素数幂且 q≡3 mod 4）的 q+1 个基。

Result: 在维度12构造5个MUBs，维度48、96、192构造6个，维度36、108构造7个，维度648构造10个；这些基可扩展至每第18维渐近超越张量积上界；对素数幂 q≡3 mod 4，维度 d=q(q+1) 可构造 q+1 个基，数量增长为 √d，且无法被小集合的张量积超越。

Conclusion: 该拟设提供了系统构造超出张量积上界的MUBs的方法，在多个维度改进了已知结果，并给出随维度平方根增长的构造，为MUBs最大数问题提供了新的进展。

Abstract: Mutually unbiased bases (MUBs) describe quantum measurements for which certainty in one basis gives uniform outcomes in the others. Their maximum number remains unknown in dimensions that are not powers of a prime. We introduce an ansatz that, in many dimensions, allows us to construct more MUBs than have previously been found. Each basis in the ansatz consists of a diagonal phase matrix applied to a fixed unitary, which is a tensor product of Fourier matrices and, optionally, any real Hadamard matrix. This construction yields five MUBs in dimension 12, six in dimensions 48, 96, and 192, seven in dimensions 36 and 108, and ten in dimension 648. These bases can then be extended to exceed the tensor product bound, asymptotically, in every eighteenth dimension. Moreover, we show that Paley's real Hadamard matrix can be used to construct $q+1$ bases in dimension $d = q(q+1)$ for every prime power $q\equiv3\pmod4$. This count grows as $\sqrt d$ and cannot be exceeded by tensor products of smaller sets.

</details>


### [217] [Explicit Capacity-Achieving Quantum LDPC Codes List Decodable in Near-linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40313)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Abhi Shukul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出同时具备LDPC性质的量子码显式构造，其列表译码半径逼近量子Singleton界且列表规模为常数，并提供近线性时间的列表译码算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典编码理论中，显式达到列表译码容量的码是核心目标，随机码易达容量但显式构造长期缺失，直至Guruswami-Rudra（STOC 2006）才实现。量子编码理论中，除"好码"外还高度期望码族具备LDPC性质；好量子码已知数十年，但附加LDPC性质一直难以获得，直到Panteleev-Kalachev（STOC 2022）才取得突破。由此自然的问题是：是否存在同时达到列表译码容量并具备LDPC性质的显式量子码族。

Method: 显式码构造基于扩展图，通过Alon-Edmonds-Luby（AEL）放大的量子类比（Bergamaschi, Golowich, Gunn, STOC 2024）实现，从而获得关键的LDPC性质。高效列表译码算法则通过将经典基于扩展图的弱正则性列表译码算法（Srivastava-Tulsiani, FOCS 2025；Jeronimo-Singh, 2025）推广到合适的量子AEL实例而得到。

Result: 给出了（据作者所知）首个达到列表译码容量的显式量子LDPC码构造：列表译码半径逼近量子Singleton界，且列表规模为常数；同时提供块长近线性的列表译码算法并逼近容量。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典列表译码容量的显式构造思想与量子LDPC码及量子AEL放大相结合，首次同时实现量子LDPC、列表译码容量逼近量子Singleton界、常数列表规模与近线性时间译码，为量子编码理论中显式容量逼近码提供了新范式。

Abstract: In classical coding theory, the quest for explicit codes achieving list decoding capacity has been an important driving force. While random codes are easily shown to achieve capacity, an explicit construction was only discovered decades later in the seminal work [Guruswami and Rudra, STOC 2006]. In quantum coding theory, it is highly desirable that a code family be LDPC. While good quantum codes were known for decades, obtaining the additional LDPC property was elusive. In fact, only very recently that good quantum LDPC codes were discovered in a breakthrough work [Panteleev and Kalachev, STOC 2022].
  In this context, a natural question is to ask for an explicit family of quantum codes achieving list decoding capacity while also possessing the important LDPC property. In this work, we provide (to the best of our knowledge) the first explicit constructions of quantum LDPC codes achieving list decoding capacity, namely, with a list decoding radius approaching the quantum Singleton bound with constant list sizes. Furthermore, we provide near-linear time (in the block-length) list decoding algorithms approaching capacity.
  Our explicit code construction are based on expander graphs via the quantum analogue of Alon-Edmonds-Luby (AEL) amplification [Bergamaschi, Golowich and Gunn, STOC 2024], and this enables the important LDPC property. Our efficient list decoding algorithms are obtained by generalizing the classical expander-based weak-regularity list decoding algorithms [Srivastava and Tulsiani, FOCS 2025] [Jeronimo and Singh, 2025] to suitable instantiations of quantum AEL.

</details>


### [218] [Quantum Sampling of Random Spanning Trees via Amortized Data Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40314)
*Yassine Hamoudi,Adrian Tanasa,Shrinidhi Teganahally Sridhara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个量子算法，仅用亚线性查询次数即可生成图的生成树上的均匀叠加态（即 q-sample），并证明其达到近似最优的匹配下界。该算法在预处理与采样查询复杂度上显著优于最优经典算法，并加速了基于量子游走的生成树计数与标记生成树查找。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作主要聚焦于生成经典样本，包括最近的量子算法（Apers, Gao, Ji, Liu, ICALP 2025）。本文旨在将量子采样能力推进到生成生成树的均匀量子叠加态（q-sample），并在查询复杂度上突破经典方法的瓶颈，同时厘清其最优性。

Method: 核心方法是对一系列缓慢变化的马尔可夫链进行量子游走采样。每条链是一个各向同性的上下游走（isotropized up-down walk），能快速混合到输入图的生成树分布。关键技术之一是维护摊销数据结构，以在整个链序列中快速实现相应的量子游走算子。该数据结构维护对谱稀疏化器（spectral sparsifier）与杠杆分数采样器（leverage score sampler）的访问，并使其随底层图动态演化。

Result: 对于 n 顶点、m 边的图，算法预处理代价为 Õ(√(mn) + m^{1-δ})，之后每个 q-sample 可在 Õ(n^{1+2δ}) 时间内生成（对任意 δ）。或者，k 个独立 q-sample 的总代价为 Õ(√(kmn))。作者进一步证明了对数因子内的匹配下界，表明该算法本质最优。相比之下，最优经典算法（Anari, Liu, Vuong, FOCS 2022）需要 Õ(m) 的预处理，之后每次采样成本为 Õ(n) 次操作。该结果还带来了更快的基于量子游走的生成树计数算法或查找标记生成树算法。

Conclusion: 本文给出了生成图生成树均匀量子叠加态的亚线性查询量子算法，达到近似最优的查询复杂度，显著优于经典最优算法，并可用于加速生成树计数与标记生成树查找。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm that generates a uniform superposition over the spanning trees of a graph -- also known as a q-sample -- using only a sub-linear number of queries to the graph. This goes beyond previous work that focuses on generating classical samples, including a recent quantum algorithm by Apers, Gao, Ji, and Liu [ICALP 2025]. For an $n$-vertex, $m$-edge graph, our algorithm admits a pre-processing step of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{mn} + m^{1-δ})$, after which each q-sample can be generated in $\tilde{O}(n^{1+2δ})$ for any $δ$. Alternatively, $k$ independent q-samples can be generated at a total cost of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{kmn})$. We also prove a matching lower bound up to logarithmic factors, showing that our algorithm is essentially optimal. Further tradeoffs are given in the paper. In comparison, the optimal classical algorithms (Anari, Liu and Vuong [FOCS 2022]) need an $\tilde{O}(m)$ pre-processing step, after which each sample costs $\tilde{O}(n)$ operations. These results yield faster quantum walk-based algorithms for counting spanning trees or finding a marked one.
  Our result is obtained via quantum walk sampling over a sequence of slowly-changing Markov chains. Each chain is an isotropized up-down walk that mixes rapidly to the spanning tree distribution of the input graph. A key ingredient is an amortized data structure supporting fast implementation of the associated quantum walk operators throughout the sequence. This data structure maintains access to a spectral sparsifier and a leverage score sampler that evolve with the underlying graph.

</details>


### [219] [Local Automorphism-Aware Syndrome Compilation for General Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40319)
*Eugenio Durazo Rocha,Olai Å. Mostad,Hsuan-Yin Lin,Eirik Rosnes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出局部自同构感知的校验编译方法 LocalASC，将 CSS 量子 LDPC 码的低深度 syndrome extraction 调度问题归约为 Tanner 图自同构子群下的边轨道变量问题，并在多类双块 CSS 码与量子 Tanner 码上得到深度最优或达到下界的调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSS 量子 LDPC 码的低深度 syndrome extraction 可建模为满足量子奇偶约束的 proper ordered edge-coloring 问题。仅保证 proper edge-coloring 不足以保证 X/Z 校验测量的有效交错，需满足重叠 X/Z 校验对共享数据量子比特上 X 先于 Z 的交互次数为偶数的量子奇偶约束。该问题的最小颜色数等于单 ancilla 测量下的最小两量子比特深度。此前已知 weight-6 IBM bivariate bicycle 码无法达到 6 层度下界，作者希望探究该限制是否具有普适性，并降低求解复杂度。

Method: 提出 LocalASC：在 Tanner 图自同构的子群下，将约束系统约化为边轨道变量，再将可行轨道分配提升到全图。为获得所需子群而不必计算完整自同构群，针对阿贝尔群上的双块 group-algebra CSS 码构造平移子群；对量子 Tanner 码，给出方形复形对称性可扩展为 Tanner 图自同构的条件。

Result: 6 层度下界对已发表的 weight-6 IBM bivariate bicycle 码不可达，但在双块 CSS 码中并非普遍。在最大校验权重等于 Tanner 图最大度的码实例中，LocalASC 为若干具有奇数分量权重（含不相等奇数权重）的双块 CSS 码找到深度最优调度，并为满足同度条件的若干量子 Tanner 码获得达到下界的调度。

Conclusion: 通过利用 Tanner 图自同构子群进行轨道约化，可在不计算完整自同构群的前提下高效求解带量子奇偶约束的 ordered edge-coloring，从而为多类 CSS 码构造深度最优或下界饱和的 syndrome extraction 调度，表明 6 层下界不可达并非双块 CSS 码的普遍性质。

Abstract: Low-depth syndrome extraction for Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) quantum low-density parity-check codes can be formulated as a proper ordered edge-coloring problem subject to quantum parity constraints. A proper edge-coloring of the CSS Tanner graph ensures that each data or ancilla qubit participates in at most one two-qubit gate per layer, but does not guarantee a valid interleaving of the X- and Z-check measurements as for every overlapping X/Z check pair, the number of shared data qubits on which the X interaction precedes the Z interaction must be even. The minimum number of colors in a proper ordered edge-coloring satisfying the quantum parity constraints equals the minimum two-qubit depth when each stabilizer check is measured with a single ancilla.
  We introduce local automorphism-aware syndrome compilation (LocalASC), which reduces the constraint system to edge-orbit variables under a subgroup of the Tanner graph automorphisms and lifts each feasible orbit assignment to the full graph. Although the $6$-layer degree lower bound is unattainable for the published weight-$6$ IBM bivariate bicycle codes, we show that this is not universal among two-block CSS codes. Among code instances for which the maximum check weight equals the maximum Tanner graph degree, LocalASC finds depth-optimal syndrome-extraction schedules for several two-block CSS codes with odd component weights, including instances with unequal odd weights. We also obtain lower-bound-saturating syndrome-extraction schedules for several quantum Tanner codes satisfying the same degree condition. To obtain the subgroups used by LocalASC without computing the full automorphism group of the Tanner graph, we construct translation subgroups for two-block group-algebra CSS codes over abelian groups. For quantum Tanner codes, we give conditions under which square-complex symmetries extend to Tanner graph automorphisms.

</details>


### [220] [Exponential quantum speedup for $\mathbb{F}_3^n$-Subset-Sum? Or, rigorous classical algorithms for Binary-Error LWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40321)
*Robin Kothari,Tony Metger,Ryan O'Donnell,Noah Shutty,Kewen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 $\mathbb{F}_3^n$ 上的向量子集和问题中，基于 Chen-Liu-Zhandry 框架构造量子算法，将所需向量数降至 $m=\varepsilon n^2$（任意固定 $\varepsilon>0$）并实现多项式时间，给出完整的样本-时间权衡；核心技术是二元错误 LWE 的确定性经典算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量子集和问题中样本数 $m$ 越小越难求解。Chen-Liu-Zhandry（EUROCRYPT'22）的量子算法需 $m\approx n^2/2$，而 Kothari-O'Donnell-Wu（STOC'2026）的经典算法只需 $m\approx n^2/3$，从而在 $\Theta(n^2)$ 参数区间内否定了指数级量子优势的可能。本文旨在重新确立该区间内量子指数加速的可能性，并刻画样本数与运行时间之间的完整权衡关系。

Method: 沿用 Chen-Liu-Zhandry 的算法框架，构造需要更少输入向量的量子算法；关键组件是二元错误 Learning-with-Errors（LWE）问题的确定性经典算法，并严格证明此前由代数启发式预测的样本-时间权衡；对更大域上的向量子集和，改进了 Kothari-O'Donnell-Wu 的经典算法。

Result: 对任意固定 $\varepsilon>0$，量子算法可在多项式时间内以 $m=\varepsilon\cdot n^2$ 个向量求解 $\mathbb{F}_3$-子集和；更一般地，建立了在指数时间与多项式时间之间插值的完整样本-时间权衡。同时给出了二元错误 LWE 的确定性经典算法及其严格的样本-时间权衡，并对更大域上的经典算法有显著改进。

Conclusion: 该工作恢复了 $\mathbb{F}_3$-子集和问题中指数级量子加速的可能性，并将样本-时间权衡从启发式提升为严格结果；其中 LWE 的确定性经典算法具有独立的密码学意义，对更大域子集和问题的经典算法改进亦具价值。

Abstract: We study vector subset sum over $\mathbb{F}_3^n$: given $m$ random vectors from $\mathbb{F}_3^n$, find a nonempty subset that sums to zero; the smaller $m$, the more difficult it is to find such a subset. Chen, Liu, and Zhandry (EUROCRYPT'22) introduced an efficient quantum algorithm that solves this problem when $m\approx n^2/2$, where a naive classical algorithm would require exponential time. Subsequently, Kothari, O'Donnell, and Wu (STOC'2026) gave an efficient classical algorithm that only requires $m \approx n^2/3$ vectors, thus removing the hope for an exponential quantum advantage in this parameter regime.
  Using the framework of Chen, Liu, and Zhandry, we give quantum algorithms that require much fewer input vectors, renewing the possibility of an exponential quantum speedup: for any fixed $ε>0$, our quantum algorithm solves $\mathbb{F}_3$-subset sum in polynomial time with $m=ε\cdot n^2$ vectors. More generally, we establish a full sample--time tradeoff that interpolates between exponential and polynomial runtime.
  The main ingredient is a deterministic classical algorithm for the binary-error Learning-with-Errors problem, which is of independent cryptographic interest. For this, we rigorously establish a sample--time tradeoff that was predicted by earlier algebraic heuristics. For vector subset sums over larger fields, we also significantly improve classical algorithms in Kothari, O'Donnell, and Wu (STOC'2026).

</details>


### [221] [Computational Work Extraction: The Complexity of Catalysts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40323)
*Atul Singh Arora,Shantanav Chakraborty,Alexandru Cojocaru,Sreyas Saminathan,Uttam Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了n量子比特系统可具备Θ(n)的ergotropy（功提取能力），但任何高效过程仅能提取可忽略的功，从而给出ergotropy与催化计算复杂度之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典/量子系统蕴含大量能量（ergotropy），但高效算法却无法提取，这一"能量存在与可提取性"的鸿沟亟需理论刻画；同时催化计算（辅助量子比特须最终还原）的能力边界尚不清晰。

Method: 证明量子空间受限算法的查询下界；构造无条件存在性分离，并在随机预言机模型下给出显式构造；在量子安全伪随机函数假设下将分离扩展到plain模型；利用催化计算构造关系问题与判定问题。

Result: （i）相对随机预言机，存在可用λ个catalyst高效求解、但用任意cλ（c<1）个catalyst无法求解的问题；（ii）存在Hamiltonian与态族，催化过程可高效提取完整Θ(n) ergotropy，而非催化高效过程仅能提取可忽略的功；（iii）催化不改变信息论ergotropy；（iv）多数构造基于经典态与Hamiltonian，故经典ergotropy亦有类似结论；并提出pseudoergotropy概念。

Conclusion: 催化在计算ergotropy中异常强大，可实现与非催化过程间的极大分离，并催生pseudoergotropy新概念；但催化不影响信息论ergotropy；通过量子性证明协议可区分经典与量子催化ergotropy。

Abstract: We prove maximal separations: $n$-qubit systems can have $Θ(n)$ ergotropy, while every efficient process extracts negligible work, even for Hamiltonians consisting of single-qubit terms. We establish an unconditional existential separation and give an explicit construction in the random oracle model. Assuming the existence of quantum-secure pseudorandom functions, this separation extends to the plain model.
  This work uncovers an important connection between ergotropy and the complexity of catalytic computation---computation where auxiliary qubits must be finally restored to their initial state. Relative to a random oracle, we establish relational and decision problems that: (i) can be solved efficiently with $λ$ catalysts; but (ii) cannot be solved by any algorithm with $cλ$ catalysts, for any $c<1$. We show this by proving query lower bounds for quantum-space bounded algorithms.
  As a consequence, for computational ergotropy, catalysts prove to be surprisingly powerful---there is a family of Hamiltonians and states for which catalysts enable efficient extraction of the full $Θ(n)$ ergotropy, while every efficient non-catalytic process extracts negligible work. Furthermore, catalysts also allow us to introduce and instantiate the notion of pseudoergotropy---analogous to pseudorandomness. On the other hand, we show catalysts do not change (information-theoretic) ergotropy.
  Finally, our work also sheds light on the classical aspect of the problem. First, most of our constructions rely on classical states and Hamiltonians and therefore imply analogous results for classical ergotropy. Second, we show that certain proof of quantumness protocols can be used to generically separate classical and quantum catalytic ergotropy.

</details>


### [222] [Quantum Enhanced Deep Reinforcement Learning for Distribution Network Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40327)
*Daniel Germain,Mason Blanchard,Audrey Versteegen,Nora Bauer,Jonathan Mei,Claudio Girotto,Paul Smith,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出深度强化学习(DRL)框架用于静态配电网重构(DNR)，在受控条件下仅改变Q网络架构，系统研究架构对解质量与学习性能的影响，发现量子增强Q网络以更少参数取得更高奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能电网具备更丰富的运行数据和更多远程可控开关设备，为数据驱动的配电网重构创造了新机会。但DNR是组合优化问题，寻找最优开关配置计算代价高昂，且Q网络架构如何影响求解质量与学习性能尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建面向静态DNR的深度强化学习框架，在控制变量的实验设置下仅替换Q网络架构（含量子增强Q网络与经典Q网络），以隔离各架构选择的贡献并对比其表现。

Result: 量子增强Q网络相比经典对应架构，可用更少的参数获得更高的奖励，表明该类架构在组合型电力系统优化中具有潜力。

Conclusion: Q网络架构显著影响DNR的求解质量与学习性能；量子增强Q网络是组合电力系统优化的一个有前景方向，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Modern smart grids generate increasingly rich operational data and deploy greater numbers of remotely controlled switching assets than ever before, creating new opportunities for data-driven distribution network reconfiguration (DNR). DNR optimizes grid topology to improve efficiency, reduce power losses, and maintain operational constraints, but finding optimal switch configurations remains a computationally demanding combinatorial problem. In this paper, we propose a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) framework for static DNR to systematically investigate how the Q-network architecture affects both solution quality and learning performance. We evaluate multiple architectures within a controlled DRL framework, varying only the Q-network to isolate the contribution of each architectural choice. Our results show that quantum-enhanced Q-networks can achieve higher rewards with fewer parameters than their classical counterpart, suggesting that such architectures are a promising direction for combinatorial power systems optimization.

</details>


### [223] [Bath-assisted cooling without resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40328)
*Xie-Hang Yu,Zherui Chen,Lin Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种无需反复重置热浴的单次相干冷却机制：通过将输入态的熵转移到随后自动解耦的热浴自由度，使系统与剩余热浴沿基态路径演化，最终只需丢弃一次热浴，即可将任意混合态制备为纯基态。作者在一类弱相互作用、有能隙、局域相互作用的费米子系统上严格实现了该协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统冷却将混合态熵转移到环境通常需要多次重置热浴；本文探究能否用单次相干热浴接触、无需重复重置热浴即完成冷却。目标是寻找一种系统哈密顿量始终开启且不改动、仅靠非马尔可夫系统-热浴动力学即可制备基态的冷却机制。

Method: 开发一种冷却机制：把输入信息转移到随后解耦的热浴自由度，系统和剩余热浴沿基态路径演化，仅在最后丢弃热浴。对一类弱相互作用、有能隙、局域相互作用费米子系统严格实现该机制。协议使用每个系统模式两个初始为空的辅助热浴模式，并对所有在位系统-热浴耦合施加同一个共享脉冲。其保证源于非马尔可夫系统-热浴动力学，不依赖有效 Lindbladian 描述。

Result: 从任意混合态出发，协议在系统规模和全局迹范数误差倒数的多对数（polylog）总物理时间内制备出相互作用基态。同一共享脉冲无需知道系统的微观参数，即可为每个容许系统制备其自身基态。

Conclusion: 单次相干热浴接触、无需反复重置热浴即可实现混合态到纯基态的冷却，且系统哈密顿量始终保持开启、不做修改。该机制在一类有能隙局域费米子系统上被严格证明，表明非马尔可夫系统-热浴动力学可绕过传统需要 Lindbladian 描述的冷却限制。

Abstract: Cooling an arbitrary mixed state to a pure ground state requires transferring its entropy to an environment. Can a single coherent bath contact accomplish this without repeated bath resets? We develop a new cooling mechanism that transfers the input information into bath degrees of freedom that subsequently decouple, while the system and the remaining bath follow a ground-state path. The bath is discarded only at the end, and the system Hamiltonian remains on and unmodified throughout. We rigorously realize this mechanism for a class of weakly interacting, gapped fermionic systems with local interactions. Starting from any mixed state, our protocol prepares the interacting ground state in total physical time polylogarithmic in the system size and inverse global trace-norm error. It uses two initially empty bath modes per system mode and a single pulse shared across all onsite system--bath couplings. The same pulse prepares each admissible system's own ground state without knowledge of its microscopic parameters. The guarantees follow directly from a non-Markovian system--bath dynamics without an effective Lindbladian description.

</details>


### [224] [Fast Quantum Algorithms for Learning Linear Threshold Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40331)
*Aleksandrs Krivcenko,Tuyen Nguyen,Ronald de Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出学习线性阈值函数（LTF）的三个量子算法正面结果：实数域查询下以 O(log(n/ε)) 次成员查询学习 LTF、相同取值稀疏支持上的同质 LTF 以 O(log k) 查询识别支持、以及在弱访问模型（高斯量子样例）下学习同质 LTF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性阈值函数是机器学习中用于线性判别正负样本的核心对象。经典算法在实数域查询下需要 Ω(n log(1/ε)) 次成员查询，效率受限；同时已有同质稀疏 LTF 的量子算法查询复杂度为 O(k^{1/4})。作者希望借助量子算法在查询复杂度和访问模型两方面改进 LTF 的学习效率。

Method: 1) 针对 f_{w,θ}，允许在任意 x∈R^n 处查询函数值，设计量子算法学习参数至欧氏误差 ε；2) 针对域 {-1,1}^n 上仅有 k 个相同非零权重的同质 LTF（即支持集上的 Majority 函数），设计识别隐藏支持集的量子算法；3) 在较弱访问模型下，给定可制备高斯测度下离散化量子样例 |x⟩|f_w(x)⟩ 的酉算子 U，基于 Jain 等人高效 Hermite 变换构造学习同质 LTF 的量子算法。

Result: 1) 使用 O(log(n/ε)) 次成员查询和 Õ(n) 个其他门学习 f_{w,θ} 至欧氏误差 ε，并在高斯分布下达到 O(ε) 误差；经典算法需 Ω(n log(1/ε)) 次查询；2) 将稀疏同质 LTF 支持识别查询复杂度从 O(k^{1/4}) 指数级改进为 O(log k)；3) 弱访问模型下使用 O(n^{1/4}/√ε) 次 U 与 U† 应用及 Õ(n^2/ε^4) 个其他门，在高斯分布下以误差 ε 学习同质 LTF f_w。

Conclusion: 量子算法在 LTF 学习上相较经典算法具有显著优势：实数域查询下查询复杂度由 Ω(n log(1/ε)) 降至 O(log(n/ε))；稀疏同质 LTF 支持识别获得指数级加速；并在更弱的量子样例访问模型下仍可实现高效学习。

Abstract: Linear threshold functions are $f_{w,θ}(x)=\text{sign}(\langle x,w \rangle -θ)$, where the weight vector $w\in\mathbb{R}^n$ is a unit vector, $θ\in\mathbb R$ is a threshold, and typically $x\in\mathbb{R}^n$ or $x\in\{-1,1\}^n$. When $θ=0$, the LTF is called homogeneous, and we write $f_w:=f_{w,0}$. Such functions are among the most important objects in machine learning, since they serve to linearly discriminate positive and negative examples. We give three positive results about learning LTFs: 1. Suppose we can make real-domain queries, meaning we can compute $f_{w,θ}(x)$ at any $x\in\mathbb{R}^n$ of our choice. We give a quantum algorithm that learns $f_{w,θ}$ up to Euclidean error~$ε$ using $O(\log(n/ε))$ membership queries and $\widetilde{O}(n)$ other gates. Then we have also learned $f_{w,θ}$ up to error $O(ε)$ when $x$ is Gaussian. Classical algorithms need $Ω(n\log(1/ε))$ queries. 2. A homogeneous LTF $f_w$ on domain $\{-1,1\}^n$ where $w$ has only $k$ nonzero entries of the same value, is the Majority function on the support of $w$. Belovs gave a bounded-error quantum algorithm that identifies the hidden support exactly (and hence learns $f_w$) using $O(k^{1/4})$ queries. We give an exponential improvement, using $O(\log k)$ queries. 3. Suppose we have a unitary $U$ that can produce (discretized) \emph{quantum examples} under Gaussian measure, corresponding to $\int_x \sqrt{γ_n(x)}|x\rangle |f_w(x)\rangle dx$. This is a weaker access model than membership queries. We give a quantum algorithm based on the efficient \emph{Hermite transform} of Jain et al.\ to learn homogeneous LTFs~$f_w$ with error~$ε$ under the Gaussian distribution, using $O(n^{1/4}/\sqrtε)$ applications of $U$ and $U^\dagger$ and $\widetilde{O}(n^2/ε^4)$ other gates.

</details>


### [225] [Tight Universal Bounds on Quantum Data Hiding with Multipartite Werner States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40332)
*Oren Akresh,Jacob Beckey,Felix Leditzky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文解决了Werner态量子数据隐藏方案的均匀安全性关于粒子数和局域维数的最优依赖问题，证明任意两个n-qudit Werner态在PPT测量下的区分偏差为O(n²/d)，且该上界可由非自适应局域测量达到，从而确立最坏情况最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eggeling和Werner提出的基于Werner态的多体隐藏方案已存在二十余年，但其均匀安全性保证对粒子数n和局域维数d的最优依赖关系一直未知。作者旨在确定这一最优标度，并探究PPT松弛测量是否保持该最优依赖性。

Method: 将区分偏差归约为偏转置算子的迹范数，并利用混合Schur-Weyl对偶性进行分析；借助为基于端口的量子传态所发展的表示论方法处理数据隐藏问题，并构造显式的Werner态对，仅用非自适应局域测量达到该标度。

Result: 证明任意两个n-qudit Werner态在跨每个二分划均保持正性的测量（PPT）下的区分偏差为O(n²/d)，且显式构造的态对可由非自适应局域测量达到此标度，确立最坏情况最优性。在固定安全性下，认证隐藏区域从n=O(d^{1/4})扩展到n=O(√d)。此外，该界意味着用自适应单副本测量测试任何非平凡酉不变性质需要Ω(√d)个副本，对任何在集体测量下样本复杂度与维数无关的性质给出了分离。

Conclusion: PPT松弛测量保持了Werner态数据隐藏关于粒子数和局域维数的最优依赖；所发展的表示论方法（源自基于端口的量子传态）可有效应用于数据隐藏与量子性质测试，并给出单副本与集体测量之间的分离结果。

Abstract: Quantum data hiding concerns pairs of states which are highly distinguishable with global measurements, yet nearly indistinguishable when restricted to local operations and classical communication. More than two decades after Eggeling and Werner introduced a multipartite hiding scheme based on Werner states, the optimal dependence of its uniform security guarantee on the number of parties and local dimension remained unknown. We resolve this problem by showing that the distinguishing bias of any pair of $n$-qudit Werner states is $O(n^2/d)$ under measurements whose effects remain positive under partial transposition (PPT) across every bipartition. An explicit pair attains this scaling using only nonadaptive local measurements, establishing worst-case optimality and showing that the PPT relaxation preserves the optimal dependence on both the number of parties and the local dimension. At fixed security, this extends the certified hiding regime from $n=O(d^{1/4})$ to $n=O(\sqrt d)$. Beyond data hiding, the same bound implies that testing any nontrivial unitarily invariant property with adaptive single-copy measurements requires $Ω(\sqrt d)$ copies, yielding separations for any property with dimension-independent sample complexity under collective measurements. Our proof reduces the distinguishing bias to trace norms of partially transposed operators and analyzes them using mixed Schur-Weyl duality, demonstrating the utility of representation theoretic methods developed for port-based teleportation to data hiding and quantum property testing.

</details>


### [226] [Local Relaxation Hierarchies for Quantum Ground State Energies: Convergence Guarantees and Message Passing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40336)
*Sheng-Ku Lin,Ricardo Rivera Cardoso,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出局部凸松弛层级与高效并行消息传递算法，用于估计量子多体系统的松弛基态能量，并给出精确性与收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统凸松弛层级虽可在多项式时间内为量子多体系统基态能量提供下界，但受限于求解器计算成本高、效率保证稀缺，难以扩展到大规模系统并获得精确近似。

Method: 构建局部松弛层级及高效、高度可并行的消息传递算法；第一层基于成对约化密度矩阵的局部一致性；另一层级基于一致区间；提出基于次梯度法和Nesterov型加速梯度下降（作用于熵平滑目标）的两种消息传递变体。

Result: 第一层层级对树上的对易哈密顿量精确；一致区间层级对链上可分哈密顿量的弱扰动随区间尺寸指数收敛；在给定消息范数为O(1)的假设下，消息传递算法在有界度图上分别以O(n/ε²)和O(n/ε)时间运行；实验验证了理论预测并可超越标准凸优化求解器。

Conclusion: 局部松弛层级与消息传递算法为量子多体基态能量估计提供了高效且具理论保证的经典方法，并发布了相应开源库。

Abstract: Convex relaxation hierarchies provide lower bounds to the ground state energy of quantum many-body systems that can be computed in polynomial time on a classical computer, at any fixed hierarchy level. However, scaling these methods to large systems and accurate approximations remains challenging due to the computational cost of traditional solvers and the scarcity of efficiency guarantees. In this work, we develop local relaxation hierarchies and efficient, highly parallelisable message passing algorithms for estimating the relaxed ground state energies. We show that the first level of the hierarchy---based on local consistency of pairwise reduced density matrices---is exact for commuting Hamiltonians on trees. We further establish that another hierarchy, based on consistent intervals, converges exponentially fast in the interval size to the ground state energy for weak perturbations of separable Hamiltonians on a chain, thereby providing an efficient classical algorithm for these systems. Then, we introduce two variants of message passing algorithms that run in $\mathcal{O}(n/ε^2)$ and $\mathcal{O}(n/ε)$ time for any fixed level of the local hierarchy on bounded-degree graphs, where $ε$ is the precision for the relaxed ground state energy per site. This assumes that the optimal messages have $\mathcal{O}(1)$ norm---a condition we observe in practical settings in our experiments. These algorithms are based on the subgradient method and the Nesterov-type accelerated gradient descent method applied to an entropy-smoothed objective. Finally, we benchmark the message passing algorithms across different quantum Hamiltonians, lattice geometries, and relaxation levels, validating the theoretical predictions and their potential to surpass standard convex optimisation solvers for this problem. We release the resulting library at github.com/rick1924/gse-message-passing.

</details>


### [227] [The Single-Copy Quantum Bandit Is Classical: An Exact Spectral Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40339)
*Siu Hin Ng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究量子多臂老虎机中，臂提供未知量子态、平均奖励由已知效果F定义的问题，证明自适应/随机测量设计无法改进渐近遗憾的主导对数系数，谱测量加经典KL-UCB在一类策略中渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典多臂老虎机的Burnetas-Katehakis下界刻画了最优遗憾。量子情形下，每次可获得新副本并可用任意自适应POVM测量，测量设计是否能降低遗憾尚不清楚；本文旨在确定单副本一致策略的渐近最优性能，并厘清量子记忆能否带来进一步提升。

Method: 通过测量博弈的鞍点分析：证明从臂态到混淆奖励半空间的测量相对熵等于F谱统计的经典Burnetas-Katehakis泛函；由矩阵对数导数构造最不利态，F的谱测量与其构成鞍点。结合KL-UCB给出有限时间界。对任意量子记忆的一致策略，用摊销相对熵论证给出基于Umegaki半空间散度的逆界，并对照两个逐臂遗憾下界1/K_inf与1/D_inf。

Result: 测得相对熵精确坍缩为经典BK泛函；谱测量+经典KL-UCB在所有有限维、满足非退化假设的奖励效果下渐近实例最优，自适应与随机测量设计以及存储副本均不能改进主导对数系数；给出有限时间界。任意量子记忆下得到Umegaki散度逆界，两个下界1/K_inf与1/D_inf当且仅当臂与F对易时相等；Umegaki下界可达性归结为复合量子Stein问题与自适应遗憾归约，作为开放问题提出并给出两副本数值证据。

Conclusion: 单副本测量类中自适应测量无益，谱测量+KL-UCB已达渐近最优；进一步改进必须来自单副本测量类之外（如多副本联合测量）。任意量子记忆下Umegaki散度给出逆界，其可达性是公开问题。

Abstract: We study multi-armed bandits whose arms supply unknown quantum states and whose mean rewards are defined by a known effect $F$. Each fresh copy may be measured by an arbitrary, adaptively chosen POVM. We prove an exact collapse: the measured relative entropy from an arm state to its confusing reward half-space equals the classical Burnetas-Katehakis functional of $F$'s spectral statistics, and the spectral measurement of $F$ together with an explicit least-favorable state, built from the derivative of the matrix logarithm, forms a saddle point of the underlying measurement game. Consequently, spectral measurement followed by classical KL-UCB is asymptotically instance-optimal among all consistent single-copy policies, in every finite dimension and for every reward effect under the stated nondegeneracy assumptions: adaptive and randomized measurement design cannot improve the leading logarithmic regret coefficient, and neither can storing copies for later single-copy measurement. A self-contained finite-time bound covers general effects and boundary distributions. Any further improvement must come from measurements outside the single-copy class. For consistent policies with arbitrary quantum memory, an amortized relative-entropy argument gives a converse with the Umegaki half-space divergence, and the two per-arm regret floors, $1/K_{\mathrm{inf}}$ and $1/D_{\mathrm{inf}}$, coincide if and only if the arm commutes with $F$. Whether the Umegaki floor is attainable involves a composite quantum Stein problem and a separate reduction to adaptive regret; we pose it as an open problem, with two-copy numerical evidence.

</details>


### [228] [Quantum de Finetti theorems for states and channels in any distance measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40343)
*Liuhang Ye,Bjarne Bergh,Nilanjana Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将标准与Renner指数型量子de Finetti定理加强为算子不等式形式，从而给出任意Schatten范数与满足数据处理不等式量子Rényi散度下的界；并应用于信道de Finetti表示，解决Berta等人提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子de Finetti定理通常以迹距离给出近似界，误差随k的标度为k^2/n，且在信道（Choi态）层面缺乏算子序意义下的表示。作者希望用更强的算子不等式统一刻画这些近似，并利用Choi态算子序与信道CP序的等价性，研究置换协变信道的de Finetti表示，回答Berta等（Math. Program. 194, 781-829, 2022）提出的开放问题。

Method: 证明标准与Renner指数型de Finetti定理的算子不等式版本，使误差在任意Schatten范数和满足数据处理不等式的量子Rényi散度下均有界；将算子不等式方法应用于信道，利用Choi态算子序等价于底层信道的完全正（CP）序这一性质，分析置换协变信道N^(n): A^{⊗n}→B^{⊗n}的k-系统约化信道，并用Choi-几乎iid信道（其归一化Choi态在Mazzola-Sutter-Renner意义下几乎iid）构造逼近混合。

Result: 标准情形下，固定局部维度时，最大相对熵误差界由k^2/n改进为k/n（经典情形亦有改进）；对置换协变信道，k-系统约化信道被张量幂信道混合以O(k/√n)误差CP-支配，且对局部维度为多项式依赖；在无信号条件下证明指数型信道de Finetti定理，当存在r个缺陷时表示误差至多为poly(n)(2d_A^4k^3/(nr^2))^{(r+1)/2}，并在适当选择r与k时随n指数衰减。

Conclusion: 算子不等式框架统一并强化了标准与Renner de Finetti定理，能导出更强范数与散度界；在信道层面给出CP序支配的de Finetti表示及无信号条件下的指数型信道定理，推广了量子de Finetti理论并回答了已有文献的开放问题。

Abstract: Standard finite quantum de Finetti theorems approximate the $k$-system marginals of permutation-invariant states of $n$-systems by mixtures of independent and identically distributed (iid) states, usually in trace distance. We prove both standard and Renner's exponential de Finetti theorems in the stronger form of operator inequalities, implying bounds in every Schatten norm and for every quantum Rényi divergence satisfying data processing. In the standard case, for fixed local dimension, our $k/n$ error bound in max-relative entropy improves on the previously best known $k^2/n$ scaling, even in the classical setting. The operator-inequality approach is particularly suited to study channel de Finetti representations because operator order between Choi states is equivalent to completely positive (CP) order between the underlying channels. For permutation-covariant channels $N^{(n)}:A^{\otimes n}\to B^{\otimes n}$, where $d_A=\dim A$, we prove that the $k$-system reduced channel is CP-dominated by a mixture of tensor-power channels with error $O(k/\sqrt{n})$ and polynomial dependence on the local dimensions, addressing a question raised by Berta et al. [Math. Program. 194, 781-829 (2022)]. Under the no-signalling condition, we also prove an exponential channel de Finetti theorem where the approximating mixture consists of Choi-almost-iid channels, whose normalized Choi states are almost-iid in the sense of Mazzola-Sutter-Renner. In the case of $r$ defects, the representation error is at most $\mathrm{poly}(n)\bigl(2d_A^4k^3/(nr^2)\bigr)^{(r+1)/2}$ and decays exponentially in $n$ for a suitable choice of parameters $r$ and $k$.

</details>


### [229] [Gibbs Sampling in the Shattered Phase by Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40345)
*Leo Zhou,Noah Shutty,Mark Sellke,Stephen P. Jordan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将解码量子干涉（DQI）应用于经典Ising自旋哈密顿量的吉布斯采样，证明该问题可归约为量子解码问题，且DQI可达温度由解码算法性能决定；在随机Erdős-Rényi超图上的Ising k-spin玻璃中，DQI与Prange方法均可突破由破碎与无序混沌构成的拓扑势垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Ising自旋玻璃的吉布斯采样在动力学相变温度以下面临由破碎（shattering）与无序混沌（disorder chaos）形成的拓扑势垒，阻碍了Glauber动力学及一类输出分布对输入扰动"稳定"的算法；作者希望借助量子方法突破此类障碍。

Method: 将经典Ising哈密顿量的吉布斯采样问题归约为量子解码问题；针对随机Erdős-Rényi超图（平均度D≥k）上的经典Ising k-spin玻璃（即Max-k-XORSAT），分别采用基于Prange方法的经典算法与配备量子解码器的DQI进行采样分析。

Result: 在从预测动力学相变β_dyn(k,D)=√(2ln k/D)×[1+o(1)]起始的温度区间内，破碎与无序混沌构成拓扑势垒，阻碍Glauber动力学及任何输出分布对扰动"稳定"的算法；而Prange方法类经典算法与DQI均能打破该势垒。例如当D=αk（α>1固定）时，二者在足够大k下均可在任意逆温度β<tanh⁻¹(1/α)采样，远超动力学阈值β_dyn∼√(2ln k/(αk))。

Conclusion: DQI能够克服阻碍"稳定"算法的拓扑势垒，表明量子解码能力可在经典自旋玻璃采样中突破传统算法的动力学与稳定性限制。

Abstract: We apply Decoded Quantum Interferometry (DQI) to sample from the Gibbs measures of classical Ising spin Hamiltonians. We show that this Gibbs sampling problem reduces to a quantum decoding problem, and the temperature achievable by DQI is determined by the performance of decoding algorithms. We then focus on the task of Gibbs sampling for classical Ising $k$-spin glasses (or Max-$k$-XORSAT) on random Erdős-Rényi hypergraphs with average degree $D\ge k$. In a temperature range beginning asymptotically at the predicted dynamical phase transition, $β_{\rm dyn}(k,D) = \sqrt{(2\ln k)/D}\times [1+o_{k\to\infty}(1)]$, we show that shattering and disorder chaos form a topological barrier that obstructs many algorithms, including Glauber dynamics and any algorithm whose output distribution is "stable" under perturbations of the input. In contrast, we prove that this barrier can be broken both by a classical algorithm based on Prange's method, and by DQI equipped with a quantum decoder. For example, when $D=αk$ with fixed $α>1$, both Prange's algorithm and DQI can sample at any inverse temperature $β< \tanh^{-1}(1/α)$ for sufficiently large $k$, well beyond the dynamical threshold $β_{\rm dyn} \sim \sqrt{2\ln k / (αk)}$. Therefore, our results show that DQI can overcome topological barriers that obstruct stable algorithms.

</details>


### [230] [Efficient Calculation of Equilibrium Correlation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40349)
*Yizhi Shen,Roel Van Beeumen,Wibe A. de Jong,Efekan Kökcü*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种将时间作为量子变量存储于O(log N_t)个辅助量子比特的算法，用单个量子电路并行计算平衡关联函数的所有时间点，仅需O(log N_t)次运行、总资源为O(N_t log N_t)，并证明该标度最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子算法（Hadamard测试、变分法、基于线性响应的方法）通过哈密顿量模拟计算平衡关联函数 G_AB(t-t') 时，每个时间点都需不同的量子电路，导致总测量次数至少 O(N_t)、总量子门数至少 O(N_t^2)，资源开销随时间点数急剧增长，亟需更高效的方案。

Method: 利用 O(log N_t) 个辅助量子比特将时间编码为量子变量，通过单条量子电路并行化所有时间点的平衡关联函数计算；该电路仅需运行 O(log N_t) 次。在大系统尺寸极限下总量子资源为 O(N_t log N_t)，并从理论上证明该标度是最优的。

Result: 总量子资源需求降至 O(N_t log N_t)（大系统尺寸极限），电路运行次数仅 O(log N_t)，并证明该标度达到最优；以一维无自旋 Hubbard 模型的格林函数计算进行了演示验证。

Conclusion: 将时间作为量子变量并利用辅助量子比特并行化，可将平衡关联函数的计算复杂度从 O(N_t^2) 量子门降至 O(N_t log N_t)，且该标度为理论最优，为量子模拟动态关联函数提供了高效且最优的资源框架。

Abstract: A majority of measurable dynamic quantities of a physical system is described as an equilibrium correlation function of the form $G_{AB}(t-t') = \left<A(t)B(t')\right>$, where $t'$ represents the impact time and $t$ represents the response time. Conventional quantum algorithms to compute such quantities via Hamiltonian simulation such as the Hadamard test, variational methods, or linear-response-based algorithms share one feature in common: each time point $t-t'$ is calculated by a different quantum circuit, leading to at least $O(N_t)$ total shots, and at least $O(N_t^2)$ total quantum gates. Here, we provide a different approach: utilizing $O(\log N_t)$ ancilla qubits to store time as a quantum variable, and effectively parallelize the computation of an equilibrium correlation function by requiring only a single quantum circuit for all time points. We show that this circuit needs to be run only $O(\log N_t)$ times, which in total requires $O(N_t \log N_t)$ quantum resources in the large system size limits. We prove that this scaling is optimal, and demonstrate our algorithm calculating the Green's function of a one-dimensional spinless Hubbard model.

</details>


### [231] [On The Simplest Quantum-Secure Block Cipher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40350)
*Gorjan Alagic,Joseph Carolan,Christian Majenz,Saliha Tokat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在理想置换模型（IPM）下，两轮 Even-Mansour 密码对于自适应量子敌手具有信息论安全性，并证明了该构造在量子环境下是极小的（单次公开置换调用无法实现安全）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪随机置换（PRP）是密码学的核心原语，抗量子查询的 PRP 愈发重要。密钥交替 Even-Mansour 框架在经典情形下安全，但在量子查询场景下其安全性尚不明确：单轮构造可被基于 Simon 算法的量子攻击攻破，而两轮及以上仅对非自适应敌手有安全性证明。因此需要澄清该框架在自适应量子敌手下的安全性。

Method: 作者在理想置换模型（IPM）中分析两轮 Even-Mansour 密码，允许敌手对所有可用预言机进行多项式次数的自适应正向与逆向量子查询。证明使用了压缩置换预言机（compressed permutation oracles）以及一个在理想实验与真实实验之间建立关联的特制等距（isometry）。此外还给出了下界结果，说明任何仅通过单次公开置换调用构造的密码在量子下均不安全。

Result: 证明了两轮 Even-Mansour 密码在 IPM 下对自适应量子敌手是信息论安全的；同时证明了该构造的极小性：本质上任何仅使用单次公开置换调用的密码构造在量子环境下都不安全。

Conclusion: 两轮 Even-Mansour 在抗量子自适应敌手方面达到了信息论安全，且轮数是必要的极小值，从而澄清了该框架在量子查询模型下的安全地位。

Abstract: Pseudorandom permutations are ubiquitous in theoretical and applied cryptography. PRPs that offer security even against adversaries making quantum queries are of increasing interest, and used in applications ranging from constructing pseudorandom unitaries to separating SZK from BQP.
  A successful framework for constructing classically-secure PRPs is the key-alternating Even-Mansour approach, which interleaves applications of public permutations with additions of round keys. The single-round construction is already classically secure in the ideal permutation model (IPM), with added rounds offering improved concrete security. However, in the quantum-query setting, the status of this framework is presently unclear. A simple quantum-query attack based on Simon's algorithm breaks the one-round cipher. For two or more rounds, security is only known against non-adaptive adversaries who must prepare all queries in advance.
  In this work, we show that the two-round Even-Mansour cipher is information theoretically secure in the IPM against adversaries making polynomially-many adaptive forward and inverse quantum queries to all available oracles. Our proof uses compressed permutation oracles and a specially crafted isometry relating the ideal and real experiments. We also show that this construction is minimal, in the sense that essentially any cipher constructed via a single call to a public permutation is quantumly insecure.

</details>


### [232] [All Unitaries Have Constant Depth Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40351)
*Barak Nehoran,Henry Yuen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 任意n量子比特幺正操作都可用深度poly(n, log 1/ε)的量子电路逼近，并借助无界扇出门进一步降至常数深度，颠覆了指数深度必要性的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用n量子比特幺正操作通常需要指数深度电路（2^O(n)）来实现，即使允许无限辅助比特，指数深度是否为必要条件一直是开放问题。本文旨在探究是否所有幺正操作都能被并行化到多项式深度。

Method: 构造深度为poly(n, log 1/ε)、使用2^O(n)辅助比特的单比特和双比特门电路来逼近任意n量子比特幺正操作；进一步，若允许无界扇出门，则可压缩到常数深度。核心手法是建立了Aaronson-Kuperberg幺正合成问题与局部可解码码及私有信息检索之间的新联系。

Result: 证明了所有n量子比特幺正操作都能以算子范数ε被深度poly(n, log 1/ε)的电路逼近；在无界扇出门条件下深度进一步降低至常数深度。也就是任意幺正操作都可并行化到多项式深度。

Conclusion: 指数深度对于通用幺正操作并非必要，通过引入复杂度理论和密码学工具（局部可解码码、私有信息检索），实现了幺正合成问题的深度大幅压缩，这对量子电路复杂度理论具有重要启示。

Abstract: It is well-known that every $n$-qubit unitary can be implemented by a $2^{O(n)}$-depth quantum circuit using single- and two-qubit gates. It has been open whether exponential depth is \emph{necessary} for general unitaries, even when allowing for unlimited number of ancilla qubits. We show, perhaps surprisingly, that all unitaries can be approximated to operator norm $ε$ by a circuit of one- and two-qubit gates of depth $\poly(n,\log 1/ε)$ with $2^{O(n)}$ ancilla qubits. In other words, every $n$-qubit unitary can be parallelized to polynomial depth. Moreover, if we allow unbounded fan-out gates, these circuits can be reduced further to \emph{constant} depth. Our construction takes advantage of a novel relationship connecting the unitary synthesis problem of Aaronson and Kuperberg to locally-decodable codes and private information retrieval from complexity theory and cryptography.

</details>


### [233] [Quantum hypothesis testing of non-mixed-unitarity: A multifaceted hierarchy of quantum channel discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40355)
*Pratik Ghosal,Pritam Halder,Ayan Patra,Aditi Sen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未知量子信道是"非混合酉信道"还是属于"混合酉信道"的判别问题，将其建模为复合信道假设检验，刻画不同探测策略（块i.i.d.、辅助记忆等）下的Stein指数，并建立严格分层与分离结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分非混合酉信道与混合酉信道是量子信道理论中的核心判别问题。该问题可归结为复合假设检验，但不同资源（探测态相关性、块大小、辅助记忆、参考纠缠）如何影响可达到的Stein指数尚不清晰，需要一个系统的分层刻画与分离分析。

Method: 将问题表述为复合信道假设检验；刻画平行策略在逐级放宽的探测态与辅助记忆限制下可达到的Stein指数；重点分析块i.i.d.探测（块内任意相关、块间i.i.d.），给出相应Stein指数的有限字母（finite-letter）表达式；通过调节块大小在完全i.i.d.与任意探测之间插值，结合块内相关限制和辅助记忆，构建多层次结构，并证明其中的严格分离。

Result: （1）无辅助记忆时，完全i.i.d.探测对任意unital非混合酉信道Stein指数为零；而三个乘积探测构成的块对qutrit Werner-Holevo信道即可给出严格正指数。（2）块大小为二时，乘积探测对O(3)-协变信道似不足够，但纠缠探测对qutrit Werner-Holevo信道可达严格正指数，尽管所有最大纠缠探测均失败。（3）有辅助记忆时，完全i.i.d.探测即足够，对每个非混合酉信道均给出严格正指数，且无需输入-参考纠缠；该指数存在由到混合酉集合的diamond范数距离给出的下界。（4）对奇数维Werner-Holevo信道，该指数为无穷大，且每个满Schmidt秩纯态均为最优。

Conclusion: 探测态相关性结构、块大小与辅助记忆共同决定了非混合酉与混合酉信道判别的Stein指数，形成多层次严格分离；辅助记忆可将完全i.i.d.探测提升为普适最优资源，而奇数维Werner-Holevo信道可达无穷指数。

Abstract: Given multiple uses of an unknown quantum channel, we determine whether it is a specified non-mixed-unitary channel or belongs to the set of mixed-unitary channels. Formulating this as a composite channel hypothesis testing problem, we characterize the Stein exponents achievable by parallel strategies under progressively weaker restrictions on probe states and auxiliary memory. In particular, we consider block-i.i.d. probes, which allow arbitrary correlations within blocks of fixed size while remaining i.i.d. across blocks, and derive finite-letter expressions of the corresponding Stein exponents. Varying the block size, which interpolates between fully i.i.d. and arbitrary probes, together with further restrictions on intra-block correlations and access to auxiliary memory, results in a multifaceted hierarchy. We establish several strict separations within this hierarchy. Without auxiliary memory, we prove that fully i.i.d. probes yield a vanishing Stein exponent for every unital non-mixed-unitary channel, while blocks of three product probes suffice to obtain a strictly positive exponent for the qutrit Werner-Holevo channel. For block size two, although product probes seem to be insufficient for O(3)-covariant channels, an entangled probe achieves a strictly positive exponent for the qutrit Werner-Holevo channel, even though every maximally entangled probe fails. In contrast, auxiliary memory makes fully i.i.d. probes sufficient by yielding a strictly positive exponent for every non-mixed-unitary channel, even without requiring input-reference entanglement, and this exponent admits a lower bound determined by the diamond-norm distance from the mixed-unitary set. Moreover, for odd-dimensional Werner-Holevo channels, we find that this exponent is infinite, with every full-Schmidt-rank pure state being optimal.

</details>
