# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Nonreciprocal Polychromatic Radiation from a Dual Electric-Magnetic Space-Time-Modulated Meta-Transceiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38542)
*Sajjad Taravati,Ya-Adama Kabia*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文实验实现了一种时空周期元收发器（omega-topology单元），通过单向行波偏置同时独立调制电、磁表面极化率，首次在同一结构中实验观测到两种非互易效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变超表面提供了无需磁偏置的非互易波调控途径，但已有实现仅调制电响应，同时电-磁调制方案仅停留在理论层面。作者希望将电-磁联合时空调制从理论推向实验验证，并展示其可重构、全双工的非互易波变换能力。

Method: 设计并制备具有omega拓扑单元的空间-时间周期元收发器，利用单向行波偏置对单元的电、磁表面极化率进行独立且同时的调制。

Result: 实验分辨出两种独立的非互易表现：发射时入射波高效转换为多色谐波梳，谐波以随偏置可重构的角度辐射；接收时同一调制抑制频率转换，并将入射波优先导向其中一个端口。

Conclusion: 上述两种效应（非互易多色转换与单向波路由）源自同一单向时空调制，但分别作用于不同物理量（频率成分与空间方向）。结果确立了电-磁同时时空调制作为实现可重构、全双工非互易波变换的实验可行机制。

Abstract: Time-modulated metasurfaces offer a magnet-free route to nonreciprocal wave control, but existing realizations modulate only the electric response of the structure, and proposals for simultaneous electric-magnetic modulation have so far remained theoretical. We experimentally demonstrate a space-time-periodic meta-transceiver whose omega-topology unit cell independently and simultaneously modulates the electric and magnetic surface susceptibilities via a unidirectional traveling-wave bias. This dual modulation gives rise to two independent, experimentally resolved manifestations of nonreciprocity: on transmit, the incident field is efficiently converted into a polychromatic comb of harmonics that radiate at distinct, bias-reconfigurable angles, while on receive, the same modulation instead suppresses frequency conversion and routes the incoming wave preferentially toward one port over the other. These two effects, nonreciprocal polychromatic conversion and unidirectional wave routing, arise from a single unidirectional space-time modulation but act on distinct physical observables, frequency content and spatial direction, respectively. Our results establish simultaneous electric-magnetic space-time modulation as an experimentally viable mechanism for reconfigurable, full-duplex nonreciprocal wave transformation.

</details>


### [235] [Metaphotonics for High-Harmonic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38727)
*Pavel Tonkaev,Anton Rudenko,Ziwen Wang,Tran Trung Luu,Yuri Kivshar*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该综述总结了光元光子结构（孤立谐振器与超表面）中高次谐波产生（HHG）的最新进展，涵盖等离激元、Mie共振及连续域束缚态等机制，并指出非整数幂依赖现象及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高次谐波产生（HHG）是获取极紫外光源与阿秒脉冲的有效手段，此前主要在气体、液体及固体中实现。谐振元光子学有望提供亚波长尺度的平台与高效集成光源设计的新策略，因此亟需系统梳理其研究现状。

Method: 综述性研究：先简要回顾气体、液体与非结构化固体中的HHG，继而总结近期在单个谐振纳米颗粒与谐振介质超表面中的实验观测，重点对比不同类型的共振机制（等离激元共振、Mie共振、连续域束缚态），并讨论强共振驱动下产生谐波的非整数幂依赖关系。

Result: 在亚波长图案化结构中，超快激光脉冲与支持多种共振的结构化平面表面相互作用可产生极端非线性效应；实验上已在孤立谐振纳米颗粒和介质超表面中观测到HHG，并观察到非整数幂的谐波依赖关系。

Conclusion: 谐振元光子学为HHG提供了新颖的亚波长平台，并可为高效集成光源设计提供新思路；文中还指出了当前尚未解决的问题及超表面HHG的新兴研究方向。

Abstract: We summarise the recent advances on the generation of high-order harmonics in optical metaphotonic structures such as isolated resonators and metasurfaces. In such subwavelength-patterned structures, extreme nonlinear effects are expected from the interaction between ultrafast laser pulses and structured planar surfaces which support various resonances. High-harmonic generation (HHG) is considered as an effective tool for realising extreme ultraviolet light sources and attosecond pulses, and it was observed previously in gases, liquids, and solids. Resonant metaphotonics can offer a novel sub-wavelength platform for the HHG effects, and it can provide new strategies for the design of efficient integrated light sources. We start our discussion from a brief overview of HHG in gases, liquids, and unstructured solids, and then move to summarising the recent experimental observations of HHG in individual resonant nanoparticles and resonant dielectric metasurfaces. We focus on different types of resonances (plasmonic vs. Mie resonances vs. bound states in the continuum), and also present the observation of non-integer power dependencies of the generated harmonics driven by strong resonances. We also mention current unsolved problems and identify new promising research directions involving HHG in metasurfaces.

</details>


### [236] [Experimental Demonstration of Chiral Bound States in the Continuum Enabled by Time-reversal Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38826)
*Hao-Chang Mo,Wen-Jin Zhang,Ze-Yu Wu,Xin-Tao He,Xiao-Dong Chen,Jian-Wen Dong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文首次在磁光光子晶体中通过外加垂直磁场打破时间反演对称性，实验观测到手性连续谱束缚态（chiral BIC），其表现为原本简并的BIC劈裂为两个具有相反圆偏振的非简并共振，微波透射谱与数值模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIC可提供辐射损耗消失、光-物质相互作用增强的光学共振，其中手性BIC因具有本征圆偏振与自旋选择性辐射而备受关注。此前手性BIC仅停留在磁光光子晶体的理论预言阶段，缺乏实验验证，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用磁光光子晶体结构，施加面外磁场以打破时间反演对称性，将一对简并BIC劈裂为两个非简并、圆偏振相反的共振；通过数值模拟揭示手性相位涡旋与强自旋选择性辐射，并利用角度分辨透射谱与圆二色性进行微波实验测量。

Result: 实验观测到时间反演对称性破缺诱导的手性BIC；角度分辨透射谱证实了劈裂行为，并测得显著的宽带圆二色性；模拟给出的手性相位涡旋和自旋选择性辐射与实验吻合。

Conclusion: 研究建立了探索磁光光子结构中手性BIC物理的实验平台，为自旋选择性光子学开辟了新机遇。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) provide a powerful route to optical resonances with vanishing radiation loss and enhanced light-matter interactions. Of particular interest are chiral BICs, whose resonant states exhibit intrinsic circular polarization and spin-selective radiation. Magneto-optical photonic crystals have recently been predicted to host such states through time-reversal-symmetry breaking. Here, we experimentally demonstrate chiral BICs induced by time-reversal symmetry breaking in a magneto-optical photonic crystal, where an out-of-plane magnetic field lifts a degenerate BIC pair into two nondegenerate resonances with opposite circular polarizations. Numerical simulations reveal the associated chiral phase vortices and strong spin-selective radiation, while microwave measurements confirm the predicted behavior through angle-resolved transmission spectra and pronounced broadband circular dichroism. Our results establish an experimental platform for exploring chiral BIC physics in magneto-optical photonic structures, opening new opportunities for spin-selective photonics.

</details>


### [237] [Surface quantum response of plasmonic heterodimers: revealing the hidden nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38870)
*Ida Juliane Bundgaard,Christos Mystilidis,Frederik Wulff Christensen,Lorenz Huber,P. Elli Stamatopoulou,Carsten Rockstuhl,P. A. D. Gonçalves,Ulrich Hohenester,N. Asger Mortensen,Christos Tserkezis*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文利用Feibelman参数的表面响应形式，研究等离激元异质二聚体的量子响应，比较了扩展源（平面波）与局域源（点偶极子和电子束）探测的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究极端等离激元结构中量子表面效应（电子溢入/溢出）如何影响光-物质相互作用，并揭示不同激励源对量子响应的探测能力差异。

Method: 基于Feibelman参数的表面响应形式主义，构建纳米球异质二聚体模型，其中一颗粒子表现为电子溢入、另一颗为溢出，并分别采用平面波、点偶极子和电子束进行激励。

Result: 发现两种方向相反的量子表面修正在扩展源下可相互抵消，使介观光谱与局域响应近似预测一致；但使用局域源时该现象显著改变，可独立选择性地探测各粒子的量子响应——电子束通过高阶模实现，点偶极子源则覆盖完整模态景观。

Conclusion: 该研究为极端等离激元结构中的光-物质相互作用机制提供了新见解，并为等离激元-量子发射体架构的理解与调控开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We probe the quantum response of plasmonic heterodimers with extended (plane waves) and localized (point dipoles and electron beams) sources. Implementing the surface-response formalism based on Feibelman parameters, we explore nanosphere heterodimers where one particle is characterized by electron spill-in, while the other by spill-out. We identify situations where the two quantum surface corrections that act in opposite spectral-shift directions can cancel out, producing mesoscopic spectra that do not differ from the predictions of the local-response approximation. This picture changes dramatically once localized sources are implemented, allowing one to selectively probe the quantum response of the particles independently. Such probing occurs in terms of higher-order modes in the case of electron beams, or for the complete modal landscape in the case of point dipole sources. This description offers a new insight into the mechanisms governing light--matter interactions in extreme plasmonic architectures, and opens new perspectives for the understanding and tailoring of plasmonic--quantum emitter architectures.

</details>


### [238] [Frequency comb generation in active optomechanical cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38944)
*Madhurendra Mishra,Adarsh Ganesan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过数值模拟研究电泵垂直腔面发射激光器与悬臂镜双机械模耦合时的频率梳产生，揭示机械品质因子、共振频率与初始条件对梳结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有源光机械腔中机械自由度如何调控频率梳的产生，明确双机械模耦合对梳谱特性的作用机制。

Method: 对电泵垂直腔面发射激光器与悬臂镜两个机械模耦合系统进行数值模拟，引入位移依赖的腔损耗与辐射压反馈，将机械运动与载流子密度和光子密度耦合。

Result: 光子密度谱中出现谐波及组合频率成分；机械品质因子主要决定梳线强度，共振频率决定其光谱分布；初始机械振幅影响阈值电流和相对谱强度，两模同时激发时产生更丰富的可观测成分。

Conclusion: 在有源光机械腔中，所计算的梳结构依赖于机械耗散、共振频率和初始条件。

Abstract: We numerically investigate frequency-comb generation in an electrically pumped vertical-cavity surface-emitting laser coupled to two mechanical modes of a suspended mirror. Displacement-dependent cavity loss and radiation-pressure feedback couple the mechanical motion to the carrier and photon densities, producing harmonics and combination-frequency components in the photon-density spectrum. Mechanical quality factors primarily control the intensity of comb lines, whereas resonance frequencies determine their spectral distribution. The initial mechanical amplitudes influence the threshold current and relative spectral intensities, with the simultaneous excitation of both modes yielding a broader set of observable components. These results establish the dependence of the calculated comb structure on mechanical dissipation, resonance frequencies, and initial conditions in an active optomechanical cavity.

</details>


### [239] [Bias-resolved comb-formation regimes in a simple two-section quantum-dot mode-locked laser for isolator-free, terabit-scale O-band interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39298)
*Ying Shi,William He,Xiangpeng Ou,Defan Sun,Weiping Li,Xin Yao,Yating Wan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文通过扫描InAs/GaAs量子点锁模激光器的增益电流与可饱和吸收体偏压平面，划分出短脉冲、宽带、低噪声、免隔离器传输及反馈稳定等不同应用区域，实现最宽3 dB带宽、最低定时抖动及2.75 Tb/s聚合速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用的O波段量子点锁模梳源需同时满足宽平坦带宽、低射频定时噪声、可纠错的裸线调制和反馈容限，但这些功能通常被分别优化，且与梳形成动力学的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 对简单双段InAs/GaAs量子点锁模激光器的增益电流/可饱和吸收体偏压平面进行映射，识别不同应用特定区域；并在免隔离器25 Gb/s NRZ传输、最大带宽偏压及反馈测试偏压下进行表征。

Result: 低注入高反偏区域产生0.80 ps脉冲；高注入中偏压区域产生16.16 nm（2.75 THz）平坦梳、110条谱线及14.98 ps波形，为已报道量子点锁模梳源中最宽3 dB带宽。低噪声偏压下拍频洛伦兹线宽0.58 kHz，4–80 MHz积分定时抖动41.6 fs。免隔离器传输中94个载波低于7% HD-FEC阈值；最大带宽偏压110个载波投影聚合速率2.75 Tb/s。反馈测试偏压下，超过约-28 dB反馈后进入反馈稳定区，线宽降低42倍。

Conclusion: 短脉冲、宽带、低噪声、传输就绪和反馈稳定等操作占据不同可选区域，而非单一通用最优点；该区域分辨映射将量子点梳形成物理与免隔离器、太比特级O波段互连联系起来。

Abstract: Practical O-band quantum-dot (QD) mode-locked comb sources must combine wide flat-top bandwidth, low radio-frequency (RF) timing noise, error-correctable bare-line modulation, and feedback tolerance, yet these functions are usually optimized separately and their relation to comb-formation dynamics remains unclear. Here we map the gain-current/saturable-absorber-bias plane of a simple two-section InAs/GaAs QD mode-locked laser to resolve distinct application-specific regimes. A low-injection, high-reverse-bias regime produces 0.80 ps pulses, consistent with amplitude-modulated operation, whereas a high-injection, intermediate-bias regime yields a 16.16 nm (2.75 THz) flat-top comb with 110 lines and a 14.98 ps extended waveform, consistent with a stronger frequency-modulated contribution. To our knowledge, this is the broadest reported 3 dB bandwidth among QD mode-locked comb sources. At a separate low-noise bias, the beatnote exhibits a 0.58 kHz Lorentzian linewidth and a 41.6 fs integrated timing jitter over the 4 to 80 MHz range, to our knowledge the lowest reported for a high-channel-count O-band QD passive comb. In isolator-free 25 Gb/s NRZ transmission, all 94 carriers across a 13.73 nm 3 dB band remain below the 7 percent HD-FEC threshold. Using all 110 carriers at the maximum-bandwidth bias gives a projected aggregate rate of 2.75 Tb/s. At a separate feedback-test bias, broadband mode locking is preserved and, beyond approximately -28 dB feedback, the RF beatnote enters a feedback-stabilized regime whose linewidth is reduced 42-fold. Thus, short-pulse, broadband, low-noise, transmission-ready, and feedback-stabilized operation occupy distinct selectable regions rather than a single universal optimum. This regime-resolved map links QD comb-formation physics to isolator-free, terabit-scale O-band interconnects.

</details>


### [240] [Driving Spintronic Terahertz Emitters at GHz Repetition Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39493)
*Thomas A. Gething,Henry De Libero,Morgan T. Hibberd,Thomas Thomson,Paul W. Nutter,Darren M. Graham*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文利用异步光学采样THz-TDS实时观测1 GHz重频激光驱动下自旋电子THz发射器（STE）的信号退化，证实THz发射损失是永久性热损伤所致；通过采用高热导率衬底，损伤阈值提升超过三个数量级，首次实现高重频激光下STE的稳定发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STE是性能优越、成本低廉且使用便捷的THz源，但近期报道其在高重频激光驱动下光学注量损伤阈值下降，这成为其更广泛应用的主要障碍。因此需要厘清损伤机制并寻找提升损伤阈值的方案。

Method: 采用异步光学采样（ASOPS）THz时域光谱系统，在1 GHz重频激光驱动下实时监测STE的THz信号退化过程；并通过更换高热导率衬底来调控STE的热耗散能力。

Result: THz发射的损失是永久性的，很可能是STE结构热损伤的结果；使用高热导率衬底后，损伤阈值提升超过三个数量级，首次实现高重频激光驱动下STE的稳定发射。

Conclusion: 高重频激光下STE的失效源于热损伤，通过衬底热管理（高热导率衬底）可显著提升损伤阈值，从而突破STE在高重频驱动下的应用瓶颈。

Abstract: Spintronic terahertz emitters (STEs) are versatile sources of terahertz (THz) frequency radiation that offer high electric field strengths and gap-free bandwidths covering the THz (0.1-30 THz) band. In addition, they are low-cost to produce and easy to use in a variety of optical setups. However, an obstacle to their wider applicability is the recently reported decrease in optical fluence damage threshold when driven with high-repetition-rate lasers. Here, we use an asynchronous optical sampling (ASOPS) THz time-domain spectrometer (THz-TDS) to observe the degradation of the THz signal in real-time when driven by a 1 GHz repetition-rate laser. We demonstrate that the loss of THz emission is permanent and is likely the result of thermal damage to the STE structure. However, by utilizing substrates with high thermal conductivities, an increase of the damage threshold of over three orders of magnitude is observed enabling stable emission from a STE for the first time when driven by a high-repetition-rate laser.

</details>


### [241] [Judd-Ofelt analysis of holmium-doped alumino-silicate optical glass prepared by MCVD combined with nanoparticle doping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39555)
*Petr Varak,Michal Kamradek,Pavla Nekvindova,Jan Hrabovsky,Pavel Peterka*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 首次对Ho³⁺掺杂铝硅酸盐光纤预制棒（溶液掺杂与纳米颗粒掺杂）进行系统的Judd-Ofelt分析，考察Al₂O₃含量对光谱参数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ho³⁺掺杂铝硅酸盐玻璃是2 μm光纤激光器的重要材料，但缺乏全面的Judd-Ofelt分析数据，需为器件设计与仿真提供基础光谱参数。

Method: 制备宽组分范围的Ho³⁺掺杂铝硅酸盐预制棒（标准溶液掺杂与纳米颗粒掺杂两种工艺），测量吸收光谱并计算各跃迁吸收截面，进行Judd-Ofelt分析，计算辐射跃迁参数（跃迁概率、分支比、辐射寿命），并结合测量寿命计算量子效率。

Result: JO参数Ω₂=(7.2--10.6)×10⁻²⁰ cm²（随Al₂O₃含量变化），Ω₄≈2.5×10⁻²⁰ cm²，Ω₆≈1.2×10⁻²⁰ cm²；⁵I₇→⁵I₈辐射寿命为16.1--17.2 ms，测量寿命0.9--1.9 ms，量子效率5--11%。纳米颗粒掺杂且Al₂O₃含量>8 mol.%的样品性能更优，测量寿命>1.5 ms、量子效率>8%。

Conclusion: 该工作为Ho³⁺掺杂铝硅酸盐玻璃JO分析的首个综合报道，纳米颗粒掺杂高铝样品具有更高的测量寿命和量子效率，所得参数可用于Ho掺杂光纤激光器及器件的计算、仿真与建模。

Abstract: We present a detailed Judd--Ofelt (JO) analysis of Ho$^{3+}$-doped alumino-silicate optical fiber preforms in a wide range of compositions and prepared by both the standard solution doping method as well as the more advanced nanoparticle doping. The absorption spectra were measured, the absorption cross sections were calculated for each observed transition, and the Judd--Ofelt analysis was conducted. The preforms exhibited the values of JO parameters of $Ω_2 = (7.2--10.6) \times 10^{-20}\,\mathrm{cm}^2$, depending on the Al$_2$O$_3$ content, and $Ω_4$ and $Ω_6$ around $2.5$ and $1.2 \times 10^{-20}\,\mathrm{cm}^2$, respectively. The radiative transition parameters, such as transition probabilities, branching ratios, and radiative lifetimes, were calculated. The radiative lifetime of the ${}^5I_7 \rightarrow {}^5I_8$ transition, in the $16.1--17.2\,\mathrm{ms}$ range, combined with a measured value in the $0.9--1.9\,\mathrm{ms}$ range, was used to calculate the quantum efficiency, which was in the $5--11\,\%$ range. The preforms prepared by nanoparticle doping containing high Al$_2$O$_3$ contents above $8\,\mathrm{mol.}\,\%$ exhibited superior values of measured lifetime (above $1.5\,\mathrm{ms}$) and quantum efficiency (above $8\,\%$) compared to the standard solution-doped samples. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first comprehensive report on the JO analysis of Ho$^{3+}$-doped alumino-silicate glass. The calculated parameters may be used in various calculations, simulations, and modelling of holmium-doped fiber lasers and other devices.

</details>


### [242] [How Shaped Waves Propagate in Scattering Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39586)
*David Gaspard,Arthur Goetschy*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文建立了波前调控波的矩阵输运理论，用复2×2矩阵输运方程描述相干波传播，并解析导出了透射本征通道的能量与电流密度空间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统辐射输运方程能描述散射介质中的波传播，但无法描述入射波前被刻意裁剪的相干波，因此需要一个能保留相位并适用于波前调控波的输运理论框架。

Method: 提出一种矩阵输运方程：不再传播标量辐射亮度，而是传播一个保留相位信息的复2×2矩阵，并服从非线性输运方程（尽管底层波动力学是线性的）。利用该方程推导任意扩散系统中从闭合到开放透射本征通道的能量密度和电流密度空间分布，并推广至吸收与泄漏到非受控通道等能量损失情形。

Result: 透射本征通道的能量与电流密度空间分布可解析地用同一介质中随机波的常规辐射输运方程解表示；理论推广到能量损失后揭示了复杂结构中透射本征通道的对称性破缺，并与波方程的数值解进行了验证。

Conclusion: 矩阵输运被确立为描述和调控复杂介质中相干波传播的理论框架。

Abstract: The radiative transport equation provides a powerful framework for describing wave propagation in scattering media. However, it cannot describe coherent waves whose incident wavefront is deliberately tailored. Here we establish a transport theory for wavefront-controlled waves based on a matrix transport equation. Unlike conventional radiative transport, which propagates a scalar radiance, our theory propagates a complex two-by-two matrix that retains phase information and obeys a nonlinear transport equation, despite the underlying wave dynamics being linear. We use this matrix transport equation to derive the spatial profiles of the energy and current densities of transmission eigenchannels, from closed to open channels, in arbitrary diffusive systems. Remarkably, these profiles can be expressed analytically in terms of the solution of the conventional radiative transport equation for a random wave in the same medium. The theory further extends to energy loss, including absorption and leakage into uncontrolled channels, revealing symmetry breaking of transmission eigenchannels in complex structures. Validated against numerical solutions of the wave equation, we establish matrix transport as a framework for describing and controlling coherent wave propagation in complex media.

</details>


### [243] [Phase-resolved wide-field CARS microscopy with speckle illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39730)
*Federico Vernuccio,Pascal Berto,Baptiste Marthy,Guillaume Baffou,Sandro Heuke,Randy Bartels,Pierre Bon,Hervé Rigneault*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种相位敏感宽场高光谱CARS显微方案，通过高功率皮秒OPA、散斑照明与QLSI定量相位成像的组合抑制非共振背景，实现大视场、相位分辨的高光谱CARS成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CARS为点扫描、单振动模式探测；高光谱CARS虽提升化学特异性，但受非共振背景干扰，导致拉曼峰展宽与频移，降低高光谱图像可解释性。需要一种无需外参比、可抑制非共振背景的相位敏感宽场高光谱CARS方案。

Method: 方案整合三个关键组件：(1) 高功率皮秒可调谐光学参量放大器（OPA），提供高效非线性激发所需峰值功率；(2) 散斑照明，将光能分布于物镜后瞳并抑制相干成像伪影；(3) 基于四波横向剪切干涉术（QLSI）的定量相位成像，同步测量CARS场的振幅与相位，从而分离共振与非共振贡献，且无需外部参考光。

Result: 在超过60×60 μm²视场下实现相位分辨CARS成像，帧率1.4 Hz；并在整个CH伸缩振动区获取了微塑料与肝脏脂肪变性的高光谱图像。

Conclusion: 该相位敏感宽场高光谱CARS方案可有效抑制非共振背景，实现大视场、相位分辨的高光谱化学成像，适用于微塑料及肝脂肪变性等生物与材料样品的无标记检测。

Abstract: Coherent anti-Stokes Raman scattering (CARS) microscopy enables label-free chemical imaging of biological samples and materials. Conventionally, CARS is implemented using a point-scanning approach that probes a single vibrational mode at a time. Hyperspectral CARS enhances chemical specificity by sequentially addressing multiple Raman modes. However, the measured CARS intensity is distorted by an undesired non-resonant background, which broadens and shifts the Raman peaks, thereby hindering the interpretability of hyperspectral images. Here, we introduce a phase-sensitive, wide-field hyperspectral CARS microscopy scheme that suppresses the non-resonant background. The proposed approach combines three key components: a high-power picosecond tunable optical parametric amplifier (OPA), speckle illumination, and quantitative phase imaging based on quadriwave lateral shearing interferometry (QLSI). The high-power OPA provides the peak power required for efficient nonlinear excitation. Speckle illumination distributes the optical energy over the objective back pupil and mitigates coherent imaging artifacts. QLSI enables the simultaneous measurement of the amplitude and phase of the CARS field, thereby allowing separation of resonant and non-resonant contributions, without the need for an external reference beam. This unique combination enables practical phase-resolved CARS imaging over a field of view exceeding $60 \times 60~\text{\textmu m}^2$ at a frame rate of 1.4 Hz. We illustrate the approach by acquiring hyperspectral images of microplastics and liver steatosis across the entire CH-stretching region.

</details>


### [244] [Topological Fermi-polaron Polaritons with Electrically Invertible Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39791)
*Xin Xie,Chenxi Liu,Yuze Liu,Lingxiao Zhou,Chulwon Lee,Yuhan Zhang,Nathanial Lydick,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Kai Sun,Hui Deng*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作实现拓扑玻色-费米混合物——费米极化激元，借助费米极化子多体相互作用、光子对称性与强光-物质耦合，获得大能隙且可由电学翻转的拓扑能带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑极化激元可用于构造光学陈能带，但半导体极化激元体系中的时间反演对称破缺通常仅由微弱塞曼劈裂提供，导致难以实现大拓扑能隙与高效电学调控。

Method: 将栅控单层过渡金属硫族化合物与对称性工程化的光子晶体集成，利用强光-物质耦合形成具有拓扑能带的费米极化激元；通过能带拓扑与可栅调多体相互作用的直接耦合来放大时间反演对称破缺。

Result: 实现了超越裸塞曼劈裂的放大时间反演对称破缺，并实现拓扑能隙、贝里曲率与陈数的电学翻转。

Conclusion: 该结果为相互作用驱动的拓扑态与可编程拓扑光子学提供了电学可重构平台。

Abstract: Topological polaritons provide a route to engineer optical Chern bands, but realizing large gaps or efficient electrical tunability remains challenging because time-reversal-symmetry breaking in semiconductor polariton systems is typically limited by weak Zeeman splittings. Here, we demonstrate a topological Bose-Fermi mixture --- Fermi-polaron polaritons ---where large gaps and electrically invertible topology emerge from the interplay of Fermi-polaron many-body interactions, photonic symmetry, and strong light--matter coupling. Using a gate-controlled monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenide integrated with a symmetry-engineered photonic crystal, we create Fermi-polaron polaritons with topological band structures. The direct coupling between band topology and gate-tunable many-body interactions provides a mechanism for amplified time-reversal-symmetry breaking beyond bare Zeeman splitting and enables electrical inversion of topological gaps, Berry curvature, and Chern numbers. These results establish an electrically reconfigurable platform for interaction-driven topological states and programmable topological photonics.

</details>


### [245] [Non-additive surface and volume backscattering from a rough interface over a correlated random medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39897)
*Mariano Franco,Esteban Calzetta*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将粗糙界面做非正交坐标变换拉平，使几何粗糙度与介电起伏化为同一Helmholtz方程中的体源并由同一格林函数传播，从而在单次计算中统一得到面散射、体散射及其干涉项，发现后向散射截面偏离可加性可达20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙面散射与随机介质体散射通常被分别处理并相加，但当表面与介电起伏两个随机场相关时，二者不可加，需要统一框架同时给出两种机制及其干涉项。

Method: 对半空间上方粗糙面做非正交坐标变换以拉平边界，把几何粗糙度转化为与介电起伏同一Helmholtz方程的体源，并用同一格林函数传播，从而无需等效面电流与场不连续性的额外规定。

Result: 几何部分振幅对任意入射角、介电常数与剖面函数均精确复现小扰动核；得到两个同极化通道的干涉项及显式表面-介电相关系数；完全相关时偏离可加性达20%，且随相关系数变号；该偏离受相关层深度的平衡条件与相干条件支配，无角度特征，仅使同极化比移动小于0.1 dB。

Conclusion: 面-体干涉可被加性反演吸收而难以察觉，但会使反演得到的介电起伏振幅产生数十个百分点的偏差。

Abstract: We compute the first-order incoherent backscattering cross-section of a rough surface over a half-space with randomly fluctuating permittivity, when the two random fields are correlated, so that surface and volume scattering are not additive. A non-orthogonal coordinate transformation flattens the rough boundary and turns the geometric roughness into volume sources of the same Helmholtz equation as the dielectric fluctuations, propagated by the same Green's function: both mechanisms and their interference follow from one calculation, with no equivalent surface currents and no prescription for the field discontinuity. The geometric part of the amplitude reproduces the small-perturbation kernel exactly, for any incidence angle, permittivity and profile function. The interference term is obtained for both co-polarized channels, with an explicit surface-dielectric correlation coefficient. The departure from additivity reaches twenty percent at full correlation, changes sign with that coefficient, and is governed by a balance condition and a coherence condition on the depth of the correlated layer. It carries no angular signature and shifts the co-polarized ratio by less than a tenth of a decibel, so an additive inversion absorbs it, biasing the retrieved amplitude of the dielectric fluctuations by tens of percent.

</details>


### [246] [Near-field Ultrafast Nondiffractive Laser Nanoscale Cutting of Glass with Quasi-null Interface Roughness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39928)
*Rajeev Dwivedi,Anton Rudenko,Guodong Zhang,Xxx Sedao,Yaya Lefkir,Ciro D'Amico,Guanghua Cheng,Razvan Stoian*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用近场增强零阶贝塞尔-高斯光束对铝硅酸盐玻璃进行单步切割，获得残余界面粗糙度低于5 nm的光学级表面，其去除机制符合Hertz-Knudsen型蒸发，实现接近几个分子层的逐层去除精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快激光玻璃切割通常由爆炸性材料去除主导，这会限制可达到的表面粗糙度，难以直接获得光学级表面。

Method: 采用近场增强的0阶贝塞尔-高斯光束对铝硅酸盐玻璃进行单步切割，并对局部热动力学进行定量分析以判别材料去除机制。

Result: 实现了残余界面粗糙度低于5 nm的无后处理光学级表面；该界面质量无法用相爆炸、熔融喷出或断裂主导去除等传统激光烧蚀机制解释，而与Hertz-Knudsen型蒸发过程一致，可实现接近几个分子层的逐层去除。

Conclusion: 纳米尺度光学限域从根本上改变了透明材料对超快激光辐照的响应，为近原子精度的确定性激光加工提供了新途径。

Abstract: Generally, ultrafast laser glass dicing is governed by explosive material removal, which restricts the achievable surface roughness. Here we report single-step dicing of aluminosilicate glass with residual interface roughness below 5 nm, producing optical-quality surfaces without post-processing, using near-field-enhanced 0th-order Bessel-Gauss beams. This exceptional interface quality cannot be explained by a conventional laser ablation mechanism, such as phase explosion, melt expulsion, or fracture-dominated material removal. Instead, quantitative analysis of local thermal dynamics indicates that material removal is consistent with a Hertz-Knudsen-type evaporation process, enabling layer-by-layer removal with precision approaching a few molecular layers. These results suggest that nanoscale optical confinement fundamentally alters the response of transparent materials to ultrafast laser irradiation and establish a route toward deterministic laser processing with near-atomic precision.

</details>


### [247] [Confocal imaging from biphoton correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39954)
*Euan Millar,Emma Pearce,Daniele Faccio,Miles J. Padgett*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用纠缠双光子的空间关联实现量子共聚焦层切成像，无需针孔与机械扫描，且轴向响应比经典共聚焦窄√2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共聚焦显微依赖逐点扫描和物理针孔来获得光学层切，限制了成像速度，因此需要一种无需扫描、可并行层切的共聚焦成像机制。

Method: 在宽场成像系统中利用纠缠双光子态的位置-动量空间关联，通过空间分辨的符合测量抑制离焦平面贡献，从而在无物理针孔、无机械扫描条件下实现全视场并行的光学层切。

Result: 实现了无需针孔和机械扫描的并行光学层切；由于位置-动量纠缠，其轴向响应比经典共聚焦成像窄√2倍。

Conclusion: 双光子关联可作为改进共聚焦成像的机制，实现并行、无针孔、无机械扫描的光学层切。

Abstract: Confocal microscopy provides optical sectioning for three-dimensional imaging but conventionally relies on point-by-point scanning and physical pinholes, limiting imaging speed. Here, we demonstrate that confocal sectioning can instead arise directly from quantum measurement, using spatial correlations in entangled biphoton states. Spatially resolved coincidence measurements in a widefield imaging system suppress contributions from out-of-focus planes, producing optical sectioning in parallel across the whole field of view without physical pinholes or mechanical scanning. In addition, this quantum approach obtains an axial response that, as a result of position-momentum entanglement, is $\sqrt{2}$-times narrower than that of classical confocal imaging. Our work establishes biphoton correlations as a mechanism for improved confocal imaging and enables parallel, pinhole-free optical sectioning without mechanical scanning.

</details>


### [248] [Design of Mechanically Compliant Membrane Reflectors for Giant Optical Phase Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40354)
*Lior Michaeli,Harry A. Atwater*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文预测了一种蛇形弹簧氮化硅蹦床膜结构可产生巨大反射相位响应（263 rad W⁻¹，等效n₂,eff = 5.4×10⁻⁷ m² W⁻¹），并提出强度-范围-孔径评价指标，显示其在同类机械谐振器中兼具高柔顺性、实用光学可达性和全2π相移下99.85%的小信号响应保持率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光机械系统中的辐射压力可通过移动柔性反射镜改变反射光相位，从而产生光学非线性，但如何在保持高柔顺性的同时实现实用光学可达性与大相位动态范围，缺乏统一评价标准与优选结构。

Method: 基于已测量的器件属性，对带蛇形弹簧的氮化硅蹦床膜进行建模与预测，计算其反射相位响应率与等效非线性折射率；提出强度-范围-孔径（strength-range-aperture）指标，并在统一的直接反射测试协议下与已有机械谐振器进行对比；同时开展光学与热学协同设计分析。

Result: 预测得到263 rad W⁻¹的巨大相位响应率和5.4×10⁻⁷ m² W⁻¹的器件等效n₂,eff；该蹦床膜在统一协议比较中表现突出，兼具高柔顺性与实用光学可达性，并在完整2π反射相移过程中保持99.85%的小信号相位响应率。

Conclusion: 该氮化硅蹦床膜是机械-光学相位元件中的优异候选，其实际实现依赖光学与热学协同设计；所提出的强度-范围-孔径框架可为未来机械-光学相位元件的设计优先级提供指导。

Abstract: Optical nonlinearities arise when the response of a photonic system depends on light intensity. Radiation pressure can create such a nonlinearity by moving a mechanically compliant reflector, thereby shifting the phase of the reflected light. Based on measured properties, we predict a giant phase responsivity of 263 rad W$^{-1}$ and a device-equivalent $n_{2,\mathrm{eff}} = 5.4 \times 10^{-7}$ m$^2$ W$^{-1}$ for a silicon nitride membrane trampoline with serpentine springs. We introduce strength-range-aperture metrics for reflective phase elements. Among reported mechanical resonators compared under a common direct-reflection protocol, this trampoline is an outlier, combining high compliance, practical optical accessibility, and 99.85% retention of its small-signal phase responsivity through a full $2π$ reflected-phase shift. Practical implementation requires optical and thermal co-design. Our framework indicates design priorities for future mechano-optical phase elements.

</details>


### [249] [Quantum-Limited Optical Vector Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25950)
*Karthik Dasigi,Pavel A. Dmitriev,Kah Jen Wo,Fumiya Hanamura,Lingda Kong,Steven Touzard*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自由运行干涉仪作为光学矢量分析仪（OVA），具备20 THz频率范围，通过抑制相位噪声在fW功率下达到单位信噪比，并用于表征薄膜铌酸锂微环谐振器，在远小于1个循环光子下获得SNR>1，测得Q值超过500万。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OVA的灵敏度比标准量子极限（SQL）低数个数量级，而集成光子学与光学定位等新兴技术需要接近SQL且宽带的高灵敏度测量，以精确表征脆弱、非线性或强散射的目标。

Method: 采用频率范围达20 THz的自由运行干涉仪作为OVA，并引入新的相位噪声抑制方法，从而在fW级功率下实现单位信噪比（SNR=1）。

Result: 将该技术用于定量评估薄膜铌酸锂（TFLN）微环谐振器的制造质量。表征在循环光子数远小于1的情况下获得SNR>1，并测得品质因数Q超过500万，明确归因于低内部损耗。

Conclusion: 该自由运行干涉仪OVA在宽带、近SQL灵敏度测量方面具有优势，可用于脆弱的集成光子器件的低损耗、非破坏性高精度表征。

Abstract: Optical Vector Analysers (OVA) are critical for emerging technologies such as integrated photonics and optical positioning. Achieving a sensitivity near the Standard Quantum Limit (SQL) while acquiring a wide spectrum allows an accurate measurement of targets that are fragile, non-linear, or that scatter most of the probe light away. Existing OVAs operate with a sensitivity orders of magnitude below the SQL. In this paper, we use a free-running interferometer with a frequency range of 20 THz as an OVA. We introduce novel methods to mitigate the phase noise and obtain a unit signal-to-noise ratio for powers at the fW level. We apply this technique towards quantifying the fabrication quality of microring resonators in thin-film Lithium Niobate. Our characterisation yields a signal-to-noise ratio above 1 with much less than 1 circulating photon and reveals a quality factor above 5 millions, unambiguously attributed to low internal losses.

</details>
